Father’s thoughts were the same as mine. On top of that, he pointed out a part I had not even considered.

“Narrow the grain on the sword handle slightly. It seems more slippery than before.”

“Understood, Cult Leader.”

“You’ve worked hard.”

“I will contact you again once it is completed.”

Father left the forge, and after I greeted Hall Master Gwak, I followed Father out. The men standing along the path Father passed all stopped moving at once and bowed at the waist. Their respect and fear toward Father were like the scorching fire they handled.

“When did you arrive?”

“I just arrived moments ago.”

“And the Fist Demon?”

“He’ll arrive in about half a quarter-hour.”

“Did you not come together?”

“At the very end, he suggested a lightness arts race.”

After a short pause, I said to Father,

“I have become the Fist Demon’s disciple.”

Father’s steps stopped for a moment. I had told Father in advance before, and had even received his permission, but for some reason I felt tense.

What would Father feel?

Would he be displeased? Or indifferent? Or would he think it was a good thing?

No matter how hard I tried to look clearly at Father, it was truly difficult to know his heart. This matter, too, would certainly carry some meaning for Father, but Father did not reveal any emotion about it at all.

“And the fist art?”

“I received the full transmission of the Thunderbolt Asura Fist up to the sixth fist.”

“It seems that man still does not know your impudence. He should have watched how you behaved before teaching you the final two fists.”

“Father. It is because masters indulge in that sort of pointless spite that martial arts end up being lost.”

We crossed the Grand Training Ground and walked toward the Heavenly Demon Hall. The demonic practitioners passing by bowed to Father.

“Father. I have a favor to ask. A very important matter.”

In the middle of the Grand Training Ground, we stopped walking. I wanted to say it here, in this wide-open place, not in the Heavenly Demon Hall. This was a matter of the Central Plains, a matter outside our cult.

“I want to kill Yaryul Han of the Sado Alliance. Please permit it.”

It was a situation where a thunderous scolding could have fallen.

But instead of a scolding, Father’s characteristic sneer formed on his lips.

“Absolutely not!”

He had even added absolutely before not. With that firm rejection, Father turned and walked away.

“I will work from now on to receive your permission. That is what I wanted to tell you.”

Father walked on without a word. His back was speaking. No matter what effort you make, I will not permit it.

I had declared war on Father. From now on, I had to persuade Father somehow. Perhaps receiving his permission would be more difficult than killing Yaryul Han.

In any case, as I watched Father’s back walking toward the Heavenly Demon Hall, I felt as though I had truly returned to the cult.

“Ah! Home really is the best!”

I had returned to my enjoyable home, where giant demon statues swung swords and sabers in every direction.

Chapter 206: The One Who Refuses.
---------------------------------
After meeting my father, I went straight to the Medical Devil’s clinic.

He happened to be treating a critically ill patient. The patient must have been badly injured, for the entire area was awash in blood. The first scent I had noticed upon my return had been the metallic smell of the Iron Workshop; the next was the smell of blood.

“Is there anything I can help you with?”

“I’m fine.”

I stood at a distance and watched him work. Covered in blood, the Medical Devil was doing everything in his power to save the patient’s life.

Whether it was the Iron Workshop’s Master Guo or the Medical Devil, people who had reached the pinnacle of their respective fields gave off a similar feeling. Their determination and concentration, their resolve to save a life no matter what, resembled Master Guo’s when he was determined to forge a fine sword.

At last, the Medical Devil finished the treatment that had felt like an entire battle and walked over to me.

“Have you been waiting long?”

“Not at all. Is it really all right for me to watch the miracle of a dying man coming back to life for free?”

“He was going to live anyway.”

After going to so much trouble to save him, how could that be true? It was the good fortune of having the Medical Devil as his physician that had saved his life.

The Medical Devil washed his hands and face with water he had drawn in the corner of the clinic. Seeing his painfully thin arms made me feel sorry for him.

“I’m sorry I haven’t visited more often, using my busy schedule as an excuse.”

“Don’t be. Seeing you once in a while is enough. I’ve been hearing plenty about you. Every time I do, you make me grateful that I chose correctly.”

“It’s all thanks to you, Medical Immortal.”

“How are your eyes?”

“No problem at all. If anything, I can see too well.”

“That’s a relief.”

Once I had made up my mind to kill Yelu Han, I wanted to see him.

One reason I had come to the Medical Devil was to gather my courage after hearing my father’s words—“Absolutely impossible.” If I followed my heart, I would tell him that I intended to kill Yelu Han starting now, but that was something I had to do only after receiving my father’s permission.

“Since you’ve come all this way, have a cup of tea before you go.”

“Gladly.”

I talked with the Medical Devil about this and that. I had wanted to tell him everything I had been unable to mention until now, but another patient arrived, and I had no choice but to leave.

“I’ll come visit again.”

“You’re busy. There’s no need.”

I would definitely come back soon.

* * *

The next person I went to see was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had planted the Blood Heaven Great Saber in the courtyard and was sitting with his back against it, practicing qi circulation. The heat of his training still lingered in the air around him. Even while I had gone out to fool around with the Fist Demon, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had never stopped training in martial arts.

“Elder! I’m back.”

In contrast to my cheerful greeting, as though I had returned home in glory, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon received it with the same indifference he might have shown someone he had seen the day before.

“If you’ve returned from a long journey, you should rest. Why have you come here?”

“Why do you think? No matter who else I might neglect, I have to come greet you. And there are some things I need to report, too.”

“What report?”

I sat down opposite the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“I have become the Fist Demon’s disciple.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon glanced at me but said nothing.

“What is this utterly unenthusiastic reaction?”

“What kind of reaction were you expecting?”

“You became someone’s disciple without asking my permission? You should have shouted in anger. Or said, ‘If you’re going to become a disciple, you should become mine.’ Or gotten jealous.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon then said something unexpected.

“There is no Demon Venerable other than the Fist Demon who would take you as a disciple.”

“What do you mean by that?”

“Personality-wise.”

“What about you, Elder?”

“I like disciples like Daeryong. Disciples I can work as hard as I please and teach.”

“Ah! Poor Inspector Seo.”

“That’s the price he pays for grabbing another man by the trouser leg.”

He was referring to the beginning of their master-and-disciple relationship, when Seo Daeryong had gotten drunk at the Pleasure Tavern and grabbed the Blood Heaven Blade Demon by the trouser leg.

Of course, I knew that despite talking like that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon cared deeply for Seo Daeryong. Otherwise, he would not have chosen him as the next Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“If I took you as my disciple, I’d develop a stress-induced illness in less than a month.”

“I can’t deny that.”

“On the other hand, the Fist Demon wouldn’t.”

“Why is that?”

“Because he has no intention of driving anyone hard in the first place. If the Cult Leader and I are fire, he is water.”

I had thought the Fist Demon was fire, like my father, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon saw him as water. Perhaps he was right. Maybe being fire and water was precisely why he got along so well with my father.

“To be honest, I was worried you might feel hurt.”

“Worried that a narrow-minded old man might sulk?”

“Narrow-minded? You simply care about me very deeply.”

“That would have been true before. The me from before I met you would certainly have felt hurt.”

“And now?”

“Now I’ve changed. Because of you.”

“Because of me?”

“Do you think a narrow-minded old man like that could go with you? How can you say such a thing?”

“……!”

I had never dreamed he thought that way.

“After forming a bond with you, I began to look back on myself. You constantly spurred me on. At first, you irritated me. Later, you astonished me. And now, you are making me move.”

“Is your training part of that as well?”

“Yes. I have a feeling that if I keep swaggering around, relying only on the skills I already possess, I’ll be swept away by the wind and rain. You have already begun your ascension.”

When I first met him, he had told me that a dragon ascending to the heavens was bringing wind and rain with it. At the time, he had meant it as a metaphor. Now, those words had become reality.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon gazed at me with a serious look in his eyes, and I did not make some flippant remark about being a loach as I had in the past. His words were right. If we swaggered around relying on what we could already do, we would all die.

That was why we had to keep moving forward.

“I’ll use the first of the thirty-three requests I can make of you.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon blinked and asked,

“Thirty-three requests? Did we ever make such an arrangement?”

“Of course not. I decided it myself because I think I’ll need your help thirty-three times over the course of my life. Before you grant all thirty-three requests, you’re not even allowed to catch a cold. Understood?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon gave a helpless little snort of laughter. It was a selfish demand, but it also contained my wish for him to live a long life.

“What is your request?”

“Please persuade my father.”

“The Cult Leader? How?”

“I want to obtain his permission to kill Yelu Han of the Evil Path Alliance.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not look surprised. He was so calm that I wondered whether it was really all right for him not to be surprised at all.

“Why aren’t you surprised?”

“Because you’re a madman. I figured you might do something like this.”

“Isn’t this the wind and rain brought by an ascending dragon?”

“No. This is simply madness.”

“Please let me commit this madness just once.”

“First, tell me why. Why are you trying to kill him?”

I told the Blood Heaven Blade Demon that Jisaeng, the Golden Pig, one of Yelu Han’s subordinates, was using the Immortal Vegetable to lead the young people of the martial world to ruin.

I could not tell him about the Medical Devil’s revenge. That was an intensely personal matter.

“If that’s your reason, the Cult Leader won’t grant permission.”

“That’s why I’m asking you.”

After a brief pause, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon asked,

“If he gives permission…… are you confident you can kill him?”

“I’ll have to try.”

“His subordinates are formidable enough, quite apart from him. And if things go wrong, this could escalate into a war between our Holy Cult and the Evil Path Alliance.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was worried as well that war might break out.

“Is it worth taking such a risk?”

“These young people have only just entered the world, yet those thoroughly corrupted men deceive them, force them to commit all manner of evil, and eventually send them to die as cannon fodder or drive them to take their own lives. You ask whether it’s worth doing? Yes. It’s worth it.”

Even without my promise to the Medical Devil, Yelu Han’s evil deeds would continue.

In my previous life, I had been too preoccupied with obtaining the materials for the Grand Ritual to pay much attention to the Evil Path Alliance. Even so, rumors had continually reached my ears about the crimes committed by Yelu Han’s subordinates.

If fate had placed me before him at this point in time, I believed it was right to deal with him now.

“Why was someone like you born in our Holy Cult? Why weren’t you born as the son of the Martial Alliance Leader?”

“Perhaps so I wouldn’t have to fight you, Elder.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon let out a soft sigh.

“There is someone in the Evil Path Alliance who can help me.”

“Who?”

“The heir to the Evil Path Alliance, Bisein.”

Bisein would certainly help me, if only to repay the debt from our past connection, and he would make an excellent ally.

After pondering for a moment, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said the same thing the Fist Demon had.

“To change the Cult Leader’s mind, all eight Demon Venerables will have to step forward. If you can demonstrate enough ability to accomplish that, the Cult Leader will have grounds to relent of his own accord.”

Because these were the opinions of the two people who knew my father best, my heart grew heavy.

“It’ll be difficult to gather all Eight Demon Venerables. The Demon Buddha and the Poison King support my older brother, so they won’t help me right away.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon pulled the saber from the ground and said,

“Are those two truly the only ones who will refuse?”

As he went inside, he added,

“Your real relationships with people may be beginning only now.”

* * *

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s prediction proved correct.

The One-Blossom Sword Venerable rejected me outright. Her refusal was as clean as her swordsmanship.

“That won’t do.”

The reason for her refusal was equally straightforward.

“Second Young Master, I’ll be honest with you because it’s you. I don’t want to fall out of the Cult Leader’s favor over something like this.”

I could understand. Ever since her youth, no one had respected my father more than she did. She had no desire to approach him with a request he would absolutely refuse.

“I understand, Senior.”

To be honest, I had expected the One-Blossom Sword Venerable to refuse to some extent. I felt I was one step farther from her than from the other Demon Venerables. Apart from sparring, I had never had the opportunity to form a deep personal relationship with her.

“Are you disappointed?”

If I answered too stiffly, I would truly hurt her feelings.

“Yes, I’m disappointed. That’s too much!”

The One-Blossom Sword Venerable smiled.

“Please understand.”

Only then did I smile as well, half joking and half serious.

“I understand. Is my father some ordinary man? I know how much pressure you’re under, Senior.”

“Thank you for understanding.”

“I’m sorry, but this may not be the end of it. I may come back to try to persuade you again.”

“I figured as much.”

Yet even obtaining the support of someone I considered a step closer to me than her proved difficult.

The next person I visited was the Drunken Demon. As soon as I asked him to persuade my father, he suddenly began pretending to be drunk.

“Ah! The alcohol’s suddenly gone to my head.”

The Drunken Demon buried his head in the drinking table and pretended to fall asleep.

When I began drinking silently, he said from where he lay facedown,

“I’ll grant any other request. What do you want? I’ll buy you drinks for the rest of your life if that’s what you want. Do you need money? Shall I go to the Valley of Evil and beat those bastards up again?”

“Forget all that. Persuade my father.”

The Drunken Demon, who had been pressing his face into the table, suddenly sprang up and leaped into the lake with a splash.

He swam wildly from one end of the lake to the other like a madman, then climbed back up to the pavilion. He returned to his original seat with water dripping from his body and said with a grim expression,

“I’d rather quit drinking.”

The Drunken Demon quitting alcohol? I could only let out a hollow laugh.

“Is it that difficult?”

“What will the Cult Leader think when I go to him? ‘Look at this bastard. He knows perfectly well that I oppose it, yet he still comes to me and tries to persuade me?’ Besides, the Cult Leader already dislikes me.”

“Why does Father dislike you?”

“He does. The Cult Leader dislikes drunkards, and he dislikes people who use poison, too. He has very clear likes and dislikes. I can’t do it! Absolutely not! I’m sorry, little brother.”

I did not torment him any further. It was one thing if it concerned something else, but this involved my father. How could I not understand? Even if the reason had nothing to do with my father, I could not force someone to do something they disliked.

“If you’re sorry, then quit drinking for ten days starting today!”

“I’ll quit! I’m sorry!”

“Why are you sorry, Brother? I’m the one who should apologize for pestering you with an unreasonable request.”

“This mustn’t damage our relationship!”

“Brother, our relationship won’t suffer because I made a request or because you refused it.”

“Then why would it suffer?”

“When you aren’t honest. You could simply say, ‘I can’t do it because of this.’ Now imagine you agreed just to humor me, even though you didn’t want to do it. If you tried to do something you hated, you’d end up irritated and furious. In the end, you might even come to hate me.”

The Drunken Demon nodded with an expression that said he could imagine exactly that happening.

“You did well to refuse, Brother. I’ll be making plenty of requests in the future, too. Come with me to beat someone up. I’m lonely, so drink with me. Help me persuade that person. You have to be this honest with me then, too. You must never think, ‘I refused him last time, so I should force myself to do it this time.’ That’s when our relationship will truly fall apart. Got it?”

The Drunken Demon stared at me quietly before saying,

“Starting today, you’re the older brother. I mean it.”

“I refuse. What are you talking about, telling the younger brother who lives for the fun of teasing his older brother to call you that? Big Brother!”

“Good grief, what a shameful older brother I am.”

“Being afraid of my father isn’t something to be ashamed of, whatever else might be. I’m leaving. I’ll probably come again over this matter. See you then.”

And with that, I left the Drunken Demon’s residence.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had been right. Perhaps persuading the Demon Venerables would be more difficult than persuading my father.

I could not even persuade the One-Blossom Sword Venerable or the Drunken Demon, whom I had considered allies, yet my father would grant permission only if all eight of them went to speak with him? I let out a deep sigh, but this was no time to despair. It was time to find a way.

The next place I headed was where my comrade in battle was staying. I was curious to see how he would react.

Chapter 207: I Will Return the Courtesy
---------------------------------------
When I entered the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon’s room, I saw a long line drawn across the wall.

The Utterly Evil Smiling Demon had his back to that line and was looking at the opposite wall.

“I definitely prefer the color of this wall.”

At my joke, the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon looked at me. My gaze met the eyes within the holes of his mask. To think I would ever see the emotion of welcome in those eyeholes.

The Utterly Evil Smiling Demon and I communicated only through the light in those eyes, yet we shared a deeper communion than I did with anyone else.

“Your mask has changed.”

“As expected, Second Young Master noticed at once.”

The reason it had changed was unexpected.

“Blue Mask made several masks and brought them to me. As he handed them over, he said he wanted to live a new life now. He said he would go to Beautiful Heart.”

Blue Mask had finally accepted the position of first squad leader of the Ghost Shadow Unit.

“So did you cut off Blue Mask’s arm?”

He was the one who had once said he would cut off even an arm before sending Blue Mask away, and that he would never let him go otherwise.

“I did not.”

“Why?”

“Did you not tell me to be sure to inform you before I cut it off? But since you were away from the cult, Second Young Master, I could not cut it.”

How could that be the reason? He had let Blue Mask go so that he could live the life he wanted.

Well done, Soma. If things had gone on as they were, Blue Mask would never have risen to the rank of Demon Supreme and would have lived an unhappy life until he grew old.

“That was splendid of you.”

“Do you truly think so?”

“Yes. I truly think it was splendid.”

“Now that I have received such praise, I suppose I will not be able to cut off any arms in the future either.”

The two eyes within the mask’s holes were smiling. At least in his relationship with me, he was not utterly evil; he was the Smiling Demon.

“I heard you left the cult with the Fist Demon.”

“I went out to play hooky and came back as the Fist Demon’s disciple.”

At the words that I had become the Fist Demon’s disciple, the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon sprang up from his seat.

“Then you must be able to fight even better now!”

The Utterly Evil Smiling Demon had no interest in whether the Fist Demon was my master or not. He was excited by the fact that I had grown stronger.

“Yes, I have become a little stronger!”

The Utterly Evil Smiling Demon was also the person to whom I could say such things most comfortably. Just as Father and the Fist Demon had kept company in their youth, such was the relationship between the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon and me.

“Lord Soma, I have a request.”

Inwardly, I was trembling. How would the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon take my request?

“I want to kill Yelu Han of the Evil Path Alliance. To do that, I need Father’s permission. Please persuade Father.”

The Utterly Evil Smiling Demon answered without hesitation.

“Very well.”

He asked nothing. Not why Yelu Han had to die, nor how I would kill him.

His attitude, as if he would have granted any request I made, made something surge in my heart.

The one and only person in this world who would grant it simply because it was my request.

I was happy. It had been a truly long time since I had felt such pure joy. I was so happy I felt the urge to leap up and down.

“Thank you, truly.”

Though I understood why the other Demon Supremes had refused, perhaps a part of me had felt disappointed. Seeing how good I felt now, that must have been the case.

I did not know you would accept so easily.

I was about to say those words, then stopped. Somehow, it seemed that no words could express my gratitude toward him.

Instead, I conveyed my gratitude in another way.

“If permission for this matter is granted, please help me then.”

Soma, let’s go. Let us go fight magnificently together again. That is the only thing I can do for you. And… thank you, truly.

Seeing my heated gaze, the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon laughed in good spirits.

“Good.”

And the more dangerous our battle was, the more it would set his blood boiling.

“Who has agreed so far?”

“The only person who has clearly said he would persuade Father is you, Lord Soma.”

“I am number one?”

“That is right. Lord Soma, you are the first.”

After all, neither the Blood Heaven Saber Demon nor the Fist Demon had yet directly said they would persuade him. Of course, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon would step forward. The Fist Demon would as well. For now, there were three Demon Supremes who would definitely help.

“For some reason, that pleases me.”

“Thank you for becoming my first.”

I looked at the long line drawn on the opposite wall. Soma’s gaze followed mine.

I hope that wall remains for a long time.

I did not say that either. Even without saying it, he would surely feel the same.

Later, when I became the Heavenly Demon, I intended to remove that wall and hang it in the Heavenly Demon Hall.

* * *

When I went to see the Fist Demon, he was standing below the cliff.

I stood side by side with him.

Now that he had returned from our journey, did this cliff begin to look different in his eyes?

“The Utterly Evil Smiling Demon said he would step forward for me.”

At that, the Fist Demon looked at me in surprise.

“Soma did?”

The Fist Demon seemed to find it unexpected that it was the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon, of all people.

“Are you two close?”

“We are.”

“He is not an easy man to get close to.”

“If you put it that way, Master, are you an easy person?”

Looking up at the cliff, I continued.

“You are someone who intends to bring this cliff down with a single blow.”

The Fist Demon’s gaze followed mine toward the cliff. The wall we were looking at now was not this cliff. It was a wall higher and harder than this.

“The Cult Leader will not permit this matter. To you, saving those young people may be important, but to the Cult Leader it is not important at all. To be honest, it is the same for me. It will be the same for the other Demon Supremes too.”

I understood Father and the Demon Supremes. They had always been such people. Even the way they appeared now had changed so much that one could almost call them different people.

Even so, the reason I was working so hard for change was because I believed that only when we changed would our martial arts change, and only then would our fate change. If we simply passed everything by as in my previous life.

Because they were people unimportant to me. Because it had nothing to do with me. If I lived a life in which I passed everything by, as I had before my regression… I felt that I would be swept away once again by Hwa Mugi, who would appear as if destined.

Just as, Master, if a person like you does not change, you cannot bring down this cliff.

“As we discussed in the snowy mountains, the Cult Leader dreams of unifying murim. If you kill Yelu Han, a variable will arise in the grand design the Cult Leader is drawing. There is no way he will easily permit it.”

“Then all the more reason to create a variable. I intend to stop Father’s unification of murim at all costs.”

I was firm, and the Fist Demon silently stared at that stubbornness.

“Will you persuade Father for me? Master.”

The Fist Demon sank into his final deliberation. Why would the Fist Demon not wish to avoid clashing with Father? No, because he was the Fist Demon, that feeling would be strongest of all.

At last, the Fist Demon nodded.

“Very well.”

“Thank you, Master.”

This is why. Why I chose you as my master. Because you are a man who does not retreat, no matter how difficult it is.

I had not received an answer from the Blood Heaven Saber Demon, but I would not go seek him out again to confirm it. I was certain he would help me.

And my certainty was accurate. The first person to go meet Father was the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

* * *

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon walked along the Path of Blood and stopped below the Taishi chair.

“It has been a long time.”

At the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s respectful greeting, the Heavenly Demon Geom Ujin said to him,

“Are you the one opening the first salvo?”

Geom Ujin had already guessed why the Blood Heaven Saber Demon had come.

“What else can I do? A young man says he will attempt something great, so even an old mouth like mine should lend a word.”

“When a young fellow tries to cause reckless trouble, should we not, as elders, stop him?”

“That is true. When I think back to our own youth, we were truly immature then. But when I think on it again, was there ever a time when we were as passionate as we were back then?”

“Do you have no passion now?”

“If these traces left after burning are passion, then I do have some.”

Their words passed calmly between them, but the Heavenly Demon Hall was filled with taut energy.

Geom Ujin rose from the Taishi chair and slowly descended the steps.

“Shall we walk a little?”

“Gladly.”

Geom Ujin walked the Path of Blood with the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and left the Heavenly Demon Hall. The two of them walked together through the front courtyard of the Heavenly Demon Hall.

“He asked me in the middle of the Grand Training Ground over there. He asked me to permit him to kill Yelu Han. It was not a request, but a declaration.”

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon asked in a slightly more comfortable tone,

“A child is one’s enemy, is he not?”

A smile appeared on Geom Ujin’s lips, one he would never show Geom Mugeuk.

“You ought to experience it too. What a pity.”

“That is the one area in which I am happier than you, Cult Leader. Please do not say such a thing even in jest.”

There had been a time when the two of them had conversed far more comfortably than this. If he had known the years would pass so quickly, he would have spent a little more time with the Cult Leader. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon felt a renewed pang of regret. What on earth had he done with all that time?

“I can understand the other Demon Supremes, but I never thought you would fall so deeply for that boy. What on earth did you like so much that you went over to him?”

“I have thought about that many times myself, but this is the only answer I can give you. Your son has a strange charm.”

“A strange charm.”

This time, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon spoke.

“Did you not feel it? I thought you would have felt it too, Cult Leader.”

“I called it cheap sentimentality, the kind that gets one killed.”

Geom Ujin did not mention that his description of cheap sentimentality had changed to innocent sentimentality.

“I have fallen completely for that cheap sentimentality.”

A faint smile also formed on the Blood Heaven Saber Demon’s lips. He had come on a sensitive and important matter, but this conversation, after so long, felt pleasant.

“Cult Leader, why not simply lose to him this once?”

At the casually tossed words, Geom Ujin shook his head firmly.

“In the future, if Bi Sain ascends to the position of Evil Path Alliance Leader, Yelu Han will become a lasting source of trouble for the Evil Path Alliance. The current Evil Path Alliance Leader may deal with Yelu Han before he dies. There is no particular reason for us to eliminate him.”

“In exchange, Bi Sain will owe the Second Young Master a great debt through this matter. As much as we lose, there will also be something gained.”

“Those of the Evil Path only carry grudges for a long time. They are not the sort to remember favors.”

“But the Second Young Master will remember. He will remember that his father made a great concession for him.”

Geom Ujin stood with his hands clasped behind his back and silently looked out over the grounds of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. The Blood Heaven Saber Demon did not press him further. With this, he had said all he had to say.

Then, a person appeared there.

It was the Fist Demon, who had been on his way to the Heavenly Demon Hall.

“Are you all determined to fight me in rounds?”

At Geom Ujin’s joke, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon laughed and replied,

“I am curious as to why those great fists went over as well.”

Meanwhile, the Fist Demon approached them.

“Have you been well?”

After the Fist Demon respectfully greeted Geom Ujin, he also greeted the Blood Heaven Saber Demon.

“You came first.”

“I thought the Fist Demon would come as well. Ah, congratulations on taking the Second Young Master as your disciple.”

At that, Geom Ujin interjected,

“Is that something to congratulate him for? It is something to console him over.”

The gazes of the Fist Demon and Geom Ujin intertwined in midair. It was a look filled with deep trust that only the two of them could feel. Because of that, this place was difficult for the Fist Demon. Fortunately, with the Blood Heaven Saber Demon present, the atmosphere did not turn too serious.

“I can roughly guess why you have come, so let us launch a joint attack.”

“I am not sure whether a joint attack is the wise choice. Our opponent is someone who grows stronger the stronger his opponent is.”

With an expression that seemed to say, And you know that!, Geom Ujin asked the Fist Demon,

“Why did you go over to that boy?”

“I must have lost my mind after playing hooky for the first time in my life.”

Geom Ujin was not satisfied with that answer.

“Tell me the real reason.”

The Fist Demon fell into thought for a moment, then revealed his honest feelings. It was an answer they had not expected.

“I suppose I had been lonely all this time.”

“!”

Both Geom Ujin and the Blood Heaven Saber Demon were startled. They truly had not expected the word lonely to come from the Fist Demon’s mouth.

“On the way back, at the end, we had a qinggong race. He was so fast that I could not catch up. As I trudged along alone, do you know what I felt? I was bored. For the first time in my life, I felt the emotion of boredom.”

Looking at Geom Ujin, the Fist Demon added,

“So I suppose that is why I went over to him.”

The gazes of the Fist Demon and Geom Ujin intertwined in the air. The Fist Demon carefully said what he had been thinking about all the way here.

“He is my first disciple, and as his master, I want to be of help to him, so I have come to commit this discourtesy. I apologize, Cult Leader.”

The Fist Demon respectfully bent at the waist and apologized. Since his loyalty to the Heavenly Demon was incomparable to that of any other Demon Supreme, Geom Ujin offered no reproach.

Just then, Geom Ujin’s gaze turned beyond the Fist Demon’s shoulder.

“Even if I say I can somehow understand the two of you, I cannot make any sense of that man.”

Someone was walking toward them. Wearing a white mask, it was the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon.

After the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon respectfully greeted the Heavenly Demon, he also exchanged greetings with the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Fist Demon.

Then he said something they had not expected at all.

“I was number one, but the two of you cut in line ahead of me.”

It was already incomprehensible that he had stepped forward, and now even such a joke?

The Blood Heaven Saber Demon spoke first, representing the feelings of the three who had been there before him.

“It seems that anyone who becomes involved with the Second Young Master goes mad like this.”

Geom Ujin asked the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon,

“What on earth is your reason?”

The moment he received that question, the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon recalled the moment when the Second Young Master had supported his slipping mask. That was all.

Had someone else asked, he would not have answered, but because it was Geom Ujin’s question, the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon answered thus.

“The Second Young Master is a man who knows courtesy.”

At those words, not only Geom Ujin, but the Blood Heaven Saber Demon and the Fist Demon were also startled. The word courtesy had come from the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon’s mouth? Since when had the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon cared about courtesy?

The three of them could feel it. The courtesy of which the Utterly Evil one spoke was not ordinary etiquette, but contained a more fundamental meaning. It was courtesy toward a person.

“I intend to answer the courtesy the Second Young Master showed me.”

The Utterly Evil Smiling Demon turned around and removed his mask. Then he put on the new mask he had brought in his robes and slowly turned back.

It was not the pure white mask he had worn until now. It was a chilling mask of a laughing evil ghost. The eyes, painted red, were slit long like a demon’s, and the crimson mouth rose all the way to the ears in a grin. This was the Undying Ghost Mask that the Utterly Evil Smiling Demon wore when going to war.

“Please grant the Second Young Master’s request.”

The Utterly Evil Smiling Demon had come to this place with the heart of going to a battlefield. He was prepared for the Heavenly Demon’s wrath, and he was willing to fall from his favor. He had come here today prepared to die.

As if saying that this much was necessary to persuade the Cult Leader, the two eyes inside the mask shone more intensely than ever before.

“This is the first and last request I will ever make of you.”

Chapter 208: The Sword Venerable Won’t Come
-------------------------------------------
A heavy silence fell.

Saying that it was his final request had been the strongest card the Extreme Evil Little Demon possessed.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon had never expected the Extreme Evil Little Demon to come out like this. It was not a situation where he could lighten the mood with a joke.

Because of his pounding heart, the Fist Demon unconsciously clenched his fist. He felt as if he, too, were standing on the battlefield the Extreme Evil Little Demon had unfolded.

Geom Ujin stared silently at the Extreme Evil Little Demon.

Before they knew it, the air around them had turned cold. Under Geom Ujin’s aura, heavy as Mount Tai, the Extreme Evil Little Demon felt a suffocating tightness. His whole body ached as if it were being torn apart. It felt as though he were falling into a pit whose walls were densely studded with sharp blades.

Feeling the pain, the Extreme Evil Little Demon closed his eyes. He had the illusion that he was standing in the middle of a battlefield. Blood flowed like streams, and shouts rang out from every direction. Flames surged upward, and hidden weapons flew everywhere. In the very center of that battlefield stood the Extreme Evil Little Demon.

And the person standing before him was not Geom Ujin, but Geom Mugeuk. With his mask pushed up on his head, he was looking at him and speaking. Though no sound could be heard, the shape of his mouth made the words clear.

I wish to see more of your world, Lord Little Demon.

The moment Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly and lowered the mask over his face, the Extreme Evil Little Demon returned to reality.

In the place where Geom Mugeuk had stood was Geom Ujin.

Their eyes met, and at the same time, the tremendous pressure that had been crushing him slowly began to disappear.

Geom Ujin spoke sternly.

“I can tolerate seeing your back while you wear the Undying Ghost Mask, but do not look at me while wearing that mask.”

The Undying Ghost Mask was a mask worn when going out to the battlefield. It was the Heavenly Demon’s solemn warning that, no matter what happened, he was not to point his blade at him.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon lowered his head.

“Forgive me, Cult Leader.”

Though he had given a stern warning, there was no displeasure on Geom Ujin’s face. The forgiveness that said he would overlook it this once was already there in his expression.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and the Fist Demon could feel it. Rather, because of this, Geom Ujin had taken a liking to the Extreme Evil Little Demon. His manliness had pleased him. The kind of person the Heavenly Demon liked was exactly someone like that.

As if to prove it, Geom Ujin added one more remark.

“If another time comes in your life when you need to ask a favor, what will you do after calling this your last request?”

“Even if such a time comes, I will endure it.”

The Extreme Evil Little Demon had given up the chance that would be granted to him.

“I will accept only that you made your first request.”

It was Geom Ujin’s consideration, saying that even if he asked again later, he would hear him out. Though it could be called rude if one wished to call it so, Geom Ujin embraced the Extreme Evil Little Demon with immense magnanimity.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon lowered his head with moved eyes.

“Thank you.”

He turned around again, removed the Undying Ghost Mask, and put his original white mask back on.

To the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, that white mask with nothing drawn on it felt like a more terrifying mask than the Undying Ghost Mask with its evil spirit painted upon it.

Just then, someone else appeared there. This time there were two of them, and astonishingly, they were none other than the Drunken Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

The Drunken Demon was thoroughly drunk.

“We greet the Cult Leader.”

The Drunken Demon and the Sword Venerable stood side by side and greeted the Cult Leader.

“It would have been proper to sober up before coming to see you, but while clear-headed, I truly did not have the confidence to face you, Cult Leader, so I had a drink. Please forgive me.”

The Drunken Demon immediately looked after the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

“Ah, the Sword Venerable said she wouldn’t come, but I dragged her here by force.”

This was the Drunken Demon’s friendship. Without burdening her relationship with the Heavenly Demon, he was being considerate so she would not be left as the only one who had not helped Geom Mugeuk. After all, there was a clear difference between having come here and not having come.

Though she had been persuaded and followed him, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable felt troubled. Her head said, You absolutely must not go! But her heart was conflicted, wondering, Is it all right if I alone stay out of it?

To her, the Heavenly Demon had always been a difficult and venerable person. Yet what entered her eyes now were the three Demon Venerables who had arrived before her.

‘Why did they all accept Geom Mugeuk’s request? Were they not afraid of the Cult Leader’s thunderous wrath?’

She wondered if there was some relationship she did not know about, and felt hurt, while at the same time thinking she might be too selfish. To be honest, she could not understand why even that Drunken Demon was suffering so much.

Geom Ujin asked the Drunken Demon.

“Did you come because of Mugeuk as well?”

The Drunken Demon had told Geom Mugeuk that the Cult Leader disliked him, but Geom Ujin showed no sign of that whatsoever.

As if making an excuse for coming late, the Drunken Demon said,

“I refused the Second Young Master’s request to persuade you, Cult Leader.”

“Then why have you come?”

“Because I was ashamed. I was the one who first pestered him to become sworn brothers, but when the situation actually came where I should help, I was ashamed of myself for stepping back.”

The Drunken Demon’s voice trembled. Considering how well he usually spoke and how good he was at joking around, one could tell how nervous he was now.

Geom Ujin did not ask the Drunken Demon why he had fallen for Geom Mugeuk. Did he truly dislike him?

The one who asked that in his place was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“How did you end up being won over by the Second Young Master? We have all said our piece, so you speak as well.”

The Drunken Demon glanced at Geom Ujin, then spoke cautiously.

“The Second Young Master intoxicates me. When I look at him, I think of alcohol. When we drink together, it is enjoyable, and when he is not there, I miss him and drink again. In truth, there have been several people like that in my life. But the Second Young Master has a different kind of special quality.”

“What is that?”

“He intoxicates me, and at the same time, he keeps making me sober up. Just like now, standing before the Cult Leader. He makes me drunk, then makes me sober. Then makes me drunk again. The Second Young Master is the only person who does both of those at once.”

The Demon Venerables quietly listened to him, each thinking of their own Geom Mugeuk.

Just then, Geom Ujin abruptly asked,

“If he is that special, why did you refuse Mugeuk’s request?”

“I am sorry, but may I have a drink before I answer?”

It was a rude request, but because he was the Drunken Demon, it was also a request he could make.

When Geom Ujin nodded, the Drunken Demon gulped down the liquor at his waist. As the alcohol entered him, vitality began to return to his eyes.

The Drunken Demon stared straight at Geom Ujin and said,

“Because of you, Cult Leader.”

At his answer, tension began to swirl around them once more.

“Because of me?”

“Yes. Because of you, Cult Leader, I became a pathetic older brother who could not even look after one younger brother.”

“Why because of me?”

“Because my desire for you not to dislike me was greater.”

The Drunken Demon thought that if he did not say it today, he would never be able to say it. At least once, he had wanted to tell the Cult Leader. Why do you hate me so much? Please do not dislike me like that.

“Did you think I disliked you?”

“Do you not?”

The atmosphere grew more and more taut.

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s sound transmission quickly flew to the Drunken Demon.

—Are you insane? Stop!

The Drunken Demon glanced at her.

—If I don’t say it now, I’ll regret it when I die.

The Drunken Demon took out more liquor and drank deeply. Then he brought up a story he had never told anyone.

“I tried to look good in your eyes, Cult Leader! Though you never once looked kindly upon me!”

He had not expected to say it in a place where the other Demon Venerables were present, but emotions were always bound to burst out at moments like this. No, perhaps it was because he had wished for someone to hear what he felt.

Geom Ujin said nothing.

“Shut up! You drunkard!”

The one who shouted was the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. Just as the Drunken Demon had looked after her, right now, she was the only one looking after the Drunken Demon.

“You drunkard! Why did you come here? Did you come to lament your own lot? You said you had to step forward for your younger brother, didn’t you? You said that even if the Cult Leader hated you and drove you out, you would step forward, didn’t you? You said I had to be here too, didn’t you? You said only then would I have something to say to the Second Young Master later, didn’t you? But what are you doing now? Why are you rambling drunkenly instead of saying what you came to say? How dare you, and in front of whom!”

Originally, she was not someone who got angry at others like this. Nor was she someone who spoke this much. But she was deliberately getting even angrier. She hoped that by doing this, Geom Ujin’s anger might be eased.

Whether he knew her heart or not, the Drunken Demon shouted back.

“When I saw the Cult Leader’s face, I forgot everything! What are you going to do about it!”

“Don’t pretend to be drunk! You’re still far from drunk!”

At that, the Drunken Demon flinched.

“You drunkard! Even I would dislike you. Borrowing the strength of alcohol to babble on and on. Why would he hate you? Because you do hateful things, that’s why he hates you.”

“Yes, yes, you’re amazing! Our noble Sword Venerable! Truly amazing! As for me, I’m already marked anyway, so it’s fine. It’s perfectly fine!”

It was precisely then that Geom Ujin abruptly spoke.

“I do not hate you.”

For a moment, silence fell. Everyone’s gazes turned to Geom Ujin.

“I hated your master.”

In that instant, the Drunken Demon was shocked. It was the first time he had heard this. Even his master, the person directly involved, had never once said such a thing.

Geom Ujin said nothing more. He did not scold the Drunken Demon for his rudeness, nor did he apologize for letting him misunderstand. He did not say why he had hated his master either.

The Drunken Demon stared at Geom Ujin and asked,

“Did you truly have not even the slightest feeling of hatred toward me?”

“I did not.”

One corner of the wall that had been stiflingly blocking the Drunken Demon’s heart collapsed with a crash. Beyond the fallen wall, a single ray of light entered.

“If I had known this would happen, I should have thrown a drunken fit long ago.”

As if he had never been drunkenly rambling earlier, the Drunken Demon was calm and serious.

“Not knowing that this was the reason… on the days I came back from seeing you, Cult Leader, I drank too much. Why does he look at me so coldly? Did I make some mistake? I couldn’t sleep from all sorts of thoughts.”

The Drunken Demon could now look Geom Ujin in the face. Ever since he had thought the Heavenly Demon hated him, he had been unable to look properly at Geom Ujin.

“I am sorry to you, Cult Leader, but the drunken misconduct I showed today seems as though it will remain the greatest drunken fit of my life.”

Geom Ujin asked,

“So? Will you also ask me to handle Mugeuk’s matter?”

The Drunken Demon answered firmly.

“No. I will not ask.”

Geom Ujin looked over the five Demon Venerables.

Since he had become the Heavenly Demon, this was the first time five Demon Venerables had come because of one person.

“That brat is rewriting the history of our cult.”

Geom Ujin spoke into the air.

“Bring Mugeuk here.”

* * *

When I arrived, Father and the five Demon Venerables were lined up and looking at me.

I had thought they would each persuade him separately, but I had not expected all five to be in one place. On top of that, even the Drunken Demon and the Ilhwa Sword Venerable, who had refused, were there.

“Your son has come at your summons.”

Thankfully, Father’s expression was not all that bad. My eyes met those of the five Demon Venerables one by one.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon gave a slight nod, as if signaling that the situation had turned out all right.

The Fist Demon clenched his fist tightly in a way only I would notice. With that action, he told me that this was what the atmosphere here had been like.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon was smiling. From the relieved look in his eyes, I could tell that he had gone through something tremendous.

The Drunken Demon was looking at Father. I could feel that another change had occurred in his life. Standing beside him, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable smiled at me.

“All five people I like are here!”

At that, the Drunken Demon, still looking at Father, spoke in a voice full of drunkenness.

“Why are you leaving out the Cult Leader? It’s six.”

“I was leaving you out, Brother.”

“…Ah.”

The Drunken Demon drank. Seeing him drink so openly in front of Father, it seemed something truly outrageous had happened. I could hear about today’s events later from the Blood Heaven Blade Demon or the Drunken Demon.

I stood before Father. Inwardly, I was tense, wondering what the result would be.

It was at that very moment.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon slowly walked over and stood beside me. With that one action, I could guess what sort of stance he had shown today.

The eyes behind his mask smiled at me.

I did everything I could.

Thank you, truly, Lord Little Demon.

We exchanged our feelings through our gazes.

Next, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon sighed and stood beside me.

“What sort of uproar is this in my old age?”

“I’m sorry.”

The next person to move was the Fist Demon.

“He is my first disciple, so it cannot be helped.”

Three Demon Venerables came to stand on my side.

Their gazes as they looked at Father were filled with apology. They were not opposing Father. They were showing their will to protect me.

Just then, the Drunken Demon, who had been looking at Father, walked over to my side.

“Now that I see Mugeuk, I simply can’t help it. I must help too. Was my master perhaps disliked because he went back and forth like this as well?”

Geom Ujin answered the Drunken Demon’s question.

“It was the opposite. When he was drunk, he never yielded in his stubbornness.”

“Then that is truly fortunate. I would probably be first among the Eight Demon Venerables when it comes to being easily swayed.”

“Is that not a problem in its own way?”

“Then please tell the next Drunken Demon this: I disliked your master because he was too easily swayed.”

Before the Heavenly Demon, the Drunken Demon finally became at ease. The one person who could ramble drunkenly even in front of the Heavenly Demon, and who would build up the tremendous trust needed to be allowed to do so. That was the path the Drunken Demon needed to walk in his relationship with Father.

And I had a premonition that this change in their relationship would change the man known as the Drunken Demon.

This time, the Drunken Demon looked at me and smiled.

“Wait until this pathetic older brother becomes impressive.”

The Drunken Demon was speaking sincerely.

“If you promise you’ll be less impressive than me.”

The Drunken Demon handed me the liquor bottle. Promises with the Drunken Demon were made through alcohol. I drank from it, then returned it to him. The Drunken Demon drank as well.

After drinking like that, the Drunken Demon walked over to the Ilhwa Sword Venerable.

Then he took her hand and brought her over to our side.

“I believe the Cult Leader’s decision is right.”

“I know. Even so, the place to stand today is here.”

It was enough that her will had been conveyed to the Cult Leader.

Even in the midst of this, the Drunken Demon placed her beside the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, then returned to his own place.

And so the five Demon Venerables lined up on my left and right. When we attacked the Valley of Villains, there had been four, but now even the Fist Demon had joined, making five.

Father silently looked at me and the Demon Venerables. Father did not seem displeased, nor did he seem pleased. Well, it was probably his first time experiencing something like this too.

That contemptuous sneer appeared on Father’s lips as he looked at me. Yes, in a situation like this, there was no smile more fitting than that one.

“Very well. Originally, I would have permitted it only if all eight Demon Venerables had come, but today, Geukak did the work of three.”

If Geukak counted for three, that made seven in all.

“If you place just one more person before me, I will permit this matter.”

“Who is it?”

From Father’s mouth flowed the name of a single person. It was the name of the final Demon Venerable, the very person I had not met even once since my regression.

“The Poison King.”

Chapter 209: The Cult Leader Has No Intention of Losing That Bet
----------------------------------------------------------------
The person Father wanted was the Poison King.

At the words “Poison King,” the Demon Supremes’ expressions stiffened slightly, and they looked at one another. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon let out a sigh only I could hear. That was how difficult this matter was.

Indeed, Father seemed certain, just as the Demon Supremes’ reactions suggested, that the Poison King would absolutely not step forward.

“Can you do it?”

“I’ll try.”

The look Father gave me was provocative.

Can you truly manage even this? If you really accomplish even this, I will acknowledge you.

Father’s gaze turned toward the Demon Supremes.

It was a gaze devoid of any emotion, yet I sensed subtle differences within that impassive look.

The way he looked at the Fist Demon was different from the way he looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, and the way he looked at the Extreme Evil Young Demon was different again.

If only he knew how deeply the Ilhwa Sword Venerable respected him. How nice it would be if he gave him even the slightest warmth in his gaze.

The last place his gaze came to rest was the Drunken Demon. With eyes calmer than ever, the Drunken Demon received Father’s gaze.

After communing with the Demon Supremes through their eyes like that, Father turned and walked toward the Heavenly Demon Hall.

The Demon Supremes and I bowed our heads in respect.

What is Father thinking right now? Will the day ever come when I sit face-to-face with him and hear him speak frankly about how he felt then, what he was thinking then?

Once Father had left, I bowed my head to the Demon Supremes.

“Thank you, truly.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable spoke with an awkward expression.

“You needn’t thank me. I was dragged here by the Drunken Demon against my will.”

“That was a great help as well.”

“What do you mean?”

“If all five of you had pushed him, Father is human too; he might have been greatly angered. But because Brother Drunken Demon and Senior Sword Venerable acted as buffers, his heart must have softened.”

The Drunken Demon put on an unnecessary show of distress.

“I’m ruined. To act drunk in front of the Cult Leader!”

At that, the Ilhwa Sword Venerable snatched the wine bottle hanging at his waist and gulped down a drink.

“I’m the one who said far too many unnecessary things in front of the Cult Leader.”

The Drunken Demon took the bottle back and drank.

“It’s because of this worthless friend of mine.”

“As long as you know.”

I cut into their conversation.

“No, you all gathered because of me, so it’s because of me.”

I bowed politely to the Demon Supremes once more.

“I will repay what happened today again and again. You have no idea how reassured I feel with the five of you here. Thank you, truly. Since we’ve all gathered like this today, allow me to say just one thing… Ah, wait! I’ll keep it brief. I’m not that talkative a person. Let’s all get together and devise a strategy to win over the Poison King…”

The five Demon Supremes had already left the place in five different directions without even looking back.

“You’re all too much!”

Since I did not have five bodies, I launched myself after one of them.

* * *

Of the five Demon Supremes, the one I chased after was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

Though he was pleased that I had followed him, he made a show of being spiteful.

“Why didn’t you chase after the Extreme Evil Young Demon?”

“To me, Elder, you are always the best.”

“The best to you seems to be Extreme Evil.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon told me exactly what had happened earlier.

“Earlier, the Extreme Evil Young Demon staked everything he had. The Cult Leader, I, and the Fist Demon as well. None of us thought he would go that far.”

I had not known that the Extreme Evil Young Demon had taken such a strong stance. Senior Young Demon, I received that answer loud and clear.

“I should have been there.”

“That made it all the more remarkable. The fact that he did it even when you weren’t there. That’s not something one can do unless one truly likes someone.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon at this very moment was remarkable as well. He could have been jealous in his heart of my relationship with the Extreme Evil Young Demon, yet he was telling me everything exactly as it was. Saying it was remarkable, truly remarkable. How could I possibly not like this man?

“When on earth did you become that close?”

“I’m not sure either. Somehow, we ended up becoming friends.”

“Friends? With that age difference?”

You can say that again. I’m the one who’s so much older. I had lived even longer than you, Elder.

“Isn’t there such a thing as friendship that transcends age?”

“That grand friendship has made me thirsty. Go bring me some tea from over there.”

I took the teapot from the table beside the bed and poured tea for the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. Seeing his still gaunt arms pained my heart.

“Have you been eating properly these days?”

“Why are you suddenly going on about meals?”

“To me, Elder, your health is the most important thing. What does the Poison King matter, and what does a mere permission matter?”

“Words you don’t even mean.”

Still, as if he was in a good mood, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon drank his tea with a faint smile.

“You were the first to go to Father today, weren’t you?”

“I’m just short-tempered. Don’t attach meaning to the fact that I went first.”

“I will attach meaning to it. The Elder who ran first for my sake!”

“Fine. To be honest, I was going to make a point of being the first, but today I was utterly defeated by that masked fellow.”

It seemed that masked fellow had moved the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s heart today.

“Though it looks like everyone’s efforts were in vain.”

When he brought up the Poison King, he sighed. The expressions of the other Demon Supremes had seemed to carry similar feelings.

“Is it that difficult to bring him in?”

“If he had said, ‘Bring all Eight Demon Supremes instead,’ that would have been easier. We could have held a meeting of the Demon Supremes and pushed politically for us to act together. It might have been handled that way. But by naming the Poison King specifically and telling you to bring him, he blocked that method in advance. The Cult Leader is subtly clever, I must say.”

“Do you know anything about the Poison King?”

“I have no personal friendship with him. Just from what I’ve seen, he’s someone with a world of his own firmly established, and his pride is strong. He’s mad about poison. That’s about all I know.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s concern was not merely about such aspects of his personality. It lay here.

“Can you remain yourself even before the Poison King’s poison?”

Could I remain myself before the Poison King’s poison?

I understood what he was worried about. There was a primal fear that poison inspired. The fear that one might die without even being able to fight properly. For that reason, together with the late Soul-Seizing Demon Supreme, the Poison King was the Demon Supreme whom martial artists were most reluctant to face.

“No matter how eloquent you are, and no matter how good you are at flattery, can you really remain unrestrained even before the Poison King, who uses poison? He is someone who could poison you without leaving any sign and make you die of illness years later.”

In truth, he was the one before whom I could be the most unrestrained. As long as poison did not work on me, he was, at least to me, the weakest among the Eight Demon Supremes.

That was precisely what worried me instead.

Because the moment I wounded his pride the wrong way, bringing him before Father would become impossible forever.

When I returned to the cult in my previous life to obtain materials for the Great Rite, the Poison King had already died, and the next Poison King occupied the seat of Demon Supreme.

I know how he died. I know how many people he took with him when he did.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon stared at me as I sank briefly into thought.

“Do you have a method in mind?”

“I do. Somehow, I must learn from you, Elder, the secret art of making the Poison King take my side.”

“If you use the methods inside me, even those close to you will grow distant.”

Saying so, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon stared straight at me.

“If I must give you advice, do what you do best.”

“What is that?”

“The Cult Leader called it cheap sentimentality just perfect for getting yourself killed.”

At those words, I burst into laughter. It surprised me that Father and the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had had such a conversation. The fact that Father had used that expression with someone else was proof that our relationship had grown one step closer.

“Use that on him, as you did with me and the other Demon Supremes.”

Look straight at the other person, and if there is something to say, do not put it off.

I had treated people with those two convictions. Whether they would work on the Poison King remained to be seen.

“Understood, Elder.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon took something from his robes and held it out to me. It was the highest-grade Poison-Repelling Pearl I had once given him as a birthday gift.

“Take this with you too. I washed it clean, so don’t say it’s dirty.”

For a moment, I silently looked down at the Poison-Repelling Pearl. This was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s heart. These days, that heart kept stirring mine as well.

I took out two Poison-Repelling Pearls from my robes and tucked one into each cheek.

“I’ll go with both cheeks stuffed full like a squirrel! Please keep that one with you, and if I fall to the Poison King, come save me with it in your mouth.”

“No need! I will never set foot in that filthy poison pit.”

Because I knew he would be the first to rush there, I would not let such a thing happen.

“Please rest. I’ll be going now.”

“Don’t overdo it. Right now, it may seem like some grand matter with your fate at stake… but once it passes, none of it amounts to much.”

“Yes, I’ll keep that in mind.”

By the time I left the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s residence, night had already fallen.

When everyone is watching me, waiting to see what path I will take, there are times when the burden of having to do well weighs down on me.

Whenever that happens, there is always one person who comes to mind. Forgetting the Poison King, forgetting Father’s permission, I followed the instinct that led me toward rest and slowly moved my feet.

* * *

Today as well, Ian was burying her efforts into the years.

From the Flying Heaven Sword Art she unfolded at nine-tenths mastery, I could feel the maturity forged by painstaking effort.

Slash.

Before her chest, her sword qi split into two. Her internal energy was still lacking, so she could not divide it further, but she succeeded in executing the Yucheon Form with precision.

Shiiik. Shiiik!

The two strands of sword qi that flew like arrows through the air embedded themselves to the left and right of my face. Her sword on the left, and the sword-shaped sword qi on the right.

She, who had once been so absorbed in training every day that she did not even notice I had arrived, was now launching the Yucheon Form at me.

“I can send you out into the Central Plains without worry now. Descend the mountain, Ian. Try not to go around killing too many people.”

“I refuse, Young Master. I’m going to stick right by your side and live comfortably like this for the rest of my life. Ah! Though that comfort has come to an end now.”

“Congratulations.”

“You heard, right? Senior Blue Mask has finally joined the Ghost Shadow Unit as First Squad Leader.”

Her voice trembled. Aside from matters related to me, this was likely one of the greatest events of her life. The successor who would become the next Extreme Evil Young Demon had become a squad leader in the organization she commanded.

“How do you feel?”

“Nervous.”

“The most important thing is to trust yourself. People know like ghosts. Ah, that person has no confidence in leading us. We’re screwed. We’re going to die like dogs in the end.”

Ian let out a sigh.

“I do trust myself. I’m just worried whether my ability will keep up.”

“If you can’t trust yourself, trust your tail.”

At that, Ian raised her head and grinned.

“That, I can trust a little.”

“Discuss it with Blue Mask and begin organizing the unit in earnest. There is only one principle. Create the finest elite organization in our cult. Look at skill, and look at character as well. Don’t create a pack of butchers who only have ability. Create an elite organization you can trust. Fill it with people like Jang Ho or Investigator Seo. Understand?”

“Yes!”

“From now on, having an eye for people is more important than techniques. That will keep you alive.”

As the leader of an organization, Ian’s life began now.

I talked with her about this and that. Being with her set my heart at ease. The moon, which had looked forlorn when I arrived, was now filling the window and warmly illuminating the world.

When parting from her, I did not forget one last tease. She had endured all those side effects and become beautiful with difficulty, yet all she ever did was train, so at least I had to say something.

“I thought you were the greatest beauty under heaven, but perhaps not.”

For an instant, Ian tensed, wondering what I meant.

“You’ve become even prettier than before you left the cult. If you’re the greatest beauty under heaven, you shouldn’t be getting any prettier, should you?”

Leaving her behind with her face turning red, I departed. Ian, you’re not getting any sleep tonight.

* * *

The next day, on my way to meet the Poison King, I stopped by the Demon Physician.

As it happened, he had no patients and was at leisure. He looked surprised.

“Second Young Master, I’ve been seeing you often these days.”

“When I watch you save people, Divine Physician, it somehow puts me in a good mood.”

How could the Demon Physician not know I had come with business?

“I never know when another patient will come barging in, so state your business quickly.”

“There’s something I’d like to ask you, Divine Physician.”

“Speak.”

“By any chance, do you have any dealings with the Poison King?”

I asked as if I did not know, but I already knew that the only person the Poison King had dealings with was the Demon Physician. It was one of the few pieces of information I knew about the Poison King.

“One of you saves people, and the other kills people with poison, does he not? They say extremes meet, so I thought perhaps the two of you might have struck up a friendship.”

“Why the Poison King?”

“There is something I want from my father, and he set the condition that I persuade the Poison King and bring him back.”

The Demonic Physician shook his head.

“Then it seems the Cult Leader has no intention of losing that wager.”

The Demonic Physician, too, did not think the Poison King would be persuaded by me.

“I know you are an extraordinary man, Second Young Master. But the Poison King will not be easy.”

“Is there anything you can tell me?”

The Demonic Physician held his tongue for a moment. He was likely wondering how much he should say, caught between his friendship with the Poison King and his relationship with me.

“In all my life, I have met many poisoners, but I have never seen anyone as devoted to poison as the Poison King. His pride is just as great. He believes the only person he cannot kill with his poison is the Cult Leader.”

That was also why I could not carelessly reveal that I was immune to all poisons. The moment I mishandled his pride and self-respect, the matter would go awry.

“Wait here a moment.”

The Demonic Physician stepped out briefly, then returned with a small medicine bottle.

“This is the finest antidote I possess. I cannot say with certainty that it can neutralize all of the Poison King’s poisons, but it should be of help.”

I handed the antidote back to him.

“Please use this medicine somewhere else where it will be needed.”

“Why?”

“If the Poison King were to poison me, it would only be when he was risking his life to plot rebellion. At that point, he would use a poison no antidote could cure.”

“You are right.”

The Demonic Physician nodded.

“Then I will come to see you again.”

I had come to the Demonic Physician because he was the person who would be of the greatest help in persuading the Poison King from here on.

‘There will come a time when you will help me. And that matter will not be for my sake, but for yours as well, Demonic Physician.’

I rose from my seat and was about to leave when the Demonic Physician spoke one last time.

“Do you know what the Poison King’s dream is? It is to compile every poison in the world and leave it behind in a book. Even at this very moment, he is silently carrying out his research on poison alone. The key will be getting that man to come out of his laboratory. He is someone who has no interest in the world.”

The Poison King had lied even to the only person he associated with. His dream was not to compile the poisons of the world.

He was a man who dreamed of becoming the Greatest Poison Sovereign Under Heaven (天下第一毒尊), one who would make the martial world kneel at his feet with his poison.

After leaving the medical hall, I headed toward the southwest of the cult. There lay the Thousand Poison Forest, where the Poison King lived.

Chapter 210: At the Center of the Grand Training Ground.
--------------------------------------------------------
At the entrance to the Thousand Poisons Forest sat an old man with a full head of white hair. At a glance, his presence was anything but ordinary, and several poison pouches hung at his waist. Those pouches were the very symbol of the poison practitioners of the Thousand Poisons Forest.

Counting the pouches on the old man, there were nine in all.

When one first learned poison arts, one wore a single pouch, and the Poison King’s pouches numbered twelve. So one could guess from that number alone just how formidable an expert this old man was here.

“I am Geom Mugeuk. I have come to see the Poison King.”

When I greeted him politely, the old man introduced himself.

“This old man is called Sangseon.”

He was the Poison King’s right-hand man and one of the Four Great Poison Practitioners of the Thousand Poisons Forest.

“A distinguished person has come out to receive me.”

“A distinguished guest has come, so this old man ought to welcome him personally.”

I had sent word in advance that I was coming, but I had not expected Sangseon himself to come out like this.

“Follow me carefully.”

I followed Sangseon into the forest. The forest truly was dense.

“Of all the territories of the Eight Demon Supremes, this Thousand Poisons Forest is the largest.”

It really was vast enough for him to boast about.

Sssssss.

The shape of a snake I saw along the path was unusual.

“That is a White-Blood Snake. Oddly enough, its blood is white. If you are bitten by that creature and fail to take the antidote, you will die before a single quarter-hour passes.”

They said a thousand kinds of poisonous things existed here. Filled with all manner of poisonous herbs, venomous insects, and poisonous snakes, the Thousand Poisons Forest was a place where, if you set one foot wrong, even your corpse would never be found.

On the way, we also encountered poison practitioners gathering poisonous herbs. The poison practitioners here were called Poison Fangs, and the number of pouches hanging from their waists varied.

After passing through that dense forest, we arrived at the Poison King’s residence. It was a circular building, low but extremely broad in scale.

“I will wait here. Go in and speak with him.”

“Thank you.”

I entered the building.

The interior was not divided, but consisted of one vast space.

Everything was there. Dozens of bookshelves and desks, all kinds of devices whose purposes I could not guess, a bed, tables, wardrobes, display cabinets, even a wooden tub for bathing. Truly, everything a person needed to live was contained in that space. This was the Poison King’s room.

In the middle of that room, the Poison King had all manner of items spread out and was squatting as he read something.

This was my first meeting with the Poison King.

Before my regression, in my youth, I had only seen him once or twice from afar, and when I returned later in life to obtain materials for the Great Art, he had already died. So today was the first time I was seeing him this directly and up close.

To describe his first impression briefly, he looked youthful and scholarly.

When people commonly spoke of the Poison King, they imagined either a hunched old man, or someone whose skin was covered in boils, inflamed, and peeling from the side effects of poison—someone whose face and body were truly in no proper state. But the Poison King of this generation was extremely neat.

Though he was middle-aged, he looked truly young. If our cult held a contest to choose who looked youngest, I was certain the Poison King would take first place.

With his youthful appearance, enough that people might call us friends if we walked around together, he also gave off an extremely intellectual air. Judging by looks alone, he seemed even smarter than Chief Strategist Sima Ming.

If not for his deep green robes and the twelve pouches worn at his waist, anyone would have taken him for a scholar. No, with his pretty-boy looks, one could even have called him a student learning letters from a scholar.

‘Ah, so the Poison King was this baby-faced.’

I even wondered if, perhaps because of poison, his growth had stopped at some point.

‘With such an innocent face, he committed those deeds? He dreamed of becoming the greatest Poison Sovereign under heaven and ruling the murim?’

He truly had lived a life that did not suit his appearance.

When I approached him, the Poison King spoke while keeping his eyes on the book.

“Bring me the blue bottle on that desk over there.”

The Poison King’s voice was as young as his appearance.

I picked up the blue bottle he had mentioned and walked over to him.

“Careful. If even a single drop of Blue Poison splashes on you, you die.”

I won’t die! Even if you poured every poison in this room over me, I wouldn’t die!

The Poison King mixed the poison I handed him with another poison placed in front of him.

The very moment he mixed them—

With a bang, poisonous smoke rose up. I stepped back and swiftly waved both hands. What a noisy welcome.

Whoooong.

I released my internal energy and gathered the rising poisonous smoke between my hands, confining it there.

I was about to drive it outside and send it flying out the window.

“No! Wait!”

The Poison King ran over and brought back a large sack.

“Drive it in here.”

I drove the poisonous smoke I had gathered before me into the sack.

The Poison King tightly tied the mouth of the sack with a cord, then tossed the sack containing the poisonous smoke beside the table.

Then he began rummaging through books again.

“What in the world went wrong?”

He sank into deep thought. He still did not give me so much as a glance. It was not that he was deliberately pretending not to see me. Right now, he was wholly immersed in his own world.

I slowly looked around the interior. On the display cabinets lining the walls on every side stood countless bottles, and I could tell that all of them were either poisons or antidotes. It looked like a study where, instead of books, poison had been shelved.

“Ah! Was this the problem? Hey, bring me the Evil Poison Grass over there.”

From among the poisonous herbs strewn all over the desk, I brought him the Evil Poison Grass. Even after seeing that I had correctly brought it from among the countless herbs, he showed no reaction at all.

“Put that here, then go over there and bring me a gourd of water.”

He kept sending me on errands. Bring this, bring that. Truly, I was moving busily. Meanwhile, he pounded and ground herbs.

When he combined the Blue Poison with the newly blended poison again, this time it did not explode.

“That’s it! That’s it! The Evil Poison Grass was the problem!”

This time, the mixture seemed to have been made properly, and the Poison King was greatly delighted.

Then he discovered me beside him.

“By the way, who are you?”

Our Poison King really does ask early.

“You made me run all those errands knowing who I was?”

“I thought you were one of our kids. Who are you?”

“I am Geom Mugeuk.”

“Ah, right. You said you were coming today. So it’s you. The talk of the cult, Geom Mugeuk. What brings you here?”

Even after learning my identity, his tone and attitude did not change at all.

He acted like a distracted eccentric, but the faint green radiance flowing from his eyes had remained constant from the moment I entered this room until now. It meant his emotions were being controlled consistently. From this alone, one must never forget that a Demon Supreme was, in the end, a Demon Supreme.

“I have a favor to ask.”

“What favor?”

“Please persuade my father.”

The Poison King suddenly thrust his face close to mine. His skin was pale and fine, too. How could someone who handled poison have such good skin?

“No.”

The Poison King refused flatly without even listening to the details.

“It’s all right for me to refuse, yes? This isn’t pressure from the cult leader’s son, is it?”

“It is not.”

“Good. A young fellow must not learn such wicked habits first. Your business is done, so why are you still here?”

The Poison King was disorienting, but he was not easy to deal with.

“No, I’m leaving. Then I will see you again.”

After greeting him politely, I walked out. At the entrance, Sangseon, who had guided me earlier, was waiting for me. He did not ask a single thing about what had happened inside.

“Please send word that I will visit again tomorrow.”

“I will.”

As I followed him out of the Thousand Poisons Forest, I memorized the path.

* * *

The next day, I went to the Thousand Poisons Forest again.

Today as well, Sangseon guided me to the Poison King’s residence, but it was a different path from yesterday. I could understand his intention. He was trying to prevent me from learning the way to the Poison King’s residence.
