But that was an unnecessary attempt made because he did not know how good my memory and sense of direction were. Today, I learned one more path to the Poison King’s residence.

When we arrived at the Poison King’s residence, he was digging in the ground near the house.

“I’m sure it was here.”

The ground had been dug up here and there. It seemed he had buried something but could not find it.

With dirt smeared on his face and body like a child, he looked adorable. The more adorable he looked, the stranger it felt because of the dissonance with what he would do in the future.

In later years, the number of martial artists who died from his poison would reach the thousands. Truly, he would cause a massacre.

The Poison King ignored my greeting and remained absorbed only in digging.

I borrowed a shovel from Sangseon as well and began digging.

A strange light flowed through Sangseon’s eyes as he watched me.

By contrast, the Poison King was concerned only with whether the thing he was looking for would come out of the ground he was digging. It was not an act; he was truly absorbed in that alone. Then he would suddenly laugh to himself. At a glance, he was no different from a madman.

Now I could clearly understand why the Divine Physician had said that the key was getting the Poison King out of his laboratory. I could also understand why everyone had said the Poison King would be difficult to persuade. This man lived thoroughly within his own world.

I dug in several places beside him as well. Whenever something came out, I gathered it all to the side. All sorts of things emerged. Boxes came out, insects and larvae came out. Snakes came out, trash came out, and strange tools came out.

As I was digging in a few places like that—

“Ah! Why is that there?”

The Poison King shouted and came over to the place where I had been digging. It seemed something he had been looking for was among the things I had gathered.

The box he picked up contained a sticky liquid that gave off a terrible smell.

“Ha! It’s aged well!”

“Was this what you were looking for?”

The Poison King looked at me and started in surprise.

“When did you get here?”

This was both an act and not an act. He had recognized me, and he had also forgotten me.

“I came earlier. It looked like you were searching for something, so I dug too.”

“Well done, well done.”

He smiled in good spirits, then soon grew stern.

“I distinctly refused yesterday.”

“Today I came purely to help you, Poison King. Seeing you yesterday, I thought you needed someone to assist you.”

“Are you scheming to steal and learn my poison arts?”

“Can the Poison King’s poison arts be stolen and learned so easily?”

“Not a chance.”

“Then it should be fine.”

The Poison King stared at me. The green radiance in his eyes seemed to pierce into mine. Of course, that light had no effect on me, who had mastered the Divine Eye Art.

Rather, I sensed something about him. At that moment, an image rose in my mind.

The image of the Poison King walking step by step out of his own world and standing at the boundary with the outside world.

In that moment, he was not an eccentric.

I could tell. The person before my eyes right now was someone who sought to become the sole Poison Sovereign of the murim. He had crossed that boundary exactly once, and when he did, thousands died.

“Let’s see how good you are at working.”

Emotion showed on his face. I’ll make it so hard after just one day that you’ll never come again!

The Poison King set me to work. With the intention of driving me away, he properly put me to work.

“Take those poisonous herbs over there, spread them out here, and sort them.”

As he ordered, I brought over the poisonous herbs piled in sacks and sorted them.

I abandoned the intention of persuading the Poison King. I also abandoned the desire to make a good impression on him. I simply thought that, by staying with him, I had to find out what kind of world his world was. If I could not understand his world, I would never be able to persuade him.

“Now, organize the newly arrived poisonous herbs over there too.”

Once a day, the poisonous things the Poison Fangs obtained from the Central Plains were brought in here. It would have been fortunate if there were only poisonous herbs, but there were also all kinds of repulsive poisonous creatures.

I handled the poisonous herbs and poisonous creatures skillfully. After becoming immune to all poisons, my aversion to poison vanished completely. Before, when I heard the word “poison,” my face would wrinkle first and I would dislike getting close to it for no reason, but now I had no such feeling.

He seemed surprised to see that I did the work he gave me rather well.

“I thought you’d show signs of being raised in luxury, but you can work, can’t you?”

I had always been well versed in poisonous herbs. That was why, back at the trials of Socheon Cave, I had easily passed the stages related to poisonous herbs.

Of course, the poisonous herbs handled here were vast enough to far exceed my knowledge. Whenever an herb I did not know came in, I asked the Poison King. What is this? What is that? Until he told me to stop asking so much.

When the day’s work was done, the Poison King asked me about the poisonous herbs he had taught me. Since he could not find fault with my work, he attacked my memory.

“What did I say this poisonous herb was called?”

“It is Wild Orchid Grass. It is a poison mainly ground up and put into spicy food. Then it leaves no trace at all.”

I even repeated the explanation he had given me precisely.

“Then this poisonous herb?”

“It is Black Mountain Hundred-Day Grass. If it poisons someone, its effect lasts for a hundred days, making them waste away before finally dying.”

I explained exactly as he had said.

“You little brat! Why are you so smart?”

His eyes burned with the look of someone determined to find fault tomorrow no matter what. At moments like this, he looked exactly like an immature youngster my age.

“I will see you tomorrow!”

* * *

The next day, I went to see him again.

The Poison King was greatly mistaken if he thought I would come for a few days and then stop. He did not know what kind of effort I was willing to make once I set a goal.

Today as well, I spent the day doing the work the Poison King assigned me.

I made no effort whatsoever to deliberately become close to him. I did not strike up conversation, nor did I try to look good. I did not unleash my Divine Art of Flattery either. I only silently did the work he gave me.

Perhaps that was why the Poison King was the first to bring this up.

“Doing this won’t change my mind. Once I say something, I never take it back. If I do take it back, then in the middle of the Grand Training Ground, with everyone watching, I’ll shout, ‘I am a dog,’ and bark five times.”

It was the moment he steeled his resolve never to be won over. So it was something he said to mean I should stop coming, but I also steeled my resolve.

“Since you’ve put up such a condition, Poison King, I should put one up too. If I fail, I will also gather everyone at the Grand Training Ground and shout it. I will shout, ‘A tiger father has a dog son,’ and bark five times.”

As if he had never expected those words, the Poison King burst into loud laughter.

“I look forward to seeing what expression the cult leader will have then.”

Perhaps excited because I had made a wager, the Poison King brought paper and brush.

“Now then, since you’ll be coming here often, let’s write our pledges first! A pledge that you came to the Thousand Poisons Forest of your own will, and that even if an accident happens while you work and you are poisoned, you will not hold me responsible. How about it? Do you have the confidence to write it?”

Looking into his excited eyes, I provoked him as well.

“If you write one too, Poison King.”

Chapter 211: A Plan Amid No Plan
--------------------------------
“What written pledge?”

The Poison King guessed my intention.

“You write a pledge, and in exchange I have to teach you one poison art a day? That sort of pledge?”

“No.”

“Then a pledge saying that even if you steal my poisons here, or secretly learn my poison arts, I won’t hold you responsible?”

“Poison King. Your poisons and poison arts frighten me. I’d like to keep as far away from them as possible.”

“Then what kind of pledge is it?”

The pledge I wanted him to write contained something the Poison King could never have expected.

“A pledge that if you lose this wager, you will come out of your world.”

The Poison King flinched in surprise. His expression changed in a strange way.

“And what sort of world is my world?”

“The world where you research alone, compound alone, think alone, laugh alone. Dig holes alone. That world.”

He thrust his face close to mine. His absurdly youthful face came right up before my nose.

But in that moment, I could feel it. Though his body had come closer, the person called the Poison King had retreated from me. Standing on the boundary between his own world and the outside world, he had stepped back and hidden himself away.

“All you need to do is take me with you and get the Cult Leader’s permission, isn’t it?”

“That’s right. That’s why I came.”

“Then whether I’m in that sort of world or not has nothing to do with you, does it?”

“It does.”

“How?”

“Because I am the one who will become the Heavenly Demon. The next Heavenly Demon wishes for the Poison King of our cult to be not a lonely Poison King alone, but a Poison King who roams the world.”

“!”

“Well? Will you write the pledge?”

The Poison King gave no answer for a while. He must have been curious about my true intentions. What purpose I had in putting forth such a condition.

But he would never know. Because he could never know that I intended to prevent the terrible incident that would occur in the future.

“Why are you hesitating? In any case, that would only happen after you’ve become a dog. It shouldn’t matter, should it?”

I had even attached the condition of losing the wager.

Though he believed there was no way he would lose, he still hesitated. It seemed that leaving this place was that much of a burden to him.

I no longer pressed him and picked up the brush.

“At any rate, fine. I will write mine. I am learning so much from you, Poison King, so I should write a pledge.”

I wrote a pledge stating that even if I were poisoned by any poison here in the Thousand Poisons Forest, it would not be the Poison King’s responsibility, but my own for having come here of my own will. I even signed it with my seal.

The Poison King was genuinely startled when I actually wrote the pledge. It seemed that even when he brought up the matter of pledges, he had not expected me to truly write one.

“Are you an idiot? Now even if I poison you and make you die in agony, I won’t be held responsible.”

“That won’t happen.”

“And what makes you so confident?”

“Because only intelligent people can learn poison arts. You are the smartest among the Demon Venerables, so you would never do something so reckless.”

I looked around and said,

“Now that I’ve come here, I understand. The person who learns poison arts is the smartest of all. You have to memorize all these poisonous herbs and venomous creatures, and to create poisons, you must make hundreds, thousands of combinations and mixtures, don’t you? On top of that, you must also make antidotes. And is that all? You have to learn the art of poisoning in earnest, don’t you? A person standing at the pinnacle of such intelligence would do something like that? Impossible. You may laugh while digging holes alone, but you would never do something that stupid.”

The Poison King’s eyes went round. His reaction was as cute as his appearance. After blinking his large eyes a few times, he said as if amazed,

“You talk a lot, and you talk well.”

In that moment, the Poison King was sincerely impressed.

Poison King, I haven’t even begun yet.

“If you are with me, you won’t be bored. Now then, I’ll continue what I was doing.”

After handing him the pledge, I continued working. I brought over a sack of poisonous herbs and untied it. As the Poison King watched me sort the herbs, he suddenly said to me,

“What should I write? You wrote one, and I don’t. Then I’m the only petty one, aren’t I?”

In the end, he had made up his mind to write the pledge.

“Ah, it’s nothing much. Just that you will leave the Thousand Poisons Sect once every ten days. That will be enough.”

“That’s enough?”

“Yes.”

“Can I stand in front of the entrance to the Thousand Poisons Sect and then come back in?”

“I don’t mind. As long as you leave this place.”

“What meaning does that have?”

“Because the scenery you see from here and the scenery you see from in front of the gate will be different.”

The Poison King stared at me in silence. You must be wondering what this is. If you’re a madman, then I’m someone who’s called a madman too. Let’s try it, Poison King. Let’s see who’s more insane.

The Poison King wrote the pledge and signed it with his seal.

“Second Young Master, you’ve lost. I will never be persuaded, so this pledge is nothing but a scrap of paper. My will is like this—how will you break it?”

In truth, I did not have any special plan to persuade him. As the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had said, I would simply treat him the way I had treated the others until now.

“I’ll have to do my best.”

“Do I really look to you like someone who will bark in the training grounds?”

“At least once in your life, yes.”

“How did the other Demon Venerables fall for someone as sloppy as you? I cannot understand it.”

The Poison King’s eyes were filled with confidence and brilliance. Right now, he was a different person from the Poison King who had been digging in the ground with his face smeared with dirt.

“Be careful. From now on, even if you die, I won’t be responsible.”

* * *

The next day, I worked diligently as if nothing had happened.

The Poison King considered every action I took to be deliberate. Whenever I said anything, he habitually replied with the same words.

“I know it’s all a ploy to look good to me. It’s useless!”

But I paid no mind to what he thought.

If there was one plan within my lack of a plan, it was this.

Do not focus on the person. Focus on the work.

I learned the work sincerely. It was not because I intended to use poison for something after learning it. It was because working here was an experience I was having for the first time. There is always a lesson that comes with a first experience.

“When would I ever live a life where I carry sacks full of poisonous herbs and sort them? This is a valuable experience for me.”

I was living in a different way than I had until now. It was something I had never experienced even before my regression.

Think differently, move my body differently.

I hoped this would have a positive influence on my life and martial arts. Sometimes, no matter how hard one concentrates to bring a certain thought to mind, it does not come easily. Yet while bathing, or while walking down the road, it suddenly flashes into one’s head.

It was the same. I hoped this unfamiliar work in the Thousand Poisons Forest would have a fresh influence on my life and martial arts. I hoped that while sorting poisonous herbs, the Wind God Four Steps would reach twelfth-star great completion. I hoped that while compounding poisons, I would realize a new aspect of life.

Once I thought that way, everything I did here no longer made me anxious, and became enjoyable.

And as that continued, I believed an opportunity would surely come to move the Poison King’s heart. That opportunity would come when I truly fell into the poison arts.

“Careful! This time, pour exactly half!”

When in my life would I ever have the chance to compound the fifteen ingredients of Severing Soul Acid?

“That’s it! Good!”

As he instructed, I followed precisely. With my martial realm, how could anything I did be imprecise? I could probably work in a way that pleased him more than anyone else in the Thousand Poisons Sect.

“But do you usually do all of this alone?”

“Yes.”

“It must be difficult.”

I knew why he did not carelessly bring poison men into this place.

There were far too many lethal poisons in his residence, and if he let disciples in and they caused an accident, the problem would become enormous.

In fact, the Thousand Poisons Forest was a place under strict surveillance and investigation by Tongcheon Pavilion. Whenever poison was used, it had to be reported, and its inventory was always inspected.

After finishing the compounding of the Severing Soul Acid, I honestly revealed my thoughts with a pleased expression.

“This is fun! What poison will you compound next?”

Of course, he did not believe me.

“I think you’d be better suited as an actor in the marketplace than as the Heavenly Demon.”

“When you go with me to the Central Plains later, how about joining a theater troupe? With your looks, Poison King, I think you could even aim for the lead role.”

The Poison King looked horrified. His expression said exactly this: I don’t even want to leave this place, and what? You want me to take the lead role in a play?

“Just now, in your imagination, everyone onstage and even the spectators were all poisoned to death, weren’t they?”

“I wonder whether you survived, Second Young Master?”

As he turned away sharply, I shouted after him,

“The performance is canceled!”

He left without even pretending to hear.

* * *

The Poison King had little interest in other people’s affairs.

If this was the level of interest he showed in me, who had come to him for such an important matter, I could imagine how he usually regarded others. To him, people probably existed below poisonous herbs.

Of course, there were times when he impulsively asked questions. Like right now. While grinding poisonous herbs, the Poison King suddenly asked,

“Hey, Second Young Master, be honest. You’re trying to secretly learn poison arts here so you can poison your brother to death, aren’t you?”

As I transferred water into a large tub, I answered,

“It’s obvious that I’m here with you, Poison King. If my brother died of poisoning, who do you think they would suspect first? If anything, I’m the one who should be worried that my brother might be poisoned to death.”

Thud, thud! Instead of refuting me, the sound of the Poison King grinding the poisonous herbs grew louder.

“I have no interest in poisoning someone to death. If I could learn even one thing from you, Poison King, I would want to learn something else.”

“What?”

He still did not look at me and remained focused only on grinding the herbs. There was no telling when he might go off into his own world again, but my answer now was one that would surely draw him back to this side.

“I want to learn detoxification. Isn’t the flower of poison arts not poisoning, but detoxification?”

In that instant, the sound of grinding herbs stopped. His gaze was fixed on me. It was the most surprised expression he had shown since meeting me. Because I had precisely spoken the thought he carried within him.

“It has only been a few days, but what I have felt since coming here is that making an antidote to perfectly neutralize a poison seems more difficult than making the poison itself. So I had this thought. A person who can create an antidote that can certainly detoxify the poison he made. A person who, when an opponent scatters poison, can neutralize that poison in an instant. Wouldn’t that person be the true expert of poison arts?”

It meant that only when not merely poisoning, but detoxification as well, was perfected could one finally comprehend the ultimate essence of poison arts.

“Where did you hear such words?”

From the next Poison King, who learned poison arts from you.

“They’re just my own thoughts. The comparison is a little strange, but martial arts are the same. Capturing someone alive is harder than killing them.”

I threw him my winning move. A contest most advantageous to me, who was impervious to all poisons.

“Choose one poison and teach me its detoxification method. I will detoxify it, and you will poison me. How about it? Let’s have a match!”

You have cultivated poison arts your whole life, so surely you can win at least that much? Isn’t this a matter of pride?

I did not provoke him that way. A clumsy provocation would only backfire.

Poison King, say you’ll do it. Say we should have a match!

As long as he said that, this wager would be my victory.

But the Poison King was not so easy to lure in.

“No.”

At the Poison King’s firm refusal, I asked,

“Why not?”

“Do you think I don’t know your little scheme? You want me to say something like this, don’t you? If you can detoxify my poison, I will consider myself to have lost the wager. Isn’t that right? That’s what you want to hear, isn’t it?”

I did not deny it. In truth, if it had not been my own business, I would have wanted to applaud him.

“You just have to win the wager, don’t you?”

“How can I guarantee that I’ll win? The detoxification technique would also be my detoxification technique.”

The Poison King began grinding the poisonous herbs again.

“Go practice barking, Second Young Master.”

* * *

That evening, I went to see Father.

The more I came to know the Poison King, the more stifled I felt. At times like this, one needed someone who could strengthen one’s resolve. In that regard, there was no one more suitable than Father.

Because I visited him right before he went to sleep, I once again saw Father’s pajamas.

“The floral pattern is different from the one I saw back then. This must be the greatest protective armor in the murim. Enemies will see it and think, ‘No way the Heavenly Demon would wear pajamas like that,’ and just pass right by. Those clothes are the safest clothes of all!”

Father looked at me with eyes that asked how long I planned to keep spouting nonsense, but he seemed to have known I would come at least once.

“You knew, didn’t you, Father? That the Poison King was not someone who would come over to my side.”

“Have you given up already?”

“Of course not. It’s just that I’ve never seen someone so trapped in his own world.”

At the corners of Father’s mouth appeared that distinctive sneer of his. Yes, I had come today to see that smile.

Seeing it gave me strength. If anything, my mood improved.

Yes, enjoy yourself as much as you like now. I will definitely bring the Poison King before you.

I asked the question I had wanted to ask for some time.

“Father, you dislike the Poison King, don’t you?”

Father neither affirmed nor denied it. Since Father truly loved martial arts, it was only natural that he disliked poison arts or soul-bewitching arts.

“If you dislike him, why do you keep him alive? You should kill him.”

“Because he is the most threatening to our enemies.”

“What if he starts a rebellion, or goes mad and poisons the people of our cult?”

“If we are to make the Poison King a weapon of our cult, we must accept that level of risk.”

“What if my brother or I die in the process?”

“That too must be accepted.”

“That’s something you can only say when you have about ten hidden children somewhere.”

“Why do you think I don’t?”

“You shouldn’t. My one brother alone is already this much of a headache.”

A faint smile formed at the corners of Father’s mouth. Perhaps if Sima Ming or the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had said it, he might have laughed heartily.

“It was the same before, but whenever I stand before a wall that feels completely blocked, I think of you first, Father. Now that I’ve seen your face, that is enough. Then I will take my leave. Ah, at this rate, I may soon show you an unfilial act that isn’t quite unfilial in the Grand Training Grounds.”

After making that joke, I was about to turn away when Father suddenly asked,

“Do you think the Poison King is trapped in his own world?”

Chapter 212: The Poison in a Certain Pocket.
--------------------------------------------
Did I think the Poison King was trapped in a world of his own?

“Is he not?”

I asked Father in return, as if it were only natural. Did Father not think so? What sort of person did Father think the Poison King was?

But Father had not brought it up to tell me what sort of person the Poison King was, or what the Poison King’s world was like. He had done it to say this.

“And how open is your world?”

Father looked at me for a moment, then went silently back into his quarters.

I stood there, thinking about what Father had said. And then I understood. I understood the meaning contained in his words.

Father was asking me this.

What difference is there between you, crying out for a new Demonic Path, and the Poison King? To others, are you not also trapped in a world of your own?

Of course, he did not mean that I was exactly the same as the Poison King, who disliked meeting people and preferred being alone.

He meant that my approach to the Poison King had been wrong. If I approached him from that perspective, the Poison King would never open the door to his heart.

The Poison King must have felt it. That I was looking at him as though he were trapped in a world of his own. The gazes of countless people must have been like that until now. I had been no different from them. The very pledge asking him to leave the Thousand Poisons Forest had been a mistake.

“Thank you, Father! As expected, Father, you are the wisest and broadest-minded person in the world, and…”

Before I could even finish speaking, the lights inside the house went out.

“…and cold-hearted.”

I bowed respectfully toward Father’s quarters, then turned back to my own.

Giving me a clue put Father at a disadvantage. Even so, Father had enlightened me. Apart from the wager, it was the affection of a parent for a child who had come to him for help.

* * *

The next day, when I went to the Thousand Poisons Forest, the Poison King was not in his quarters.

I looked around the nearby woods in search of him. After a long while, I found him deep in the forest.

He was lying flat on the ground, having a staring contest with a snake.

When the Poison King conversed, he could converse perfectly well, but once he became absorbed in something, he was the sort of person who would not notice even if someone died beside him.

Even now, he had no idea I had come nearby and was sunk in a trance. Seeing him like this, I had thought he was trapped in a world of his own. I had considered him an eccentric, and without realizing it, I had vaguely thought myself superior to him.

This was what Father had made me realize. Do not do that. He was not trapped; he was simply living his own life.

When I approached and looked closely, it was no ordinary snake.

It was a Flower King Seven-Step Snake, a spiritual creature with an extremely deadly poison even among poisons. It was called Flower King because it loved flowers, and Seven-Step Snake because even a martial artist with profound internal energy would die before taking seven steps after being bitten.

Just then, the Poison King spoke to the snake.

“Hey, you’ve gotten big lately and become arrogant. Since when did you start lifting your head so stiffly at me and talking back?”

Then the Flower King Seven-Step Snake, which had been flicking its tongue, lowered its head. It could not possibly be true, but it really looked as if it understood him.

“And you. I told you that whatever else you do, you mustn’t eat ghostfire grass berries carelessly, didn’t I? Why don’t you listen? Do you need to be steeped in fire liquor before you come to your senses?”

The Flower King Seven-Step Snake coiled itself and tucked its head inside. Did it really understand human speech? It had to be a coincidence, right?

I had lived long enough to see all manner of things, but this was the first time I had ever seen a sight like this.

Then another snake passed by beside it. This one was a Yin-Yang Blood Venom Snake, possessing venom no less powerful than the Flower King Seven-Step Snake’s. It, too, was a snake difficult to come across.

“Wol!”

At that, the Yin-Yang Blood Venom Snake stopped and looked at the Poison King. Watching it flick its tongue, the Poison King said,

“You know that Wang is a womanizer, don’t you?”

The Yin-Yang Blood Venom Snake looked at the Flower King Seven-Step Snake and flicked its tongue with a hissing sound.

“If Wang bothers you, come and tell me. Got it?”

I could not tell whether it truly understood his words or not, but when he finished speaking, the Yin-Yang Blood Venom Snake disappeared into the undergrowth.

“You go on, too!”

Then the Flower King Seven-Step Snake vanished into the forest.

Truly, it was a sight difficult to believe even after seeing it.

As the Poison King rose from his place, he saw me and was startled.

“Good heavens! You scared me!”

“I was more startled. What sort of Poison King is so surprised whenever someone is beside him?”

Of course, if someone had approached with killing intent or malice, the Poison King would have realized it at once. One must never look down on those who had reached the position of Demon Venerable in terms of martial arts. A Demon Venerable was still a Demon Venerable. If they went mad and ran wild, no one could know who would defeat whom, or how. They were merely refraining from revealing their true abilities.

“If you asked assassins which of the Eight Demon Venerables they would kill, every last one of them would line up behind the sign with your name on it! Even an assassin on his first kill mission would succeed and leave alive.”

“If he tried, Wang would bite him to death.”

“Don’t tell me you even named the snakes?”

“Is this your first time seeing a Poison King who named a snake?”

The Poison King strode off.

Of course it was my first time! I followed close behind him and said,

“When did you become so friendly with snakes?”

“I was seven when I started roaming around catching snakes. Wang and Wol were there even then.”

“You were young.”

“In our day, everyone started at that age.”

The Poison King’s eyes, as he recalled his childhood, held sorrow and hardship. It was enough for me to understand why he disliked leaving this place.

“If you have been here since you were seven, then this place is your world, Poison King.”

The Poison King stopped walking and glanced at me.

“You said you wanted to take me out of this world, didn’t you?”

“No. Now, I regret saying that.”

“Why?”

“My desire to show you the outside world is still the same, but I should not have said it in that manner. I sincerely apologize for acting arrogantly.”

When one is sorry, one must apologize sincerely. I did not attach excuses to my apology, explaining why I had no choice but to act that way. Reasons were something for the person forgiving to attach to forgiveness.

But whether he heard my words or not, he had already stopped walking and was staring at a poisonous insect clinging to a tree.

Seeing the Poison King absorbed in something again, I smiled without realizing it.

What was he thinking about now? Perhaps he was trying to converse with that poisonous insect.

This is his world, and this is an open world.

* * *

Several more days passed.

I silently devoted myself to the work, and the Poison King still liked sinking into his own world.

What changed over those days was my heart toward the Poison King’s world. Now, I tried to understand his world. The world where a seven-year-old boy had gone around catching venomous snakes.

Today, too, the Poison King was lost in thought as he stared at the poisonous mushrooms growing in a wooden box.

Then, as he turned his head absentmindedly, he shouted.

“You scared me!”

Because I had been quietly standing beside him, looking at the wooden box he had been looking at.

“Are you going to keep startling me?”

“Seeing those poisonous mushrooms growing so well puts my heart at ease. At this rate, haven’t I become a poison man too?”

“How long has it been for you to already be calling yourself a poison man?”

“I might as well just live here. The Thousand Poisons Forest feels like home now.”

“If you think acting this friendly will change anything, you’re mistaken.”

“It’s better if nothing changes. I like you exactly as you are now, Poison King.”

“What?”

The Poison King shouted as he looked at me.

“It won’t work! Petty tricks like this won’t work on me!”

His voice denying me was growing louder little by little. That was proof of just how much closer I was getting to him.

* * *

Sangseon always guided me from the entrance of the Thousand Poisons Forest to the Poison King’s quarters.

“I can go in alone now.”

“Even so, dangerous poisonous creatures may appear.”

That was probably not the only reason. He still guided me in a confusing way through several paths, and he did not lower his guard against me.

I could feel that it came from deep loyalty toward the Poison King. The eyes of someone who genuinely likes and is loyal to another will inevitably show it in some way.

“There is something I would like to ask you, elder.”

“Speak.”

“Since when have you known the Poison King?”

“Since he was young.”

“What was he like in his childhood?”

Sangseon stopped walking.

“Why do you ask?”

“I am curious.”

“He was not very different from now. Intelligent, considerate, cheerful, bright. He had the qualities of a leader as well.”

I forced myself to swallow the words, “Isn’t that completely different from now?”

It was because Sangseon did not seem like he would take my joke as a joke. His affection for the Poison King was not simple loyalty; it was more like that of an uncle, or a parent.

Seeing him made me think of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

* * *

I did not know whether the Poison King was intelligent, considerate, cheerful, bright, and possessed of the qualities of a leader, but one thing was certain. He was cute. And not only because of his fresh-faced appearance. For example, there was this.

The Poison King wore twelve pouches at his waist, and each pouch had a picture drawn on it. They were twelve animals representing the zodiac, but the paintings were anthropomorphized in a colorful, adorable style that might be drawn on children’s clothes. At first, I had passed over them without thinking, but now I could tell.

“You chose these yourself, didn’t you?”

“I drew them.”

I was startled. He had drawn all of these himself?

“No one in the world would know. That the Poison King’s deadliest poisons are inside such cute pouches.”

When one thought of the Poison King’s poison pouches, should they not have evil spirits drawn on them, or, if words were engraved, frightening characters such as Demon, Kill, Evil, or Death? If all those pouches opened, hell would unfold. In truth, that made them feel even stranger and more terrifying.

“Which pouch holds the most frightening poison? Surely it isn’t inside that cute rabbit pouch, is it?”

“Shall we check?”

He tried to put his hand into the rabbit pouch.

“I decline!”

I waved my hands and quickly backed away. Seeing that, the Poison King smiled, then immediately put on a straight face. It was the first time he had smiled brightly at me. He seemed startled himself after smiling. Though I saw all of it, I pretended not to.

* * *

The next day, there was an important poison mixture to prepare.

Unlike usual, the Poison King explained the poison to be made today with a grave expression.

“This Heavenly Heart Poison here is one of the ingredients for the Dark Soul Severing Poison, one of the deadliest poisons I use. A single drop of this Heavenly Heart Poison can easily kill dozens of people. Now, you stand far over there… You have already stepped back.”

I was standing by the door, ready to bolt out at any moment.

The Poison King searched for something on the desk.

“It was here. Where did it go?”

“What are you looking for?”

“My gloves.”

He was looking for the deerskin gloves he used when handling poison.

“Do you mean these, perhaps?”

I held up the gloves I was wearing.

“Why are you wearing those?”

“You said it was an extremely dangerous poison.”

“Then what about me?”

“Why would the Poison King need gloves?”

“Bring them here this instant.”

I quickly took them off and brought them to him.

“What is in your mouth?”

I took out the Poison-Avoiding Beads I had been holding in both cheeks and showed them to him. Seeing me holding not one but two Poison-Avoiding Beads in my mouth, the Poison King shook his head with a dumbfounded expression.

“At this rate, even I won’t be able to kill you.”

Of course, it was his joke. If the Poison King made up his mind to kill me, even if I held a hundred Poison-Avoiding Beads in my mouth, I would not be able to block his poison.

I, too, was playing around with him. It was also part of my effort to become closer to him. If you truly had been a bright and cheerful person when you were young, then somewhere in your heart, those things must still be sleeping.

The Poison King put on the gloves and began making the poison in earnest.

“If the amount is even slightly off, blue smoke will rise. Then it is a failure. You must truly be careful and focused. This is a manufacturing method that only I can perform.”

The Poison King showed me how to mix the Heavenly Heart Poison to make the Dark Soul Severing Poison. Since I had no way of knowing what the other ingredients were anyway, rather than showing me how to make the Dark Soul Severing Poison, he was showing me how carefully this process had to be carried out.

He could have sent me out and done it alone, but it seemed he wanted to boast to me.

“Did you see? How prudent and careful one must be when handling it?”

“Yes.”

Just then, blue smoke rose from behind him.

“Poison King!”

“You hurry outside!”

I swiftly went out. After drinking an antidote from his robe, the Poison King poured a liquid that had been placed nearby onto the smoking spot.

Chiiiiik!

The smoke rose even more.

“Poison King! Poison King!”

Because he did not come out quickly, I hesitated over whether I should run in and save him.

Then the Poison King came outside, waving away the poisonous smoke.

“Are you all right?”

“Of course I am.”

He scattered a wisp of poisonous smoke that came out of his mouth with a fan of his hand.

“The path of poison arts truly is not an easy one.”

“I failed on purpose. To warn you that you must be careful.”

“……”

“……”

“Of course you failed on purpose. Surely the Poison King, the greatest handler of poison in the martial world, could not have failed at making a poison he uses every…”

“Enough.”

“Yes.”

I smiled brightly as I looked at him. The Poison King looked back at me with a face that did not know how to react. Poison King, you still do not know. You still do not know who will be standing in the training ground.

It was at that very moment. Sangseon came running to where we were and swiftly reported.

“An investigator from Hwangcheon Pavilion named Seo Daeryong has come looking for the Second Young Master, saying it is urgent.”

Chapter 213: Because We Are Demons.
-----------------------------------
“If it had not been truly urgent, I would not have come all the way here.”

I hurriedly spoke to the Poison King.

Since it was the first time one of my subordinates had come looking for him, the Poison King also told Sangseon to bring Seo Daeryong at once.

A short while later, Sangseon brought Seo Daeryong here.

“Master of the Pavilion, it seems you must go at once.”

“What happened, Investigator Seo?”

“A major incident occurred in Maga Village. A martial artist who had been drinking peacefully suddenly swung his sword, killing or injuring dozens of people.”

I was startled and cried out.

“Where?”

“Yanghwa Pavilion.”

Yanghwa Pavilion was one of the many pleasure houses in Maga Village.

“Did he get into a fight while drunk?”

“No. They say blood suddenly began flowing from the eyes and nose of the man who committed the slaughter, and then he went berserk. His martial arts were exceptionally strong, so the damage was even greater.”

The moment I heard those words, I recalled an incident that had taken place before my regression.

I immediately bid farewell to the Poison King.

“Poison King, I will take my leave first today.”

I hurried out of the place with Seo Daeryong.

When I arrived at Yanghwa Pavilion in Maga Village with Seo Daeryong, the enforcement martial artists were keeping control of the surroundings. Even before entering, the stench of blood burned my nose.

One of the onlookers standing nearby caught my eye. A boy who looked to be twelve or thirteen was shouting at the enforcement martial artists.

“My mother and little sister are inside! Please go and check on them! They work in the kitchen! Mother! Suya!”

I left the boy behind and entered with Seo Daeryong.

The inside was a sea of blood. Corpses were scattered everywhere, while the enforcement martial artists checked to see whether any survivors remained.

“Everyone was drunk, so the damage was even worse.”

The dead were not martial artists alone. The courtesans had died, as had even the cooks who prepared the food.

In a corner of the kitchen lay a middle-aged woman, dead while holding a young girl in her arms. The child looked exactly like the boy who had been shouting outside.

I silently gazed down at the bodies of the mother and daughter and let out a sigh. The child must have run about the kitchen, helping her mother.

Seo Daeryong, standing beside me, could not bear to keep looking. He turned his head away and stared elsewhere.

“Where is the bastard?”

“He is on the third floor.”

I moved on again. The entire way there was filled with scenes of carnage.

The man had died leaning against the wall at the end of the third-floor corridor. He was a young man who looked to be in his early thirties. The blood that had flowed from his eyes and nose had reached his chin and dried there. His eyes were rolled back, and the whites had been stained red like blood.

Seo Daeryong, who had followed me, spoke in a trembling voice.

“This is the first time I have ever seen such a horrifying sight.”

I knew what had caused this incident.

‘狂暴—Rampage!’

Rampage was an internal-energy enhancer that temporarily increased one’s inner power when taken.

Depending on the amount consumed, an internal-energy enhancer could temporarily increase one’s inner power from fifteen minutes to roughly an hour. The increase was usually around ten to twenty percent. Of course, once the time passed, it returned to its original state.

The reason Rampage had exploded in popularity was that the amount of inner power it provided was far greater. Rampage increased it by thirty percent, and in some cases as much as fifty percent.

But its side effects were as deadly as its benefits were potent. The result before my eyes was one such side effect.

The people selling Rampage were the Evil Path Alliance. The Dissipating-Strength Poison Black Rain that I had been afflicted with during the Martial Arts Tournament after my regression was also something they sold. Rampage and Black Rain—the two main products of the Evil Path Alliance.

“What is this man’s identity?”

“He is one of the Demon Swords belonging to the Northern Heaven Sword Family. Apparently, he had never caused any trouble before. He was even promoted last year.”

That must have been the problem. He must have secretly taken Rampage in order to earn that promotion.

“Send his corpse to Physician Ma immediately. And take care of the aftermath here.”

“Master of the Pavilion. Regardless of why this happened, I want to uncover the truth.”

The look in Seo Daeryong’s eyes as he gazed at me was the same look he had worn when we first met, when he had been an investigator of the Yellow Springs Pavilion. It was the look of Seo Daeryong, who had been determined to uncover the truth behind his senior’s death.

I looked at Seo Daeryong and nodded once.

“Take good care of that boy who was outside earlier.”

Seo Daeryong nodded vigorously as well.

* * *

I watched Physician Ma examine the corpse.

As he inspected the body from every angle, Physician Ma deduced the cause of death.

“Judging by the way his dantian has swollen and ruptured, it seems a powerful internal-energy enhancer was used. His loss of reason and subsequent rampage were probably side effects.”

Physician Ma had accurately determined the state of the corpse.

“This is not the first corpse like this that I have seen. A corpse in this condition was brought in late last year. That man went berserk alone and died. This time, he committed a massacre.”

Rampage had not yet fully spread throughout the martial world. The side effects that had initially been ignored were beginning to cause serious problems as the number of users increased.

“When he committed the crime, he probably was not in his right mind. You fool, you should have increased your inner power through training. Why were you so greedy?”

After reproaching the corpse, he turned away. This was one of the reasons Rampage continued to spread. They should not have taken it in the first place. Since responsibility would inevitably fall first on the person who had taken the drug, the Evil Path Alliance took advantage of that attitude and continued to make money.

After covering the corpse with a white cloth, Physician Ma washed the blood from his hands. The news that so many innocent people had died had cast a pall over his mood as well.

As if trying to change the atmosphere, Physician Ma asked me,

“What happened with the Poison King?”

“I have been meeting him every day since that day.”

Physician Ma was startled.

“You meet him every day? Really?”

“I am learning about the work of the Heavenly Poison Forest.”

“As expected. You really are different. So, now that you have met him, what do you think of the Poison King?”

“He is a brighter person than I expected.”

Physician Ma nodded. He seemed to have sensed that bright side as well.

“It took me quite some time to realize it, but you noticed it already.”

“These days, we are together almost all day long.”

“Do you really want to win your wager with the Cult Master so badly that you are willing to go this far?”

I looked down at the corpse covered by the cloth and spoke quietly.

“Now I have even more reasons why I must win.”

* * *

My father and Supreme Commander Sima Myeong were waiting for me in the Heavenly Demon Hall.

I strode along the bloodstained path and reported to them.

“Physician Ma has determined that the cause of this incident was the side effects of an internal-energy enhancer.”

Neither of them was surprised by my report. That meant they already knew about the symptoms of the side effects.

“You already knew, didn’t you?”

Sima Myeong answered my question.

“Yes. Several major and minor incidents have been reported in the Central Plains.”

“It was the work of the Evil Path Alliance, wasn’t it?”

“How did you know?”

“During the last Great War of the Divine and Demonic, I was afflicted by a Dissipating-Strength Poison. When I investigated, I learned it was a poison called Black Rain, made by the Evil Path Alliance. I thought this incident might also be their doing.”

“The internal-energy enhancer is called Rampage. It was sold by the Evil Path Alliance.”

As expected, Sima Myeong already knew about Rampage.

I asked him again.

“Who is in charge of manufacturing Rampage for the Evil Path Alliance? You know, don’t you, Supreme Commander?”

Sima Myeong looked toward my father. My father had been watching me for a moment, and then he nodded.

Only then did Sima Myeong reveal who it was.

“Aecha.”

I knew very well who Aecha was.

“The Black Snake.”

“That is correct.”

He was one of Yulhan’s four subordinates. Just as Jisaeng, who had created the Divine Immortal Stronghold, had tattooed a golden pig on his body, Aecha had sworn his loyalty by tattooing a black snake.

This was my chance to speak.

As you can see, Yulhan is behind every evil deed. He is absolute evil.

But I did not say it. If I failed to win the wager by persuading the Poison King and bringing him before my father, then the Black Snake was also beyond my reach.

I remained silent for a moment. Through silence, I displayed my anger, and through silence, I lent strength to my resolve.

“A detailed report concerning the incident will be submitted by this pavilion. If anything else is confirmed, I will report it to you.”

I left the Heavenly Demon Hall by way of the bloodstained path.

It was probably the first time I had met the two of them and left without making even a single lighthearted remark. And so, upon the anger called the Divine Immortal Stronghold, I quietly piled another anger called Rampage.

My father and Sima Myeong silently watched my retreating back.

I was taking to heart the lesson my father had taught me this time.

From someone else’s perspective, my world, too, was a closed one.

Yes, I admitted it. The task of establishing a new demonic path and eliminating absolute evil still existed only within my own world.

* * *

That night, I stood alone before the Fist Demon’s cliff.

Just as the Fist Demon had once looked up at the cliff, I stared at it as well.

I clenched my fist and infused it with inner power.

Whoooosh!

Air currents swirled around my fist, and I heard the sound of storm clouds gathering.

Shiiiiing!

I was about to throw a punch at the cliff when I stopped at the final moment.

Because I knew I could not bring it down yet. In my entire life, there would be only one time when I struck this cliff with a true punch.

Then a welcome voice came from behind me.

“Your hand will only hurt if you hit it with those baby hands of yours.”

I turned around with a smile.

The Fist Demon was walking toward me.

“Master!”

Seeing him after so long filled me with joy. After looking at the Poison King’s youthful face, seeing this frightening face and enormous body made me truly feel that I was in the Demonic Cult.

The Fist Demon’s gaze fell on my tightly clenched fist.

Having read my anger, he spoke calmly.

“Disciple.”

“Yes, Master.”

“When you are truly angry, open the fist you have clenched like this.”

The Fist Demon spread his enormous palm before me. It was larger than my face.

“And then!”

Clap!

The Fist Demon brought his hands together with a resounding clap.

“Clap once, then clench your fist again. That way, your fist will hurt even more.”

“There would be no need to clench it again. If the enemy were between those clapping hands, it would be no different from being struck by a fist.”

The Fist Demon, of all people, was telling me to open my fist. I engraved this profound and grand lesson in my heart.

And the lighthearted remarks I had been unable to make before my father and Sima Myeong, I made to the Fist Demon instead.

“When Master stood here, I thought you had been standing with the single-minded determination to bring down the cliff.”

“And?”

“Were you not simply standing here whenever you got angry? Am I right?”

Unable to deny it, the Fist Demon laughed.

I greeted him and walked away. I walked with both fists, which had been tightly clenched, spread wide open.

* * *

The next day, I went to the Heavenly Poison Forest as usual.

The Poison King was wandering through the forest near his home, carrying a collection basket.

“Regarding the incident that happened yesterday…”

At that moment, astonishing words flowed from the Poison King’s mouth.

“Rampage, right?”

The Poison King had also heard only about the side effects the previous day, yet he had realized that they were caused by Rampage.

“You knew?”

“Of course I did. As you know, the Poison Children travel throughout the Central Plains in search of poisonous herbs and venomous creatures. The bastards of the Evil Path Alliance also travel around gathering medicinal herbs to make their drugs. That is why we know their movements better than anyone.”

The Poison King slightly knitted his brows.

“I even warned them. I told them not to make such a drug.”

“Do you know who makes it?”

“Of course. There is a fool named the True Poison Hermit.”

The True Poison Hermit. He was a poison arts master famous for his poisons in the Unorthodox Path. The Poison King seemed to dislike even thinking of him, as his face twisted into all manner of grimaces.

“Even poison users have things they refrain from doing, but that bastard is playing around at far too low a level.”

“Many innocent people died because of that low-level game. There was even a woman who died while holding a child. Those facts will not even appear in the report submitted to our cult. ‘Total number of dead: so many.’ The deaths of that mother and daughter will be buried beneath that single line. If their vengeful spirits became ghosts and watched over this world, the child would ask her mother, ‘Why does no one care that we died?’”

The Poison King was staring at a venomous insect clinging to a tree, as though he had not heard me at all. He did not get angry anymore. Just as I was living in my own world, he was living in his.

“What is that venomous insect called?”

I asked about his world, and he answered with mine.

“What meaning would there be in avenging them?”

“I remember it was an ingredient in the Azure-Killing Poison, but I cannot recall its name.”

“It is hypocrisy. The self-satisfaction one gains from helping someone.”

We did not listen to each other’s words. We each spoke only our own.

“Ah, what was it? I feel as though I remember the name, but I cannot quite grasp it.”

“The dead are dead. That is the end of it.”

The Poison King looked at me, and I looked at him. We stared at each other without a word. Since I had met him, this was the longest the Poison King had ever looked at me.

Then we returned to our respective worlds.

“Her name was Su. The little girl who died in her mother’s arms yesterday.”

“That venomous insect is called a White-Spotted Insect. It is not an ingredient in the Azure-Killing Poison, but in Five Poison Incense.”

The Poison King asked me with the most serious expression he had shown since meeting me.

“Tell me. What is the real reason you are acting like this?”

“When I say what I think, everyone asks me the same thing. ‘Are you from the Orthodox Sect? Are you some righteous hero?’ Whenever they do, I end up asking questions too, like that child who became a vengeful spirit. A powerless woman dies, a child dies, young people die one after another—and because we are members of the Demonic Cult, are we supposed to say that we do not care? If that is what those Orthodox Sect bastards think, then what exactly are we supposed to think as we live?”

The Poison King did not answer.

I did not press him any further. I clapped my hands once and spoke energetically.

“Let us go. There are many venomous insects we have to catch today, aren’t there?”

But he did not move. Instead, he sank down right where he stood.

With no other choice, I sat down beside him.

The Poison King watched the venomous ants passing beneath his feet, while I, since I was already seated, continued saying what I wanted whether he listened or not.

“The dead girl had an older brother. Outside that pleasure house, which had become a sea of blood, he was shouting to ask whether his mother and little sister were safe. I want to tell him this: ‘I killed every last one of the people who killed your family and came back. Normally, I would not have been able to kill anyone, but because we are members of the Demonic Cult, I was able to do it.’”

Still keeping his gaze on the ants, the Poison King said,

“You truly are impossible to understand.”

“Never support me. If you are careless, the next Heavenly Demon might be someone like this.”

“I will never support you, even if I die.”

But the person who was truly impossible to understand was the Poison King. Because he added,

“Do I really have to bark five times?”

Chapter 214: We Are Hellhounds.
-------------------------------
For a moment, I could not say anything.

It was a silence of surprise, and a silence of joy. Those words meant he had lost the bet.

"Careful! You're stepping on ants!"

I flinched and nearly crushed the ants underfoot.

I could not know the reason why he let me win. Whether it was because of the relationship he had built with me until now, because of the True Poison Spider of the Sacred Demon Alliance that he found displeasing, or because of that boy who had lost his family. Or perhaps because of those poison ants marching in a line.

Either way, the reason did not matter.

"Poison King!"

I hugged the Poison King tightly. With all my might.

"Thank you. Truly, thank you."

Before I knew it, the Poison King was shaking one of the poison pouches at his waist beside my face.

"Want to die? Not letting go?"

"No, sir!"

I scrambled backward.

The Poison King rose from his seat and spoke.

"Let's go. Let's go bark, and then go to the Cult Leader."

He meant to put words into action at once.

"Poison King, allow me to bark in your stead."

"You?"

"You granted permission so readily; I must repay the favor. Have I not only learned from you until now?"

"You would truly bark in my place?"

"Yes."

At my answer, filled with sincerity, the Poison King smiled.

"Is that not too bright a smile?"

"I am becoming human from a dog. I should be happy."

"Let us go."

I had to move quickly before he changed his mind.

We made our way straight to the Grand Training Ground.

"Please wait here at a distance. I will go and bark to my heart's content."

As I tried to head to the training ground, the Poison King grabbed me.

"You stay here. I shall bark. I am not a man who breaks his promises."

"Will you be alright?"

"Are you alright? You possess a body that could become the Heavenly Demon."

"Did you not say you would not support me even if you died?"

"I will not."

The Poison King's gaze and mine intertwined in the air. Though his face still looked young, his eyes were deep. Not supporting me, yet worrying about the day I become the Heavenly Demon—that mature consideration. That is the true nature of the man called the Poison King.

"I will bark. You stay."

The Poison King, who hated even stepping outside the main gate of the Thousand Poison Gate, began walking toward the training ground.

Watching that sight, I added a new principle to my life.

Never arbitrarily conclude about anyone's world.

Look at people properly; when there is something that must be said, do not delay; never arbitrarily judge anyone. I was adding principles to my life one by one.

As soon as the Poison King appeared, every gaze in the area fixed upon him. Though he revealed no special demonic energy, he naturally drew the eyes. The presence of a demonic lord was beginning to manifest.

The demonic practitioners were startled. The Poison King, whom they could barely catch sight of, had appeared in the center of the Grand Training Ground.

Not only did they halt their steps, but people also poked their heads out of windows. Gradually, people began to gather around. Among them were those seeing the Poison King for the first time.

At first, some approached thinking, *That man is the Poison King? He's that young?* but then belatedly covered their noses and mouths and retreated far away.

The Poison King waited for as many people as possible to gather. He could have quickly barked while there were few people and called the promise kept, but he did not. He was a man among men. Simply a man who hated going outside.

Word spread, and people continued to pour in.

Among them was Demon General Jang Ho. Jang Ho, whom I had not seen in some time, looked as dependable as ever. I greeted him with my eyes, and from afar he bowed his head respectfully.

Upon hearing the news, Seo Dae-ryong, the inspectors of the Yellow Heaven Pavilion, and even the executioners came rushing out. Seo Dae-ryong sent a telepathic message with a surprised face.

*—Pavilion Lord, what is going on?*

*—I am on my way to uncover the truth behind this.*

*—What? What do you mean?*

*—Did you not say so at the brothel where the accident occurred? That you wanted to uncover why this happened.*

*—I did.*

*—I am going to uncover it.*

*—At the training ground?*

*—Yes. This is the starting point.*

Seo Dae-ryong made a face as if to ask what I meant, but I explained no further.

In this manner, the area around the Grand Training Ground was packed with people. Every gaze was fixed on the Poison King. The Poison King who conversed with snakes wearing a childlike face, the Poison King who fell into contemplation watching venomous insects, the Poison King who drew cute pictures on his poison pouches—he stood dignified before the stares of so many demonic practitioners without batting an eye.

Everyone knew. If the Poison King decided to kill everyone present right now, if the twelve poison pouches at his waist were to open, everyone here would be annihilated.

When it seemed people had gathered as much as they would, the Poison King raised his hand. The murmuring died down in an instant.

At that moment, when so many people were not even drawing breath.

The Poison King let out a loud dog bark.

Everyone was startled. They had not expected the Poison King to gather them and bark like a dog. Some even fled in surprise, thinking the Poison King had gone mad and would release poison.

Right then, another cry echoed across the grounds.

"Even a tiger father begets a dog son!"

The one who shouted was, of course, me. I too walked toward the Poison King and barked loudly.

The Poison King looked at me with an expression asking what I was doing.

How could I let him bark alone?

"Let us bark together."

"No! You……"

"It is fine."

I gave him a confident smile. It was a smile of self-esteem, believing that such a trivial thing could never touch the dignity of one who would become the Heavenly Demon.

And this was not a matter of self-esteem. It was friendship with the Poison King; it was a show of gratitude. I would not leave him to bark alone.

The second time, I barked first.

The Poison King looked at me with an expression saying he could not understand me at all. Yet an irrepressible smile curved his lips.

I would grow closer to the Poison King. So that later, when I revealed, *I am invulnerable to all poisons!* I would see joy, rather than any other emotion, on his face. That is my goal. By no means an easy goal. Because the Poison King and invulnerability to all poisons were polar opposites.

We barked together.

Since we were barking anyway, we barked long, magnificently, like the howl of a wolf. Are all dogs the same? We are not neighborhood mutts, but magnificent dogs loyal to the Heavenly Demon Cult! Hellhounds that bite evil!

The onlookers murmured. Everyone wondered what was happening, but no one dared laugh at us.

It was then. Someone among the watchers barked along with us.

It was none other than Seo Dae-ryong. When Seo Dae-ryong barked, the Yellow Heaven Pavilion inspectors barked, and the executioners barked. The Poison Children began to bark, and Jang Ho barked too. When the Demon General barked, everyone was shocked, and the loyal demon soldiers began to bark as well.

Other demonic practitioners followed suit. In an instant, the Grand Training Ground descended into chaos.

We barked together amicably five times in that pandemonium.

This moment of barking together was far more exhilarating than watching the Poison King lose the bet.

* * *

After creating a chaotic scene, I entered the Heavenly Demon Hall with the Poison King.

Before opening the door to the Heavenly Demon Hall, the Poison King took out several pills and swallowed them.

"What medicine are you taking?"

Then came an unexpected answer.

"Medicine to calm the mind."

I asked him in bewilderment.

"But you did not take it when barking before all those people?"

"Because this is ten times more nerve-wracking than standing in the training ground. Right now, I am not going to meet the Cult Leader. I am going to defy him."

Father had been certain the Poison King would never yield to me. Yet since he had yielded so completely, one could say he was going beyond defiance to pick a fight.

"Furthermore, the Cult Leader dislikes me."

"Father is a martial artist to the bone, so he does tend to be cold toward the unorthodox. But he dislikes poison arts, not you, Poison King. Father often invites misunderstanding in such matters."

"I refuse your consolation."

"It is not consolation. Consider this: if he truly disliked you, would he have made this bet with you? Would a man like you, Poison King, have tolerated making such an important bet with someone you truly hate?"

Perhaps it sounded somewhat reasonable, for the Poison King made no retort.

"And so what if Father dislikes you a little? You simply live while being hated."

"If you keep that up, you get dragged away without anyone knowing."

"Do not worry. They may boil all other demonic practitioners in a pot, but they will not boil you, Poison King."

"Because they cannot eat me due to the poison?"

"No. Because if you are not here, the Martial Arts Alliance would invade immediately."

"You need not flatter me to look good anymore. I will keep my promise regardless."

"I refuse. I shall keep flattering you. Today's emotion will last forever."

"You are doing this because you know I will not survive today, yes?"

We entered the Heavenly Demon Hall.

We walked side by side down the Path of Blood toward Father.

Father seemed to have already heard the news that we barked at the Grand Training Ground. The atmosphere was solemn and icy.

After respectfully paying his respects, the Poison King gazed at Father and spoke respectfully.

"Cult Leader. Please permit the Second Young Lord's request."

Father glared fiercely at the Poison King and spoke.

"You were the only one I trusted."

The Poison King made a troubled expression.

"I apologize."

"What in the world made you yield?"

Father asked with an expression of genuine curiosity.

The Poison King seemed to contemplate his answer for a moment.

"I was moved by the Second Young Lord's mourning."

At the unexpected words, both Father and I were surprised.

"Many died in this incident. I was moved by the Second Young Lord's mourning for those he did not know."

I had not known the Poison King harbored such thoughts. It had not seemed so, but he had heard everything and thought deeply on it.

"I believe you can tell what kind of person someone is by the way they mourn others."

The Poison King, who had been trembling outside the door, spoke boldly and well before Father.

Father's gaze turned to me.

"You feel good?"

"I feel as though I could fly. For the first time, I accomplished something with the support of the demonic lords. It will be a day I never forget in my life."

I conveyed my joy honestly.

In truth, all of this was thanks to Father. Father's sharp rebuke.

*Just how open is your world?*

With those words, I had realized the Poison King's world was not a closed one. In fact, it had not been closed at all. Had he truly been someone living in a closed world, he would not have barked at the training ground, nor would he be standing here now. I had simply looked at him with a closed perspective.

It was thanks to Father.

But I did not speak those words here. Because right now was the moment to give Father a chance to be angry.

I believe. At least half of Father's heart must have been hoping I would win. I decided to believe so.

As always, Father kept his promise.

"Very well. I grant your request."

I performed a deep bow right there.

"Thank you, Father."

I know this was not an easy decision for Father, either. That is why I know I must handle this matter well.

"If you made such a request, you must have the confidence to leave no evidence that we were the ones who handled it?"

"Yes, I will do my utmost."

"Trying is not enough. You must handle it without ever revealing that we killed them."

Handling Ya Yol-han, the second-in-command of the Sacred Demon Alliance, and the Four Devilmates who served as his limbs, without anyone knowing it was our doing.

This, too, was a mission close to impossible, but I answered energetically.

"Yes! I shall do so."

To receive this permission, all the demonic lords had stepped up; the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon had even staked his life; and the Poison King had barked at the training ground.

Then I must accomplish it. Perfectly.

Father had already returned to his usual self. Father was not one to second-guess himself once a decision was made. He was a man without lingering regrets.

"Who will you go with?"

"I will go with the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon."

"Two may not be enough. Three would be reassuring."

At that moment, the Poison King made an expression of disbelief.

Father's gaze turned toward the Poison King.

"You will go along and assist."

"That will not do, Cult Leader!"

The Poison King jumped up in disgust.

"Did you say it will not do?"

At Father's low question, the Poison King quickly answered.

"No. It will do."

As if Father would simply let that slide. It was a *you'll get a taste of this* expression. I stand corrected. Father is indeed a man who holds grudges.

Father rose from his seat.

"Poison King, listen."

When Father revealed his Heavenly Demon aura, the Poison King bent at the waist in respect.

"Yes, Cult Leader."

"From this moment, I permit the use of the Twelve Extreme Poisons."

"I shall receive your august command."

The Twelve Extreme Poisons, the Poison King's main poison, could only be used with Father's permission. It was also testament to how fearsome the Poison King's poison was.

After respectfully greeting Father, the Poison King and I exited the Heavenly Demon Hall.

"You said you were nervous, yet you spoke even better than I."

"It is all thanks to the medicine."

The Poison King sighed as though the ground would sink. He, who hated even going outside the Thousand Poison Forest, was now going to the Central Plains.

"Outside the Thousand Poison Forest is dangerous!"

"Actually, you, Poison King, are the most dangerous being of all."

The Poison King glared at me with eyes that said this was all my fault.

"I am sorry. I did not know it would turn out this way either."

"I am not going. I refuse."

"Yes. I shall go with Lesser Demon Soma. Please hide yourself well in the Thousand Poison Forest."

"If I do that, I will really be dragged away."

The Poison King vented his irritation on some random spot.

"If I go out, I will kill them all. I will stop at every village and annihilate them. You think I cannot?"

He truly must hate going outside immensely.

"Given the opponent, you must be worried about me. Seeing as you are sending out the cult's greatest trump card."

"That level of comfort is not enough, not at all!"

The Poison King walked toward the Thousand Poison Forest with deeply slumped shoulders.

I shouted after him.

"We will be departing as soon as possible, so please prepare! I truly thank you for today!"

At that, the Poison King walked away barking. He was a very angry dog.

Smiling as I watched his retreating figure, I turned my steps toward the Valley of Villains.

Chapter 215: Join From the Beginning.
-------------------------------------
The Extreme Evil Soma was seated alone in the white room again today, staring at the wall.

Once I learned that the white wall helped with his martial training, my worries that he might go mad disappeared.

The first words I spoke upon entering his room were these.

“Lord Soma, let’s go fight.”

At the fact that I had obtained permission, Soma’s eyes behind the mask curved in a broad smile. He was the Extreme Evil Soma who had even worn the Immortal Ghost Mask and met my father in order to help me.

“Good.”

The Extreme Evil Soma answered readily. Even though he knew the opponent was Yaryul Han, he showed no fear at all. He was simply delighted to be going to fight alongside me.

“We’re not only going to deal with Yaryul Han, but also the four men under his command.”

The Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes smiled within the holes of his mask. The stronger the enemy, the happier he became. But that did not mean he was arrogant or that he failed to assess his opponent accurately.

“Yaryul Han is strong. This may become an even harder battle than the ones we have fought before.”

The Extreme Evil Soma was by no means letting his guard down. He understood the danger of this matter better than anyone. That was why I was all the more grateful. Even knowing what kind of matter this was, he had stepped forward for me.

“The problem is that we have to kill them without leaving evidence that we did it.”

“Second Young Master is clever, so you will find a way.”

“You have to help me, Lord Soma.”

“I’ll only take charge of the killing.”

He said it lightly, but I felt the light in the Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes deepen. It was an opponent that left one no choice but to think deeply. In a calm tone, the Extreme Evil Soma said to me,

“When I fight, there are times I forget who I am. That is when it is most dangerous. The Second Young Master I have seen knows who he is better than anyone. That is why I am not worried.”

“I’m not worried because you’re with me, Lord Soma.”

We looked at each other and laughed. We treated each other with goodwill, and were grateful for the other’s kindness.

I suddenly felt like bantering with him. I wanted to joke around too.

But I held back. Mistakes were made precisely at times like this. Because courtesy was something that had to be upheld precisely because one was close, yet became harder to uphold the closer one grew, I treated him with even deeper respect than before.

“Ah, and another Demon Venerable will be joining us for this matter.”

“Who is it?”

“By Father’s order, the Poison King will be going with us.”

At the mention of the Poison King, the Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes showed a slight look of discomfort.

“I have no personal ties with that man.”

Who did? That applied to both the Poison King and the Extreme Evil Soma.

“In the process of making this matter happen, I came to know a little about the Poison King. I thought he was a decent person.”

The Extreme Evil Soma still seemed uneasy. Liking me was one thing, but his relationships with the Demon Venerables were another.

The Extreme Evil Soma walked to the opposite wall and stood before the line he had drawn there.

“With the Poison King, he would be about here.”

The Extreme Evil Soma pointed to the starting point of the line.

“At least he’s on the line.”

At my words, the Extreme Evil Soma laughed. Since it had already been decided, he accepted the situation.

“We can only hope the Poison King’s poison doesn’t accidentally kill us.”

I, too, could not quite picture the two of them together. Combinations of Demon Venerables were not easily matched. Which two would be the best fit together?

“When can you depart?”

“Any time is fine with me.”

“Then let’s leave tomorrow morning.”

“Good.”

I was about to turn and leave when I said to the Extreme Evil Soma,

“This matter is thanks to you, Lord Soma. Thank you once again.”

“If we fail to return alive, you will have to use the word responsibility instead of thanks. Save your gratitude until after the matter is over.”

Having finished speaking, Soma looked back at the wall.

I stared at that white wall with him for a moment, then bade him farewell and left the room.

As I walked out of the Valley of Villains, the gazes of the Faceless Guests I encountered held even more goodwill than before. There were even some who bowed and said they had liked what happened earlier. After barking in the Great Training Ground, my popularity had risen even more. In any case, they were people who liked strange antics more than anything.

* * *

After leaving the Valley of Villains, I went straight to the Thousand Poison Forest.

The Poison King was in the middle of packing. I had come to tell him that we would leave tomorrow morning, but as if he had already expected it, he was busy gathering this and that.

“How did you know? That we would leave tomorrow morning.”

“It’s a chance you obtained after causing such a commotion. Of course we would leave right away.”

“As expected, you are clever.”

“If I were clever, would I have fallen into this crisis?”

To him, the crisis was not facing Yaryul Han. Leaving the Thousand Poison Forest was the crisis.

The Poison King packed poisons into a bag he could sling over his shoulder. Poisons in pouches, in containers, in bottles. He gathered all sorts of poisons.

“Is there any need to bring this much poison?”

Even the poison the Poison King normally carried on him would be enough to kill an enormous number of martial artists. And yet the Poison King packed what seemed like an excessive amount of backup poison.

“Who knows what might happen?”

Regardless of how much he hated going, he was thorough in his preparations. That was his nature. Without such meticulousness, he would never have been able to create so many poisons and antidotes.

The Poison King put on a thin long robe to conceal the poison pouches at his waist. Several pockets were sewn into the inside of that robe as well, and he tucked poison into those too.

“I’ll be carrying a leather satchel, so if you pack some for me, I’ll carry it too.”

“Are you planning to destroy the Evil Dao Alliance? Forget it.”

“You already look like you have enough to do that.”

Pretending not to hear me, he packed something else. This time, he wrapped a leather belt around himself, into which some twenty poisoned daggers had been stuck.

“You throw daggers too?”

“Better than you do.”

There was no way that was true, but I was surprised that the Poison King used daggers.

On top of that, the Poison King also packed a fan.

It was the Poison King’s unique weapon, called the Wujian Fan, and he mainly used it when administering poison. It was not difficult to scatter poison and kill everyone. What was difficult was killing only one person with poison in a room full of dozens.

One would have to be careful whenever the Poison King gently waved the Wujian Fan. There was no telling where the wind that led to hell would blow.

In later years, the next Poison King would evaluate his master, this very Poison King, as follows.

The greatest master of poison arts in three hundred years.

“I’m telling you in advance, but I’m not killing Yaryul Han. I’m just going along. I’ll only watch!”

“You say you’re only going to watch, so why are you packing so much poison?”

“So I can watch with peace of mind.”

This was his nature. Both his meticulousness and this anxiety.

“How long will it take?”

“I don’t know. I intend to deal with it as quickly as possible, but I don’t know what the situation will be like when we arrive.”

“Let’s deal with it and return as quickly as possible. Understood?”

“It will be faster if you help.”

“I don’t know about anything else, but I’ll have to deal with that fellow Jindok Geosa myself.”

The Poison King seemed irritated about this incident.

“If he was going to sell medicine, he should have made it properly before selling it. This is why we get cursed. It’s the reason the Cult Leader dislikes me.”

“But you must not leave any trace that you used poison arts, Poison King.”

“Am I going to leave corpses behind to put on display?”

He meant that he would melt even the corpses away without a trace.

In any case, if the Poison King dealt with Jindok Geosa, I would not have to reveal that I was impervious to all poisons.

“Then I will see you tomorrow morning.”

Leaving him behind as he busied himself packing poisons he might have missed, I left the Thousand Poison Forest.

As he led me out of the Thousand Poison Forest, Sangseon said,

“Please take good care of the Poison King.”
