Their martial prowess was formidable, but from a young age they had been raised as merciless blades trained to carry out orders without question. They were men who possessed no human emotions.

In the case of Cheonhwa House, clumsy schemes would not work, so they had sent in the Black-and-White Twin Blades immediately.

They intended to abduct the House Lord personally and force her to stamp her seal on a contract. The nine women here had all suffered the same fate.

“Tell them to bring her back safely without laying a finger on her!”

It was the worry of a lecherous old man who never changed.

* * *

“A precious guest will be arriving today.”

From early morning, Cheonhwa House Lord Yeo Jeong had the residence and inner courtyard cleaned. She applied her makeup and dressed with greater care than usual.

The maids and servants bustled about diligently.

They had learned from experience that whenever the Cheonhwa House Lord said a precious guest would arrive, a guest always came that day. At first, they assumed she knew because she had received prior notice, but after realizing that many guests arrived without warning, they all understood that the Cheonhwa House Lord was an extraordinary person.

But today, even she seemed to have been mistaken.

The people who entered the inner courtyard were not precious guests, but uninvited intruders.

The Black-and-White Twin Blades had burst in, dressed in black and white long robes.

The Cheonhwa House Lord sent the servants and maids inside. At the same time, the martial artists guarding the inner courtyard revealed themselves.

They greatly outnumbered the intruders, but the Black-and-White Twin Blades showed no fear whatsoever. Rather, the momentum of the two overwhelmed everyone present, and tension spread across the faces of the Cheonhwa House’s martial artists.

The Cheonhwa House Lord asked calmly,

“Where do you gentlemen come from?”

The Black Blade among the Black-and-White Twin Blades spoke to her.

“Our master wishes to see you. Come.”

His tone was cold and blunt, devoid of all emotion. In one sense, he sounded like a child; in another, like a piece of wood that had learned to speak.

“Who is your master?”

“You will know when you come.”

“I am sorry, but a precious guest is arriving today, so I will have to postpone this until another time.”

Of course, such words did not work on the Black-and-White Twin Blades.

“There is no one more precious than our master.”

With that, the two men strode forward. Of the eight martial artists from Cheonhwa House who blocked their path, four launched themselves forward at once. The remaining four surrounded the Cheonhwa House Lord.

Swords and blades clashed rapidly.

The masters who had guarded Cheonhwa House until now were formidable, but the Black-and-White Twin Blades were stronger.

Before they could withstand even ten exchanges, two of the four who had charged forward collapsed, their arms and waists slashed deeply.

At the very moment the Black-and-White Twin Blades raised their blades to finish them off, the Cheonhwa House Lord launched herself forward and blocked their path.

“If you want to kill these men, you will have to kill me as well.”

Since their mission was to bring her back, the Black-and-White Twin Blades lowered their blades.

She pressed the blood-sealing points of the injured men to staunch their bleeding.

“Treat them and wait here. I will be back.”

“You cannot do that, Lord!”

The other martial artists stepped forward to stop her, but the Cheonhwa House Lord firmly restrained them.

“Everyone, stop!”

At her command, the martial artists halted. They all knew that if they continued fighting, they would only die pointless deaths. Since Cheonhwa House had been founded, never had such powerful intruders appeared.

“If you lose your lives here for nothing, I will carry the guilt for the rest of my life and never be able to live properly. Withdraw and protect the establishment. A precious guest will be arriving today, so tell that person what happened.”

She forced the martial artists back, then approached the Black-and-White Twin Blades. She showed no fear whatsoever.

At her appearance, the Black-and-White Twin Blades glanced at each other and tilted their heads. They had abducted many women until now, but they had never seen one remain so calm.

The Cheonhwa House Lord stared intently at the two men.

“I am quite good at seeing people’s futures. But the futures of the two of you are so pitch-black that I cannot see them clearly.”

The Black-and-White Twin Blades gave a cold laugh and strode toward her.

Just as the Black Blade reached out to seal her pressure points—

A thread of finger force flew toward them as swiftly as light.

Pheeing! Ppeok!

The incoming attack pierced the back of the Black Blade’s hand, leaving a hole through it.

At the very moment the Black Blade let out a short cry and tried to staunch the wound—

Pheeing! Ppeok!

This time, the elbow of his other arm, the one he had been using to stop the bleeding, was pierced through.

“Ugh!”

The moment the Black Blade instinctively hunched his body—

Pping! Ppeok!

His shoulder was pierced. The attack had flown too quickly for him to evade.

The instant he turned toward the place from which the finger force had come, more streams of finger force flew toward him in succession.

His abdomen, waist, and knees were pierced one after another. The finger force poured down like rain, shredding his body completely.

“Aaaaaaaagh!”

The Black Blade screamed desperately, as though he were finally giving voice to every scream he had suppressed throughout his life.

Pheeing! Ppeok!

At last, his forehead was pierced, and he crumpled to the ground.

The finger force had flown in such rapid succession that all anyone saw was him dying. Even the White Blade could only stand there and watch the Black Blade die, unable to comprehend the unexpected turn of events.

Only then did everyone’s gaze turn toward the place from which the finger force had come. A bright smile spread across the Cheonhwa House Lord’s lips.

Geugak Soma, wearing a white mask, was walking toward them with steady steps.

Chapter 226: The Man Who Comes to Rescue the Courtesan.
-------------------------------------------------------
Only Geukak Soma was visible in the Cheonhwaru Lord’s eyes.

Everything around her vanished, leaving only the sight of Geukak Soma walking toward her against a white background.

“You’ve come, Brother?”

The martial artists of Cheonhwaru burst into cheers of joy. They did not know Geukak Soma’s true identity, but they knew he was the only person the Cheonhwaru Lord called Brother.

At that moment, Baekdo moved like lightning and hid behind the Cheonhwaru Lord. He quickly drew his blade and pressed it to her throat.

“Stop! If you come any closer, this wench dies!”

Geukak Soma halted his steps.

Using the Cheonhwaru Lord as a shield, Baekdo poked his head out slightly and glared at Geukak Soma. The two eyes behind Geukak Soma’s mask were smiling.

The blade Baekdo held to the Cheonhwaru Lord’s throat trembled violently. Until now, he had committed countless abductions and murders on Yeobulge’s orders, but he had never once felt fear in the process. Raised as a killing machine that knew no fear, he was now feeling fear. This emotion was unfamiliar even to him.

“Who are you?”

Then, instead of answering, Geukak Soma looked at the Cheonhwaru Lord. At his gaze asking if she was alright, she smiled even more brightly.

“Who else would come to save a courtesan? My husband, of course.”

Though he heard the word husband, Geukak Soma’s eyes were smiling.

Meanwhile, Baekdo could not understand.

That the Black Blade was killed by the husband of some brothel keeper? That he himself had to resort to taking a hostage? That he was feeling terror for the first time in his life? Ridiculous!

Geukak Soma spoke lowly.

“Close your eyes.”

The Cheonhwaru Lord closed her eyes.

The moment she closed her eyes!

Fwoosh!

Baekdo’s exposed ear flew off with a sickening smack.

The very moment Baekdo tried to slash the Cheonhwaru Lord’s throat without hesitation.

Thwack!

His elbow was pierced. The finger wind flew precisely toward a place that would pass through his body without harming her.

He gritted his teeth, trying to slash her throat, but—

Thud!

This time, the back of the hand holding the blade was pierced. The finger wind that pierced the back of his hand struck the sword’s hilt, so she was not harmed.

He really hadn’t known that attacks could be launched with such precision and delicacy.

The instant Baekdo tried to catch the falling blade with his other hand, his body was exposed.

Swish! Thud!

His left eye was pierced. The moment he staggered back, this time his right eye was pierced.

“Kuaaaaargh!”

With a wretched scream, he stumbled backward.

Fwoosh! Thud!

This time, his mouth was pierced.

Everyone could feel that he was being punished—for the hand that pointed a blade at the Cheonhwaru Lord, and for the mouth that threatened to kill this wench.

Even in this dying moment, Baekdo could not believe it. How could one attack so boldly when there was a hostage? And how could those attacks be so precise and delicate? And how could a person fire finger winds in succession like that?

Swish! Crack!

Baekdo’s forehead was pierced, and he collapsed just like that. He died harboring all manner of doubts. Really, a husband?

A mixture of joy and emotion flashed across the Cheonhwaru Lord’s face. The taciturn Geukak Soma had shown his rage not with words, but with action. He had always been that kind of man. One who showed through deeds rather than words.

The Cheonhwaru Lord looked down at the corpses of the Black-White Twin Blades and the blood flowing around them, speaking with disappointment.

“I had cleaned everything up because I thought you were coming.”

“It’s fine. I like the smell of blood.”

The Cheonhwaru Lord strode over and threw herself into Geukak Soma’s arms. Geukak Soma did not embrace her in return and simply stood still, but the Cheonhwaru Lord was not the least bit upset.

Considering Geukak Soma’s personality and the first time they met, for him to simply stand there like this was no different from squeezing her tight in a hug.

The martial artists of Cheonhwaru standing behind also bowed in unison, conveying their gratitude for saving their master.

Geukak Soma said to the Cheonhwaru Lord.

“There are guests who came with me.”

Realizing the meaning behind those words, the Cheonhwaru Lord cleared the hall.

The injured martial artists were sent to a physician, and the corpses of the Black-White Twin Blades were removed. And the remaining martial artists were all sent to the outer court so that no one could enter or leave.

Then, Geom Mugeuk’s party entered among others.

The Cheonhwaru Lord greeted Geom Mugeuk first.

“Second Young Master, have you been well?”

“I have been well.”

“This warrior didn’t come with you?”

“I was terrified someone would steal our pretty little heart away, so I hid her away securely in the main sect.”

The Cheonhwaru Lord smiled and said.

“She is one who will become precious. Even if you send her out into the world, she will fare well.”

Geom Mugeuk knew well that she was an extraordinary woman. Just by being able to see through Yi An’s beauty during her plump days, she had proven herself to be no ordinary person.

“Even if you don’t believe my words, believe this warrior.”

“No, I believe Lady Lord’s words and will send her out.”

Even if only because of the Gwiyeongdae, she would have to go out into the world. Since the Bicheon Swordsmanship had reached Nine Star, he was not very worried about sending her out. Now, Cheongmyeondo was with her as well.

Geom Mugeuk next introduced the Poison King to her.

“This is the Poison King of our main sect.”

To be introduced to a mere brothel keeper? Under normal circumstances, this would have been unthinkable, but the Poison King did not feel uncomfortable in the slightest. If she was a woman who called Geukak Soma Brother, she was no simple brothel keeper.

The Cheonhwaru Lord showed him polite courtesy. As she was about to bow where she stood, the Poison King released a gentle internal energy to stop her.

“There is no need for such excessive courtesy.”

Instead, the Poison King courteously cupped his fists and greeted her.

“I am honored to meet you.”

It was consideration for Geukak Soma.

“I greet your esteemed self. I am called Cheonhwaru Lord Yeojeong.”

She was not at all surprised that the Poison King looked so youthful. Just as she had seen the true form hidden within the outer appearance, perhaps she was looking at the true form hidden within the Poison King’s exterior.

At the sight of her treating the Poison King with respect, Geukak Soma’s gaze toward the Poison King softened. Their relationship was still awkward, but when they first left the sect, they hadn’t exchanged a single glance for days. Compared to then, they were now sworn brothers.

Next, Seo Daeryong was introduced.

“This is my right-hand man.”

“It is an honor to meet you. I am Seo Daeryong.”

The Cheonhwaru Lord stared at Seo Daeryong intently, then smiled and said.

“It has been a long time since I have met someone with such a pure soul.”

She seemed to recognize Seo Daeryong’s kindness at a glance. Seo Daeryong was moved. He had likely never heard anyone say his soul looked pure since the day he was born.

Seo Daeryong shrugged his shoulders at Geom Mugeuk with a look that said, “See, I’m this kind of person.”

“When you introduce me, make sure to put in ‘pure soul’! You can leave everything else out.”

“Isn’t that just a fancy way of saying he gets taken advantage of wherever he goes, is easily deceived by women, and has a face perfect for being swindled?”

Seo Daeryong looked at the Cheonhwaru Lord with an *ah, surely not* expression, sending her a desperate gaze as if to say, *You have to tell me that’s not true*, but she merely smiled.

Next, Gowol was introduced.

“If that one is my right hand, this one is my brains.”

The Cheonhwaru Lord’s eyes as she looked at Gowol were similar to when she looked at Yi An. The eyes of reverence and support for one who would accomplish great things, but she did not speak it aloud.

The Pungcheon Cult Leader’s eyes sparkled, wondering what Geom Mugeuk would introduce him as.

“He is my steadfast sponsor.”

The Pungcheon Cult Leader said.

“You have to have more to take to be a sponsor.”

“You have plenty left to take.”

“Left?”

“A wealthy man goes bankrupt, but it hasn’t even been three years yet, has it?”

When Geom Mugeuk put on an expression as if he knew all the hidden things, the Pungcheon Cult Leader needlessly threw a remark at Gowol.

“Don’t go around talking about me!”

Even at the words implying more would be taken, the Pungcheon Cult Leader was not upset. He knew Geom Mugeuk was speaking in jest, and besides, what could be sadder than becoming an unnecessary existence to someone?

And so, Geom Mugeuk introduced all his people.

The Cheonhwaru Lord watched Geukak Soma, the Poison King, Seo Daeryong, Gowol, and the Pungcheon Cult Leader standing to Geom Mugeuk’s left and right with an inscrutable gaze. Just what was she seeing now?

The Cheonhwaru Lord bowed her head courteously.

“Welcome to Cheonhwaru.”

Geom Mugeuk’s party also bowed their heads courteously in return.

The Cheonhwaru Lord guided them inside.

“I shall prepare rooms for you to stay in first.”

Then Geukak Soma said to her.

“Put mine beside yours.”

“Yes.”

At those words, the Cheonhwaru Lord must have understood—that this visit of ours was related to the safety of Cheonhwaru. Even so, she did not ask before we spoke first. She was calm yet cautious.

While everyone was settling in, I met with the Cheonhwaru Lord together with Geukak Soma first and explained the situation.

“The reason we came this time is because we heard information that our enemy, who runs brothels, is targeting the Gui Province.”

“Could that person be Yeobulge?”

As expected, she already knew about Yeobulge. Since Cheonhwaru was also a place that gathered information for Geukak Soma, she had already taken measure of him, who was called the King of the Night.

“That is correct.”

“I know that Yeobulge has been expanding his brothels throughout the Central Plains. His reputation is very poor.”

“It seems time for him to pay the price for those rumors.”

A glint of joy flickered in the Cheonhwaru Lord’s eyes. As one who was in the brothel business herself, she must have heard of many evil deeds related to Yeobulge.

“Lady Lord, you have no idea what kind of look Soma had the moment he heard that Yeobulge was advancing into the Gui Province.”

The Cheonhwaru Lord looked at Geukak Soma.

“But I think I know?”

She looked at Geukak Soma with a gaze that could only come from deep trust.

Meanwhile, Geukak Soma was still in low spirits despite having killed the Black-White Twin Blades. What if he had arrived late? Because of that, his rage toward Yeobulge had deepened further. One could feel the fury that wanted to rush to Yeobulge right this instant.

On the other hand, the Cheonhwaru Lord was so bright and calm despite having faced a life-threatening crisis.

Geom Mugeuk felt something from this composure. He thought she was a woman who knew at least how her own fate would flow, and that if they had not arrived in time, she would have made a different choice from the beginning.

“How enviable!”

It was something he said pointlessly to embarrass Geukak Soma, but Geukak Soma merely examined the Cheonhwaru Lord in silence.

Shortly after, everyone gathered in one place.

Gowol revealed the plan for what would follow.

“Today’s incident was unexpected.”

They had intended to deal with him slowly after coming here, but Yeobulge had struck at Cheonhwaru faster than anticipated.

“If it is revealed that the Black-White Twin Blades have died, he may strike Cheonhwaru in earnest. If we do not evacuate this place, there may be casualties. Even if we fend him off, problems will arise. The Demonic Alliance will definitely learn that Yeobulge suffered a setback while attacking Cheonhwaru.”

This place could no longer serve as a sanctuary for Geukak Soma.

“What do you think?”

At my question, Gowol gauged the expressions of Geukak Soma and the Cheonhwaru Lord.

“The plan I have devised is something that requires Lord Soma’s and Lady Lord’s permission.”

After saying so, Gowol revealed the plan he had devised.

“He wants Lady Lord, so you must go. Young Master and Lord Soma will play the role of the Black-White Twin Blades and take Lady Lord to his quarters. Since they are ones who normally wear distinctive clothing, if the two of you wear black and white garments and press down bamboo hats as well, you should be able to get inside as deep as possible.”

It was a surprising and audacious plan. And the core of this plan was secrecy.

“His death must be revealed as late as possible.”

Because the plan was to push through to Yayulhan before his death became known.

Of course, Gowol added a word regarding the part he was most concerned and careful about in this plan.

“If those going together were not the Young Master and Lord Soma, I would never have devised a plan to send Lady Lord into such a dangerous place.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Geukak Soma. Since it was a matter where the Cheonhwaru Lord had to go as well, he intended to follow Geukak Soma’s decision.

The Cheonhwaru Lord spoke up first, calmly.

“I will go.”

Geukak Soma’s hesitation was not long. He nodded, granting permission for this matter.

Gowol bowed his head, expressing his gratitude to the two for accepting this plan.

And then he spoke to the Poison King.

“There is also something I must ask of the Poison King. After Yeobulge is eliminated, please protect Lady Lord until this matter is concluded.”

It could have been an unwelcome request, but the Poison King answered readily.

“I shall.”

Swish!

The Poison King unfolded his fan and fanned himself gently. Now that he had permitted it, who in the world could kill the Cheonhwaru Lord?

Geukak Soma’s and the Poison King’s gazes intertwined in the air. The two of them did not say words befitting this moment like “I ask this of you” or “Don’t worry.” Neither was one to boast of favors, nor was either one to repay debts with mere words.

Finally, Gowol spoke to Geom Mugeuk.

“I will go first to prepare to face Yayulhan.”

It was words filled with conviction that the two would succeed and return.

The Cheonhwaru Lord looked alternately at Geukak Soma and Geom Mugeuk and said.

“If such handsome Black-White Twin Blades had come, I would have followed them first.”

At her joke, everyone laughed together.

Geom Mugeuk asked Geukak Soma.

“Will you take Baekdo? Or Heukdo?”

Geukak Soma answered curtly.

“Because white suits me.”

Chapter 227: I’m a Kept Man’s Friend.
-------------------------------------
“Are you not getting out of the way, you damned bitch!”

Yeo Bulgae went berserk and began beating her mercilessly. Even while taking the blows, the middle-aged woman shielded the other woman with her body. It was not enough for him to merely satisfy his lust—he tormented them with such perversion that she could not bear it and had stepped forward to help. She had suffered the same treatment herself, so she knew all too well how painful and humiliating it was.

Yeo Bulgae seized the woman by the hair and flung her backward. Though he was called the King of the Night, when he was angry he was nothing more than a brute. No, it would have been fortunate if he had merely beaten them like a brute. Yeo Bulgae tormented people with relentless persistence.

“Wonderful, simply wonderful. An old bitch like you has to play a part like this if she wants to survive among the rest of you, I suppose?”

Yeo Bulgae injected his internal energy into the woman’s body, intending to inflict pain.

“Aaaaargh!”

Unable to endure it, the woman screamed.

“You have to endure it! You came forward to sacrifice yourself, didn’t you? Were you only pretending to stop me?”

The young woman whom she had tried to protect stepped forward and pleaded.

“I’ll do it! I’ll do it! Please, stop!”

Tears streamed down her face. The women watching them also wept with their heads bowed.

The reason they could not move against Yeo Bulgae was not simply that they had been captured and brought here.

Yeo Bulgae had threatened their families’ lives. Some had children, some siblings, and some parents. Whether they resisted, ran away, or took their own lives, he threatened to find their families and kill them. And he had actually brought in the family of one woman who had escaped and slaughtered them cruelly before her eyes.

What made them even sadder was that they were adapting to this humiliating, bestial reality.

“You’re nothing more than lumps of flesh that exist for me. How dare lumps of flesh like you claim to protect one another?”

The middle-aged woman squeezed her eyes shut. Tears flowed down her cheeks. Every day, she wished she could simply die, but she could not because of her son. If she took her own life, he would certainly bring her son there as an example and kill him before the other women.

‘Please! Someone, save us!’

The woman’s desperate gaze turned toward the vivid blue sky far beyond the window.

‘Please!’

Just as her desperation seemed to reach the ends of the heavens, a subordinate reported from outside.

“The Heukbaek Ssangdo and Cheonhwa Ruju have arrived.”

As though he had been waiting for them, Yeo Bulgae hurriedly opened the door and came outside.

Cheonhwa Ruju was entering the large courtyard. Behind her came the Heukbaek Ssangdo, their bamboo hats pulled low over their faces. Of course, they were Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma.

They had anticipated all sorts of difficulties on the way in. Geom Mugeuk had been prepared to rely on his quick wits, but unexpectedly, everything had been far too easy.

One of the martial artists guarding the entrance had escorted them all the way there, as though this was what he had always done. Since the Heukbaek Ssangdo had brought women there more than once or twice, no one had suspected that these Heukbaek Ssangdo were fakes. Besides, the Heukbaek Ssangdo must have been so terrifying in their usual dealings that no one dared investigate them—or even meet their eyes.

That was how they had come all the way to Yeo Bulgae without trouble.

The moment Yeo Bulgae saw Cheonhwa Ruju, he exclaimed in admiration.

“Oh! Now that is something!”

He liked the atmosphere she gave off. She was elegant yet haughty. Was there any greater pleasure in the world than breaking such haughtiness and making a woman like that his slave?

He went back into the room, seized the middle-aged woman by the hair, and dragged her outside. Then he flung her to the ground without mercy.

“Look at her. A woman with that kind of beauty has come here. She ought to be licking my toes all day and it still wouldn’t be enough, yet you claim that you’ll protect one another?”

Yeo Bulgae had not the slightest intention of making a good impression on Cheonhwa Ruju. Rather, he meant to instill fear in her.

He flung the door wide open. The scene inside was an imperial palace to him, but hell to the women. The women kneeling, the women sprawled in the corners, the women lying on the beds—all of them raised their heads to look at Cheonhwa Ruju.

“They used to run courtesan houses, just like you.”

The moment she saw them, Cheonhwa Ruju’s expression hardened. She was not usually one to show anger, but now her face displayed unmistakable fury.

“You! Stealing their courtesan houses was not enough—you intend to steal their souls as well.”

Yeo Bulgae laughed in delight.

“You even speak so elegantly! I like you. I’ll show you the most love of all!”

The women in the room looked at her with pity.

Cheonhwa Ruju spoke in a calm tone.

“I’m told I’m very good at seeing a person’s future.”

It was one of those moments when a truly furious person became calmer instead.

“Can you see my future as well?”

“Your skin has been flayed off, your body is skewered on an iron spit, and you are being lowered into a pit of flames.”

She was describing him falling into Avīci, the most painful of all the hells.

His expression hardened for an instant, but Yeo Bulgae soon smiled leisurely.

“Even the Yama of Hell wouldn’t accept me. He’d be worried I might dirty the place.”

After delivering the line he always used, he sneered at Cheonhwa Ruju.

“Only the weak prattle about hell. So, does it make you feel good to imagine me falling into the fires of hell?”

“You’re a coward. You pretend to be broad-minded on the outside, but you’re a coward who fears death more than anyone.”

“The bitches over there all acted just like you at first. They put on airs and shouted loudly, without knowing what kind of state I would reduce them to after just an hour, or what pleas they would make to me. You don’t think you could ever end up like them, do you? You think someone will come to save you, don’t you? That’s how people are. They don’t know what lies even an inch ahead of them.”

Just then, a situation unfolded in which no one knew what lay even an inch ahead.

Hwang Yeom appeared there, leading a group of experts. They blocked Yeo Bulgae’s path and stood guard around him as though protecting him.

“Who are you?”

Hwang Yeom asked Cheonhwa Ruju coldly.

Only then did Yeo Bulgae realize that they were fake Heukbaek Ssangdo. He had failed to examine them closely because he had been captivated by Cheonhwa Ruju’s appearance.

“Fakes? You crazy bastards!”

His heart had lurched at the thought that he had nearly been caught off guard, and fury surged to the top of his head. Soon, however, he mastered his emotions.

When Yeo Bulgae snapped his fingers, the women inside the room rushed out, knelt down, and lay flat on the ground with their chests pressed against it.

Yeo Bulgae sat on them as though they were chairs. It was not because he needed chairs, nor was it merely to display his authority. He intended to use them as hostages if the need arose. That was the kind of man Yeo Bulgae was.

“If those men are fakes, then that woman must be a fake as well.”

“Does it matter whether I’m Cheonhwa Ruju or not?”

“It matters. Beautiful women are a dime a dozen in this world. But there can only be one mistress of the place I have claimed.”

“I am the real Cheonhwa Ruju.”

Yeo Bulgae thought she was telling the truth. There could not be many women capable of projecting such an aura.

“That useless Heukbaek Ssangdo must already be dead.”

“My pillar lover has a rather unusual personality.”

She laughed, perhaps because she found her own words amusing. At the sight of her smile, Yeo Bulgae grew flushed with excitement. She was exactly his type. He had seen countless women, but never one who attracted him like this.

Yeo Bulgae’s gaze shifted toward the Heukbaek Ssangdo.

‘So she came here trusting those two.’

They did not look like much at a glance.

“If you’re really Cheonhwa Ruju, why did you come here?”

“I heard you were looking for me.”

“What do you think?”

“Ugly, insignificant, pathetic. I came here for nothing.”

Yeo Bulgae’s expression instantly turned cold.

“I don’t know what you were thinking, coming here and making this kind of fuss, but if you thought the Heukbaek Ssangdo were all of my subordinates, you made a grave mistake.”

Yeo Bulgae felt no anxiety. No— even if he wanted to feel anxious, he could not.

To begin with, the three subordinates standing to his front left were the Heukya Samo, the Three Crows of the Black Night. They were his longtime right-hand men, experts a level above the Heukbaek Ssangdo. Beside them stood another of his trusted men, the spear expert Hyeolmyeongchang, Blood-Crying Spear. These four experts, together with the Heukbaek Ssangdo—these six masters were the reason he had been able to become the King of the Night.

But today there were even more.

After Jisaeng and Aech’a had died, he had brought in experts from outside. The Akgyeong Osa, the Five Evil Shadow Warriors, whom he had hired for a great deal of money this time, were infamous even among the unorthodox factions. He had never seen them fight personally, but their reputations were formidable.

And above all, there were two people he could rely on more than anyone.

A man and a woman sat on a rock in front of the garden. Both wore bizarre makeup, making it difficult to tell which was the man and which was the woman.

They were the Eumyanggwi.

They were devils who used strange sorcery to drain their opponents’ internal energy. Naturally, the male Yanggwi had mastered a demonic art that absorbed a woman’s yin energy, while the female Eumgwi had mastered one that absorbed a man’s yang energy.

They enjoyed fighting almost as much as they enjoyed absorbing vital essence. Their internal energy was so profound that most experts were thoroughly toyed with by them before dying after having their essence completely drained.

Their personalities were cruel and savage as well. If anyone displeased them, they would slaughter that person’s family and companions as well, making them truly mad men and women. They caused so many problems that even the Sado Alliance disliked employing them. But in a situation like this, there was no one more reliable.

So whom could he possibly fear? Yeo Bulgae was completely at ease.

“So you were angry that I tried to bring you here, and came in person to take revenge on me?”

Yeo Bulgae became even more sexually excited. Hwang Yeom, meanwhile, felt an inexplicable sense of unease, but after once nearly dying by interrupting a man who was engrossed in a woman, he never interfered again. Whatever the matter, women were one domain that absolutely must not be touched.

Instead of answering, Cheonhwa Ruju spoke to the women lying face-down on the ground.

“Everyone, close your eyes. When you open them again, you will be able to return home.”

The women’s eyes trembled at her words.

As they looked into Cheonhwa Ruju’s calm and gentle eyes, it seemed as though her words might truly come to pass. They all closed their eyes and prayed to the heavens. Please, let us return home.

At the sight, the old lecher’s lust grew even more intense.

“What an alluring old bitch you are.”

Yeo Bulgae spoke to the Akgyeong Osa.

“Five heroes, kill those men and prove your worth.”

In other words, he was telling them to earn what he had paid them.

As the Akgyeong Osa stepped forward, Geom Mugeuk also advanced. While Cheonhwa Ruju had bought them time by speaking with Yeo Bulgae, Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma had already formulated a plan through telepathy: how to fight these men, how to protect Cheonhwa Ruju, and how to rescue the women.

Yeo Bulgae asked Geom Mugeuk,

“If he’s the pillar lover, what are you?”

Geom Mugeuk answered,

“I’m the pillar lover’s friend.”

The gathered martial artists burst out laughing. Not one of them was tense.

“Even if you love your friend, is it wise to follow him all the way to hell?”

“I like that pillar too. He’s a pillar in my life as well.”

Though they could not be seen beneath the bamboo hat, Geukak Soma’s eyes shone with a clear light.

Geom Mugeuk’s composure made Yeo Bulgae feel a vague sense of foreboding. Thus, he issued his command with even greater force.

“Break all those damned pillars!”

The instant Yeo Bulgae’s order fell, the Akgyeong Osa drew their swords in unison.

Geom Mugeuk walked toward them slowly, as though out for a stroll.

He lightly evaded the attack of the Third Warrior, who came flying at him first, and drove his fist into the man’s abdomen.

With a dull thud, the Third Warrior crumpled. Geom Mugeuk twisted his body and slammed his fist into the face of the Second Warrior, who was stabbing at him from the side.

Lightly evading, lightly tapping.

It was a fight that made those watching wonder, What am I looking at?

The Fourth Warrior attacked from behind, leaping into the air because the fallen Third Warrior was in his way. But that movement had already been included in Geom Mugeuk’s calculations.

Geom Mugeuk leaned his back backward and lightly stabbed a dagger into the Fourth Warrior’s heart.

Stabbed in midair, the Fourth Warrior flew forward with his momentum. Using the Fourth Warrior as a shield, the First Warrior abandoned his attack and twisted aside to evade.

But Geom Mugeuk had anticipated that direction of retreat. His fist struck the First Warrior squarely in the chest. As the First Warrior crumpled and Geom Mugeuk turned around, the Fifth Warrior collapsed, clutching his throat. A dagger that no one had seen fly had lodged itself in his neck.

Geom Mugeuk had faced them by combining the Fist Demon’s boxing techniques, the Wind-God Four Steps, and throwing-dagger arts. His martial intent had reached such an exquisite level that it was difficult to recognize what he was doing.

There were no complicated movements, nor any dazzling footwork. He was simply fast and precise. His movements were utterly free of wasted force.

That was why, to everyone’s eyes, it looked as though the men were deliberately acting out their falls. They seemed to have been struck lightly by a fist, yet they could not get back up.

Yeo Bulgae was confused. The Akgyeong Osa looked like complete amateurs, while their opponent seemed like an unparalleled master. He had never seen a fight like this before.

The ones who had grasped their opponent most accurately were, surprisingly, the Eumyanggwi.

They looked at each other and smiled. They had recognized at a glance that Geom Mugeuk was a formidable opponent, and they were delighted. The stronger the opponent, the more internal energy he would possess. The Eumgwi was impatient to taste it as soon as possible.

It was then.

Boom! Rumble!

Thunder rang out from the sky. Dark clouds, seeming ready to pour down rain at any moment, cast the surroundings into darkness.

Geukak Soma slowly walked forward and stood beside Geom Mugeuk. In a flash of lightning, his white mask shone brightly beneath his bamboo hat. Geom Mugeuk knew how furious Geukak Soma was at Yeo Bulgae.

“Filthy old lecher.”

It was the first time Geukak Soma had spoken since arriving there. He did not want to exchange even a single word with Yeo Bulgae, and what followed was his final statement—one that needed nothing after it.

“You keep talking about hell every time you open your mouth. I’ll send you to the real thing.”

Chapter 228: What Happens If You Touch My Woman.
------------------------------------------------
The instant Geugak Soma finished speaking, three crows took flight. The Black Night Three Crows (黑夜三烏) had launched their attack.

The killing intent in his words had been unmistakable, so they did not hesitate. If their opponent had made up his mind to kill Yeo Bulgae, then there was no way for them to survive without killing him first.

The Black Night Three Crows rushed in from three directions.

From the left, the shoulder; from the right, the ribs; from the front, the throat.

Until now, no one had ever withstood this combined assault. These night crows were indeed masters skilled enough to raise Yeo Bulgae to the throne of the night. Save for the fact that their opponent was Geugak Soma, it was a perfect attack.

With a single step, Geugak Soma evaded the pincer attack. Three swords grazed past his body by a hair’s breadth, but the eyes behind the mask remained calm. Serene and precise—ping!

The Blood Calamity Finger that flew in from right before his nose was something First Crow could not dodge.

As First Crow’s throat was pierced through and he died, Second Crow let out a monstrous cry and swung his sword.

The opening created by rage was more than enough for a strand of finger wind to pierce through.

Thud!

The sword of the last remaining Third Crow unleashed the finest move of his life, but all it managed to do was cut off the end of his opponent’s bamboo hat.

Had he known who his opponent was, even that would have been something he could boast of for the rest of his life. But after a lifetime of committing evil deeds under Yeo Bulgae, he was not given even a single chance to boast. A hole was pierced through his forehead as well, and he breathed his last.

It was just then.

Screeeeeech!

Something came flying with tremendous force, aimed at Geugak Soma’s back. It was the spear of the Blood-Crying Spear.

Screech.

The spear, which had flown in at a terrifying speed, stopped behind Geugak Soma’s back.

The one who had caught the spear by hand was Geom Mugeuk.

The Blood-Crying Spear was shocked. His spear was not something that could be caught barehanded. If it could be seized with bare hands, how could he have become a renowned spear master of the Evil Faction?

Geom Mugeuk planted the spear in the ground.

Geugak Soma turned around and lightly flicked the shaft embedded in the earth with his finger.

Clang!

The clear sound that rang out was Geugak Soma’s way of expressing his thanks.

Taking that sound as his starting signal, Geom Mugeuk charged toward the Blood-Crying Spear.

The Blood-Crying Spear swiftly joined together the two short spears he wore at either side of his waist. At this distance, there was enough time to connect the spear—had Geom Mugeuk not rushed in with the Myeongwang Steps.

Slice!

The newly joined spear was cut in two along with its owner’s body. In Geom Mugeuk’s hand was not the Black Demon Sword, but the blade used by the Black-White Twin Blades; even so, Geom Mugeuk was a master calligrapher who did not care what brush he held.

At that very moment.

Sssssss!

Vines surged up from the ground and wrapped around Geom Mugeuk’s entire body. It was a sorcerous art that bound an opponent’s body for a brief instant.

The Yin-Yang Ghosts, who had been sitting in front of the flower garden, were suddenly at Geom Mugeuk’s left and right, snatching his wrists.

Rumble-rumble-rumble!

Through both of Geom Mugeuk’s wrists, the internal energy of the Yin-Yang Ghosts poured in like a waterfall.

Their martial art was the Heart-Shattering Star-Absorbing Art (破心吸星功).

It was an utterly vicious evil art that injected their mighty internal energy into an opponent to burst their heart and kill them, then drew back all of the opponent’s internal energy and vital essence.

Even alone, either of them could easily kill a fairly skilled master with it. Now that both had latched onto him, they were certain their opponent would never be able to endure.

But that was a misconception born of not knowing what kind of person Geom Mugeuk was when it came to internal energy.

Their internal energy continued to pour in.

By now, he should have been vomiting blood from internal injuries, yet Geom Mugeuk was perfectly fine.

Yang Ghost sent Yin Ghost a grave look. It was a signal that they should stop even now, but Yin Ghost firmly shook her head and refused. She knew from experience that the stronger the opponent was, the more delicious their internal energy and yang essence would be.

Just a little more, just a little more!

It seemed as though Geom Mugeuk would cough blood and collapse at any moment.

More than half their internal energy had been poured in, then more than two-thirds. The more time passed, the more they could not give up. If only because of all the internal energy they had already spent, they could not stop.

Yang Ghost attempted the Strange Shadow Art (怪影術).

While absorbing internal energy, one must never use another martial art, but the situation left him no choice.

Geom Mugeuk’s shadow rose to its feet. Normally, the shadow should have burrowed into Geom Mugeuk’s mind and inflicted internal injuries.

Sssssss!

The shadow merged with Geom Mugeuk, then quietly returned to its original place.

‘Sorcery doesn’t work on him!’

Yang Ghost vomited a bowlful of blood.

Only when the two of them had reached a point of no return did Geom Mugeuk reveal his true ability. Far from dying, he spoke to them with ease.

“What should I do? Sorcery and internal energy are the fields I’m most confident in.”

The Yin-Yang Ghosts realized they had been played by Geom Mugeuk. From the beginning, he had deliberately given them his wrists. Now, even if they wanted to stop, they could not. Their internal power was being sucked away endlessly, as if it had fallen into an abyss.

And so, with their internal energy completely depleted and their blood vessels utterly dried up, the Yin-Yang Ghosts collapsed with a thud. They had lost their lives pouring water into a bottomless jar.

The internal energy of the two merged into Geom Mugeuk’s dantian. Born with the Heavenly Martial Body to begin with, and now having reached a new realm, Geom Mugeuk’s internal energy made theirs his own far faster and more purely than before. Just as no matter how many rivers flow into the sea, the sea remains a vast sea.

Yeo Bulgae, who had been staring blankly as even his last hope, the Yin-Yang Ghosts, died, looked around the scene in a daze. Every master except himself and Hwang Yeom was dead.

‘If only this were a dream.’

Amazingly, he wished all of this were a dream. He had lived his life with Yaryulhan at his back, fulfilling every base desire he possessed.

‘I can’t give up!’

Yeo Bulgae gritted his teeth.

—Take that woman hostage!

At Yeo Bulgae’s transmitted voice, Hwang Yeom did not move.

—What are you doing? Hurry up and take her hostage!

But Hwang Yeom’s mute acupoint and paralysis acupoint had already been sealed. Just when, in the midst of this chaotic melee, had he been subdued?

Originally, he had intended to have Hwang Yeom take the Mistress of Cheonhwaru hostage while he took the women here hostage.

But if matters had come to this—

Yeo Bulgae hurled himself toward the Mistress of Cheonhwaru.

‘If I can just take that woman hostage, I can live.’

Just as he was about to subdue the Mistress of Cheonhwaru, Geugak Soma stepped in front of him a moment faster.

Yeo Bulgae unleashed palm force with all his strength. But how could an old man who had spent his life chasing women possibly contend with Geugak Soma, whom even one who had trained all his life would find difficult to face?

Geugak Soma lightly dissolved the incoming palm force with palm force of his own.

Only then did Yeo Bulgae see his face beneath the bamboo hat. As he stared blankly at the white mask, he shuddered in shock.

“You are—!”

Before the next words could come out, the Blood Calamity Finger was released.

Ping! Thud!

A strand of finger wind, like a beam of light that had been held back since his arrival, pierced through his mouth. Yeo Bulgae let out a short scream and clutched his mouth. Blood streamed down between his fingers.

Then the Mistress of Cheonhwaru spoke to the women.

“Now, everyone, open your eyes and look.”

The women opened their eyes. They were startled by the sight spread out before them. As they listened to the sounds of battle, they had desperately hoped for a situation like this, but they had never imagined that Yeo Bulgae’s powerful subordinates would truly be lying around as corpses.

Amid the bodies strewn everywhere, holes began to multiply in Yeo Bulgae’s body.

Ping! Thud!

His shoulder was pierced. The back of the hand reaching to clutch that shoulder was pierced.

Realizing that his opponent was Geugak Soma of the Demon Cult, Yeo Bulgae lost all will to fight and floundered. He begged to be spared, but because his mouth had been pierced by the first finger wind, the words did not come out properly.

He crawled across the ground and begged for his life.

Geom Mugeuk knew. He knew what that desperate plea would become later. He knew how much mercy and forgiveness granted in a single moment could cause so many people to die.

Fortunately, his opponent today was Geugak Soma.

Crack!

Geugak Soma mercilessly kicked him in the crotch. The desire that had led him into evil all his life was shattered, and he let out a wretched scream.

“Aaaaaaargh!”

Bloody froth poured from his screaming mouth.

‘I’ll survive somehow and take revenge no matter what!’

Yeo Bulgae crawled toward Geugak Soma again.

‘You dog! You damned bastard! I’ll repay this pain a hundredfold, a thousandfold!’

Covered in blood, Yeo Bulgae forced a smile.

“……I’ll live virtuously.”

His pronunciation was unclear, but it was obvious that he was talking about turning over a new leaf.

Of course, it did not work.

This time, the joints of his arms and legs were pierced. Geugak Soma chose only the spots where he could feel the greatest pain and shot finger wind into them. He did not even offer a single word of advice, such as telling him to live kindly in his next life. Geugak Soma thoroughly ignored him.

Lastly, he pulled out the spear planted in the ground and drove it down into the stomach of Yeo Bulgae, who was writhing on the floor.

The future the Mistress of Cheonhwaru had spoken of was unfolding not in hell, but right here.

Geugak Soma stared coldly at the dying man, then extended his palm toward him. The pit of fire the Mistress of Cheonhwaru had spoken of was none other than Geugak Soma’s Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm.

Kwahhhhhng!

His entire body was blown away.

Thud.

He vanished into a bloody pulp, and only the broken spear rolled across the ground. This was Geugak Soma’s answer to what happened when someone touched his woman.

At the sight, some women cried out in cheers, some shed tears of joy, and still others could not bear to look upon the gruesome scene and turned their heads.

Their reactions were all different, but the emotion they felt was one. All that came to mind was the joy that they had now escaped this hell….

Rumble, crash!

As though it had been watching this moment, rain began to pour from the sky.

Shwaaaaaaaaa!

All the women looked up at the heavens. It felt as if all the suffering they had endured until now was being washed away by the rain.

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru walked over to them.

“It’s time to go home now.”

Of course, until this matter was fully concluded, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru intended to keep them with her. Geom Mugeuk could tell. More than anyone else, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru was the person who would help them now. She would soothe their wounds better than anyone.

The women bowed to the three of them.

“We will never forget the grace of being saved by you.”

At the thought that they could see their families again, tears would not stop flowing from their eyes. How much must their families be missing them? How desperately must they be searching?

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru helped them to their feet.

The middle-aged woman who had earlier tried to save the young woman asked with a tear-streaked face.

“Please, at least tell us the names of our benefactors.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and said,

“As you heard earlier, this lady here is the owner of a courtesan house, that man is her kept husband, and I am the kept husband’s friend.”

Realizing that he did not intend to reveal their identities, the woman did not ask further.

Instead, she expressed what was in their hearts.

“We will live the rest of our lives never forgetting this kindness.”

Now it was time to leave this place.

Geom Mugeuk approached Hwang Yeom, who had survived alone with his paralysis and mute acupoints sealed.

Undoing Hwang Yeom’s mute acupoint, he asked,

“Do you want to live?”

“Yes!”

Hwang Yeom nodded frantically.

He was a subordinate suited to his master. With his master dead, what loyalty could there be? Who in the world could dispose of Yeo Bulgae and his subordinates this easily?

Using sword aura, Geom Mugeuk quickly dug into the earth, then used the Void Grasping Art to throw the corpses into the pit. In an instant, he filled it back in.

The pouring rain washed away all the blood.

Meanwhile, the women cleaned up the scene, and Geugak Soma brought over the large carriage that had been standing in the backyard.

Geom Mugeuk said to Hwang Yeom,

“When we leave, tell the subordinates that Yeo Bulgae has somewhere to go for a while. If you want to try anything funny, go ahead. I’ll throw you into a torture chamber until the day you die. I’ll keep you alive like my own life and drag you around for the rest of your days.”

“That won’t happen.”

The women boarded the carriage. Hwang Yeom took the reins himself, and Geom Mugeuk sat beside him.

Thus, the carriage raced out of Yeo Bulgae’s residence. Hwang Yeom acted even better than instructed, forbidding entry into the inner courtyard. At this rate, news of Yeo Bulgae’s death would not spread before they reached Yaryulhan.

About ten li from that place, the Poison King and Seo Daeryong were waiting.

Seo Daeryong took the reins, and now the Mistress of Cheonhwaru and the women were entrusted to the Poison King.

“Please spare me as promised!”

“I never promised. I only asked you. Whether you wanted to live.”

Just as Hwang Yeom was about to protest—

Thud!

Geom Mugeuk’s fist struck his chest. The reward for helping them was a painless death. In truth, this bastard who had served as Yeo Bulgae’s minion deserved to die painfully as well, but there was no time for that now.

Geom Mugeuk dug deep into the nearby forest and disposed of the corpse.

In the meantime, the Poison King and Seo Daeryong had finished preparing to depart.

“Then I leave them in your care.”

When Geom Mugeuk entrusted the women to the Poison King, the Poison King said something unexpected.

“When you return, let’s make that poison we failed to create last time again.”

It was his way of telling him to come visit the Thousand Poisons Forest again. In other words, he was telling him to handle this matter safely and return.

“If you allow me to stay far away by the door again this time.”

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru, too, did not send off Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma too gravely.

“Please take good care of my dear husband.”

“I always tell you this, but you should be asking Lord Soma to take care of me.”

The person most worried was Seo Daeryong. Handing over the Black Demon Sword he had been keeping, he said it over and over.

“I know your skills very well, Pavilion Master, but please be careful anyway. Be careful, and then be careful again. If things go wrong, run! Don’t go acting tough for nothing!”

“If you’re that worried, come with me.”

At that, Seo Daeryong flinched and took a step back.

“Regrettably, there’s no one else to drive the carriage.”

Beside him, the Poison King casually remarked,

“I can drive the carriage. Shall I try driving one for the first time in a while?”

Pretending not to hear, Seo Daeryong said to Geom Mugeuk,

“Then I’ll be waiting. As always.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and patted his shoulder once. Keep growing, Seo Daeryong. Someday, the day will come when I fight alongside you.

With their farewells done, Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma began running toward the place where Gowol was waiting. Now they had to face Yaryulhan, but Geom Mugeuk was not afraid. Because the one guarding his back was Geugak Soma.

Shwaaaaaaaaa!

Cutting through the pouring rain, the two of them ran without stopping.

Chapter 229: Have You Come to Collect a Life Debt?
--------------------------------------------------
A man stepped down from the carriage.

He had a sleek, well-proportioned build and a cold impression.

As if the phrase “not a single drop of blood would come out if you stabbed him” existed for him alone, his expressionless face suited him perfectly. He was chilling, yet immaculate.

The man who had stepped down from the carriage entered the wide-open manor. At the entrance hung a signboard reading Great Night Gang.

As soon as he went inside, a horrific scene unfolded before him.

More than a hundred corpses lay scattered everywhere. The members of the Great Night Gang had been massacred. Among the bodies, warriors uniformly dressed in red martial robes were searching for survivors and killing them.

It was a gruesome sight reminiscent of a battlefield, but the man walked on without so much as blinking.

Blood flowing across the ground splashed up, soaking his shoes and trousers, but he paid it no mind.

If anything, the sight of him walking through a sea of blood suited him all too well. His very stride revealed his presence. It was a terrifying presence, as though no one could stand in his path unless he himself chose to stop.

The man was Yaryul Han, the second-in-command of the Sado Alliance.

The warriors in red martial robes who had massacred this place were none other than the warriors of the Extreme Blade Corps.

The Extreme Blade Corps. The Sado Alliance’s most elite organization, led by Yaryul Han.

Whenever Yaryul Han passed by, the warriors of the Extreme Blade Corps bowed their heads respectfully. The aura emanating from each and every one of them was far from ordinary.

Yaryul Han entered the main hall.

In the large chair at the center sat Jong Sim, leader of the Great Night Gang, leaning crookedly against it. Gravely wounded, he was covered in blood, and around him lay the corpses of Extreme Blade Corps warriors as well.

“You bastard!”

The leader of the Great Night Gang roared. But his insides churned, and he vomited blood in a gush.

“How could you! How could you do something like this!”

In contrast to the agitated gang leader, Yaryul Han was utterly calm.

He walked somewhere. In a corner, there was still a warrior of the Extreme Blade Corps clinging to life. He was breathing in pain. His wounds were too severe; he was already beyond saving.

Without hesitation, Yaryul Han placed his palm against the man’s chest.

Soon, the warrior breathed his last as if falling asleep. From behind him came the voice of the Great Night Gang leader.

“I’ll say it now, but whenever I saw your face, it always gave me the creeps and put me in a foul mood.”

Yaryul Han showed no stirring of emotion whatsoever.

“You always hid your feelings.”

The Great Night Gang leader had never once sensed any emotion from Yaryul Han. He had never seen him rejoice, never seen him grieve, never seen him become angry.

“Are there even human emotions inside that body of yours?”

At that, Yaryul Han rose and answered calmly.

“Must there be?”

His deep yet pleasant voice rang through the main hall.

“What?”

“Those human emotions you speak of. I asked if they must necessarily exist.”

Faced with such a brazen question, the Great Night Gang leader did not know what to say.

“To hear such words from you is rather bewildering. You are not a man who has lived all that humanely either.”

“Shut your mouth!”

The old man roared, then vomited blood again. Dark red blood soaked his clothes.

“Even so, I never lived a life where I framed an entire sect and slaughtered them to the last.”

Today, the Great Night Gang had been exterminated for the crime of colluding with the Murim Alliance and plotting rebellion. The evidence had been clear. A letter had been found that the Great Night Gang leader himself had never written, yet the handwriting was identical to his. A huge sum of money he knew nothing about, received from the Murim Alliance, had been stored at a bank. There were traces of the Sado Alliance’s secrets having been leaked, testimony had emerged, and there was even a witness who had seen him in secret talks with people from the Murim Alliance. Even in his own eyes, he was clearly a traitor.

“At least tell me the reason. Why?”

The Great Night Gang leader was certain that the one who had trapped him was Yaryul Han.

“You have forgotten.”

At Yaryul Han’s words, the Great Night Gang leader flinched. It sounded as though he had made some grave mistake.

“Forgotten what?”

“In the past, you said this to me. That if a person is too much like that, it could put the other party in a bad mood.”

The Great Night Gang leader had no memory of saying such a thing. Had I said that? It was hazy.

“You could not hold back what you wanted to say. Living in murim, yet unable to control your emotions? I despise such people.”

“So?”

Yaryul Han said no more.

In that instant, the Great Night Gang leader was struck with shock.

“Are you saying that is the entire reason?”

“To kill someone I despise—do you need a reason more certain than that?”

The Great Night Gang leader roared.

“You mad bastard!”

Compared to the agitated gang leader, Yaryul Han remained calm.

“There are as many people in this world as grains of sand. Is there any need to go on together with someone one dislikes?”

Yes, the Great Night Gang leader could understand up to that point. It made no sense, but let him say he understood. But this part, at least, he simply could not comprehend.

“Then you should have killed only me!”

“My mood was far too foul for that.”

“No matter how foul it was… you killed even innocent people for something so trivial?”

The Great Night Gang leader let out a pained sigh.

Tears flowed from his eyes. If he had suffered this for some grand grudge, he would have felt less wronged. He would have felt less sorry to his family, to the warriors of his gang. His clan was being exterminated over a single remark he could not even remember?

The Great Night Gang leader spoke in a self-mocking tone.

“I suppose this is all fate.”

“You may want to blame fate, but this is your fault. The fault of an insensitive man like you, who attaches the word ‘trivial’ to another person’s feelings.”

The Great Night Gang leader beckoned him closer.

“There is something I must say to you before I die.”

Had he sensed death? The anger vanished from the Great Night Gang leader’s face.

Yaryul Han approached him.

A little closer, a little closer. The Great Night Gang leader gestured with difficulty.

Yaryul Han obediently granted his final request.

The distance between the two men narrowed.

Swish!

The Great Night Gang leader drew upon every last bit of his remaining internal energy and thrust out a palm strike. The labored gestures from moments before had been an act for the sake of this one move.

Flash! Slice!

But Yaryul Han’s sword was faster. His sword was so swift and precise it could not even be seen.

Thud, roll.

The severed head of the Great Night Gang leader rolled across the floor. Looking down at the head, Yaryul Han said coldly,

“You old fool, did you really think I would do all this for such a reason?”

After leaving behind words that would keep the Great Night Gang leader from closing his eyes even in death, Yaryul Han went outside.

When he emerged, rain had just begun to fall.

Swaaaaaa.

Yaryul Han stood beneath the eaves for a moment and looked up at the pouring rain.

A man ran through the rain toward him and offered him a wind cloak. It was Cha Hwan, whose entire body was covered in dragon tattoos. The last man alive among the Four.

Though his whole body was covered in dragon tattoos, the animal he brought to mind was a black panther. Dark skin, a lithe physique, and the whites of his eyes standing out all the more because of his black skin.

Yet he was a man who wished to become a dragon rather than a panther. Among the Four, he prided himself on having the greatest loyalty. He also believed that the reason Yaryul Han kept only him by his side was because he recognized that loyalty.

“No need. It has been a while since I last stood in the rain.”

Yaryul Han walked into the rain, and Cha Hwan followed silently behind him. Behind him again, the warriors of the Extreme Blade Corps lined up and walked after them.

* * *

I arrived at the safe house with Geugak Soma.

Gowol was waiting for us with new information.

“Something unusual is happening inside the Sado Alliance.”

“What kind of thing?”

“The Great Night Gang was recently annihilated.”

“By whom?”

“The Great Night Gang committed the crime of rebellion by colluding with the Murim Alliance. The evidence was clear, and during the process of arresting them, they were wiped out by the Extreme Blade Corps led by Yaryul Han. I believe this was Yaryul Han’s doing.”

Gowol had grasped the situation accurately.

Because even before my regression, the Great Night Gang had been exterminated by Yaryul Han. However, the timing had changed. Things that were meant to happen still happened, but the timing or cause could shift subtly.

“Why do you think so?”

“First of all, the Great Night Gang leader I know is not someone who would ever betray the leader of the Sado Alliance.”

And there was another decisive reason for Gowol’s suspicion.

“The timing is too coincidental. Do you know when the Sado Alliance received the tip about this matter and began investigating the Great Night Gang?”

“When?”

“When Aecha died.”

It truly was an uncanny timing.

Gowol judged it this way.

“When two of his limbs among the Four died, Yaryul Han seems to have become convinced that someone intervened.”

“Surely not?”

“Yes. He seems to think the Sado Alliance leader is behind it.”

It was something I truly had not expected.

“And as revenge, he struck the Great Night Gang?”

“The Great Night Gang leader was famous as a supporter of the alliance leader. Yaryul Han was warning them what happens if they touch him.”

It was a sufficiently plausible guess.

It was an open secret that Yaryul Han coveted the position of alliance leader. At present, the Sado Alliance leader was too powerful for him to dare reach for that seat, but if Bisain took that position, the situation would change.

There were even rumors circulating in the world that if the Sado Alliance leader wanted to pass the position to Bisain, he would have to kill Yaryul Han.

In a situation where a tense power relationship of mutual restraint was being maintained.

“Yaryul Han seems to believe that the Sado Alliance leader has begun cutting off his hands and feet in order to make Bisain his successor.”

Something that would have happened someday had occurred early because of our movements.

“This is a perfect opportunity. If we can make good use of this situation, we won’t have to worry about what comes after killing Yaryul Han.”

A truly unexpected situation was waiting for us.

“You mean we kill Yaryul Han and make Bisain the person who killed him?”

“That’s right. It isn’t bad for Bisain either. He will firmly establish himself as the successor, and we will succeed in this operation.”

I looked at Geugak Soma, who had been listening beside us. Geugak Soma, who had only listened in silence, had eyes that said he would follow my will no matter how the situation unfolded.

If so, what had to be done was set.

“First, I suppose I should meet my friend. Though I don’t know if he still considers me one.”

* * *

Bisain was eating at a high-class inn he often frequented.

His face was still hideous. Cut, crushed, distorted. An appearance that sent a chill through one’s heart just by looking at it. But compared to the dark and heavy air he had before becoming successor, he seemed somewhat brighter.

Around him, the Sado Thirteen Wolves were eating with him while standing guard.

A thread of sound transmission reached Bisain.

—Have you been well?

Bisain’s chopsticks paused for a moment, then soon began moving again.

—You truly have no fear. To think you would come all the way to the main headquarters of this alliance.

The content of his words was blunt, but his tone was gentle. He owed me his life. If not for me, he would never have become the successor either.

—There is something I must discuss with you quietly.

—Do it here. Do you even know how difficult it is for the successor of the Sado Alliance to go out alone?

—Later, you will live a life where everything is done once you merely say the word. So for now, do a few difficult things. Let us meet at midnight on the peak of the mountain behind the Sado Alliance.

Before leaving the inn, I saw it. A faint smile had formed at the corners of Bisain’s mouth. I did not know if it was the smile of a friend, but at least it was not the smile of an enemy.

* * *

I stood on the summit of the mountain overlooking the main headquarters of the Sado Alliance.

Before my regression, in the course of searching for the materials for the Great Art, I had once stood here and looked down at that Sado Alliance. I had stood here thinking that once I regressed, everything would be resolved.

Then I heard Bisain’s voice from behind me.

“What important matter do you have that you would call someone out at this hour? You could have done it by sound transmission at the inn.”

Without turning around, I said,

“I was afraid you might overturn the dining table.”

“Everyone knows I have a filthy temper, so it would have been fine.”

Bisain stood beside me.

“What if I shoved you from behind? You don’t even turn around?”

“Haven’t we become close enough that I need not worry about such things?”

When I looked at him, Bisain snorted.

“That’s not it. You’re acting relaxed because even if you fell, you could land without so much as harming a hair.”

“That is also true.”

When I looked at him with a smile, Bisain turned his gaze away and looked at the Sado Alliance buildings in the distance.

“Have you come to collect the debt of my life?”

“Surely it is not only the debt of your life. I must collect the debt of making you successor as well.”

“What do you want?”

“Will you grant me anything?”

“If it is something I can grant.”

I looked at him. Bisain looked at me as well.

“Let us kill Yaryul Han.”

At my sudden words, he was startled.

“I knew long ago that you were a strange man, but I did not know you were such a desperately insane bastard.”

A brief silence flowed between us, and then he asked,

“Are you saying you intend to go to war with us?”

“If that were so, I would not be having this conversation with you. I came to kill Yaryul Han.”

“Why do you intend to kill him?”

“The grudges he has raised by watering them with blood have gone round and round and come to me.”

Because Bisain knew well what kind of person Yaryul Han was, he did not ask further. How could there be only one or two people who had become his enemies?

“When you ascend to the seat of alliance leader, Yaryul Han will certainly aim for your position. That is why the current Sado Alliance leader must be troubled. Over when to eliminate Yaryul Han. And if he is not thinking such a thing?”

“If he is not?”

“Then the Sado Alliance leader has made Yaryul Han his successor. It would mean he is handing over the seat of alliance leader while knowing you will die.”

“!”

He said nothing. His mind must have been complicated. Yaryul Han was an enemy he absolutely had to kill in order to become alliance leader, but at the same time, he was the greatest force supporting the Sado Alliance.

“His attack on the Great Night Gang was nothing more than a preliminary battle before attacking you.”

Bisain could not deny those words. The death of the Great Night Gang leader must have been a tremendous shock to the Sado Alliance leader and to him as well.

“Do you remember? That one day, when I become the Heavenly Demon and you become the Sado Alliance leader, we would meet again, reminisce about this time, and shake hands.”

I gazed into Bisain’s eyes, which were caught in conflict. I knew very well that this was not something he could decide easily. But my success or failure also depended on this matter.

“I want to shake hands with you, not Yaryul Han.”

Chapter 230: What Was Your First Decision?
------------------------------------------
A breeze blew, heralding the approach of a new destiny.

Countless thoughts flashed through Bi Sain’s mind.

The first thought that came to him was doubt.

What if all of this was a scheme by the Demon Cult to devour them?

But he soon dismissed the thought.

Perhaps someone else might, but Geom Mugeuk, as he knew him, was not the sort to employ such shabby tricks.

“You need not intervene directly. You need only do one thing for me.”

“What would you have me do?”

“If I eliminate Yayul Han, handle it so that it appears you were the one who eliminated him.”

Bi Sain was startled. He had not imagined that the favor would be something like this.

“Why ask such a thing of me?”

“Naturally, because I wish to prevent a war from breaking out between our cult and the Gwimaeng.”

A war between the Demon Cult and the Sado Maeng.

Bi Sain had imagined a war with the Murim Alliance, but he had never once contemplated a war with the Demon Cult. If their forces were compared, the Sado Maeng would be destroyed. Of course, the Demon Cult would suffer enormous damage as well, and the Murim Alliance would never let that opportunity slip by.

*Hand Yayul Han over to the Demon Cult to be killed?*

The frustration of leaving his nemesis to another’s hands rather than executing him himself outweighed any pleasure in borrowing a knife to kill. His pride was wounded. Even if the man were to die, should he not be the one to kill him?

Hiding such feelings, Bi Sain expressed difficulty on different grounds.

“The Alliance Lord will not believe that I killed him.”

“If it is you, you can make him believe it. At the very least, you will be able to smooth this matter over well.”

It was not an empty remark. Geom Mugeuk believed in Bi Sain’s ability and potential.

After a brief pause, Bi Sain asked.

“Can you eliminate Yayul Han?”

“That is something I will see to myself.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward the Sado Maeng. Torches blazing without a gap guarded the night of the Sado Maeng.

“Whether it is you or me, taking that place will truly not be easy. Those who already occupy it despise change and do not trust us. It cannot be taken without effort. If taken easily, it will be stolen away just as easily.”

After a moment of consideration, Bi Sain spoke resolutely.

“I refuse. Even if he is to be eliminated, I will be the one to do it.”

Bi Sain turned and walked away. Geom Mugeuk spoke from behind him.

“If you change your mind, come here at midnight. I will come every day.”

Bi Sain disappeared into the darkness without a word.

* * *

“What of the Alliance Lord’s reaction?”

Cha Hwan answered Yayul Han’s question.

“He is quiet.”

Yayul Han kept no strategists. He judged and decided everything himself. When someone once asked him why he kept no strategists, he had answered thus:

That he had done well enough without them, so he saw no need. It was an arrogant remark, yet as those words spread, the number of those following him within the Sado Maeng grew ever greater.

“Income has suffered greatly with the deaths of Ji Saeng and Ae Cha. The Sinseonchae that Ji Saeng handled can be taken over by another, but the problem is the Gwangpok that Ae Cha and Jindok Geosa manufactured and sold. Production of Gwangpok has halted with Jindok Geosa’s death. Why did you not extract the Gwangpok formula from Jindok Geosa in advance?”

Cha Hwan could not understand. Perhaps if it were Ae Cha, but Yayul Han would certainly have obtained it long ago. Jindok Geosa had been utterly terrified of Yayul Han.

“If I had done so, what would you have thought?”

“I would have thought you were thorough and meticulous.”

Yayul Han fixed a cold gaze upon Cha Hwan. Cha Hwan intuitively sensed that his answer was wrong.

“A leader who seizes what his subordinates possess can never become the greatest leader.”

Cha Hwan put on an expression of deep emotion. His face revealed how moved he was and how much he revered Yayul Han, but Yayul Han regarded it as habitual sentiment.

Being easily moved. Being easily respectful. It was an attitude Yayul Han did not particularly like.

However, because there was no deception in Cha Hwan’s habitual sentiment, he kept him by his side. There was a definite difference in temperature between how the two men viewed each other.

“We must not lose sight of the big picture for small gains! We have a long road ahead!”

“Yes!”

At that moment, a subordinate reported from outside.

“Young Master Bi has arrived.”

A faint smile formed at Yayul Han’s lips. For some reason, he had felt that Bi would come, and his expectation proved correct.

“Show him in!”

He gestured with his eyes for Cha Hwan to withdraw.

“This lowly one shall take his leave.”

Cha Hwan exited, and shortly after, Bi Sain stepped inside.

“Welcome, Young Master Bi.”

“Danju, have you been well?”

Their gazes tangled in the air amidst taut tension. The two were acknowledged nemeses by all. Everyone believed that one of them would die at the other’s hands. They merely did not know when that time would come.

“I hope I am not taking up the time of a busy man.”

“Busy? Our Young Master Bi is the busier one. Come, have a seat.”

The two sat facing each other at a table. A servant brought tea, but Bi Sain did not even touch his cup.

“I heard about the Daeya Bang.”

“The resistance was too fierce; it could not be helped.”

“Was it not because you pressed them too hard?”

Reproach was laced within the soft question.

“The Geukdo Legion may be somewhat rough, but they are not people who take pleasure in needless slaughter.”

Yayul Han had placed the Geukdo Legion at the forefront. From this point, any misspoken word would become an insult to the Geukdo Legion; with these words, he had sealed Bi Sain’s mouth.

Bi Sain quietly stared at Yayul Han. Judging by their faces alone, Bi Sain was the more fierce-looking, but Yayul Han’s two eyes, devoid of any emotion, overwhelmed that fierceness.

Bi Sain had to admit it. That he had feared Yayul Han all this time. Because he could not know what thoughts lay within that head, which never once revealed its emotions. It was because he did not know that he was all the more afraid.

“One can never truly know a person’s heart. To think that the Lord of the Daeya Bang, who devoted his entire life to our Alliance, would join hands with the Murim Alliance. Honestly, it is frightening. When I become the Alliance Lord, is there any guarantee that such a traitor will not emerge again?”

“Young Master Bi, with your excellent character, will lead our Alliance well.”

“It is not as if the Alliance Lord was betrayed because his character was lacking.”

Yayul Han read a certain provocation and confidence from Bi Sain. From this demeanor, different from usual, Yayul Han’s misunderstanding deepened.

*It seems the Sado Maengju has given him a word.*

He must have told him that an invisible war had begun.

Yayul Han did not know that this unusual attitude was because of Geom Mugeuk, not the Sado Maengju.

*Can a brat like you truly lead the Sado Murim?*

*By the time I reach your age, I will be a far greater man than you.*

*A brat remains a brat even after growing up. You will never sit in the Alliance Lord’s seat.*

These were the true feelings contained in their gazes. Yet from their mouths flowed soft words contrary to their hearts.

“I have made a careless remark that could cause misunderstanding.”

“Not at all. Given the gravity of the situation, I have been overly sensitive. I apologize.”

After apologizing politely, Bi Sain rose from his seat.

“I should take my leave before I commit further blunders.”

Before leaving, Bi Sain left behind one final remark.

“I trust only you, Danju.”

Yayul Han nodded reassuringly.

Bi Sain left the room. He had thought he should meet Yayul Han once before making his decision. He had hoped that seeing him would make the decision easier, but it remained difficult.

To become the Sado Maengju, he had to kill Yayul Han. But was the time now? And by borrowing the Demon Cult’s blade?

When he had harmed the grandson of Seok Gwan-chu to become the successor, he had been confident that he could overcome anything that followed.

He realized that he had still never made a grand, life-altering decision.

Having stepped outside, Bi Sain looked up at the sky. The weather had been gloomy for the past several days.

* * *

The next day, while eating at an inn, a voice transmission flew in again.

*—Eating at the same place where we meet is practically inviting an assassination.*

It was Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission.

*—If you were going to seek me out like this after less than a day, why did you say you would wait at that cliff?*

*—Waiting is waiting. Right now, I have come to persuade you. Because our destinies hang on this matter.*

Bi Sain’s chopsticks paused for a moment. He drank a cup of wine with his meal. Illang of the Sado Sipsamrang, who had been watching, asked worriedly.

“Is something troubling you?”

“Illang.”

“Yes, Young Master.”

“Do you think I will become the Sado Maengju?”

“What do you mean? Of course the next Alliance Lord is you, Young Master.”

“I mean, will I still be in the Alliance Lord’s seat ten years from now?”

For an instant, Illang hesitated for a split second. Bi Sain could tell. The existence that made him hesitate was none other than Yayul Han.

Illang answered.

“We will protect you safely for a lifetime.”

Bi Sain smiled and began eating again.

Geom Mugeuk’s voice transmission came again.

*—Those people will all die too.*

*—There is no need to drag others into this to persuade me.*

*—I am saying this is not your problem alone. The same goes for me. Those I have cherished, those close to me. My failure will become their failure, whether I wish it or not.*

Bi Sain gave no answer. Geom Mugeuk did not press him further. Instead, he asked to meet once more.

*—I have something to say. Let us meet at the cliff later.*

* * *

“Why did you ask to see me?”

When midnight came, Bi Sain came out to the cliff.

Geom Mugeuk, who had arrived first, was looking at the dense scatter of stars in the night sky.

“The days have been cloudy for several days, so the stars could not be seen, but today they are all too visible.”

Bi Sain looked up at the night sky following Geom Mugeuk’s gaze. Truly, the stars were cascading down.

After a long while, Geom Mugeuk spoke first.

“There is something I tell people. My demonic path does not shatter tables.”
