Bi Sain turned his head to look at Geom Mugeuk. Geom Mugeuk continued speaking while still gazing up at the sky.

“I cannot stand the sight of the strong trampling the weak. Seeing it, whether they are of our cult, the orthodox factions, or the unorthodox factions, I cannot endure it. You may or may not know, but Yayul Han’s subordinates created something called Sinseonchae to toy with and lead young people to ruin. They made a drug called Gwangpok and led even innocent people to their deaths. My inability to stand by and watch that madness is my demonic path.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze slowly shifted from the sky to Bi Sain. When their eyes met, Geom Mugeuk asked.

“What is your sacred path?”

Bi Sain could not answer. He had a vague resolve and ambition to rule the Sado Maeng well, but he had no concrete governing philosophy that he could articulate. Perhaps it was only natural. Bi Sain was still young.

“If you find that answer, this decision may become easier.”

Today, Geom Mugeuk left first.

Bi Sain stood there for a long time, late into the night.

* * *

The next day, Bi Sain arrived first at the cliff.

And the moment he saw Geom Mugeuk, he spoke coldly.

“Do you know what you are trying to do?”

It was a low utterance, yet it contained the anger he had failed to vent the day before.

“Do you speak knowing what killing him entails? If it were an easy task, would I have endured like this, knowing full well that he will kill me someday?”

“I know it is difficult.”

“No, you do not. Let me tell you why you do not. I once heard words from the Alliance Lord. Yayul Han is attended by absolute masters, so you must not take this lightly. I shall tell you the name of one among them.”

“Who is it?”

“Oroe Singeom, Baekhaeng.”

Geom Mugeuk was startled.

“That man still lives?”

A former titan of the unorthodox, he had been a master soaring high enough to devour the Sado Murim of his era.

“So leave while he lets you go peacefully. Go back while your two legs are still intact.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said something unexpected.

“Our cult has also identified someone.”

“Who is it that you know?”

“Hyeollyun Geop, Damal.”

This time, Bi Sain was surprised. He was a true villain among villains who had massacred hundreds of orthodox martial artists in one place, inciting a blood tribulation. His martial prowess could not compare to Oroe Singeom’s, but his wickedness was indescribable.

“These two may not be the end of it. Do you still intend to go through with this?”

At Bi Sain’s question, Geom Mugeuk nodded.

“I will.”

At the answer without a moment’s hesitation, Bi Sain shook his head.

“You truly are a strange kind of madman.”

“Even if I fail, you will suffer no loss. Then why do you hesitate? Are you perhaps worried about me? Is that why you told me about Oroe Singeom?”

Bi Sain was slightly flustered.

“What nonsense? Why would I worry about you?”

“Why are you angry? I was merely asking.”

Geom Mugeuk continued to persuade him in a gentle tone.

“I know this is not an easy decision.”

“And yet you are pushing me.”

“Have you not experienced it yourself? The bigger the decision, the more often the very first decision that comes to mind is the correct one. If you ignore that primal instinct, you only end up making a bad move after long deliberation. What was the first decision that came to your mind?”

After a brief pause, Bi Sain answered.

“It was to do it.”

“Then let us do it.”

Bi Sain knew. That there would be no end to worrying over this. That even after a month, even after a year, he would still be torn. He simply had to go for it.

And Bi Sain went for it.

“Very well. Let us do it.”

He decided to think simply.

Because if not for Geom Mugeuk, there would be no him as he was now.

The man had come to collect a debt of life; let this be how he repaid that great debt.

“Let us do it!”

Because Geom Mugeuk was, at the very least, someone who did not seem like he would lead him down a bad path. To him, Geom Mugeuk was such a person.

Geom Mugeuk was overjoyed. And he was grateful to Bi Sain for accepting his proposal.

“Thank you. You have truly chosen well. Had it not been for you, I would never have conceived of proceeding with this matter in the first place.”

“I only hope that when I look back on this moment later, I will not reproach myself for having made such a pathetic decision.”

The two men’s gazes intertwined in the air.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to him with eyes full of gratitude.

“Let us not look back now. Let us look back later, after we have become the Heavenly Demon and the Sado Maengju.”

Chapter 231: What’s Worse Than a Misguided Life.
------------------------------------------------
“Do you have a plan to kill Yaryulhan?”

At Bisain’s question, Geom Mugeuk shook his head.

“Not yet.”

Seeing Bisain look at him in disbelief, Geom Mugeuk said,

“How could I make a proper plan before I had your permission?”

“Are you saying that the moment I give permission, a plan that didn’t exist will suddenly appear?”

“It will.”

“You truly are a man with no countermeasures.”

Though he said that, Bisain did not seem displeased. Rather, now that he had made his decision, he looked relieved.

“Promise me one thing.”

Bisain set forth a single condition. Since he had already given his permission, it was closer to a request than a condition.

“You may kill the Five Thunder Divine Sword or the Blood Wheel Tribulation, but you must not touch the Extreme Blade Corps. They are not Yaryulhan’s personal hands and feet. They are the finest elites of our Alliance.”

In terms of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, it was like asking him to leave the Demon Army alone and kill only the Demon Army Lord. Losing Yaryulhan alone would be a great blow, but he was still trying to minimize the damage.

“Understood.”

Geom Mugeuk readily accepted.

Bisain let out a sigh.

“I don’t know what I’m doing. If this goes wrong, being killed by Yaryulhan will be the least of it. I may be driven from my position as successor at once.”

“If you’re driven out, come to me. I’ll take you in.”

“I’d rather die than go there.”

Bisain’s hideous face twisted. Geom Mugeuk could tell. He had smiled in good spirits. It was an emotion one could never discern unless one observed him with the utmost care.

“Contact me if there is anything I can do to help. For the time being, I’ll be eating lunch at that inn.”

“Thank you.”

With that, Bisain left the place first.

Geom Mugeuk remained there for a long while, looking up at the nightscape of the Sado Alliance and the night sky, before finally leaving as well.

* * *

After returning to the safe house, I immediately gathered Gowol, Geugak Soma, and Cult Leader Pungcheon in one place.

“The successor of the Sado Alliance, Bisain, has promised to take responsibility for cleaning up afterward. Now all we have to do is kill Yaryulhan.”

At my words, everyone was startled.

“How in the world did you change that man’s mind?”

Not only Cult Leader Pungcheon, who had asked the question, but even Geugak Soma and Gowol looked at me with astonished eyes. They had thought it would take time even if I managed to persuade him, yet I had succeeded far too quickly.

“Perhaps young successors have something in common.”

“You really are…”

Unable to find the words, Cult Leader Pungcheon merely shook his head.

“It is good news that Bisain agreed to our request, but there is bad news as well. We have learned of another subordinate under Yaryulhan.”

We already knew that the Blood Wheel Tribulation was Yaryulhan’s subordinate.

“The person we have newly discovered is none other than the Five Thunder Divine Sword.”

At the mention of the Five Thunder Divine Sword, everyone was shocked.

Even Cult Leader Pungcheon of the Outer Regions remembered him.

“Wasn’t he the one who once stirred up a storm of blood through the murim? He was even branded a public enemy of the martial world.”

“That’s right. That is the man.”

“I thought he was dead, but he was hiding in Yaryulhan’s shadow.”

The Blood Wheel Tribulation was already no easy opponent, but the Five Thunder Divine Sword was a level above him in skill.

Just then, as if a thought had suddenly occurred to him, Cult Leader Pungcheon asked,

“Then couldn’t you leave them aside and kill Yaryulhan when he is alone? They are human too; they cannot be with him at all times.”

Everyone’s gaze gathered on me. All three of them felt the same way, but there was a reason we could not do that.

“As I said, once Yaryulhan dies, Bisain will be the one considered to have killed him.”

Gowol was the first to grasp my intention.

“You are worried they will take revenge on Bisain.”

I nodded.

“We don’t know what kind of relationship they have. Whether it is a relationship in which they would avenge him, or one in which they would simply leave. But if, by any chance, they decide to take revenge, Bisain will certainly die.”

“Now I clearly understand why Bisain chose to help you, Young Master.”

He meant that Bisain, too, must have realized I was taking him into consideration.

Cult Leader Pungcheon stepped forward again.

“I will help with this matter as well.”

I had not expected him to offer his help in this.

“Do you mean that?”

“I should help, if only out of shame.”

He meant he was ashamed that he had asked whether we couldn’t simply kill Yaryulhan without considering that Bisain might be avenged.

But I knew that was only an excuse, and that he had stepped forward in order to help me.

“Are they not opponents too burdensome for the two of you to face alone? Why? Do you not trust my skill?”

“If I cannot trust your skill, Cult Leader, then whose could I trust? It is just that there is something more important you must do.”

“What is that?”

“Please protect Strategist Go as you have until now.”

My gaze turned toward Gowol.

“If, by any chance, our side is exposed, Strategist Go may be in danger. At that time, you must protect him, Cult Leader. Because you have been here until now, I have never once worried about Strategist Go.”

“Will you be all right?”

“I have Soma with me.”

My gaze turned toward Geugak Soma, who had been quietly listening until now. In this burdensome situation, Geugak Soma smiled and said,

“Trust only in me.”

“Yes. I will trust only in you, Soma.”

At that moment, my true feelings were these.

Soma, you trust only in me. No matter what happens, I will never let you die.

This is my answer to the favor he has shown me.

* * *

Chief Strategist Sama Myeong was in the operation command room.

In this place, said to be safer even than the Heavenly Demon Hall, a top-secret operation related to Geom Mugeuk was underway.

Sama Myeong had been tense the entire time since Geom Mugeuk left the cult. If by any chance an incident broke out, he would have to support Geom Mugeuk swiftly, and he would have to steer relations with the Sado Alliance so they did not plunge into war. And these movements had to remain hidden from the Sado Alliance.

Thus far, Geom Mugeuk was carrying out his mission far better than Sama Myeong had expected.

When he eliminated Jisaeng, eliminated Aecha, and even got rid of Yeo Bulgae without revealing their identity, the chief strategist of Tongcheon Pavilion had said this:

“If I had been Yaryulhan’s strategist… I would have been helplessly taken in as well.”

Sama Myeong could not tell him that would not have happened. He felt that even he himself could not be certain.

“Gowol’s abilities are far from ordinary. The information network he built in the Central Plains was also created so that it does not overlap with our Tongcheon Pavilion’s, but can complement it.”

“He is thinking ahead to after the Second Young Master becomes the Heavenly Demon.”

“He is truly drawing a grand picture.”

Just as Geom Mugeuk was pushing forward a new demonic path, Sama Myeong thought that if Gowol sat in his own seat, Tongcheon Pavilion too would become a new Tongcheon Pavilion.

And today, a new report arrived.

The chief strategist entered Sama Myeong’s office with a grave expression.

“The Second Young Master’s side has requested information on one person.”

“Who is it?”

The chief strategist took a deep breath before reporting. It was his way of expressing in advance just how shocked he had been.

“The Five Thunder Divine Sword.”

Sama Myeong shot up from his seat.

“Yaryulhan is said to have even the Five Thunder Divine Sword with him.”

They had already known that the Blood Wheel Tribulation was among Yaryulhan’s subordinates. But the Five Thunder Divine Sword was there as well?

“Yaryulhan, the Blood Wheel Tribulation, and even the Five Thunder Divine Sword. Can the two of them handle all of them?”

“I believe it will not be easy.”

“What is the probability of success for this operation?”

“When we did not know of the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s existence, we estimated it at forty percent.”

That was the probability analyzed by Tongcheon Pavilion. They did not know the exact level of Geom Mugeuk’s martial arts, but it was the result of analysis based on the many things he had accomplished thus far.

“However, if we assume the Five Thunder Divine Sword is assisting Yaryulhan, we expect it to drop to the twenty percent range. I will report the detailed analysis as soon as it is complete.”

“Twenty percent.”

Tongcheon Pavilion’s analysis of operational success rates was almost always accurate. A figure of twenty percent had to be viewed as failure.

“If by any chance another expert besides the Five Thunder Divine Sword appears, it may fall below ten percent.”

It was not an exaggeration. Sama Myeong once again keenly realized that this matter was no ordinary affair.

“Send over every piece of information we have related to the Five Thunder Divine Sword.”

“Understood.”

Sama Myeong asked the chief strategist, who was about to turn and leave,

“What do you think?”

“Pardon?”

“I am asking what probability of success you think it has.”

“I…”

He was the strategist among his subordinates who knew the most information about Geom Mugeuk. After hesitating for a long while, he offered a figure Sama Myeong had not expected.

“I see it as fifty-fifty.”

Sama Myeong was startled.

“Twenty percent and fifty percent. That is too great a difference.”

“It is my emotional analysis.”

“Are you saying this pavilion’s analytical ability is a mess?”

“No, sir. It is only my intuition.”

Sama Myeong relentlessly pressed the flustered chief strategist.

“Why fifty percent?”

The chief strategist said,

“Are there not people in this world to whom analysis does not apply? I believe the Second Young Master is such a person.”

Before another barrage of questions could come, the chief strategist quickly bowed and withdrew.

Sama Myeong gathered the report in order to present it to the Heavenly Demon and rose from his seat. Yes, he was right. The Geom Mugeuk he had seen until now was not a man who could be explained by numbers.

The same went for Heavenly Demon Geom Ujin, who had permitted this affair. Thinking that perhaps this very point—that analysis did not apply to them—might be the greatest resemblance between the two, Sama Myeong headed for the Heavenly Demon Hall.

* * *

Gowol racked his brains to devise a plan.

This time, I did not tell him to enjoy the situation. This time, more than ever, we had to make a proper plan, and we had to follow that plan with certainty.

While Gowol was devising the operation, we each spent our time in our own way.

Geugak Soma pasted white wallpaper on the walls of his room and entered training while gazing at it, and I immersed myself in martial training in the backyard.

In this fight, I would have to display everything I had. It would not be an easy battle, but I would definitely get rid of Yaryulhan. Because there was something I had to say into his ear as he lay dying. Because I had to allow someone who, for long years, had been bound by revenge and had never once lived his own life, to at least spend the life remaining to him in peace, living as himself.

I was in the middle of martial training in the backyard when someone spoke from behind me.

“If you work this hard, how am I supposed to slack off?”

I turned to see Cult Leader Pungcheon walking toward me.

“Gowol nagged me so much that I got chased out. He asked what I was doing when the Young Master was still sweating away.”

“Since you’ve come out, let us train together. These days, as you lose weight, your appearance is becoming quite striking, Cult Leader.”

“Is that so?”

Cult Leader Pungcheon looked intrigued. While I was at it, I gave him one more pleasant remark.

“I imagine quite a few women followed you around when you were young. Am I wrong?”

“Well, the line of women following me was rather long.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon lifted his chin with a smug expression. Seeing the thick flesh beneath that chin, I knew he still had a long way to go.

“You should regain the popularity you had then.”

“How could that be easy at my age?”

“If you train and make your physical age ten years younger, you will return to yourself from ten years ago. If you make it twenty years younger, you will return to twenty years ago.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon stared at me.

“You have a way of making a man’s heart flutter with words that are nothing at all.”

“How could regaining your youth be nothing at all? Please show it to me! Who knows what the future holds? Perhaps you will transform into a version of yourself twenty years younger, Cult Leader. When we first met, did we ever imagine we would be having this conversation here?”

“That is true. You turned my monotonous life completely upside down.”

“Do you regret it?”

He seemed to ponder for a moment, then shook his head.

“If I were a little younger, I might have regretted it. But now I know. Worse than a mistaken life is a life lived in regret.”

Since he and I had always mostly exchanged jokes, it had been a long time since we had had such a serious conversation.

“When I was the cult leader, I believed that position was an enormous one.”

Whoooooosh.

A sandstorm blew in. Cult Leader Pungcheon opened the Time-Space Transposition Art and brought me into the scenery of the Outer Regions. Far in the distance, the majestic form of the Pungcheon Cult could be seen.

“To be honest, from the top of that place, I thought several times a day: I am the king of the Outer Regions. Countless people must envy me. Back then, even the sand that crunched in my mouth was as sweet as candy.”

His gaze turned to me.

“Now that I have realized it was all meaningless, I no longer regret it.”

“You are magnificent.”

“Magnificent, my foot. It is not as though the me of now is better than the me of then.”

“You are better.”

“Better? In what way?”

“You have people now who look at you properly, do you not?”

Our gazes met in midair.

He shook his head and said,

“To have exchanged this gaze for that! I will remain the greatest fool in the history of the murim.”

“You will remain as the man who made the greatest choice in the history of the murim.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon smiled. I intended to keep telling him. That his decision had been precious. What makes a person suffer is not what they sacrificed for that decision. It is that no one recognizes it. At least in his relationship with me, I would not let him feel that sense of loss.

Cult Leader Pungcheon slowly began to walk.

I followed him, walking through the wasteland where the sandstorm blew.

“I asked you about that before, didn’t I?”

There was another reason Cult Leader Pungcheon had gone out of his way to open the Time-Space Transposition Art today. He brought up something he had forgotten for a while.

“Whether the time that flows within the Time-Space Transposition Art and the time in reality can be made different.”

Chapter 232: Are You Going to Regret It Again Later?
----------------------------------------------------
I stopped walking.

It was not because of the sandstorm blowing in. The question I had asked him in the past was extremely important to me.

“When I asked whether it was possible to make time flow more slowly within the Space-Time Transposition Art, this was how you answered. ‘You brat, does that even make any sense!’”

I remembered his exact words.

“You also said that you and the previous cult leaders had actually tried it, but that it was impossible.”

“You have a good memory.”

After a brief pause, the Fengcheon Cult Leader spoke.

“That statement is both true and false.”

“What do you mean?”

“In truth, no matter how much we researched and trained, it was impossible. I still feel the same way. You brat, does that even make any sense!”

“Then what part was false?”

“I told you it was nonsense, but in fact, there was a secret art passed down quietly among the cult leaders. It is called the Shitian Secret Art (時天祕術).”

The moment I heard those words, my heart surged. Reading my emotions, the Fengcheon Cult Leader quickly said,

“Do not get your hopes up too much. No cult leader has ever truly mastered the Shitian Secret Art. No matter how much they studied and trained, it was nothing but a waste of time. I am even doubtful whether it is a proper secret art at all.”

Even so, I was filled with emotion. That was how important this matter was to me.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader continued his explanation.

“According to the records left behind, the Shitian Secret Art was created two hundred years ago by the cult leader at the time, Yan Mingde, after he stepped down from his position and lived in seclusion. He succeeded in making the time inside and outside the Space-Time Transposition Art flow differently. They say that one shichen passed inside, but when he came out, not even half a shichen had gone by.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader emphasized once more that this was an impossible feat.

“But after Cult Leader Yan, no one else ever accomplished it. And it was only natural. Cult Leader Yan possessed the deepest internal energy in the history of the Fengcheon Cult, and he was born with the Heavenly Martial Body, so his realm in martial arts was beyond description. That is why ordinary people could not understand the secret art he left behind. Or else the secret art he left behind was nonsense.”

“But why are you telling me this?”

His gaze and mine tangled in midair.

“Before that, let me ask you first. Why are you interested in this impossible matter? Ah, of course anyone would dream of such a space, but you seem to have a reason beyond vague desire.”

There was a reason. It was because of the Nine Flames Demonic Art.

If I inherited the Nine Flames Demonic Art, I had to achieve great completion without fail. Moreover, my goal was not great completion at the tenth star, but great completion at the twelfth star.

But I lacked absolute time. If it were any other martial art, perhaps not, but the twelfth-star great completion of the Nine Flames Demonic Art was by no means a simple matter.

“There is someone I want to train to my heart’s content and beat to a pulp.”

“You want to add even more training to your current ability and beat him? Whoever it is, he has picked the wrong opponent.”

Yes, just as the cult leader said, he had picked the wrong opponent. Whether it was Hwa Mugi or me. One of the two of us had truly picked wrong.

“When you first asked me that, I never imagined a moment like this would come.”

Soon after, astonishing words flowed from his mouth.

“I will pass the Shitian Secret Art on to you.”

Our gazes tangled in midair. Though it was a secret technique no one had achieved, it was certainly not the kind of secret technique one would ever want to hand over to another.

“Though I did not succeed… perhaps you might.”

At the trembling look in my eyes that asked why he was showing me such favor, the Fengcheon Cult Leader boldly revealed his feelings.

“Did you not say so? That inside this place, you would take me as your master.”

“!”

“Is it not only natural for a master to pass a secret art on to his disciple?”

I was sincerely moved. I knew, to some extent, what kind of person he was. As human as he was, he was also full of desire, and he had regretted everything he had given me thus far.

And yet he was trying to give me more. He was trying to hand over yet another secret technique he possessed.

“Are you planning to regret it again later?”

“If I regret it, then I regret it. Am I not someone with considerable experience when it comes to regret?”

The moment I heard those words, my heart swelled. He was truly giving everything he had for my sake. Seo Daeryong had said this to me before: that I was someone who returned ten for one. That was what I should have said to the Fengcheon Cult Leader. I had given him one, and he was repaying me with ten.

I dropped flat on the spot and bowed to him.

“Be as spiteful as you like and make as much of a show of it as you like. I will accept it all for the rest of my life. Truly, thank you.”

Instead, the Fengcheon Cult Leader raised me to my feet with reddened eyes.

“I do not even know whether this is something to be thanked for or not. Rather, I am worried I may steal your mental strength and time by passing on a secret technique that even I could not succeed in.”

“Please leave what comes after to me.”

“Now, listen carefully from this point on.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader transmitted the mnemonic formula of the Shitian Secret Art to me. He checked several times to make sure I had memorized it properly.

“If this is possible, then the degree to which time slows would differ depending on the person, would it not?”

“I suppose so.”

“Then perhaps one day, if the realm of the Shitian Secret Art reaches its extreme, it might even be possible to make time stop there.”

“You cannot even crawl yet, and already you dream of flying?”

“I am quite greedy when it comes to martial arts.”

We looked at each other and laughed.

“That would be a matter beyond human ability.”

“I will try surpassing it.”

“Even as I think it will not be possible, because it is you, I find myself looking forward to it in some measure. To just how much you will astonish me with this.”

He probably did not know. Just how grateful I was at this moment. I would surely refine and temper the Shitian Secret Art and make him gape in shock.

* * *

I opened a space alone with the Space-Time Transposition Art, and within it, I used the Shitian Secret Art once more.

Whoooooosh.

The sound of the wind circling around me sounded mysterious. I had yet to move beyond the introductory stage of the mnemonic formula, but already the internal energy it demanded was tremendous. I could understand why everyone had failed to achieve this and given up.

The difficulty of the secret art was a problem, but the amount of internal energy required was simply too great. In that case, even if one succeeded in the secret art, the time one could remain here would become so short that the art itself would have felt meaningless.

Moreover, if one were to train in martial arts here, additional internal energy would be required, so it would have seemed even more unthinkable.

But to me, it had meaning.

Because my internal energy was so pure and vast that it could not be compared to anyone else’s in this martial world.

In addition, I had a firm belief. Just like other top-tier martial arts, the higher the realm of the Shitian Secret Art rose, the more rapidly its efficiency in using internal energy would improve. The Wind God Four Steps had been the same. The higher the realm climbed, the faster the speed became, while the internal energy required decreased.

I learned the Shitian Secret Art with the certainty that I would absolutely succeed. I believed in myself. I, too, possessed the Heavenly Martial Body, and I was confident that my current level in martial arts was higher than Yan Mingde’s level had been when he created this secret art.

* * *

The next day, I ran into Gowol in the yard.

“Has something good happened?”

At Gowol’s question, my gaze turned toward the Fengcheon Cult Leader standing beside me.

“Something good did happen.”

Quick-witted Gowol realized it had something to do with the Fengcheon Cult Leader.

“You always say you dislike the Second Young Master, and yet what have you given him this time?”

At Gowol’s question, the Fengcheon Cult Leader looked off into the distance and played innocent.

Smiling, I asked Gowol,

“How about you?”

“I think I need a little more time. Right now, it is not the time to use my head, but my eyes.”

It meant he was watching and observing the enemy. He was trying to find a weakness in Yelü Han’s daily life.

He was busy all day. Receiving and sending letters, meeting and returning from those who conveyed information. Of course, since the Fengcheon Cult Leader was with him, I was not worried.

“It does not matter how much time it takes. Handle it carefully.”

“Yes!”

“I will bring back something delicious, so let us eat dinner together.”

Tonight, I intended to feed them something truly special.

* * *

That day, I sold a portion of the night pearls I had entrusted to the money house and bought four roots of thousand-year snow ginseng (千年雪蔘).

Having traveled all over the Central Plains before my regression and experienced all manner of things, I knew well of a black-market dealer near the main headquarters of the Evil Dao Alliance who sold spiritual medicines.

Since he dealt in goods that were hard to obtain, the prices were extremely high, but I did not spare money in buying them.

After buying the thousand-year snow ginseng and returning to the safe house, I called Geugak Soma, Gowol, and the Fengcheon Cult Leader. I placed the thousand-year snow ginseng before them and spoke firmly, as though driving in a nail.

“Now, from this moment on, each of us takes one root. Refusal is refused. You must take it in front of me right now, no matter what.”

As I had said when sharing spiritual medicine with Geugak Soma, I was not someone who shared spiritual medicine with others. Even so, I wanted to share this spiritual medicine with them.

It was encouragement for Geugak Soma, who was facing a fight; a gift for the Fengcheon Cult Leader, who had even given me his final secret technique; and a tonic for Gowol, who had suffered until now.

The Fengcheon Cult Leader joked.

“Why would anyone refuse something this good? Anyone who wants to refuse may give theirs to me!”

Gowol, more than anyone, had his thoughts written plainly on his face: I do not need it, so please take it yourself, young master. But he knew. The words telling them not to refuse had been meant for him.

Gowol bowed his head, and Geugak Soma expressed his gratitude with his eyes.

And so the four of us took the thousand-year snow ginseng together.

My internal energy was so deep that, by now, one could say even a thousand-year snow ginseng would barely make a ripple in me, but for the other three, it was still a spiritual medicine that would be of considerable help. Especially for Gowol, who had the least internal energy, it would have the greatest effect not only on his internal energy but also in restoring the vitality of his exhausted body.

—Take good care of your health, Strategist Go.

I even sent him a sound transmission especially.

Gowol conveyed his gratitude to me with deepened eyes.

* * *

At last, Gowol found a method.

“During this time, I conducted extensive investigations into Yelü Han. His residence is in the inner compound of the Evil Dao Alliance. He wakes up in the morning, trains, eats, spends his day as the commander of the Extreme Blade Corps, and if he is summoned by the Alliance Leader’s Hall, he goes in. He spent all his time inside the Evil Dao Alliance. After the deaths of Jisaeng and Aecha, he never once went outside.”

Having misunderstood that the Evil Dao Alliance Leader was targeting him, Yelü Han had been thoroughly lying low, and thanks to that, finding an opportunity to strike him was no easy matter.

“It will not be easy.”

“No. Striking him while he is in the alliance is several times more difficult.”

Even if it were not difficult, killing him inside the Evil Dao Alliance was something that had to be avoided. Since Bisa-in had permitted this matter, we ought to maintain courtesy toward the Evil Dao Alliance.

“I was curious. Would the Five Thunder Divine Sword and the Blood Wheel Calamity also be living inside the Evil Dao Alliance like Yelü Han?”

Gowol looked at me and asked for my opinion.

“They would not.”

“What is your reason for thinking so?”

“Because both of them are public enemies of the martial world. There is an agreement made between the Martial Alliance and the Evil Dao Alliance. An agreement that those branded public enemies of the martial world must never be hidden.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader cut in and said,

“Would those unorthodox bastards keep such an agreement?”

“The one who would want to keep this agreement is probably Yelü Han.”

“Why would that bastard?”

“Because he would not want the Evil Dao Alliance Leader to gain a weakness over him. If the Evil Dao Alliance Leader were to attack him, there would be no better pretext than this.”

The Fengcheon Cult Leader nodded with an expression that said he had not thought that far.

Gowol stepped forward again.

“You have grasped it precisely. That is correct. The Five Thunder Divine Sword and the Blood Wheel Calamity are not living inside the Evil Dao Alliance. So I tried to find out where the two of them were living.”

“They must have a residence near the main headquarters.”

“Yes. But it was hidden so tightly that it was not easy to find. So I changed methods. I watched someone who might visit that place.”

“Who? Yelü Han?”

“No. Cha Hwan.”

Cha Hwan, the last survivor of the Four.

“He is serving as Yelü Han’s right hand. Surely someone must entertain and assist an old master like the Five Thunder Divine Sword, and they would not entrust that to just anyone.”

I judged that to be the proper approach. As expected, Gowol had judged it intelligently.

“While watching Cha Hwan, I discovered one peculiar fact. I found out that, without fail, he buys Shanxi Fenjiu once a month.”

Gowol added with a meaningful look in his eyes.

“The Five Thunder Divine Sword’s hometown is Shanxi. According to the information sent from the Tongtian Pavilion, the liquor he liked most was Fenjiu.”

“He is taking that liquor to the Five Thunder Divine Sword.”

“That is correct. Based on that, I tailed Cha Hwan and identified the residence of the Five Thunder Divine Sword.”

“Well done!”

“The problem is Yelü Han. Because of this incident, he is refraining entirely from going outside, and at the same time, he is not meeting with the Five Thunder Divine Sword either. But while looking through the materials sent from the Tongtian Pavilion, I discovered this. Please look.”

As I looked over the materials he handed me, I stared at Gowol with a meaningful glint in my eyes.

“It will soon be the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s birthday!”

“And his ninetieth birthday, at that. I do not know about any other day, but on this day, Yelü Han and the Blood Wheel Calamity will go to congratulate him.”

Gowol’s eyes sparkled.

“We will deal with them that day. There is no need to take the risk of disposing of them all at once. We will eliminate them one by one, with time between each. First, kill the Five Thunder Divine Sword, then remove everyone who visits that place in order. One by one, in sequence.”

I gave Gowol’s shoulder a firm pat.

“You truly worked hard to find this out.”

“It is thanks to the thousand-year snow ginseng.”

He smiled, but Gowol’s expression was still worried.

I looked at Geugak Soma and said,

“It is the old man’s birthday. We should go congratulate him too, should we not?”

The two eyes within Geugak Soma’s eye sockets began to burn with blue flames.

“From now on, leave it to us!”

Chapter 233: It Would Be Strange If It Were Easy.
-------------------------------------------------
A sound transmission flew to Bisain as he was eating at an inn.

—Eat a variety of things. You always seem to order only that dish.

At Geom Mugeuk’s sound transmission, Bisain answered with an incredulous look.

—Are you trying to meddle with my tastes now, too?

—I’m looking out for your health. I’ve always been a bit nosy.

—It’s truly fortunate you’re not of the unorthodox faction.

—Don’t worry. Everyone says I seem like someone from the orthodox faction, never the unorthodox.

After exchanging such light jokes, Geom Mugeuk revealed the reason he had come to find him today.

—In two days, it will be the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s birthday. I intend to settle everything that day.

For an instant, Bisain’s chopsticks stopped, and intense emotion flashed across his face. At last, the day that would change his fate was only two days away.

—Is it all right for you to tell me all this?

—At least in this matter, you’re on the same side as me.

—What if I change my mind and tell Yaryulhan everything? Or if I accidentally let it slip to someone?

—I trust myself. I wouldn’t have joined hands with such a fool in the first place.

The corner of Bisain’s mouth twisted slightly. To anyone watching, it might have looked as if he was grimacing because the food tasted bad, but he was smiling.

Bisain said to Geom Mugeuk,

—Let’s meet at the cliff tonight.

—Let’s.

He looked as though he had something to say.

* * *

That night, I arrived at the cliff early and waited.

Once tonight passed, it would be the eve of the decisive battle. This time, even I felt tense and on edge.

“I don’t know why, but whenever I see your back, I want to shove you off.”

At Bisain’s words from behind me, I turned around with a laugh.

“It’s because you’ve built up too much anger.”

“Is there a way to live as the successor of the Evil Dao Alliance without building up anger?”

“At times like that, drinking yourself senseless with the Drunken Demon and swimming around a lake does help a little. Do you have anyone to drink with?”

“I don’t particularly like alcohol.”

“Reading books with the Blade Demon isn’t bad either.”

“I dislike books even more. But the Blade Demon reads?”

“Or you could spar with the Sword Venerable.”

“Do you think there are only one or two people who would use a spar to kill me?”

“How about traveling with the Little Demon?”

“I have no one to travel with.”

“What about late-night martial arts training with the Fist Demon?”

“Listening to you is making me even angrier! Are you bragging that you’re close with the Demon Venerables?”

“You believe me? I thought you’d assume I made it all up.”

Then Bisain replied,

“If it’s you… I feel like you would be close with all of them.”

Surprisingly, he believed everything I had said.

“There is a way for you to relieve your anger.”

I smiled as I spoke to him, who looked curious.

“Come play with me!”

“!”

“After this is over, if your anger piles up so much that you truly can’t stand it anymore, come find me. Let’s go out and have fun.”

Bisain’s eyes trembled slightly. In the past, he would have asked if I was insane before anything else, but he said nothing.

Perhaps Bisain wanted to say, “Let’s!” After all, I had always felt loneliness from him.

“No.”

Bisain refused at once and took several sheets of paper from his robes.

“This is why I asked to see you.”

What he handed me was a secret document related to the Five Thunder Divine Sword.

It contained not only his martial arts, but where he had been born, what sort of growth he had undergone, and with whom, where, and for what reason he had fought.

“I don’t know whether this will help, but read it just in case.”

“Thank you. It will be a great help.”

Bisain stared intently at me.

A look of hesitation flashed across his face, as if he was debating whether to speak, and in the end he said to me,

“When this is over, there may not be time to have conversations like this, so I’ll say it now. Lately… I’ve been thinking about my Evil Dao.”

It seemed my question about what his Evil Dao was had lodged deep in his heart. Perhaps the reason he had asked to meet today was because he had wanted to say those words.

Before hearing my answer, he turned and strode away.

“I’ll look forward to the day I hear about your Evil Dao.”

As Bisain disappeared into the darkness, he answered,

“Just don’t die before then.”

* * *

On the day before the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s birthday, Geom Mugeuk scouted his residence.

It had yet to be determined how many people were inside that manor, or who lived there. To carry out tomorrow’s operation safely, he had to prepare thoroughly. Even with such preparation, variables always arose endlessly.

The manor was small and cozy.

In a straight line, it was located at a distance one could reach from the main headquarters of the Evil Dao Alliance in an instant, but if one were to walk or take a carriage, it was in an exquisite location that required winding through the forest for quite some time.

Furthermore, it was hidden deep in the woods, in a place where even someone who passed nearby every day would be surprised and say, “There was a house there?” unless someone told them there was a path and a house in that spot.

Geom Mugeuk climbed a giant tree overlooking the manor.

He flew up to that height and landed on a branch without making even the slightest sound of wind. The thin branch merely swayed lightly and did not break.

There, Geom Mugeuk began emitting his qi.

Several strands of qi crossed over the wall. The qi emission he had learned from his father on his first hunt shone in moments like this. From a distance where the other party could not detect his presence, he could send such thin, long strands of qi out like scouts.

One person was detected in the courtyard of the manor.

Geom Mugeuk used the Divine Eye Art to confirm him. He looked like someone who served the Five Thunder Divine Sword in the manor and handled menial tasks.

Another presence was detected near the kitchen. Judging by the qi and movements he sensed, this person also had not learned martial arts and was elderly. She likely appeared to be an old woman who prepared meals.

Geom Mugeuk’s qi roamed all throughout the manor. Seeing no one else, it seemed those two people were serving the Five Thunder Divine Sword.

Geom Mugeuk’s qi entered the building. When it passed through the corridor and entered the innermost room, he sensed a powerful presence.

‘It’s the Five Thunder Divine Sword!’

Geom Mugeuk did not overreach. For now, it was enough to confirm his presence. He quietly retrieved the qi he had emitted.

At the very moment he collected all his qi,

the Heavenly Demon Protective Divine Art activated and sensed danger.

The next instant!

Tap.

In the next moment, a man was standing on the branch where Geom Mugeuk had been. The man who had suddenly appeared had cut through the air like an arrow and landed on that thin branch. It was truly a lightness skill that had reached an extraordinary realm. Geom Mugeuk, who had been there, was already beneath the tree.

The man looked around, then peered down below the tree. Geom Mugeuk was on the opposite side of the giant tree, out of his sight.

The man quickly turned and checked beneath the tree behind him as well.

Once again, Geom Mugeuk was standing on the opposite side. Though both had moved quickly, not a sound was made. They were both displaying exquisite movement arts.

The man tilted his head slightly, then lightly launched himself and landed at the distant manor.

Then he entered the building where the Five Thunder Divine Sword was.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze deepened as he watched.

This man, who had appeared from who knew where and when, had sensed the qi as Geom Mugeuk was retrieving it and flown to this place, the source of that qi. Not only were his senses excellent, his lightness skill was also of a high level.

‘He’s no ordinary expert.’

Unfortunately, he had not seen the man’s face properly. That was how fast the other party’s movements had been.

Geom Mugeuk also quietly disappeared from the place.

* * *

At that time, Yaryulhan was eating alone at a large table in a spacious hall.

Chahwan entered carrying a box.

“The gift for the Five Thunder Divine Sword has arrived.”

Yaryulhan gestured for him to bring it closer. Chahwan approached him with the box. Every movement he made was careful.

He opened the box at a spot a little away from Yaryulhan.

When the box opened, a fragrant scent wafted out.

“It is a Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.”

It was the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng, said to be difficult to obtain even for a thousand pieces of gold.

In the end, Chahwan could not hold back and spoke what was on his mind.

“This is far too precious to give as a gift.”

Every year on his birthday, they gave the Five Thunder Divine Sword a valuable gift. But this was the first time the gift had been as precious as this Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.

Despite his complaint, Yaryulhan did not get angry at all. He kept Chahwan at his side precisely because of this sort of frankness.

“It’s a special birthday, so we should give a special gift.”

“Of course, that is true, but…”

“He is a man worth the money.”

“Yes.”

Unable to voice any further complaints, Chahwan carefully closed the box and placed it on the table.

The words, ‘Then what in the world will you give that old man when he turns a hundred?’ rose all the way to his throat, but he could not spit them out.

“Will you go in person tomorrow?”

Yaryulhan nodded.

“Considering the situation, how about sending only the gift this time?”

After pondering for a moment, Yaryulhan said to Chahwan,

“I should go. If I don’t, I’ll invest a Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng only to be cursed for it. Instead, this time I’ll go at a slightly different hour.”

Chahwan bowed respectfully.

“I will make preparations accordingly.”

“And summon the Three Evils as well.”

Chahwan flinched. As always, merely hearing their name chilled a person’s heart.

“Then I can feel at ease.”

After Chahwan left, Yaryulhan opened the box containing the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng again.

He silently looked down at it. For someone who had learned martial arts, how could one endure with a Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng before one’s eyes?

Yaryulhan lifted it and opened his mouth wide.

But he only pretended to eat it in one bite, then put the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng back into the box and closed it.

What he would eat was not the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng. By enduring and enduring again, he would devour something far greater.

* * *

I returned straight to my quarters.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon, Gowol, and Cult Leader Pungcheon were waiting for me to return from my reconnaissance.

“How was it?”

At Gowol’s question, I let out a light sigh.

“I was nearly discovered.”

At my words, the three of them were startled.

Cult Leader Pungcheon said to me,

“It seems that old villain didn’t simply idle about and eat his way into old age.”

“It wasn’t him.”

“It wasn’t?”

“I’m not certain whether he was a visitor or someone originally guarding the place, but his martial arts were no ordinary matter.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon thought of someone.

“Could it have been Yaryulhan?”

Gowol answered,

“No. Yaryulhan has not taken a single step outside the Evil Dao Alliance at present.”

“Not Yaryulhan? Then the Blood Wheel Calamity?”

At Cult Leader Pungcheon’s question, Gowol shook his head again.

“The Blood Wheel Calamity is known to be on rather poor terms with the Five Thunder Divine Sword. The possibility that the two of them held a private meeting is low.”

I agreed with that opinion as well.

“I couldn’t get a close look, but he wasn’t as old as the Blood Wheel Calamity.”

It was not a good situation. If that expert had visited to celebrate the birthday, then he would be at the manor tomorrow as well. That meant there was one more expert to deal with.

Naturally, worry rose on Gowol’s face.

“Didn’t we anticipate from the beginning that there might be someone else?”

“Yes, that is true, but if someone came today, does that not mean a new expert could come tomorrow as well? To be honest, I am worried.”

“Strategist Go. Strategists devise excellent operations, but unexpected situations like this arise in the field. This, too, will be a good experience for you.”

“I do not want such experience in an operation against Yaryulhan and the Five Thunder Divine Sword. All the more so if the person carrying it out is you, young master. Should we not at least know who that man is?”

I looked at the Extreme Evil Little Demon. He had been listening silently until now, and he spoke indifferently.

“Would knowing who he is change anything?”

This was the very nature of the man known as the Extreme Evil Little Demon. Even if the expert I had discovered today had not been one person but ten, he would have said the same thing.

The Extreme Evil Little Demon’s words neatly settled the current situation.

“You’re right.”

I smiled as I looked at him. What made me smile was the same thing that dwelled in the Extreme Evil Little Demon’s eyes. Deep in my chest, fighting spirit boiled up. Yes. Whoever was there, however many there were, what did it matter?

Wooooong!

The Black Demon Sword, having read my heart, let out a long hum. When the sword rang of its own accord, everyone’s gaze turned toward it. When I grasped the sword hilt, the Black Demon Sword stopped humming.

“We are making the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s birthday into the day of his funeral rites, and on top of that, we are even sending Yaryulhan along on the same boat to the underworld… If this were easy, that would be the strange thing.”

I made the final decision.

“We proceed according to plan.”

* * *

At dawn the next day, before departure, I went to find the Extreme Evil Little Demon.

“What is it?”

Instead of answering his question, I untied the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk from my sword hilt and gave him half. The Extreme Evil Little Demon knew very well what it was.

“Wrap it around your heart.”

“I am fine.”

“There will be more than enough left even if both of us wrap it.”

“Then wrap it twice around yourself!”

“My movements will become sluggish, so I can’t. I’ll be wearing the Ghost Tiger Garment over it as well.”

Knowing that I would absolutely not back down, the Extreme Evil Little Demon accepted it.

“I will return it once the fight is over.”

“Of course you should. That’s precious.”

The Extreme Evil Little Demon smiled brightly.

There on the spot, we wrapped the supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Silk around our hearts and stomachs.

After finishing all our preparations, I drew the Black Demon Sword.

Then, as if he had been waiting, the Extreme Evil Little Demon lightly flicked the blade of the Black Demon Sword with his finger.

Tiiing!

Listening to that clear sound that always felt good to hear, the sound announcing the beginning of the decisive battle, we quietly left the room.

Chapter 234: If You Miss Even One.
----------------------------------
“Master, happy birthday.”

The man bowing respectfully was Sahyo, disciple of the Five-Thunder Divine Sword. This Sahyo was the very man Geom Mugeuk had seen yesterday.

Before him sat the Five-Thunder Divine Sword.

The first impression of the Five-Thunder Divine Sword was ‘truly bizarre.’ Despite being ninety years old, his face was taut like the skin of a youth. But his arms and hands were covered in dense wrinkles, making this imbalance exceedingly grotesque.

“A birthday gift.”

Sahyo withdrew a small box from his bosom and presented it.

“This time, it is a Rejuvenation Pill made by extracting the vital essence of girls no older than fifteen.”

At the words ‘Rejuvenation Pill,’ the Five-Thunder Divine Sword’s expression brightened delightedly.

“From how many?”

“Ninety.”

Even though it meant ninety precious lives were contained within this small pill, a radiant joy spread across the Five-Thunder Divine Sword’s face. As he smiled, his skin stretched even tighter, making him appear all the more grotesque.

“It couldn’t have been easy?”

Gathering the vital essence of ninety people must have taken years to produce. It was truly a rare and precious medicine.

“You have reached the venerable age of ninety, Master. As your disciple, it is only proper I exert this much sincerity.”

The Five-Thunder Divine Sword smiled in satisfaction. At some point as he aged, he had become obsessed with youth. The deeper that obsession grew, the more blood stained Sahyo’s hands.

“I shall pass on the next formula to you. This time, I shall especially bestow three passages.”

At that, Sahyo prostrated himself flat on the ground and bowed.

“Thank you, Master.”

But his expression, with head bowed, was stiff.

‘Damned old man, only three measly passages!’

Each time he offered such loyalty, he received martial arts teachings.

Now, if he only inherited the final fifth form, he would have learned the entire Five-Thunder Sword Technique, but the process of learning that final form was anything but smooth.

Sahyo was a figure born with innate talent for martial arts. His qinggong was especially outstanding, and he possessed innate gifts not only in his five senses but his sixth sense as well. Moreover, his memory was excellent; once he saw or heard something, he never forgot it.

The single problem in his life was that his master was the Five-Thunder Divine Sword. Twenty years had passed since he became a disciple, yet he still hadn’t received the complete transmission.

‘This is truly going too far. Look at how much blood I’ve stained my hands with for you.’

The expression of the Five-Thunder Divine Sword looking down at him was cold beyond compare.

‘Did you think I wouldn’t know your scheme?’

His disciple possessed a ruthless nature that might try to kill him once the martial arts were fully transmitted. From then on, he wouldn’t be able to consume the Rejuvenation Pills with peace of mind, fearing they might be poisoned.

‘I’ll teach you the final formula the moment I die!’

By the time Sahyo raised his head, both their expressions had returned to normal.

“I shall take the Rejuvenation Pill, so leave for a moment.”

“Yes.”

Once Sahyo went outside, the Five-Thunder Divine Sword consumed the Rejuvenation Pill with a satisfied face.

Then he quietly closed his eyes and began circulating his qi.

* * *

Geom Mugeuk quietly leaped over the manor wall.

The first place he headed was the annex building where the workers of this place slept.

He entered each of their rooms and sealed their acupoints. By the time they awoke, everything would already be over.

Having exited the annex, Geom Mugeuk cautiously moved toward the main house.

Right then.

Swoosh!

Someone pressed a sword against Geom Mugeuk’s neck.

“Move and you die.”

The one holding the sword from behind was none other than Sahyo. He had just come out of his master’s chamber and discovered Geom Mugeuk.

“You were there yesterday too, weren’t you?”

Geom Mugeuk answered without turning around.

“Yes, it was me.”

“If you lack fear, you should at least have skill. Turn around slowly.”

Geom Mugeuk turned to face him. Despite being caught trespassing, his expression showed no tension whatsoever.

“Who sent you?”

“Does that matter? Sahyo.”

Sahyo was startled for a moment. He had believed no one knew his name besides his master.

But he didn’t know. The top-secret documents on the Five-Thunder Divine Sword provided by Bisain contained records of a disciple named Sahyo being taken in over twenty years ago.

“How do you know my name!”

In that exact moment his composure broke.

Swoosh!

Blood needles flew in from behind.

Sahyo was undoubtedly a born expert. He twisted his body to dodge the dart while simultaneously trying to cut down Geom Mugeuk before him.

But in that fleeting instant as he twisted, Geom Mugeuk’s fist embedded itself in his chest.

Boom!

His figure slid backward. It was an attack that would have burst his internal organs and killed him had he not instantly raised his protective astral qi.

“Damn it!”

He was in no situation to be spitting curses.

Ping! Swoosh!

Two more streaks of blood needles flew in continuously.

Judging he couldn’t dodge and block them all, Sahyo turned and swung his sword.

Sword qi erupted, neutralizing the incoming darts.

In that very moment, Geom Mugeuk’s blade fell from behind, splitting toward his head.

It was too late to block.

He barely twisted his body to avoid it, but blood spurted from his shoulder.

Sahyo was deeply shocked at being cut by a blade after so long, but he had no time to examine the wound.

Swoosh!

An incoming dart tore at his side.

Once again, had he not dodged in that instant, the attack would have pierced his stomach.

Attacks flew from front and back without giving him room to breathe, let alone think.

Watching Geom Mugeuk’s skill, Sahyo realized.

‘He got caught on purpose!’

They had set a trap on both sides to lure him in.

He couldn’t block the attacks flying frantically from front and back.

“Master! Please save me!”

In the end, he screamed desperately.

In the time it took to shout those words, blood spurted from his body once more.

“Master! You bastard!”

Despite his desperate cries, his master did not emerge.

Sahyo could tell. His master considered consuming the Rejuvenation Pill more important right now. Because if he stopped circulating qi at this moment, he would only obtain a portion of the medicine’s efficacy. Would he come to save him? The one who had sacrificed himself like a dog his whole life?

“Uwoaaaaah!”

Attacks of fury poured out.

In a state where his entire body was torn and tattered, the First Form, Second Form, and Third Form of the Five-Thunder Sword Technique erupted in succession.

Sword qi poured down from all directions.

But the frenzied thrashing of a wounded beast merely struck empty air and scattered.

No matter how much of a martial prodigy he was, he remained incomplete.

Therefore, he could not block the combined assault of Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma pouring from front and back.

Shhk!

In the very moment the Black Demon Sword cut diagonally across his back!

Thud!

The final dart pierced his forehead.

As he perished and collapsed, Sahyo saw fleeting images for a very brief moment.

Not his own life, but the faces of the girls he had killed to make the Rejuvenation Pill flashed past rapidly.

Thud.

Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma looked at each other and nodded once.

They had already known through Bisain’s documents that Sahyo was someone who committed all manner of evil deeds for his master. Thus, Geom Mugeuk hadn’t hesitated for an instant in eliminating him. The documents provided by Bisain were certainly proving helpful.

Right then.

Shhhaaaaack!

The two twisted their bodies to dodge, and a streak of sword qi passed between them.

It was truly powerful and swift sword qi.

The one who launched the sword qi was the Five-Thunder Divine Sword.

He had heard his disciple calling for help. But he couldn’t stop absorbing the medicinal effects.

“You damn bastards!”

Because he had been paying attention to the fight outside, he hadn’t been able to fully absorb the Rejuvenation Pill’s efficacy.

He was angrier about that than his disciple’s death.

He was furious that he wouldn’t be able to obtain another Rejuvenation Pill for the time being.

His desire for youth took precedence over everything else.

He had heard that Yayulhan was currently engaged in sharp conflict with the Evil Path Alliance Leader. Were they now raiding his residence?

‘How could they handle things like this!’

The target of his rage was Yayulhan.

He showed no sign of fear or dread even in this situation. He was someone who had once devoured the unorthodox martial world.

The Five-Thunder Divine Sword’s gaze turned toward the two men.

‘One is a greenhorn, and the other wears a mask?’

Sahyo had fallen to guys like these? The moment that doubt arose—

His gaze fixed once more on Geukak Soma.

“It can’t be?”

How could a man of ninety not know of Geukak Soma’s white mask?

Had Sahyo not died, he would have thought him merely a masked fellow, but if they had killed Sahyo in such a short time, it had to be a Majon of the Demonic Cult.

“Why would riffraff from the Demonic Cult come after me?”

Geukak Soma’s eyes smiled coldly.

Geom Mugeuk answered in his stead.

“What a monstrous face, indeed. To kill so many children just to become such a measly monster!”

The top-secret documents Bisain had obtained also contained information about the Rejuvenation Pills.

“Even the maggots that will devour you after you die will spit in disgust and turn away!”

Would the Five-Thunder Divine Sword, who had spent ninety years rolling around the unorthodox faction, fall for such provocation? He did.

Flash!

The Five-Thunder Divine Sword’s blade was drawn.

Boom!

Sword qi impaled the spot where Geom Mugeuk had stood.

The sword qi was as fast as lightning.

Geom Mugeuk had already sidestepped from that spot.

“Ugly, hideous old man! Filthy, vulgar, evil monster!”

Boom! Crack!

Two more streaks of sword qi crashed down, but he dodged with Flicker Step.

The Five-Thunder Divine Sword was enraged at hearing curses after so long. And from a young man at that.

Moreover, it was the first time in his ninety years of life he had heard an insult about maggots spitting in disgust.

“I’ll tear that mouth—!”

In that very moment he tried to speak while excited!

Geom Mugeuk severed his breathing and charged.

With him excited and speaking, this was the moment his qi and blood were most disrupted.

Penetrating with the Bright King Step, what unfolded was the Equal Heaven Form, the First Form of the Soaring Heaven Sword Technique.

Shiiiiick!

In the moment a streak of sword light bisected him horizontally—

Clang!

The Five-Thunder Divine Sword blocked the Black Demon Sword with his blade.

He was appalled by Geom Mugeuk’s attack speed. This was no mouth he could easily tear apart.

Swoosh!

Geukak Soma’s blood needles flew toward the Five-Thunder Divine Sword. Today’s battle would start and end with a combined assault from beginning to end.

Since their opponent was one who had committed evil deeds deserving death, the courtesies of a martial artist were omitted.

The continuous combined assault bewildered the Five-Thunder Divine Sword.

Geukak Soma’s blood needles flew toward the most troublesome spots, so they couldn’t be ignored, and Geom Mugeuk was an opponent who couldn’t be allowed at his back.

After only a few exchanges, a dart grazed his left shoulder, and the Five-Thunder Divine Sword unleashed his ultimate technique without reservation.

He had made the judgment befitting a master: if he dragged this out, he absolutely couldn’t win.

Thud! Crack! Thud!

His sword qi curved and stabbed down like lightning strikes.

The sword qi of the Five-Thunder Sword Technique was an attack that impaled the crown of the head; those unaccustomed to such attacks would die in a single blow.

Earlier, Sahyo had died without being able to properly display his swordsmanship, but the Five-Thunder Sword Technique was a peerless sword art modeled after the shape of lightning, a martial art that rivaled those mastered by the Majons.

Crack! Crack! Thud-thud!

Along with bizarre sounds, the sword qi grew even stronger.

The Second Form was stronger than the First Form’s lightning, and the Third Form displayed fearsome power.

Meeting a strong opponent after a long time, the Five-Thunder Divine Sword grew excited as he poured out his ultimate techniques.

He laughed like a madman and unleashed sword forms continuously.

Against the cascading sword qi, Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma displayed their footwork to evade.

They moved away in opposite directions, crossed paths, and gave way to one another.

Having fought together many times, the two could read each other’s intentions with just a glance.

The Five-Thunder Divine Sword poured his final Fifth Form onto Geukak Soma. He had realized from the way they dodged his attacks that Geom Mugeuk’s footwork was superior.

He decided to kill the relatively weaker Geukak Soma first, then deal with Geom Mugeuk.

In the moment the most powerful martial art was about to unfold!

Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma’s gazes met. The two spoke simultaneously with their eyes.

Now!

Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma also staked everything on this gamble.

Boom-boom-boom-boom-boom-boom-boom-boom-crack!

Violent sword qi poured down like rain toward Geukak Soma.

Though it was an enemy’s martial art, it was a sight that warranted admiration.

But instead of blocking the sword qi falling from the heavens, Geukak Soma unleashed the Demonic Radiant Blast Palm toward the Five-Thunder Divine Sword.

Kwaaaaah!

The Five-Thunder Divine Sword’s eyes went wide. It was a truly unexpected reaction.

‘Mutual destruction!’

But it wasn’t mutual destruction.

Thirty-six streaks of sword qi flew to intercept the sword qi hurtling toward Geukak Soma.

It was the Seventh Form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Yucheon Form, whose number of sword qi had increased along with his internal energy. Geom Mugeuk unleashed the greatest number of sword qi he could muster.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwak!

Shwik-shwik-shwik-shwik-shwik-shwik-shwik-shwik-shwik!

If even one was missed, Geugak Soma might die!

Geugak Soma had unleashed the Demonic Extreme Raging Palm, trusting that Geom Mugeuk would surely block the attack flying toward him.

Puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-puh-pung!

Above Geugak Soma’s head, the sword qi of the Yucheon Form and all the sword qi of the Fifth Form of the Five Thunder Sword Art were neutralized and vanished.

The Yucheon Form, into which he had poured his heart’s blood, did not miss even a single strand of sword qi.

At the very moment the sword qi was dispelled!

Kwaaaaaang!

The Demonic Extreme Raging Palm struck the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s body. Unable to dodge while pouring out the Fifth Form, the Five Thunder Divine Sword raised his protective qi to its limit.

Caught in the Demonic Extreme Raging Palm, he was sent flying and slammed into the wall.

Whooooo!

The rising dust settled.

Leaning against the wall, the Five Thunder Divine Sword gave voice to his rage.

I’ll tear both of you bastards to pieces!

But he could not hear the words he had spat out. The Five Thunder Divine Sword felt puzzled. Had his ears been injured?

Moreover, in this urgent situation, Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma were looking at each other.

Why aren’t they looking at me? The fight isn’t over yet.

The Five Thunder Divine Sword’s gaze drifted downward without thinking.

‘!’

Everything below his chest had vanished entirely.

The moment he saw it, the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s torso fell from the wall. The last thing he saw was his own grotesque face reflected in the pool of blood on the floor.

Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma looked at each other and smiled.

No words were needed. At this moment, Soma had become a true Soma (Laughing Demon), his smile overwhelmingly captivating.

Geom Mugeuk found it immensely enjoyable to fight alongside him. With him, he wanted to fight and fight again until his heart burst.

In particular, that final move had been an attack they could never have attempted without trust in each other. The longer the battle continued, the deeper their trust in each other became.

“Let’s recover our internal energy first.”

But the two of them were not given that chance.

Rumble-rumble-rumble.

The wall into which the Five Thunder Divine Sword had been hurled and killed collapsed.

“Ah! No!”

Geom Mugeuk cried out in distress. If the wall had collapsed, the enemies would not come in, and his plan to kill them one by one in order would go awry.

But there was no need to worry about that.

As the dust settled, people appeared beyond the collapsed wall. People who should not yet have arrived appeared all at once. They were none other than Yaryulhan and the Three Evils, Blood Wheel Calamity, and Cha Hwan.

Among them, Yaryulhan, who stood in the center, stepped over the broken wall and came inside.

He silently looked down at the Five Thunder Divine Sword, now cut in half. A cold chill that could freeze the heart flowed from his body, but no emotion at all dwelled in his indifferent eyes or expression.

“Cha Hwan, as you wished, there is no longer any need for a gift.”

Chapter 235: If the World Can't Kill Me.
----------------------------------------
Five people came over the broken wall behind Yelü Han.

The one who entered at once and stood beside Yelü Han was Cha Hwan, his body covered in dragon tattoos. His expression hardened when he saw the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s gruesome corpse.

He had said the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng was wasted on him, but he knew better than anyone what kind of expert the Five Thunder Divine Sword was.

Who in the world could kill the Five Thunder Divine Sword like this?

And that was not the only corpse. His disciple, Sahyo, was dead as well. Truly, if this had not been a gathering with Yelü Han, the Three Evils, and Blood Wheel Calamity all present, he would have fled without even looking back.

Cha Hwan gauged Yelü Han’s mood. As always, he was staring at the enemy with a face devoid of all emotion.

Beside him stood Blood Wheel Calamity.

Blood Wheel Calamity was an old man as gaunt as a skeleton, with a huge iron disk fitted with sawtoothed blades strapped to his back. It looked heavy at a glance, and that disk was none other than Blood Wheel Calamity’s signature weapon, the Blood Wheel, known to smash any weapon to pieces.

Finally, Cha Hwan’s gaze turned to the Three Evils.

They were identical triplets. Their faces were the same, and their clothes were exactly alike. They were so identical that it looked as if one person had split into three.

Cha Hwan, who knew the things the Three Evils had done, always shuddered when he saw them. Grotesque atrocities no human being could ever commit.

And yet the Three Evils were almost unknown in the murim. Because they had killed, without exception, every single person connected to their evil deeds. That was why seeing them did not feel like three villains gathered together, but like evil itself made three times more vicious.

In any case, with even them here, today’s opponent would not leave this place alive.

Cha Hwan stepped forward on everyone’s behalf.

“Who are you bastards?”

Then the Three Evils recognized him and answered at the same time.

“Geugak Soma.”

When the three of them answered in perfect unison, as if they had rehearsed it, it gave off an uncanny feeling.

Yelü Han, too, asked calmly, as though he had long since recognized Geugak Soma.

“The Cult Leader would not have given permission. Is this your own independent action?”

Geugak Soma gave no answer.

This time as well, Geom Mugeuk was in charge of the conversation.

“Does wearing a white mask make anyone Geugak Soma? We aren’t people from the Demon Cult.”

“Then who are you?”

“When a bastard who’s eaten ninety years starts raving like a madman because he wants to grow young, King Yama in hell orders him brought in. That bastard will be suffering for the next ninety thousand years.”

Geom Mugeuk swept his gaze over them and did not forget to add one more remark.

“The sentient beings over there also have faces that say they’ve sinned plenty. How about it? Let’s board the boat to hell together. There happen to be six seats left.”

Yelü Han’s expression remained unchanged, but his eyes grew colder.

“Very well. If you have the skill to kill the Five Thunder Divine Sword, you may be this arrogant.”

Yelü Han did not send his subordinates out one by one to die. The enemies were men who had killed the Five Thunder Divine Sword and his disciple.

He stepped forward at the head and said,

“We kill them together!”

The six experts formed their formation at once.

Geom Mugeuk had a premonition.

This would be the hardest battle he had ever fought, before or after his regression.

Geom Mugeuk drew the Black Demon Sword with a refreshing ring and said,

“Fine. If the world can’t kill you, we will.”

Geugak Soma’s eyes were burning blue. The stronger the enemy, the more frenzied Soma became. Geugak Soma looked at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“One more time!”

Geom Mugeuk understood what he meant, so without another word he extended the Black Demon Sword.

Claaang!

This sound was different from the usual bright, crisp ring. Just as the enemy had changed, and just as the relationship between the two of them had changed, a deeper resonance spread outward.

Both sides strode toward each other.

At least in this moment, among everyone gathered here, not one person was afraid.

* * *

Go Wol, who had been eating, ran out and vomited up what he had eaten.

Cult Leader Pungcheon followed him out and patted his back.

“It’s my fault for forcing you to eat.”

Since yesterday, Go Wol had been so tense that he had not eaten anything, and Pungcheon had forced a meal on him.

“Cult Leader, I want to hear the sound of the Yin Thunder Bell.”

At Go Wol’s words, Cult Leader Pungcheon snapped for no reason.

“Aren’t you sick of it? You were bound to that bell for so long!”

Thinking of that incident, Cult Leader Pungcheon had much that pricked his conscience.

“I’m not saying it to make you feel sorry, Cult Leader. I really want to hear that bell. When I listened to the sound of the Yin Thunder Bell, my heart felt much calmer.”

“You don’t mean you want to talk with the demons carved into the bell?”

“I do miss them a little too.”

“Dream on. You’ll never see or hear them again.”

Ssssss.

When their surroundings changed, the two were standing in a field. He had used the Space-Time Transposition Art to bring them to the field Go Wol liked.

“I wasn’t going to come here anymore.”

“Then why did you open it?”

“Because you looked too anxious.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon looked up at the distant sky.

“They must be in the thick of fighting by now, right?”

“Probably.”

“Should I go and help?”

Go Wol shook his head.

“Leave it to those two. This is their fight.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon looked at the faraway sky and said,

“It’s a fight for three people.”

“What do you mean?”

“You’re fighting right now too, aren’t you? Even starving yourself.”

“If you put it that way, are you not fighting too, Cult Leader? The Poison King, the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion, Inspector Seo. The people waiting in the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Everyone is fighting together.”

At that, Cult Leader Pungcheon suddenly said,

“Don’t worry. My disciple won’t lose.”

“Disciple?”

“There’s something like that.”

Cult Leader Pungcheon shouted as if Geom Mugeuk were right before his eyes.

“You bastard! After taking that much from me, you’d better not lose! Don’t you dare try to die!”

He had even passed on the Shicheon Secret Art, after all.

He had joked around to ease Go Wol’s mood a little, but Go Wol was still full of worry.

Go Wol looked at the brilliantly clear sky and the vast field and said,

“Will a peaceful day like this ever come to the murim?”

“It mustn’t!”

“Why?”

“Because you’d be the first to lose your job.”

In the end, Go Wol laughed. In this situation where he could not even swallow water, Pungcheon had made him laugh. Fine, Cult Leader, you win.

“Today is going to be a long day.”

* * *

Yelü Han attacked together with the others, but he did not turn it into a chaotic melee.

They stayed tightly grouped and fought as if they were one person. They had judged themselves to be at an advantage and were deliberately avoiding any situation that might create variables.

That was why Geom Mugeuk decided he had to kill Yelü Han first. He was the smartest among them, and if their leader was broken, the morale of the rest would naturally break as well.

But the problem was that if Geom Mugeuk charged in to kill Yelü Han, the other five might not help Yelü Han and instead attack Geugak Soma together.

If that happened, could Geugak Soma withstand the combined assault of those five? If he could, for how long? The problem was that they did not know the exact level of those identical triplets.

Whirrrrrrrr!

The first to attack was Blood Wheel Calamity. The Blood Wheel he hurled flew toward Geom Mugeuk with tremendous force.

It was not only large, but fast as well.

The power contained in the Blood Wheel was too great to deflect with a sword or inner force. Of course, the Black Demon Sword would not break, but facing that heavy Blood Wheel with a sword was far too inefficient.

Whirrrrr!

As if it were alive, the Blood Wheel circled around and attacked again.

When Geom Mugeuk dodged to the left, the Blood Wheel changed direction and flew toward the side he had evaded to.

Geom Mugeuk hastily twisted his body the other way, and the Blood Wheel grazed past his face. The blades attached to the Blood Wheel were so sharp that they looked as though they could slice a human body cleanly apart.

Blood Wheel Calamity retrieved the Blood Wheel and said with a mocking smile,

“Soon you’ll be dancing with the Blood Wheel.”

It meant he could move the Blood Wheel as he pleased.

At times like this, rather than struggling to avoid the Blood Wheel, one had to kill Blood Wheel Calamity himself.

The problem was that they expected that too.

Ping!

The one who blocked the Blood Anguish Finger shot toward Blood Wheel Calamity was First Evil. Second Evil and Third Evil also stayed clustered together, protecting Blood Wheel Calamity. Then, when an opportunity arose, they would attack together.

Seeing First Evil’s movement, Geom Mugeuk thought of the Five Thunder Divine Sword’s disciple, Sahyo. His skill was on Sahyo’s level. In that case, it was as if there were three Sahyos. And since they were twins, their joint-attack technique would be outstanding. They were no ordinary opponents.

Geom Mugeuk made an instinctive decision about whom to kill first.

If Yelü Han was impossible, then while they at least thought they were not his first target.

—Let’s kill the twins first.

—Understood.

Geugak Soma followed Geom Mugeuk’s decision without a word.

Whirrrrrrrr!

The second Blood Wheel flew in again. The moment it brushed past and came circling back, Geom Mugeuk charged toward First Evil.

Kwaaaaaaa!

At the same time, Geugak Soma unleashed the Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm.

Its target was Geom Mugeuk’s back as he charged toward First Evil!

It was as if Geom Mugeuk were rushing in while driving the Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm behind him like an army.

Because he had never expected such an unorthodox attack, First Evil was flustered. Moreover, Geom Mugeuk’s body blocked his view, so for an instant he did not know what to do.

Clang!

The moment they exchanged a single sword blow, the Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm swept over that place.

Geom Mugeuk slipped out with Flash Step and charged toward Second Evil on the right.

First Evil, who had been facing Geom Mugeuk, threw himself to the left a step too late.

The Black Demon Sword cut toward Second Evil.

Flash!

It was the fifth form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Azure Heaven Form, a swift-sword technique.

Kaaang!

The sound of sword striking sword!

The one who blocked the Azure Heaven Form was Yelü Han. He had seen through Geom Mugeuk’s intent to face First Evil and then kill Second Evil.

Their gazes met between the clashing swords.

A cold chill that seemed to freeze the heart flowed from Yelü Han’s sword.

The sword art he had mastered was the Primordial Light-Splitting Sword Art, based on extreme yin inner force.

Clang clang clang clang clang clang!

The sword lights created by the two men’s blades flashed through the air.

“Urgh!”

At that moment, the one who let out a short scream and collapsed was First Evil.

Because Yelü Han had blocked him, Geom Mugeuk had failed to kill Second Evil, but Geugak Soma had killed First Evil.
