Earlier, Geom Mugeuk had evaded with Flash Step, and First Evil, who had dodged a step too late, had one arm swept away by the Demonic Extreme Berserk Palm. Geugak Soma did not miss that chance and attacked tenaciously until he finally killed him.

When he died, Second Evil and Third Evil went berserk.

Swish! Swish!

Sword qi flew continuously toward Geugak Soma.

The stable formation collapsed, and the melee Geom Mugeuk wanted began.

Cha Hwan, whose loyalty was high, jumped into the fight to help Yelü Han.

He thrust his sword at Geom Mugeuk’s back, but Geom Mugeuk instead used that attack as an opportunity. As he evaded the attack from behind and the swords of the three men tangled for an instant.

This time, he used Shadow Step. In an instant, Geom Mugeuk vanished from the sight of Yelü Han and Cha Hwan. It was the power of the Four Steps of the Wind God brought to great completion.

The moment Yelü Han, thinking he might be the target, sharpened his nerves!

Geom Mugeuk was stabbing someone else in the back.

It was Second Evil, who had been charging madly to kill Geugak Soma.

For this single move, Shadow Step and Nether King Step were unleashed in succession, and in that moment Geom Mugeuk had become the god of wind.

Swiiiiish!

Once again, the Azure Heaven Form was unleashed.

This time, Yelü Han could not block the attack for him.

Slice!

Second Evil’s back was split in two, and he died on the spot.

At that moment, Geugak Soma was sending finger winds at Third Evil in succession.

Ping! Piiing!

Geom Mugeuk’s fierce sword qi was added to Geugak Soma’s Blood Anguish Finger.

It was the fourth form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Flame Heaven Form, the sword-qi emission technique.

After launching the sword qi, Geom Mugeuk turned around and drew up his protective qi.

The incoming Blood Wheel struck his chest.

Kwaaaaang!

Skrrrk!

Geom Mugeuk was pushed backward together with the flying Blood Wheel, then was flung away and rolled across the ground.

“I got him!”

Blood Wheel Calamity shouted in triumph.

“Damn bastard! How dare you run wild in front of me!”

Geugak Soma threw himself forward and landed before Geom Mugeuk.

Blood Wheel Calamity smiled in satisfaction and said to him,

“He’s already dead. No one has ever been struck by Blood Wheel Calamity and survived…”

At that instant, Blood Wheel Calamity stopped speaking.

Geom Mugeuk had risen to his feet like a ghost.

“Here’s someone who survived.”

Geugak Soma turned his head and asked with his eyes. Are you all right?

Geom Mugeuk rubbed his chest and nodded.

His chest ached as if it would burst, but fortunately he had avoided fatal internal injuries. With the Heavenly Demon Protective Art, the Ghost Tiger Robe, supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm silk, and protective qi all activated, even the Blood Wheel had failed to kill Geom Mugeuk.

Blood was soaking Geugak Soma’s arm. While he had been dealing with Third Evil, the sword qi Yelü Han sent out had nearly cut off his arm. Judging by the blood flowing out, the wound was not shallow.

This time, Geom Mugeuk asked with his eyes if he was all right, and this time Geugak Soma nodded.

On the other hand, thanks to the two of them taking those risks, Third Evil too lay fallen as a corpse. He had been unable to avoid the Blood Anguish Finger and Flame Heaven Form that flew in together.

Though they had been wounded, they had removed the greatest variable in this battle.

The most shocked person now was Blood Wheel Calamity. He could not believe that someone had taken a direct hit from his Blood Wheel and was fine.

“Who are you?”

His voice trembled.

He was not the only one shocked. Cha Hwan too was staring at Geom Mugeuk with stunned eyes.

Not only was there the footwork that had made him vanish in an instant right before his and Yelü Han’s eyes, he could not believe that the Three Evils, in whom he had placed such faith, were dead.

‘The Three Evils are dead? Those terrifying Three Evils? Those demon-like Three Evils?’

He could not accept that the corpses sprawled on the ground were the Three Evils.

But the method the opponent had used just now was the kind of attack that not only the Three Evils, but even Ten Evils would have been forced to suffer.

For the first time, emotion appeared on Yelü Han’s face.

When the battle did not go according to his will, the corners of his mouth curled upward, and he gave a self-mocking sneer.

Geom Mugeuk let the Black Demon Sword, dripping blood, hang at his side and said to him,

“That’s right. Life is something that makes you smile bitterly several times a day, but you’ve had it far too easy until now, haven’t you?”

Chapter 236: That's Why This Fight Is Difficult.
------------------------------------------------
Clatter.

The bowl fell to the floor and shattered.

The Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion silently looked down at the broken bowl.

“Are you all right?”

Seo Daeryong, who had been bringing ingredients into the kitchen, asked in surprise.

“Yes, I’m fine.”

When she tried to pick up the shards, Seo Daeryong stepped forward and stopped her.

“Please leave it. I’ll clean it up.”

This was a safe house of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. The owners of the pleasure houses they had rescued from Yeobulgae were being kept here for the time being. Under the care of the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion, they had regained much of their composure.

The Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion had been bright all along, but today, her expression was dark.

“Has something happened?”

After a brief pause, she answered.

“I had a bad dream.”

Seo Daeryong had heard from Geom Mugeuk that she was no ordinary woman. He knew he could not dismiss her dream as nonsense, but pretending not to know, he said to her,

“Don’t they say dreams mean the opposite?”

“Do they?”

She forced a smile as she spoke, but her expression remained dark. Perhaps those ominous dreams of hers had always come true.

Then Seo Daeryong suddenly said,

“It won’t work.”

“Pardon?”

“I heard from the Pavilion Lord that you are no ordinary person. So I know it may be rude of me to say this. Still, I will say it. That bad dream won’t work on our Pavilion Lord. And if it doesn’t work on the Pavilion Lord, it won’t work on Lord Soma either.”

Seo Daeryong crouched down and began gathering the broken pieces of the bowl.

“My life before I met the Second Young Master was like this broken bowl. I was ugly, gloomy, and crooked in character.”

His face was reflected in one of the shards he had picked up. Seo Daeryong thought the distorted figure, barely recognizable in the fragment, was what he had been like in the past.

“No one considered me important, and I thought of myself as worthless too. But after meeting the Pavilion Lord, my life changed. In someone else’s eyes, I may still be nothing but a broken bowl, but to myself, I believe I am a vessel that must surely be useful somewhere.”

Seo Daeryong finished collecting the shards and rose to his feet.

“To tell the truth, I was being modest just now. I think I am more than that. The Second Young Master put the broken bowl back together and turned me into an expensive porcelain vessel.”

He had become the disciple of the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, and perhaps he might even become the next Lord of Hwangcheon Pavilion.

“He is someone who can make even a life like mine into this. So bad dreams and the like won’t work on the Second Young Master.”

At last, a smile appeared on the face of the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion.

“Yes. They won’t work.”

Seo Daeryong came outside carrying the broken bowl. The Poison King was digging up the ground in a corner of the yard. Seeing him digging around a uniquely shaped flower, it seemed poisonous snakes or insects must be living around it.

Seo Daeryong was usually too afraid of the Poison King to speak to him, but today, he approached him.

“The Pavilion Mistress had a bad dream. Her dreams tend to come true.”

Then the Poison King said without looking at Seo Daeryong,

“When we return to the Cult, I’ll drive the carriage, so you may go help them if you wish.”

“Ah, dreams don’t always come true, I suppose.”

There was one thing Seo Daeryong found strange.

Why had Geom Mugeuk not asked the Poison King to go with him in the first place?

Was it to protect the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion and the rescued women? That was important too, but the fight against Yaryul Han was a matter of life and death.

It was just then. As if he had read Seo Daeryong’s mind, the Poison King suddenly said,

“You’re curious, aren’t you? Why the Second Young Master did not ask for my help?”

“Yes, to be honest, I am.”

The Poison King answered while intently examining an insect in the ground.

“Because his only purpose is not to kill Yaryul Han.”

Seo Daeryong asked with a startled expression,

“There is another purpose?”

The Poison King looked at Seo Daeryong. Seo Daeryong did not dare meet those clear eyes, which looked far younger than his own.

“You’re his right arm, are you not? Then you should know.”

“I’m a right arm with a poor head.”

“I heard you entered Hwangcheon Pavilion at the top of your class.”

Seo Daeryong was shocked.

“The Pavilion Lord even told you that?”

“Your Pavilion Lord does have quite a lot to say.”

For no reason, Seo Daeryong felt pleased, and his chest swelled with pride.

“So? What do you think?”

After a brief pause, Seo Daeryong said,

“I am someone who is only ever amazed by what the Pavilion Lord does, so how could I know what lies within him? But while watching him, there are times when I think this. Why is he trying to go this far? Why is he trying to become this strong?”

Seo Daeryong continued as he watched the Poison King, who was still digging something out.

“Is he perhaps looking toward something beyond becoming the successor? Sometimes I get that feeling. Just where is the Pavilion Lord looking?”

The Poison King turned back to him and asked,

“Why are you asking me that?”

“Ah! You’re right. My apologies.”

Seo Daeryong bowed politely, then hurriedly withdrew.

“I will go help the Pavilion Mistress prepare the meal.”

Seo Daeryong ran into the building.

The Poison King seemed lost in thought for a moment, then began digging up the ground again.

* * *

Yaryul Han was confused.

When he first saw the corpse of the Five Thunder Divine Sword, he had thought he had been killed by a blade sent by the leader of the Sado Alliance. But astonishingly, it had been the work of Geukak Soma. Along with an unidentified young master.

‘The Alliance Leader joined hands with the Demon Cult?’

He, too, could not understand it. The Sado Alliance Leader he knew was not the kind of man who would join hands with the Demon Cult, no matter how much he wanted to get rid of him.

‘Then the Demon Cult intervened on its own, without the Alliance Leader’s involvement? But why?’

When conducting various businesses in the murim, clashes with the Demon Cult could happen. But there had never been a conflict serious enough to risk their lives like this.

Yaryul Han’s gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk. The movements the other man had shown while disposing of the Three Evils earlier had truly been an astonishing display of divine might. He was this young, and yet his movements were better than Geukak Soma’s? How could that be?

“I have never heard that the Demon Cult had someone like you.”

Yaryul Han had not realized Geom Mugeuk’s identity. Of course, as the second-in-command of the Sado Alliance, he could hear all sorts of information sent up to the alliance by spies. Naturally, that information included the fact that the Demon Cult’s Second Young Master had recently begun to distinguish himself.

But at this moment, the thought that the young man before him was the Demon Cult’s Second Young Master did not occur to him. First of all, his martial arts were far too outstanding for his age, and the son of the Heavenly Demon would never personally undertake something this dangerous.

He thought he must be a secret weapon raised by the Demon Cult. Or else a master of the Demon Cult who had undergone rejuvenation.

“I told you, we’re not from the Demon Cult. We are grim reapers specially sent by the King of Hell.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s mockery, an aura like a cold blizzard surged from Yaryul Han’s entire body.

This was Yaryul Han’s presence. It froze the whole body with cold, and when fear was added to this aura, the opponent would die without even being able to lift a hand.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk did not stop provoking him.

“With cold like this, maybe the fires of hell will feel a little less hot when you enter them?”

The fact that he continued to provoke him even in this urgent situation meant he acknowledged his opponent that much. Just from Yaryul Han’s aura, he could tell his skill. The outcome would be decided by a single choice, a single mistake.

Then Yaryul Han said,

“I should take you with me. If someone as irritating as you is there, I feel like even the pits of fire might go out.”

Cha Hwan, who had fallen back because his martial arts were comparatively weak, was startled.

It was the first time he had seen it. Yaryul Han saying something like that to an opponent. He could see for himself that the greenhorn was strong, but was he really to that extent? If it was such an evenly matched fight…

‘I must play a major role.’

His heart pounded quickly. The desire to achieve merit somehow took hold of him. He would watch for an opportunity and leap in. At the decisive moment!

This time, Blood Wheel Calamity chuckled and said to Geukak Soma,

“I never thought my Blood Wheel would get to taste Geukak Soma’s blood.”

Geukak Soma showed no reaction at all, and Blood Wheel Calamity was somewhat embarrassed by this complete disregard.

“Arrogant Demon Cult bastards, all of you.”

He was confident he could defeat Geukak Soma. He, too, had noticed that the wound Geukak Soma had suffered from Yaryul Han’s earlier surprise attack was deep. In terms of original skill, he was one step below a Demon Venerable, but in this situation, he had enough chance of victory.

Moreover, Geukak Soma had expended more internal energy. He had used his internal force earlier while facing the Five Thunder Divine Sword and his disciple as well.

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze turned toward Geukak Soma’s arm, stained red. The fact that blood was seeping out even though he had pressed his acupoints to stop the bleeding meant the wound was deep.

‘We have to end this fight as quickly as possible.’

But if he fought hastily because of that, Yaryul Han would kill him. His heart was urgent, but he could not show it. He had to put forth everything he had, yet end the fight as quickly as possible. That was why this battle was difficult.

“By the way, if you mess with the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, your Alliance Leader won’t like it. Will that be all right?”

Geom Mugeuk threw out words meant to shake the hearts of his enemies.

Then Yaryul Han asked,

“Didn’t you say you weren’t from the Demon Cult?”

“As if. If not us, who would try to get rid of you?”

“Why is the Demon Cult trying to kill me?”

“I’ll tell you when you die.”

“You die first. So tell me now.”

“I don’t want to.”

For the first time in a very long while, Yaryul Han felt the emotion called irritation. In his life, no one had dared irritate him. Even if there had been someone, they would already be lying as a corpse.

But soon, Yaryul Han brought his emotions under control.

“Fine. I’ll hear it when you die.”

Seeing his calm gaze, Geom Mugeuk also steadied his mind. Provocation had no effect on Yaryul Han whatsoever. He had no choice but to kill him with skill alone.

Whirrrrrrrrr!

The Blood Wheel sliced through the air, and the battle between Geukak Soma and Blood Wheel Calamity began.

Geukak Soma lightly avoided the Blood Wheel as it flew swiftly through the air.

At the same time, Geukak Soma launched himself toward Blood Wheel Calamity.

Screeetch!

Shiiing!

At that moment, two streaks of sword energy tore through the air at the same time.

One was Yaryul Han’s sword energy, meant to stop Geukak Soma’s approach, and the other was Geom Mugeuk’s sword energy, meant to neutralize it.

Boom!

The instant the two sword energies collided in midair!

Piing!

Geukak Soma unleashed a finger wind, and the returning Blood Wheel tried to strike the back of Geukak Soma’s head.

Geukak Soma twisted his body and avoided the Blood Wheel, and Blood Wheel Calamity likewise threw himself aside to avoid the Blood Ang Finger.

The tense first exchange ended, and for a moment, tension flowed through the place. It was a razor-thin contest whose outcome could not be known.

Geom Mugeuk made his judgment.

‘A fight like this is disadvantageous.’

Because Yaryul Han could abandon Blood Wheel Calamity at any time, but he could never abandon Geukak Soma.

The moment he reached that conclusion, Geom Mugeuk flew toward Yaryul Han.

He decided not to concern himself with Geukak Soma’s injury. From the beginning, worrying about him had been the wrong thing to do. He had to trust him.

And if he truly wanted to help Geukak Soma, then instead of offering half-hearted concern, he had to focus on his own fight and kill Yaryul Han as quickly as possible. He would make sure there would be no more occasions for him to dare send sword energy at Geukak Soma.

Clang clang clang clang clang clang!

The sword lights created by Geom Mugeuk and Yaryul Han flashed through the air so quickly they could not be seen.

The two of them were truly fast. The two who had clashed on the left were, before anyone knew it, exchanging swords on the right. Their movements were as though they were freely traversing space itself.

Geom Mugeuk’s pure white sword light and Yaryul Han’s deep blue sword light collided countless times.

Kakakakakang-kang-kang!

The two struck sword against sword and soared into the air. The sword lights flashing in the sky were like fireworks being set off for this astonishing battle.

They leapt down from midair and fought while running across the rooftops. Even as they ran, their swords clashed endlessly.

The sword light continued to flash until they dropped from the roof onto the top of a wall.

The clash between the Flying Heaven Sword Art, mastered to the twelfth stage, and the Primordial Dividing Light Sword Art, also mastered to the twelfth stage, was truly a match between dragon and tiger.

As the fierce battle continued, Geom Mugeuk instantly injected more internal energy into the Black Demon Sword.

At the unexpected force, blood burst from Yaryul Han’s mouth.

But before those droplets of blood could fall to the ground, a sword flew in, cutting through them.

Paaak!

Blood sprayed from Geom Mugeuk’s side.

Injecting more internal energy in the midst of that rapid, fierce battle had been a strain, and a forced attack always demanded a price. As expected, his skill was worthy of the second-in-command of the Sado Alliance.

They were neither surprised nor moved by each other. They focused only on the fight. From the exchange just now, they knew that the moment their attention strayed elsewhere, they would die.

At the very instant the distance between the two briefly widened!

Yaryul Han’s first secret technique was unleashed.

Sut-sut-sut-sut-sut-sut!

Yaryul Han’s form split into ten and surrounded Geom Mugeuk. This clone technique was the Ten Illusory Soul-Dividing Art, one of the essences of the Primordial Dividing Light Sword Art.

Sharararararak.

At the same time, thirty-six sword-shaped strands of sword energy surrounded Geom Mugeuk.

It was the seventh form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Flowing Heaven Form. It was not the old Flowing Heaven Form. What had originally split apart in front of his chest was now formed into a circle around Geom Mugeuk’s body.

The martial art he had mastered to the twelfth stage was ripening further and further, revealing a new realm.

The moment the illusions charged all at once, ten of the floating strands of sword energy shot out in every direction.

Swish swish swish swish swish swish swish swish!

Puff puff puff puff puff puff puff!

The sword energies and illusions collided and vanished, and the real Yaryul Han swung his sword to neutralize the sword energy that flew at him.

After the illusions were eliminated, twenty-six strands of sword energy remained!

As if hidden weapons were being launched from a mechanism, the sword energies shot toward Yaryul Han all at once.

Swish swish swish swish swish swish swish swish!

Cha Hwan, who was watching, gaped. Since the day he was born, he had never seen nor heard of such divine might.

And what received that divine might was another divine might.

Boom boom boom kwa-kwa-boom kwa-boom!

The flying sword energies were blocked by something and collided against it.

It was a sword wall. Not an ordinary sword wall. It was a wall of sword energy as cold as an ice wall, the Blue-Cold Ice Wall, another essence of the Primordial Dividing Light Sword Art.

Srrrrrr.

The wall of sword energy disappeared as if snow were melting, and beyond it, Yaryul Han came into view.

His eyes were shining a fiendish blue like an evil spirit, and he was emitting a vicious killing aura.

At his presence, which declared that the real fight began now, the Black Demon Sword began to hum at length.

Wuuuuuuuu!

Geom Mugeuk gripped the Black Demon Sword tightly and walked toward that vicious killing aura.

‘My friends, hold on just a little longer!’

It was something he said to both of his friends.

Chapter 237: The One Who Sent Me.
---------------------------------
Yelü Han released the whole of his aura.

Whiiiiiiish!

A truly bitter wind blew. It was a chill that seemed as if it would freeze one’s lungs upon drawing breath. And lodged within that chill was an evil qi of the foulest kind. That immense evil qi seemed to say:

You dare try to challenge me?

But Geom Mugeuk did not stop walking as he stared into Yelü Han’s eyes.

“It may have worked on everyone else, but it won’t work on me.”

Geom Mugeuk also drew his aura up to the limit and released it.

The two men’s auras collided.

Yelü Han was shocked by Geom Mugeuk’s aura.

Aura was not powerful simply because one possessed abundant internal energy. Aura was tied to the totality of a martial artist’s nature and qualities, his temperament and values, the martial arts he had learned. In that sense, aura touched upon the very essence of the martial artist.

And the aura Yelü Han now felt from Geom Mugeuk was something he had never experienced even once before.

The first impression of Geom Mugeuk’s aura was soft and refreshing. It felt like gazing up at an endlessly vast, blue sky.

In truth, Yelü Han was standing alone amid that blueness. All around him was blue sky. The sky, the walls, the floor.

He reached out his hand. Splash. In that instant, he realized it. This was not the sky, but water so clear it was almost unbearable. What he had been looking at was the sky reflected upon the water.

An invisible force pulled Yelü Han down into the water.

He continued to sink. When Yelü Han looked below, he could not see the bottom. The terror of the deep sea seemed to seize his heart.

‘A brat that young has an aura like this?’

Geom Mugeuk, walking toward him, felt like a giant.

Geom Mugeuk, in turn, was facing an overwhelming aura.

He was walking along a snow-covered road in a raging blizzard. He could not see ahead, nor could he see the end. It felt as if he were walking alone across a snowy plain in the midst of terrible cold.

But Geom Mugeuk was not afraid, nor was he lonely. He simply moved forward in silence. It was a road he had walked before. This brutally lonely road was the road he had walked his entire life.

When he passed through the fierce snowstorm, Yelü Han stood beneath a clear sky.

Yelü Han, too, had barely managed to swim up from the endless depths and stood before Geom Mugeuk.

Yelü Han asked in a trembling voice.

“Who are you?”

“I am…”

Geom Mugeuk added in a whisper.

“I told you I’d tell you when you died, didn’t I?”

Shaaaaaak!

Geom Mugeuk’s sword cut through the air in a sudden strike.

Kaaang!

Yelü Han barely blocked it. Absorbed by Geom Mugeuk’s aura, he had almost lost his head.

At the fear that he might die, Yelü Han grew excited. Joy came before anger. Because he had never experienced such a maddening fight before.

Kakakakakang!

The two men’s swords began clashing again, sending up flashes of sword-light.

Their attacks were even more powerful than before, but neither man gave an inch.

Geom Mugeuk knew well where Yelü Han’s strength came from. Spiritual medicines bought with money he had scraped together by using the Four Men, the results of all manner of evil deeds that had driven countless people to their deaths—those were what had accumulated in his dantian.

Kaaaaang!

Sword met sword.

Beyond the blades, Yelü Han glared as if to kill him and shouted.

“Who in the world raised a monster like you?”

“You did!”

“What?”

“I was born to devour bastards like you.”

Killing intent entered Geom Mugeuk’s gaze.

For an instant, Yelü Han saw an illusion. Within a black form like a shadow, only a pair of crimson eyes glared at him.

More strength entered Yelü Han’s hand around his sword.

“Then devour me if you can!”

Now emotion was showing on Yelü Han’s face.

Anger, fear, excitement…

Once the dam of emotion he had held back collapsed, every kind of feeling came pouring out.

In that instant, he unleashed tremendous power.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwarrung!

Yelü Han’s immense killing intent became an avalanche and swept down upon Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk’s Heavenly Demon Protective Art activated, preventing his body from shrinking back before the killing intent flying toward him.

Had it not, the attack now coming at him would have been utterly lethal.

Shaaaaaaaak!

It was Yelü Han’s sword qi. It was different from all the sword qi he had shown until now. It was a blow that carried the illusion of a fiend made of ice flying in with a sword in hand.

Geom Mugeuk did not dodge, but unleashed the fourth form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Flame Heaven Form.

It was because Yelü Han’s attack was a dynamic sword qi that seemed alive; if he dodged carelessly, it felt as though it would follow him to the end and strike true.

Two rough, tyrannical sword qi tore through the air and collided.

Kwaaaang!

The impact sent both men sliding backward.

Surprise filled Yelü Han’s expression. He blocked even that attack?

Geom Mugeuk answered with his expression as well. I told you it wouldn’t work!

Hwirururururu!

Ping!

Between the two men, who had briefly drawn apart, a blood wheel and finger wind crossed past each other. The battle between Geugak Soma and Hyeollyun Geop was still continuing.

He could feel that the power of the blood wheel had noticeably fallen. But Geom Mugeuk could not be pleased. He had seen blood on the blood wheel.

Geom Mugeuk did not spare Geugak Soma even a single glance.

The moment he was caught being conscious of him and worrying for him, a fatal weakness would be exposed.

That was why, since the fight began, he had not once looked toward their battle. Not that he had the leisure to do so.

It was then that Cha Hwan leapt in.

At the very moment when Geom Mugeuk and Yelü Han had exchanged great attacks and both slid backward, he judged that this was the chance to attack. All the while he had been watching, he had waited for this one instant.

Shiiiik!

Just as he thrust his sword toward Geom Mugeuk in a surprise attack—

Puaaak!

Cha Hwan’s chest split open, and something flew out like light. It was as if a hidden weapon he had kept in his chest had been launched.

Blood spurted from Geom Mugeuk’s arm. Had he not barely avoided it with Flicker Step, his chest might have been pierced through by that terrifying attack.

Cha Hwan stared blankly down at the hole blasted through his chest.

The sword qi Yelü Han had unleashed from behind had pierced through Cha Hwan’s chest and flown toward Geom Mugeuk.

Yelü Han was merciless. He had used the instant when Cha Hwan blocked his line of sight as an opportunity.

Cha Hwan had never imagined it. That the man to whom he had devoted such loyalty would use and discard him in this way.

‘What is this?’

Cha Hwan was too deeply shocked.

Others had only a small tattoo on their chests, but he had covered his whole body with them. He had served him without taking a single day off. If Yelü Han had at least sent him a sound transmission telling him to sacrifice himself, he would have gladly been willing to give his life for him.

‘This isn’t right, is it?’

Yes, those who used others always did things this way.

Still, dying this miserably was embarrassing before the dragon carved into his body, wasn’t it? With the final thought that even if Yelü Han had used a broom left standing in a corner, he would not have used it so worthlessly, Cha Hwan breathed his last.

At the very moment Cha Hwan’s breath ceased, another contest was being decided.

Even then, Geugak Soma and Hyeollyun Geop were still fighting with all their strength.

At first, Hyeollyun Geop had chosen a long battle. He had the advantage in internal energy, and his opponent was even injured.

‘If I hold out, I win!’

He did not fling his blood wheels recklessly, but focused on blocking the Blood Calamity Finger.

But the longer the fight went on, the more he unknowingly became immersed in it. There were two reasons.

The first was an inexplicable heat.

When he looked at the icy-blue gleam pouring from the eyes behind the mask, his internal energy surged without his realizing it, and his blood flowed faster. Before he knew it, he was hurling his blood wheels at Geugak Soma.

The second was the clear gap in skill.

Geugak Soma never moved according to his opponent’s intentions. A long battle? Who decided that?

If Geugak Soma had not suffered internal injuries, the fight would have already been over. But his circulation of internal force was not smooth.

Hyeollyun Geop had certainly been confident he could win when the fight began. But as the battle continued, his fear only grew.

‘Just die already! You damned demon bastard!’

Hyeollyun Geop drove Geugak Soma back with the last of his strength.

Hwirururururururu!

Unleashing a hidden secret technique, the blood wheels danced a dance of death, and Geugak Soma’s movements grew more and more precarious.

‘That’s it! Just a little more!’

Hyeollyun Geop even brought out his final secret technique.

Hwiruru! Hwiruru!

Small blood wheels used like throwing stars cut through the air and joined the attack.

Just as the chaotic, dizzying, savage blood wheels were driving Geugak Soma into a corner—

Ping!

Puk!

The sound Geugak Soma had long been waiting for rang out.

Kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa-kwa!

The blood wheels that had embroidered the air as if alive plowed into the ground and flew onward, smashing through the wall before coming to a stop. The small blood wheels, too, were hurled to the ground here and there.

Hyeollyun Geop watched the sight with a startled face.

‘Why did I suddenly lose control?’

Then something trickled down his face.

Hyeollyun Geop absentmindedly wiped it with his hand.

It was blood. Blood was streaming down from his forehead.

Hyeollyun Geop lifted the small blood wheel in his hand and looked at his reflection in it. He saw himself with a hole pierced through his forehead.

‘When in the world?’

With that question as his last, Hyeollyun Geop’s lifeless body toppled forward.

Astonishingly, Geugak Soma had fired the Blood Calamity Finger through the tiny hole at the center of that blood wheel as it moved madly about. He had not missed the instant when that hole aligned perfectly with Hyeollyun Geop’s forehead. Hyeollyun Geop had never imagined such an attack, and because his own blood wheel had blocked his sight, he could not possibly have stopped it.

Geom Mugeuk and Yelü Han’s battle, too, was racing toward its peak.

When even the move that used Cha Hwan missed its mark, Yelü Han’s eyes changed. His black pupils vanished completely, and both his pupils and the whites of his eyes turned blue. They were not emitting a blue light; they had turned blue outright.

Geom Mugeuk sensed it. Yelü Han was about to unleash his final secret technique.

Considering the divine might he had shown until now, it was time for Geom Mugeuk to unleash his own final move as well.

The final sword form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art was unleashed.

It was the eighth form, the Return to Heaven Form, which he had never once used in real combat despite having achieved Great Completion at the twelfth level.

The Return to Heaven Form was a forty-four-hit chained technique.

It was powerful, but it had one fatal weakness. Its consumption of internal energy was far too great; it was a martial art that required nearly all of the internal energy one possessed.

Thus, if by any chance one failed to kill the opponent, one would be put in danger—a true final technique.

Not only Yelü Han’s eyes now, but his entire body began turning blue.

Primordial Chaos Extreme Cold!

It was the final secret technique of the Primordial Chaos Splitting Light Sword Art, drawing up every bit of energy in the body to slay the opponent. It was a secret technique that exerted power greater than one’s original ability, and it too was a final technique that could cost the user his life if it failed.

Geom Mugeuk unleashed the eighth form of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, the Return to Heaven Form.

Seuk! Seuuuk! Seuk!

With the sound of wind brushing past a hem, lines of the sword began to be drawn over Yelü Han’s body.

Emitting extreme cold, Yelü Han swung his sword and began to block. If he blocked the entirety of the Return to Heaven Form, Geom Mugeuk would die.

Saaak! Saak! Saaaak!

The speed at which the sword lines were drawn grew faster and faster.

Yelü Han’s sword did not fall behind and blocked the attacks.

Jiiing—

The Black Demon Sword cried out.

This was the first time the Black Demon Sword had rung while attacking.

Geom Mugeuk felt that the Black Demon Sword had become one with him, emitting demonic qi to protect him from the terrible evil qi Yelü Han gave off.

‘Yes, let us end him!’

Geom Mugeuk poured in all of his mind and strength.

Sasasasasak!

Chachachachachang!

Fifteenth strike, sixteenth strike, seventeenth strike…

Yelü Han blocked with vicious persistence.

Twenty-third strike, twenty-fourth strike, twenty-fifth strike…

“Is this all you have?”

Yelü Han even shouted.

The sword lines were drawn even faster.

Thirty-fourth strike, thirty-fifth strike, thirty-sixth strike…

Yelü Han was blocking attacks so fast they could not be seen with the secret technique of Primordial Chaos Extreme Cold.

If this continued to the forty-fourth strike, Geom Mugeuk would lose.

But Geom Mugeuk did not grow anxious and trusted himself. Yes, you must have worked hard to reach that position. But you could not have worked harder than I did!

Thirty-seventh strike, thirty-eighth strike, thirty-ninth strike…

At the moment they raced toward the end—

Puuuuk!

With the sound of flesh tearing, the flashing sword-light stopped. The sound of sword clashing against sword also ceased. It was the forty-second strike.

Geom Mugeuk’s sword was buried in Yelü Han’s heart. In the end, Yelü Han had failed to block Geom Mugeuk’s Return to Heaven Form.

Yelü Han’s body, which had turned blue, began returning to its original color.

Looking into Yelü Han’s eyes, filled with shock, anger, and fear, Geom Mugeuk spoke.

“The person who sent me was a father who had to watch his family die before his eyes.”

Yelü Han wore an expression that said he could not understand. He had likely thought he was dying because of some grand reason, like a plot by the leader of the Evil Alliance or a scheme of the Demon Cult.

And perhaps that made this moment an even greater punishment for him. I’m dying for that reason? It would feel even more unjust. He would die in even greater fury.

“Even though he saved your subordinate, you killed the physician’s entire family because he kept treating the patient he had been saving.”

In that instant, Yelü Han’s gaze wavered. It seemed as if he remembered, and also as if he was asking whether he was truly dying for such a reason—one he could hardly even recall.

“They said you had a principle that anyone who disobeyed your orders must die, didn’t they? This is the Demon Physician’s answer to that ridiculous principle of yours. It is the answer of a husband who lost his wife, and the answer of a father who lost his children! Go to hell!”

Puuuuk!

As the Black Demon Sword was pulled free, blood gushed from his heart like a fountain.

Chwaaaaaaaaa!

“Uwaaaaaaaaaaaah!”

Yelü Han let out a wretched scream as blood erupted from him.

His blood poured down like rain.

Geom Mugeuk did not avoid it. Drenched in the rain of blood, he slowly turned toward Geugak Soma.

Geugak Soma, who had finished his fight, was standing there looking this way.

Geom Mugeuk looked at him and smiled brightly.

“It’s all over, Lord Soma.”

The moment he took one step toward him—

Geugak Soma began to collapse slowly, as if crumbling.

Chapter 238: Now It's My Turn.
------------------------------
“Lord Soma!”

I threw myself toward Geukak Soma with Flash Step.

Fortunately, I was able to catch his form before he collapsed.

After laying him down comfortably, I injected a thread of internal energy into his chest and examined his insides. His qi and blood were blocked and twisted, leaving him with severe internal injuries.

It was more serious than I had thought. The moment I tried to pour internal energy into him for treatment, my heart sank. My inner power had been drained from using the Gwicheonsik.

I gathered what little inner power remained in my body as if wringing it out. A small amount of internal energy flowed into Geukak Soma’s body again. I tried carefully to open the blocked places, but I lacked the power. If he was left like this, his qi and blood would begin to run wild, and there would be no turning back.

Before I knew it, my heart was pounding and panic was rising inside me. So when I drew up every last bit of inner power I had left, blood surged up into my throat. My own body was far from normal, thanks to the injuries I had suffered fighting Yaryul Han and the exhaustion of my mental strength, but now was not the time to look after myself.

After I regulated his insides with all my strength, Geukak Soma regained consciousness and opened his eyes.

I spoke in my usual bright tone.

“That fight was incredible, wasn’t it?”

The two eyes behind the mask smiled. If not for Geukak Soma, I would never have achieved such a magnificent fight, nor such a magnificent result.

“…Yes, it truly was incredible.”

His voice was weak, unlike usual. He was sensing his own end.

“Second Young Master.”

“Yes, Lord Soma.”

“…I was glad to have met you, Second Young Master.”

The instant his eyes began to slip shut, I wrung out even the very limit of the internal energy I could squeeze forth and injected it into him. Like a single drop of water in the desert, that handful of internal energy precariously held on to his life.

“When I become the Heavenly Demon, you have to protect me.”

Geukak Soma’s eyes trembled. His gaze, filled with sorrow, spoke to me. That he was sorry. Sorry that he would have to leave without seeing me become the Heavenly Demon.

“You know my temper, don’t you? You know I’ll never give up on you, Lord Soma. Unless you intend to take me with you too, hold on. I can’t let you go like this. So come to your senses.”

Geukak Soma tried not to give up as well, but this was not a situation he could do anything about.

“If you die like this, I’ll blame myself for the rest of my life. So don’t die!”

At that moment, he coughed and spat blood. His qi and blood had begun to run wild. If I could not calm it down, he would die.

“No!”

At the thought that Geukak Soma might truly die, I felt as if I would go mad.

“Please! Lord Soma! Hold on!”

I wrung out my internal energy, then wrung it out again. All I could think of was saving Soma somehow. I had regressed so that I would not lose anyone… I could not lose Soma like this.

But no more internal energy remained in me, and Geukak Soma convulsed even more violently.

In that instant, my vision blurred. My eyes had filled with tears.

Had I ever cried before?

Then Geukak Soma grasped my hand. He must have realized it. That I cared for him this much. I realized it too. That I had cared for him this much.

Geukak Soma slowly lifted his hand and brought it to his mask.

He was trying to show me his face at the very end.

Within the eyeholes, Soma’s eyes were smiling. They smiled more brightly than any smile he had ever shown before.

I caught his hand and stopped him from taking off the mask.

“I won’t look! I won’t look at that face because I feel like you’ll be better-looking than me! Show me fifty years from now!”

Geukak Soma laughed, then vomited blood again.

With his last strength, he left me these words.

“Second Young Master… you will become a good Heavenly Demon.”

Even as life faded from him, he was smiling at me until the very end.

“I do not regret the time I spent with you, Second Young Master.”

For the first time, I prayed to the heavens.

Please let him live!

Even when I wandered the Central Plains unable to obtain the materials for the great art, even when I was so lonely and exhausted that I wanted to simply die, even when I was in danger and on the verge of death… I had never prayed to the heavens.

But now, for the first time in my life, I prayed.

Please! Let him live! Help me just this once!

It was at that very moment.

Thud.

As the dead Yaryul Han sat with his head drooping, something fell from his bosom to the floor.

It was a box.

Seeing what protruded from the open box, I ran toward it.

Astonishingly, it was a Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng. It seemed he had brought it as a birthday gift for the Five Thunder Divine Sword.

‘If it’s this!’

I brought it over and helped Geukak Soma sit up. Then I placed the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng in his mouth.

“Hold on with the energy of the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng! Just hold on until I complete one heavenly circulation!”

Under ordinary circumstances, it was a choice that should never be made. Taking a potent elixir like Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng while suffering severe internal injuries could instead ruin the body even more.

But this was the only way. I trusted the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng, and I trusted Geukak Soma.

“Lord Soma, chew and swallow it with the last of your strength!”

Soma’s eyes met mine.

Do not give up!

Geukak Soma struggled to chew and swallow the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.

I immediately circulated my true qi through one heavenly cycle. It was the most desperate qi circulation of my life. The time felt unbearably long.

‘Please! Please!’

I must have said the word “please” more today than I had in my entire life.

After completing the fastest heavenly circulation of true qi in my life, I injected internal energy into Geukak Soma again and examined his body.

It was a relief. Geukak Soma had succeeded in using the energy of the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng to hold back his rampaging qi and blood. But he had not refined the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng’s energy into inner power; he had merely suppressed it temporarily with that enormous energy.

There was no telling when his qi and blood would run wild again. I had to take him to the Demon Physician as quickly as possible.

“Thank you, Lord Soma.”

Moisture clung to my voice. I was grateful to him. Grateful that he had held on. Grateful that we had become the kind of people where I could give someone a Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng and be the one to say thank you.

Geukak Soma’s eyes spoke to me.

Thank you, Second Young Master.

What more needed to be said?

I untied the Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Thread that had bound him and me at the chest.

Then I carried Geukak Soma on my back and bound him tightly with the Supreme Heavenly Silkworm Thread.

He had done his part, so now it was my turn.

Soma, who said you could leave as you pleased? On the day some bastard like Yaryul Han boards the boat to the afterlife, do you think I’d let you ride that boat with him?

After wrapping him in my energy so that Geukak Soma’s body would not shake and would remain comfortable, I began to run.

I ran with all my might, as if I had learned Swift Step precisely for this moment.

* * *

Bi Sain was sitting before the gate of the manor.

Ilang Baek Cheolgi arrived there.

“You must not go around alone like this. Did you not promise you would not?”

“Please let it pass just this once.”

Bi Sain had called only Ilang here. The only person in the world he trusted was Ilang.

Ilang’s gaze turned toward the manor behind him. Judging by the smell of blood flowing out through the broken walls, there was no doubt that a fierce battle had taken place here.

“No matter what you see inside, do not be too surprised.”

“This is not the smell of one or two people’s blood. Just what is this place?”

Bi Sain answered quietly.

“The place where my future will be decided.”

At that, Ilang said as he went inside,

“Then my future will be decided as well.”

The place, turned into a sea of blood, still bore every trace of the fierce battle.

As Ilang looked around, he discovered Yaryul Han’s corpse and was shocked.

‘Yaryul Han!’

So startled was he that Ilang stood blankly for a moment, looking down at the body. He could not believe it even after seeing it with his own eyes.

Yaryul Han was dead? That Yaryul Han?

Truly, one phrase was enough to describe Yaryul Han. That Yaryul Han.

And that was not all.

Another corpse caught Ilang’s eye.

‘Even the Five Thunder Divine Sword!’

The Five Thunder Divine Sword.

He already knew that the man had been in league with Yaryul Han. He did not know whether he was stronger or weaker than Yaryul Han, but at least one thing was certain. If one were to compare his strength, Yaryul Han had to be the standard.

‘He died with his torso split in two?’

It was truly a sight difficult to believe, even while seeing it with his own eyes.

And how could that be the only shocking thing?

He was startled when he saw the Blood Wheels fallen on the ground.

‘Those weapons are Blood Wheels. The only one who uses Blood Wheels is the Blood Wheel Calamity, isn’t it?’

After the Blood Wheel Calamity, Ilang also recognized the Three Evils.

‘Triplets worthy of keeping company with these people would be the Three Evils!’

At this point, Cha Hwan’s death was hardly surprising.

Ilang came back out of the manor. Bi Sain was still sitting before the main gate.

“Just what in the world happened here?”

“Sit.”

Ilang sat beside him. His trembling heart had still not calmed down.

“With these people, they could have declared war on the Murim Alliance.”

Of course, it was an exaggeration, but it was not empty boasting.

If these people attacked someone, what murim sect could possibly stop them?

When Bi Sain first saw this sight, he had been as shocked as Ilang.

Even when Geom Mugeuk said he would kill Yaryul Han, a part of his heart had still held the doubt, ‘Can he really kill him?’

Yet he had truly done it.

This unbelievable deed had been accomplished by the man who had nagged him to eat a balanced meal.

‘You really are…!’

It finally struck him anew that the man was the son of the Heavenly Demon. And he had accomplished something no successor in history could have done.

“Who did this?”

At Ilang’s question, Bi Sain answered.

“I did.”

“What?”

Ilang was startled.

“This will be handled as something I did.”

“Are you serious?”

“Yes. I promised to take care of the aftermath.”

“Who on earth killed them?”

“You do not need to know that. For your sake as well, it is better that you do not know.”

Ilang was confused, but that was not what mattered now.

“Very well. Let us say that is the case. But will the Alliance Leader believe that you killed Yaryul Han?”

“That is why I called you, Ilang.”

“What?”

“From now on, we have to handle the aftermath. We have to decide how to make them believe that I dealt with Yaryul Han.”

Ilang understood. As Bi Sain had said earlier, Bi Sain’s future and fate were tied to this matter.

“My lifelong archenemy is dead, but my battle begins now.”

Bi Sain had once imagined the Demon Cult invading someday. Would Geom Mugeuk, the Heavenly Demon, truly be able to stop the Demon Cult?

And where did this baseless certainty that such a thing would never happen come from?

Bi Sain wanted to see Geom Mugeuk. Though he was such a terrifying man, he wanted to see him. He wanted to hear his frivolous banter.

How is it? I told you to leave this matter to me, didn’t I? Now it’s your turn. I trust you’ll do well. Eat a balanced meal!

It felt as though his voice were sounding by his ear.

If he became the leader of the Evil Alliance, he wanted to be sure to tell him.

What his evil path would be.

‘So you must become the Heavenly Demon too.’

* * *

Even when night fell, Geom Mugeuk and Geukak Soma did not return.

Seo Daeryong could not leave the courtyard out of anxiety.

Then the Mistress of Cheonhwaru came out to the courtyard.

“It seems the two of them are having a drink to celebrate killing Yaryul Han.”

At Seo Daeryong’s absurd joke, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru smiled and replied.

“It seems so.”

“Did you perhaps have a new dream?”

“No.”

“Then your face looks at ease.”

“You said it would not work, Investigator Seo.”

Seo Daeryong let out a light sigh.

“That was until before the sun set.”

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru asked Seo Daeryong,

“Are you worried?”

“The Pavilion Master has never once failed to return on time. He is always someone who opens the door and strides back in as if nothing at all happened.”

At that exact moment, someone opened the door and entered.

Seo Daeryong and the Mistress of Cheonhwaru looked over in surprise, but unfortunately, it was a martial artist of the Demon Cult who managed the safe house.

“This is an urgent message sent from Tongcheon Pavilion.”

Startled, Seo Daeryong hurriedly took the message and read it.

“It says the Pavilion Master went ahead to the main cult because Lord Soma was injured.”

At that moment, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru’s expression hardened. Looking at her, Seo Daeryong cautiously added,

“There was no report on how badly Lord Soma was injured.”

Then the Poison King walked out from behind them and said,

“He must be badly hurt. Seeing as they went straight to the main cult.”

The Poison King did not say anything to reassure her.

“You prepare to leave at once.”

“Yes.”

Seo Daeryong was worried about the Mistress of Cheonhwaru, but since he had received the Poison King’s order, he moved quickly.

Seeing the Mistress of Cheonhwaru filled with worry, the Poison King abruptly made an unexpected suggestion.

“Let us go together.”

At the Poison King’s words, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru was startled.

“You have never been to the main cult, have you?”

“That is true, but…”

“Then take this chance to have a look.”

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru understood. Though he did not say it aloud, the Poison King was saying this.

‘It may be the last time, so go and see him.’

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru bowed her head politely and said,

“I will follow the words of one so esteemed. Thank you truly for your great consideration.”

“No need. That man Soma will also like it if the Tower Mistress is there when he wakes.”

“I will be fortunate if he does not scold me, asking how I dared come.”

“You know better than anyone that there is no way he would do that, do you not, Tower Mistress?”

The Poison King snapped his fan open with a sound and, waving it lightly, went inside to gather his things. Watching his back, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru bowed her head once more.

Then she immediately went inside and said farewell to the rescued women. The martial artists of the safe house would see them safely home.

A short while later, the three of them boarded the carriage.

Seo Daeryong, on the driver’s seat, shouted,

“Now, we’re departing.”

The gaze of the Mistress of Cheonhwaru, looking out of the carriage, deepened. She was worried about Geukak Soma. She wanted to see him so badly. Though she could see many things, she could feel nothing regarding Geukak Soma’s fate.

At times like this, she grew angry. She could see the fates of others so well, yet often could not see her own fate at all.

Was she afraid of going to the Demon Cult? Of course not. If only she could see Geukak Soma, she would have climbed aboard without hesitation even if this carriage were bound for hell.

‘Orabeoni, don’t die.’

And the words she had always said to Geom Mugeuk as a joke, she now said in earnest.

‘Please take good care of our orabeoni.’

Because the one holding the reins understood her heart better than anyone, the carriage began to race toward the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult faster than ever before.

Chapter 239: Did the Two of You Fight the Entire Murim?
-------------------------------------------------------
The Demon Physician was looking out the window.

There were few patients today. Some days were like this. Days that seemed to say, like the clouds drifting leisurely across the sky, You too should rest at ease for a day.

But it seemed that day was not today.

The door to the medical chamber flew wide open, and someone stepped inside.

It was Geom Mugeuk, drenched in sweat from head to toe, carrying Geugak Soma on his back. An astonishing heat surged from him.

“……Please save him!”

After saying those words, Geom Mugeuk collapsed where he stood.

Startled, the Demon Physician first took Geugak Soma in his arms and laid him on the bed.

He was about to lay Geom Mugeuk on another bed when Geom Mugeuk, coming back to his senses, said,

“I’m fine…… Please treat Soma-nim.”

“You are not fine.”

The Demon Physician could tell that Geom Mugeuk was in a state of complete exhaustion. Even the fact that he was speaking and moving now was only because he was enduring through sheer willpower.

“Soma-nim first.”

Since Geugak Soma was the more urgent case, the Demon Physician hurriedly took his pulse. He realized that some mighty energy was suppressing the wild rampage of his meridians.

“What is this energy?”

“Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.”

Geom Mugeuk got up and came to the bed. His legs trembled so badly that even walking was difficult, but he was too worried about Geugak Soma to remain still.

As the Demon Physician removed Geugak Soma’s upper garment, he said,

“If not for the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng, he would already be dead.”

Geugak Soma’s condition was still perilous.

The Demon Physician quickly opened his acupuncture bag. The leather bag was densely packed with needles large and small.

The Demon Physician began to insert the needles. His hands moved with practiced ease. The essence of medical skill, something no other physician could imitate, resided in every single needle.

After inserting the needles, the Demon Physician began applying moxibustion over Geugak Soma’s dantian with some unknown medicinal herb. A distinctive medicinal fragrance filled the chamber.

“Uuuugh.”

Geugak Soma let out a groan of pain.

This time, the Demon Physician took out a long red needle and inserted it. He pushed it in so deeply that one wondered whether such a long needle could really go that far.

Then Geugak Soma’s expression seemed to ease, only for his body to immediately convulse.

The Demon Physician swiftly withdrew the needle and began inserting needles into new acupoints. His hands were fast and precise.

At that, Geugak Soma’s convulsions stopped.

Until now, the energy of the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng had kept Geugak Soma alive, but now it had become the greatest danger obstructing treatment. Somehow, he had to drive it into one place and finish the treatment.

Crisis came after crisis, and the Demon Physician skillfully overcame each one, proving why he was called a divine physician. If it had not been him, Geugak Soma’s condition would not have allowed anyone to even attempt treatment in the first place. The process consumed an enormous amount of mental strength, and sweat poured from the Demon Physician’s forehead and body like rain.

Geom Mugeuk watched the treatment tensely. He felt so exhausted he could die. He had truly run here like a madman. Without exaggeration, it had been harder than fighting Yayul Han.

Even so, he could not take his eyes away for even a moment.

At last, the treatment that had been like a war came to an end, and Geugak Soma entered a stable period.

“For now, he has passed the first crisis. It is not yet the stage where we can be at ease, but he is an exceptionally strong-willed man, so I believe he will endure.”

“Please save him!”

The reason he had run that long road without rest was because the one waiting here was the Demon Physician. Because he believed that even if no one else could save him, the Demon Physician would.

The Demon Physician nodded and said,

“I will do my best.”

“Thank you.”

Only then did the tension leave Geom Mugeuk, and he sank down where he stood.

The Demon Physician took Geom Mugeuk’s pulse and checked his condition. Geom Mugeuk, too, was in a state of complete exhaustion.

“How on earth did you come all the way here in that body?”

The Demon Physician was astonished. With a body like this, let alone running while carrying someone on his back, it would have been difficult just to move on his own.

“Here. Take this first.”

The Demon Physician handed him a pill. It was medicine that replenished vitality when one was exhausted.

“I’m fine.”

“I am not fine with it. Take it at once.”

“Thank you.”

Geom Mugeuk swallowed the medicine.

The Demon Physician also applied golden wound medicine to Geom Mugeuk’s injuries. Judging by the wounds on Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma, it was clear they had fought a truly fierce battle.

Just then, a warrior came in and delivered a written dispatch.

After checking the contents, the Demon Physician was shocked and asked Geom Mugeuk,

“From where, exactly, did you run here?”

The dispatch said that Geom Mugeuk had set out with the injured Geugak Soma and that preparations should be made in advance. Astonishingly, Geom Mugeuk had arrived faster than the dispatch.

‘Is such a thing possible?’

And while carrying Geugak Soma on his back, no less.

If it had been anyone else, he would naturally have thought there had been some mistake. But seeing Geom Mugeuk’s current state, he found himself thinking that perhaps he truly had run faster than the dispatch.

In truth, there was something the Demon Physician wanted to ask.

Geom Mugeuk had left to kill Yayul Han. He was curious to know what had happened.

But he felt he already knew the outcome without asking. They had returned injured like this, after all.

He felt sorry toward the two of them. Though he had known it was not a revenge that could be easily accomplished, his greed had been too great.

‘I am sorry. Truly sorry.’

The Demon Physician was turning around to check Geugak Soma’s condition when Geom Mugeuk spoke from behind him.

“I conveyed your words to Yayul Han.”

“!”

Startled, the Demon Physician turned back to see Geom Mugeuk sitting with his back against the wall, looking up at him.

“I told him clearly while looking into Yayul Han’s dying eyes. That this was your will.”

The Demon Physician was so shocked that his eyes widened, and for a moment he could not say anything. Only after a long while did he ask,

“You killed Yayul Han?”

“Yes, I killed him.”

“Truly…… you killed him?”

The Demon Physician’s voice trembled, and soon his entire body began to tremble.

“Yes, I killed him.”

“Thank you. Truly, thank you.”

The Demon Physician sat down facing Geom Mugeuk and clasped his hands.

“Please live the rest of your life in peace. Your family would want that as well.”

The moment he heard those words, intense emotion surged up within the Demon Physician. Still holding Geom Mugeuk’s hands, he bowed low before him. Feelings difficult to express in words welled up inside him. How long had he longed for this moment? Thousands, tens of thousands of times, he had imagined this very moment.

Suddenly, his son’s figure came to mind.

Father, you’re busy for us, aren’t you? If you weren’t busy, you’d play with me, right?

Tears flowed from the Demon Physician’s eyes. If he had lived, he would have been middle-aged by now…… but to the Demon Physician, his son still remained a child.

‘I’m sorry I couldn’t play with you more. I’m sorry I couldn’t tell you more often that I loved you.’

In the Demon Physician’s heart, his son was smiling brightly.

The tears poured down even harder.

‘Yes. When we meet later, I will play with you all you want.’

It was the moment the Demon Physician laid down the heavy stone he had carried on his heart all his life.

After shedding tears as he thought of his son, the Demon Physician raised his head to see that Geom Mugeuk had fallen asleep while sitting with his back against the wall. With the relief that everything was over, all the fatigue that had accumulated until now had come crashing down on him. He had fallen into such a deep sleep that he would not have noticed even if someone carried him away.

‘Thank you, Second Young Master. I will never forget this grace.’

Just then, someone arrived there.

The person who entered with a face full of worry was none other than the Blood Heaven Blade Demon. He had heard that Geom Mugeuk had arrived and gone straight to the medical chamber, and had rushed over in a single breath.

Geugak Soma, lying on the bed receiving treatment, entered his eyes first, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon went to check on Geom Mugeuk first.

“Is he all right?”

“He carried that man on his back and ran here without resting. How could he be all right?”

But there was ease in the Demon Physician’s expression. He could tell that Geom Mugeuk was safe.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon acted needlessly spiteful.

“What’s so precious about that masked bastard?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon crouched in front of Geom Mugeuk. Between the parted edges of his clothes, he could see the Ghost Tiger Robe he had given him. It was soaked in blood.

“Causing me trouble in my old age.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon picked up the blood-soaked supreme-grade Heavenly Silkworm Thread that had fallen beside the bed and wrapped it around the hilt of the Black Demon Sword. Then he stood the sword beside Geom Mugeuk.

“Is it really trouble?”

“What do you mean?”

“Look at your face reflected in that bronze mirror.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon raised his head and looked into the mirror.

“What am I supposed to see?”

The Demon Physician merely smiled an inscrutable smile. He could clearly feel it. Recently, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon had changed. He also knew what had begun that change.

Just then, the Fist Demon arrived.

“What happened?”

When his large frame entered, the medical chamber seemed to fill completely.

After greeting the two of them, the Fist Demon first asked after Geugak Soma, who lay on the bed.

“Is Soma all right?”

“We will have to watch him a little longer, but it seems he has passed the crisis.”

It looked as though he had come out of concern for Soma, but the Demon Physician knew. He had come because he was worried about his disciple’s safety.

“Your disciple is merely exhausted. After he rests well for a few days, he will be fine.”

Relief flickered across the Fist Demon’s terrifying face. This was the first time the Demon Physician realized that the Fist Demon had expressions too, and he also realized now that his feelings toward Geom Mugeuk ran deep enough for him to reveal that relief without hiding it. To change these fearsome Demon Venerables like this.

‘Second Young Master, you are remarkable.’

But it did not end there. Someone stepped into the medical chamber and spoke.

“What are these sorry sights? Did the two of them fight the entire murim?”

The person who entered, reeking strongly of alcohol, was the Drunken Demon.

Skipping even the greetings, he crouched in front of Geom Mugeuk. After silently gazing at Geom Mugeuk for a moment, the Drunken Demon said to the other two Demon Venerables,

“Once he wakes up, he’ll go around boasting about his heroic tale, so you’d better avoid him for a while.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon added to his words.

“We’ll be lucky if he doesn’t launch into a long speech.”

Just then, another person entered.

“What are you all doing around an injured man?”

The last to arrive was the Ilhwa Sword Venerable. Her beautiful appearance, untouched by age, remained as ever.

The Demon Physician was inwardly surprised. He already knew that Geom Mugeuk mingled with the Demon Venerables, but he had not expected all the Demon Venerables to come here.

The Drunken Demon clicked his tongue as he looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“Little brother, what is this? No pretty women came.”

“I’m here.”

At the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s words, the Drunken Demon spoke again.

“Little brother, what kind of situation is this, with no pretty young women coming and only old people like us gathered here? Back in my youth, you see……”

Just then, Ian and Cheon Sohui arrived.

Ian’s eyes widened in shock at the sight of the Demon Venerables gathered there. She could not dare approach, but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon gestured for her to come closer.

When the Demon Venerables opened a path for her, she sat down in front of Geom Mugeuk.

“He is only asleep, so do not worry.”

At the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s words, Ian began to cry. The moment she heard that Geom Mugeuk was safe, the tears simply poured out.

Rather than the sight of him injured and collapsed, the way he slept from sheer exhaustion felt even more pitiful. How exhausted must he be that he could not wake even in a situation like this?

She felt so upset and sorry that she had not been by his side. If the Demon Venerables and the Demon Physician had not been there, she might have shouted then and there that she would quit being the Ghost Shadow Unit Leader and follow him from now on. That he should take her with him.

She wanted to offer him her shoulder so he could lean against her and sleep comfortably. Of course, she could not bring herself to do that in front of the Demon Venerables.

Cheon Sohui, too, watched the scene from behind with worry. Since the Fist Demon was there, he stood a little distance away and did not step forward.

And there was someone else who marked the final visit of the day.

Everyone’s gazes turned to the door at once. Even before he entered the room, they knew who had arrived. Even without releasing his aura, his very presence announced his arrival.

The Heavenly Demon, Geom Ujin, had appeared.

The Demon Venerables, Ian, and Cheon Sohui lined up to either side and paid their respects solemnly.

“We greet the Cult Leader.”

After receiving their greeting, Geom Ujin first asked about Geugak Soma’s condition.

“What is Soma’s state?”

“Fortunately, he has passed the dangerous crisis. He is an extremely sturdy man, so he should regain his original condition before long.”

“Do not spare any medicine.”

“You need not worry about that. The energy of the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng has not yet dissolved inside his body, so once he recovers and absorbs it, he will become much stronger.”

Everyone was shocked at the mention of the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked back at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“That greedy fellow must have even yielded the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.”

Only then did Geom Ujin’s gaze turn to Geom Mugeuk.

His eyes toward his son were calm. After looking down at the sleeping Geom Mugeuk for a moment, Geom Ujin said something unexpected.

“If we leave him here, he will only get in the way of your treatment. I will take that one with me.”

Geom Ujin strode over and lifted Geom Mugeuk into his arms.

At the sight, everyone was startled. The image of Geom Ujin holding someone in his arms was something they had quite literally never imagined even once.

Even if he were to hold someone, they had thought he would lift them with the Void-Grasping Art and then carry them. But Geom Ujin bent at the waist and personally took Geom Mugeuk into his arms.

To the Demon Venerables, that sight was far too unfamiliar, and shocking.

Whether the Demon Venerables were surprised or not, Geom Ujin walked out of the room with his son in his arms.

“Are you still envious?”

At the Ilhwa Sword Venerable’s question, the Drunken Demon answered. She naturally expected an answer like, “Being held by the Cult Leader? No! I hate it! I hate it more than anything in the world!”

“I don’t know. I’ve never been held by my father.”

The Ilhwa Sword Venerable looked back at the Drunken Demon.

“Are you drunk?”

The Drunken Demon chuckled and drank.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon also acted needlessly spiteful.

“That shameless brat! He stubbornly took my spiritual medicine, but yielded the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng?”

At that, the Demon Physician took Geom Mugeuk’s side.

“If he had not yielded it, that man would be dead.”

“Then let him die!”

After the Blood Heaven Blade Demon left, the other Demon Venerables also bid farewell to the Demon Physician and departed the medical chamber. Ian and Cheon Sohui, too, greeted the Demon Physician before leaving.

Left alone, the Demon Physician let out a light sigh. It felt as though a storm had swept through and passed.

The Demon Physician walked over to the bed where Geugak Soma slept.

The gaze with which he looked down at him was gentle. This was the man who had fought alongside Geom Mugeuk for the sake of his revenge. How could he repay that gratitude with words? He would repay it by saving him.

“I intend to save you no matter what, so you must also hold on. If nothing else, you should hold on for the sake of the man who carried you on his back and ran that long road, should you not?”

The Demon Physician looked out the window. The sky he had seen earlier now seemed to be saying this:

Like the clouds drifting leisurely across that sky, live the rest of your life with an unburdened heart.

Chapter 240: Finally Opened My Eyes.
------------------------------------
I opened my eyes.

Though I had woken up in an unfamiliar place, my body felt so refreshed that I remained lying on the bed for a moment. Usually, when I slept for a long time, I had all sorts of dreams, but this time, I had slept deeply without dreaming at all.

How long had I slept?

A day? Two days? My stomach was so empty that I might have slept for a full ten days.

Then, remembering someone, I shot up into a sitting position.

“Lord Soma!”

Looking around, this place was not the infirmary.

It was an unfamiliar bedchamber I had never entered before. The clean bedding gave off a fragrant scent, and the bed and furniture radiated not a feeling of age, but the dignity of antique pieces.

Then, something caught my eye.

That thing hanging on the wall was…!

Surprisingly, this was my father’s bedchamber.

My last memory was the infirmary, so why had I been sleeping here?

A clean martial uniform had been placed beside the bed.

I changed my clothes and looked around the bedchamber with a dazed mind. Though I was his son, this was my first time entering this room.

‘Father gave me his room?’

I did not know what the situation was, but just this fact alone left me deeply moved. I lay back down on my father’s bed.

Thinking that my father slept here, I wanted to lie down a while longer for no particular reason. It might be an opportunity that would never come again.

Then, my father’s voice came from outside the window.

“If you are awake, come out.”

I sprang up from the bed and went outside.

Father was sitting at a stone tea table in the courtyard, drinking tea.

“To realize this is the Heavenly Demon’s bedchamber, shouldn’t one normally see the symbol of the Heavenly Demon Soul or the Heavenly Demon Sword? And yet, you figured it out from a pair of flower-patterned pajamas!”

That thing hanging on the wall had been my father’s flower-patterned pajamas.

At my jest, Father set down his teacup and spoke.

“It seems you are well enough to live now.”

“I feel like I could fly away.”

In fact, my body had been completely healed.

“Why am I in Father’s bedchamber?”

“Because I brought you here.”

“Did you come to the infirmary?”

At my surprised question, Father nodded.

“I was not the only one who went. All of your few connections came as well.”

I could roughly guess who had come.

“Those few people are basically one-man armies, are they not?”

As if they were merely one against a hundred? They were one against a thousand, one against ten thousand.

“What happened to Lord Soma?”

My heart pounded with anxiety. Father was someone who could sip his tea casually and say, “Soma is dead.”

“Ma-ui is still treating him.”

I was relieved at those words. The power contained in the single word “Ma-ui” was immense.

Then, maidservants from the Heavenly Demon Hall came and brought me rice porridge. Since it was a meal after starving for several days, it meant I should eat lightly. This was Father’s consideration.

“Have you eaten?”

“I have, so eat.”

“Yes, then I shall eat gratefully.”

Who knew porridge could be this delicious? After emptying the bowl cleanly and drinking a cup of tea, I was still hungry, but I felt like I would survive.

“Where did the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng come from?”

“Yaryeolhan brought it as a ninetieth birthday gift for Oroesingeom.”

“What about cleaning up after them?”

“Bisa-in said he would take care of it.”

“Do you think he can do it?”

“Yes, I believe he can.”

“So you have marked that child as the next Cult Leader.”

“I believe it is not a bad choice.”

Then, a martial artist from the Heavenly Demon Hall brought something, placed it before me, and left.

“What is this?”

“Open it.”

When I opened the box, there was a Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng inside.

I was startled.

“Where did this come from?”

“What? Do you think I cut open Soma’s stomach to retrieve it?”

He must have brought a miraculous elixir from the Heavenly Demon Treasury. What lay within it was known only to the Heavenly Demon, but I had a rough idea.

“Are you giving this to me?”

“Yes.”

“Why are you giving me such a precious thing?”

“Since you willingly gave up the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng for my Demon Venerable, I should naturally return it.”

He emphasized the words “my Demon Venerable.” It meant that Soma was not yet my Demon Venerable.

“Are you afraid of losing Geukak Soma to me?”

Then, my favorite expression appeared. That unique scornful smile with the corners of his mouth curling up.

“Do you not want to eat it? Then do not.”

“As if!”

When he tried to take it back, I quickly stuffed it into my mouth.

It was sweet and bitter, more fragrant and delicious than any elixir I had ever eaten.

I circulated my true energy through one full cycle and completely dissolved the Snow Ginseng’s energy, converting it into internal energy. As the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng’s internal energy was added to my internal realm, which had ascended to a new stage, my internal energy grew even more refined and profound.

While I was circulating my qi, Father stood with his hands behind his back, gazing into the distance at Mount Daechon.

I went to his side and stood beside him. Instead of words of gratitude, I first offered an apology.

“I am sorry, Father.”

A son who falls into danger is already an unfilial son by that fact alone.

“As long as you know.”

“I will not forget the grace of bestowing upon me the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng.”

As if he would give me something so precious simply because I yielded it to Geukak Soma? There was another meaning behind Father bestowing the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng upon me. And I had guessed that meaning.

“You said it yourself. That you would fight a bloodless succession battle.”

“Yes.”

“Does that thought remain unchanged?”

“It remains unchanged.”

Father said nothing more.

I could tell. That Father would decide on his successor before long. The Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng was a gift bestowed with the meaning that I should bring that succession battle to a proper conclusion. Because I had guessed as much, every time I chewed the ginseng, these words rang in my ears:

‘Eat this and do well!’

If Father was going to make his decision, I too had to settle things with my older brother one way or another.

“We brothers will join hands and come to see you soon.”

This was what Father wanted. The final test of successor selection.

Because I knew my older brother’s personality, I thought that perhaps this matter might be more difficult than killing Yaryeolhan.

Because he was no longer my match in martial arts, and because most of the Demon Venerables had become my people, it was instead even harder. Because there was no telling what desperate move my brother might make.

“If you have finished eating, be on your way now.”

“May I go back into the bedchamber, put on your pajamas, and sleep for just one more hour? They are so soft and comfortable that I do not want to leave.”

Just as Father was about to speak, a martial artist from the Heavenly Demon Hall came and reported.

“The Poison King has returned.”

Upon hearing those words, I bowed deeply and ran off.

“Then I shall take my leave.”

I could feel Father’s dumbfounded gaze on the back of my head.

I understand, Father. I will end the succession battle this time. Because I, too, have a long road ahead.

* * *

The carriage carrying the Poison King and the Heavenly Flower Pavilion Lord entered the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.

“Look over there!”

Where Seodaeryong pointed, a giant evil demon statue stood.

“Look at the opposite side.”

On the opposite side as well, stone statues pointed swords and blades at each other. There were statues reenacting past Heavenly Demons, and a statue in the likeness of the Heavenly Demon Soul.

“It is amazing.”

She answered as if in admiration, but right now, the stone statues did not enter the Heavenly Flower Pavilion Lord’s eyes.

Her heart was beating violently. Soon, she would hear news of Geukak Soma.

During the journey here, no news of Geukak Soma had been delivered. So she did not know whether he was alive or dead.

The Poison King did not mention a single word about Geukak Soma’s life or death. He did not console her by saying it would be alright.

From the Poison King’s demeanor, Seodaeryong felt that he was a truly different person from what he appeared to be.

He was not someone to be judged by that young, fresh appearance. Dazedly staring at poisonous herbs or digging in the dirt was only a part of him.

Passing through the outer court amid strict security, the carriage entered the inner court. The security became several times stricter.

Now all members of the party disembarked from the carriage.

As the Heavenly Flower Pavilion Lord looked around, her gaze stopped dead at one spot.

From afar, Geom Muguk was walking toward them.

In that moment, her heart pounded as if it would burst. What news would Geom Muguk bring? Please, let Geukak Soma be safe.

Geom Muguk approached her and spoke.

“Lord Soma is still under treatment.”

Geom Muguk spoke honestly to her.

“I have done everything I can. The Divine Physician of the main cult has also done his best. Now it is up to Lord Soma.”

At that, the Heavenly Flower Pavilion Lord broke into a bright smile.

“Then there is no need to worry.”

She was someone who believed in Geukak Soma like the heavens.
