Because it was none other than Geom Muguk who told her, she felt relieved. If Geom Muguk had done his best, then it truly was the best that could be done.

“You must be tired from the long journey, so please rest first. We shall receive you as distinguished guests of the main cult until Lord Soma awakens.”

The Heavenly Flower Pavilion Lord bowed her head in gratitude. The maidservants who had accompanied her guided her to a detached house.

After she left, Seodaeryong ran to embrace Geom Muguk.

“Why are you being so creepy?”

“I am happy to see you, but is this something you need to dodge using footwork? Would you have done this even if a martial artist ran at you?”

Seodaeryong rushed at him again. This time, Geom Muguk did not dodge and let himself be hugged.

“I was really worried this time.”

“Then you should have come running then. When Yaryeolhan surrounded me with the clone technique, you should have come and taken on three of the clones.”

“Hah! He used the clone technique?”

“As if that was all? Demonic ghost-shaped sword qi came rushing at me as if to devour me.”

Seodaeryong subtly took a step back.

“As you know, I am a man suited to being an investigator. Do not waste your top student admission on the wrong place!”

If you are going to say that, your eyes should not be gleaming like that.

“I shall go greet my master.”

Even Seodaeryong left, and now only the Poison King remained. He had deliberately held back because the two wanted to talk quietly.

“You have worked hard, Poison King.”

“Have I worked hard at all? You all are the ones who suffered.”

The Poison King asked Geom Muguk.

“Did you obtain what you sought from this battle?”

After a brief pause, Geom Muguk spoke.

“Yes, I think I have.”

Staring intently at Geom Muguk’s deepened gaze, the Poison King moved toward the Heavenly Poison Forest and spoke.

“Come visit the Heavenly Poison Forest when you have time.”

At his waist, the Blood Poison Heavenly Silkworm gloves that Geom Muguk had given him as a gift hung dangling.

* * *

Finally, Geukak Soma opened his eyes.

By the unique scent of medicine piercing his nose, he could tell he was lying in the infirmary.

His last memory was Geom Muguk’s back. Running so frighteningly fast, and yet why is my body so comfortable? He had fallen asleep thinking that.

When he examined his body, the battlefield that had been inside was peaceful. It was not a complete end to the war, but at least there was a truce.

He could tell by his condition. That he had arrived safely at the main cult and that Ma-ui had treated him. Because there was no one else who could treat a body like his.

Geukak Soma slowly sat up. He perched on the bed and looked at the mirror. The white mask still had blood on it from the battle. They had not even touched the mask during treatment.

Geukak Soma suddenly recalled a moment.

The moment when, sensing death, he had tried to remove his mask, and Geom Muguk grabbed his arm.

“I think you are probably better looking than me, so I am not looking at that face!”

A smile formed at the corners of Geukak Soma’s lips beneath the mask.

Then, someone entered the room and spoke.

“You have awakened?”

The one who entered was Ma-ui.

Even though he knew that Ma-ui had saved him, Geukak Soma omitted greetings and asked about Geom Muguk first.

“What happened to the Second Young Lord?”

“In all my years as a physician, I have never seen someone so exhausted. Carrying the Demon Venerable on his back in that state…”

A hint of worry flashed through Geukak Soma’s eyes.

But soon, Ma-ui smiled and spoke.

“You need not worry. Right now, he could run back along the path he carried the Demon Venerable even faster.”

Only then did Geukak Soma feel relieved.

“I heard the Second Young Lord slept for several days as well. Indeed, he arrived before the messenger, so it is no wonder he was exhausted.”

Faster than the messenger? Yes, he remembered. How desperately Geom Muguk had run. Even amidst that, he had shared his energy to make his body comfortable. Geukak Soma knew well how much mental and physical strength that consumed.

Only after hearing the news of Geom Muguk did Geukak Soma politely express his gratitude to Ma-ui.

“Thank you for saving me. I apologize for the late greeting.”

Ma-ui shook his head and spoke.

“I am not the one to whom gratitude should be expressed.”

Ma-ui bowed his head respectfully and spoke.

“Thank you for resolving the grudge of my lifetime.”

The son in Ma-ui’s heart was now beginning to age.

“Now, first dissolve the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng’s energy remaining in your body. If the Second Young Lord had not administered it, the Demon Venerable would not have survived until now.”

This time, Geukak Soma remembered the moment Geom Muguk had fed him the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng. He also recalled the sight of Geom Muguk wailing, fearing he would die.

Feeling that he had too many things to be grateful to Geom Muguk for, Geukak Soma closed his eyes and began to dissolve the Snow Ginseng’s energy.

While Geukak Soma circulated his qi, Ma-ui sent someone to inform Geom Muguk that Geukak Soma had awakened.

When Geukak Soma had fully dissolved the internal energy, the gaze behind his mask grew even colder and deeper.

Shortly after, Geom Muguk, who had been waiting outside, entered.

Geom Muguk spoke to Geukak Soma, who was wearing his mask.

“Your complexion looks especially good today.”

How he had wanted to hear Geom Muguk’s banter! Seeing the blood on the mask, he said this:

“You even have a rosy flush on your face.”

Geukak Soma laughed out loud.

They could tell how grateful they were to each other without having to say it. This one phrase from Geukak Soma, who had just barely survived, was enough.

“Let us go fight again later.”

“Very well.”

No more words were needed.

“Oh, and a distinguished guest has come to the main cult.”

When Geom Muguk opened the door, the person waiting outside entered.

She was none other than the Heavenly Flower Pavilion Lord. She had come all the way to the main altar of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult because she was so worried about Geukak Soma, and had been worried that it might be a place she should not have come to.

But the moment she saw Geukak Soma, all such thoughts vanished. She could tell. That there had been no need to worry in the first place.

She was so happy just seeing him.

What more reason did she need? Nothing else mattered. Even if Geukak Soma was angry, she thought she had done well to come.

And Geukak Soma responded to her feelings all too well.

“Did you not say before that you wanted to see my room?”

Chapter 241: Please Stop Getting Stronger!
------------------------------------------
The Mistress of Cheonhwaru was deeply moved.

Far from getting angry, Extreme Evil Soma had said the very words she had longed so desperately to hear.

“Yes. I really want to see your room.”

She struggled to hold back the tears threatening to fall. As the mistress of Cheonhwaru, she had endured all manner of hardship, yet she had never cried. And now, at that one sentence from Extreme Evil Soma, tears were welling in her eyes.

She felt as though the bond she had built with Extreme Evil Soma, the affection between them, had been completed in this moment.

They had never once spoken to each other about love, but the words Extreme Evil Soma had just spoken sounded to her like a confession of love. He had merely said he would show her his room, and yet.

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru said to Geom Mugeuk,

“Thank you, Second Young Master.”

While waiting for Extreme Evil Soma to awaken, she had heard from Seo Daeryong that Geom Mugeuk had carried Extreme Evil Soma on his back and run a great distance. On top of that, he had even yielded the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng, something any martial artist would desire.

“All I did was carry him and run. Your thanks should go not to me, but to the Demon Physician.”

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru expressed her gratitude to the Demon Physician as well.

“Thank you, Medical Immortal.”

“Not at all, madam. The Demon Venerable was so robust that there was no real danger, so there is no need to thank me. I will send the medicine he must take from now on to the Valley of Villains, so the two of you may go.”

Thus, Extreme Evil Soma and the Mistress of Cheonhwaru left the infirmary first.

Watching the two of them, the Demon Physician thought of his wife. We had times like that too, he thought.

And surprisingly, even though he had thought of his wife, he felt at ease, unlike before.

The Demon Physician said to Geom Mugeuk,

“There were times when I wondered if I might feel hollow after finishing my revenge.”

“Do you feel hollow?”

The Demon Physician shook his head firmly.

“Not at all. My heart feels so peaceful and good that I wonder if it is all right for me to feel this way.”

“You may feel even more exhilarated than that.”

“Could you spare me a little time? There is something I want to show you.”

The Demon Physician took Geom Mugeuk into his quarters. Passing through a secret door, he led him to the place he had shown him once before.

There, he had recreated the house where he used to live, but now it had changed.

Bookshelves had been placed on all sides, and it had been turned into a study where one could sit comfortably and read. He was in the midst of changing in order to live a new life.

“Oh, this has a fine atmosphere. Elder Saber Demon also loves books very much.”

“I know. That fellow and I often talk about books.”

To the Demon Physician, the Blood Heaven Saber Demon was the only person he could call a friend.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk noticed the wood placed in one corner.

“What is that?”

There was not only wood, but also tools for trimming and carving it.

“My father was a carpenter. When I think of my father, what I remember is the sight of him carving wood in the yard. Now I am thinking of trying my hand at it, even if only as a hobby.”

The Demon Physician let out a light sigh and added,

“There has never been such an unfilial son as I. Only now that my son has found peace in my heart do I think of my father.”

“Your father must have been the same.”

The two looked at each other and smiled pleasantly.

Geom Mugeuk was grateful. He was grateful that the man was trying to change like this, and above all, that he was showing him the changed version of himself.

Because of you, I am becoming this happy.

That was what the Demon Physician was saying now. Yes, this was how it had to be said. Only when it was said like this could he know it. If it was not spoken, one could not know.

Thank you, Demon Physician.

For making me feel that risking my life had been worthwhile.

And please, be happy.

* * *

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru was experiencing walking through the inner court of the Demonic Cult for the first time in her life.

The demonic practitioners they encountered bowed respectfully to Extreme Evil Soma in greeting.

They were all vicious-looking demonic practitioners. Those terrifying men, who looked as though they could overturn the murim if they emerged into the world, greeted Extreme Evil Soma as meekly as newlywed brides.

She whispered so that only Extreme Evil Soma could hear.

“I had forgotten what kind of person you were. You were this extraordinary.”

Extreme Evil Soma was a man too, so the words were not entirely displeasing to him.

“From now on, I should hang your mask in front of Cheonhwaru. You bastards, just try getting drunk and causing trouble!”

At her joke, Extreme Evil Soma merely smiled silently with his eyes. Since he was a man of few words even in ordinary times, she did not mind at all when he gave no reply.

I am so nervous right now, that building is truly unusual, are there martial artists lying in ambush over there? She chattered on about all sorts of things, then added one last sentence.

“I’m sorry. For coming here as I pleased.”

He had only listened to everything else, but to those words, Extreme Evil Soma answered.

“It’s fine.”

And then he added a sentence he would ordinarily never have said.

“I had intended to bring you here once, someday.”

Because Extreme Evil Soma was usually so taciturn, each of these sentences moved her deeply.

In the meantime, the two arrived at the Valley of Villains.

The sight spread out at the entrance startled the Mistress of Cheonhwaru.

There, countless Faceless Guests were lined up in formation.

At the news that Extreme Evil Soma had recovered from his injuries and was returning, all of them had gathered. They all wore white masks, and every mask was smiling. It was a frightening yet magnificent sight. Today was the first time she learned that Extreme Evil Soma had this many subordinates.

As the two passed, the Faceless Guests nearby bowed their heads. The greetings of the Faceless Guests continued like a wave. They were fervently welcoming Extreme Evil Soma’s safe return.

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru felt fear, but at the same time, she felt a certain excitement. In the gazes directed at her, respect for the leader’s woman and curiosity coexisted.

It was the moment Extreme Evil Soma, who had never once brought a woman here, came to the Valley of Villains with a woman for the first time.

She felt that from this moment on, her fate, and also her relationship with Extreme Evil Soma, would change.

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru whispered a question.

“But how do you recognize one another? You are all wearing masks like that.”

“You cannot recognize them, but we can all tell.”

“How fascinating.”

Now that she heard that, it did seem as though each mask had a slightly different shape. Some had unique patterns, or were painted with various colors.

After walking for quite some time, they arrived at his residence.

Upon entering Extreme Evil Soma’s room, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru was startled. She had not expected it to be a room with white walls on every side.

She placed her hand on the white wall and said,

“It is such a clean room.”

“It will be uncomfortable for you to stay here.”

She was someone who could endure anything if she was with Extreme Evil Soma, but when she looked at the room with not a single piece of furniture in it, her heart ached for some reason.

“Do you sleep here?”

She thought surely not, but Extreme Evil Soma nodded.

“There is no bed.”

“The only time I sleep lying down is when I go to Cheonhwaru.”

“Ah!”

Extreme Evil Soma liked to lie with his head on her lap. And yet when he was in the Demonic Cult, he lived in a place like this. She had thought he would sleep on a truly large and splendid bed.

Then Extreme Evil Soma brought up something unexpected.

“Why not take this chance to pursue your dream in earnest?”

At those words, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru was startled. Her dream was to become the Empress of the Night. To establish her own pleasure houses throughout the entire Central Plains.

“Now, with Yeo Bulgae dead and a vacuum in power having formed, should be an opportunity.”

It meant Extreme Evil Soma would back her. It was certainly an opportunity. And the Mistress of Cheonhwaru was not a woman who would let such an opportunity slip away.

“Yes. I will give it a try.”

The two looked at each other and nodded. She knew. When it came to work, Extreme Evil Soma was thorough and rational.

For the Mistress of Cheonhwaru to achieve her dream would be of great help to Extreme Evil Soma as well.

To rule the night was also to rule information. Secrets tended to lose their strength before wine and beautiful women.

This time, the Mistress of Cheonhwaru asked about the line drawn on the wall.

“What is that line over there?”

It was the line he had drawn together with Geom Mugeuk. Since it was the only thing that stood out in the white room, she had been holding back the question from the beginning.

“It is the line of my life.”

It was an answer she could never understand, but Extreme Evil Soma’s eyes as he looked at the line seemed more content than ever.

The Mistress of Cheonhwaru stood beside Extreme Evil Soma and leaned her head against his shoulder.

“That must be why it was drawn so beautifully.”

* * *

When I returned to my residence, Ian was waiting in the courtyard.

“What are you doing here?”

“What else? I was waiting for you, sleepyhead young master.”

“How did you know when I would come?”

“I was going to wait until you did.”

Through the Demon Physician, I had heard that when I fell asleep in the infirmary, she had come and cried her eyes out. How worried she had been, and why she had come now, were all written plainly on her face.

“Did you starve yourself? Your face looks gaunt.”

On top of that, perhaps she had cried again afterward, because her eyes were swollen.

“I thought you had gotten a little prettier, but you have become ugly again.”

“I lived just fine being a hundred times uglier than this, you know.”

“Come on. I’ll buy you something delicious. Today, the two of us will bankrupt Pungryu Tavern!”

“I have something to tell you.”

“Tell me on the way. I’m hungry.”

When I started walking ahead, Ian followed behind me.

She had been trailing after me like a shadow when she suddenly said,

“Take me with you from now on. I will be your guard again.”

I kept walking as I answered.

“All right.”

“I am not joking. I mean it.”

“I know.”

When I answered too readily, Ian quickly ran ahead and stared intently at my face.

“Really?”

“Yes.”

Just as she was about to jump in joy, she flinched and asked,

“There is a condition, isn’t there?”

“Of course.”

“What condition?”

“Once you achieve great completion of the twelfth star of the Flying Heaven Sword Art, you may guard me.”

Ian looked disappointed, as if to say, I knew it.

“I have not even achieved ordinary great completion yet, so when am I supposed to reach great completion of the twelfth star? I might not manage it until I die of old age.”

“Otherwise, I would have to carry you back on my back too, wouldn’t I?”

“!”

Knowing very well what that meant, she could not continue insisting. If it was a situation where Extreme Evil Soma was on the verge of death, even if she had achieved great completion of the twelfth star, she might not be of any help.

“Please stop getting stronger!”

“‘I will become stronger’ is the correct answer, our dear Ghost Shadow Unit Commander.”

In that manner, we arrived at Pungryu Tavern in Maga Village.

“Pavilion Master, it has been a long time!”

Jo Chunbae welcomed me with the same unchanged manner as ever.

“Owner, have you been well?”

“I am always the same. I heard you have been very busy lately.”

“Even in the middle of all that busyness, I suffered because I wanted to eat your cooking. Please make everything I like.”

Leaving Jo Chunbae behind as he headed toward the kitchen with excited steps, we went up to our seats on the second floor.

“Did you know? The owner always keeps this seat empty.”

“For us?”

“Yes. So that whenever we come, we can sit here.”

“We only come once in a while. Why go that far? He must be taking a loss.”

“No. Word has spread that this is the seat where the Cult Leader and the Demon Venerables sat, so business is doing even better because of all the customers who come to see it.”

Ian lowered her voice and said,

“Do you think the owner planned it?”

“He is such a seasoned man that he might have.”

Ian and I laughed at the same time. The way she looked when she was worried and the way she looked when she smiled so brightly were so different that one could say they were different people.

In any case, seeing Ian’s smiling face made it feel real that I had returned to the cult.

“How is the work with the Ghost Shadow Unit going?”

“Blue Face is meeting several people. He said there are trustworthy ones. Things are going so smoothly that I am too worried.”

“About what?”

“I am the unit commander, but I have almost never met people, have I? The only person I knew was you, young master. Can someone like me really serve properly as a unit commander?”

“If you dealt with me, who could you possibly not deal with?”

“Ah, does it become like that again?”

Just then, Jo Chunbae came upstairs with wine and dishes.

“Owner, it has been a while, so receive a cup of wine from me.”

“No, no. Please continue your conversation.”

As always, Jo Chunbae declined, but I tugged him down by the sleeve and seated him.

“Just one cup.”

“Oh dear, I should not do this. Thank you, Pavilion Master.”

Jo Chunbae accepted my wine.

“I hear this seat has become a famous spot?”

“Somehow, that is what happened. It is all thanks to you, Pavilion Master.”

“Next time Father or the Demon Venerables come, I will ask them to leave traces of their martial arts on the wall.”

“Traces of their martial arts?”

“That way, the people who come to see this place will have something to look at, won’t they? Beside the mark Father leaves, Extreme Evil Soma can pierce a hole with the Blood Yang Finger. And on this table, I can ask Elder Saber Demon to write a poem.”

Jo Chunbae waved his hands, saying it was absurd.

“How could I make such a request of such esteemed people? And if they did, I would be unable to sleep for fear someone might tear off the wall or damage the table. Now then, please enjoy your meal.”

Jo Chunbae hurriedly rose from his seat and went downstairs. Though he had said it was absurd, the expression on his face as he went down to the first floor was delighted, as though just imagining it pleased him.

I said quietly to Ian,

“If you ever feel you do not understand human relationships well, asking the owner here will help.”

There was likely no one who understood human relationships better than Jo Chunbae, who had lived through every sort of person imaginable.

“I will.”

I drank with Ian and ate the food. Jo Chunbae’s cooking, which I had not had in a long time, was a taste I had missed terribly.

“Leave the cult together with Blue Face. Go meet Strategist Go and Cult Leader Pungcheon. Also bring back martial artists to bring into the Ghost Shadow Unit.”

Now it was time to send her out of the greenhouse.

“And before long, Father will choose his successor.”

At my words, Ian was startled. She knew better than anyone how important this matter was in my life, and how much attention I had been paying to it.

“Do you have a plan?”

“I have something in mind. I do not know whether it will go as intended, though.”

“What plan is it?”

“If I had to give it a name…”

I emptied my wine cup and said,

“I suppose it would be something like Operation Golden Grand Plan.”

Chapter 242: If Not for This Chance, Then When?
-----------------------------------------------
Eldest Young Master Geom Muyang stood alone at the edge of the Daecheonsan cliff.

Far in the distance, the buildings of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult looked small.

Geom Muyang reached out his hand. Grasped like this, the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult fit into one hand.

He opened the empty fist he had clenched in the air and recalled something Geom Mugeuk had once said.

—There’s no coexistence between brothers in a power struggle? In the end, they’ll kill each other? Who decided that? Why should we be swayed by the precedents and suspicions of pathetic bastards who couldn’t even control their own desires? So don’t even dream of being swayed by it. If you ever feel anxious that I might kill you, come find me.

Then he should have gone to him ten times over. Lately, he had been having nightmares often. Each time, in his dreams, Geom Mugeuk killed him with a smile.

—Hyung, you’re so naive. Did you believe everything I said? Well, I suppose that’s why you ended up like this. Did you think power could be shared? The greatest power in the murim, no less? I’m grateful you’re this naive, but still. How pathetic. Truly pathetic.

Whenever that happened, he would wake up cursing him. He hurled every insult imaginable, but the one who lost every time was himself. And Geom Muyang knew. Those words Geom Mugeuk spat out were, in the end, his own thoughts.

Geom Muyang had been living busily these days as well.

He handled affairs within the cult, met demonic practitioners of various ranks, and went out to take care of external matters. He was doing all sorts of work, cutting even into his sleeping time.

But he could not keep up with Geom Mugeuk’s unrestrained stride.

When he opened his eyes, Mugeuk had become the Fist Demon’s disciple. When he opened his eyes, he was wandering the Thousand Poison Forest searching for poisonous insects. When he opened his eyes, he had left the cult with the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon. And when he opened his eyes again…

Because he himself was not someone who could bark in the training ground, nor someone who could carry the Extreme Evil Smiling Demon on his back and run until he was on the verge of death.

Waaaaaaaah!

It was as if the people’s frenzied cheers for Geom Mugeuk rang in his ears. He did not want to become petty and mean-spirited, but it was hard to endure this maddening jealousy.

Just as he felt like shouting at the top of his lungs, the Demon Buddha arrived there. His short stature and golden radiance were unchanged.

“You called for me?”

“Welcome.”

The Demon Buddha stood beside him at the cliff.

“The Extreme Evil Smiling Demon has awakened.”

“I heard as well.”

Geom Muyang’s voice was dry. The Demon Buddha could tell. He was not in a good mood. With the Demon Venerables turning to Geom Mugeuk one by one, it was only natural that he could not be.

The Demon Buddha understood Geom Muyang’s feelings better than anyone.

“The Fist Demon still supports you, Eldest Young Master.”

The corner of Geom Muyang’s mouth curled up slightly.

“Though his disciple is Mugeuk.”

He had already judged that even the Fist Demon’s support would not last long.

“The Fist Demon is a man who distinguishes between public and private matters. He will not carelessly change words he has spoken.”

The Demon Buddha quickly added.

“The Poison King will also support you, Young Master.”

“While barking in the training ground with Mugeuk?”

“That is why the Second Young Master has grown even farther from becoming the successor. Someone who will become the Heavenly Demon should not have done that.”

“Are you saying that knowing Mugeuk’s popularity within the cult rose even higher after that day?”

“What matters is not popularity, but the Cult Leader’s will. Eldest Young Master, you are unquestionably the Cult Leader’s eldest son, and since childhood you have given people faith that you would become the successor.”

The Demon Buddha tried to give him strength, but Geom Muyang asked in a drained voice.

“When I look at you and myself lately, Demon Buddha, this is what I think. A defeatist and a groundless optimist. Isn’t that so?”

“Who is the defeatist?”

At the Demon Buddha’s question, Geom Muyang’s expression creased slightly.

Did Geom Muyang know or not? That in his relationship with him, the Demon Buddha had been frustrated and disappointed so many times that he had nearly fallen into true defeatism? That he had steadied his heart again and again to come this far?

The Demon Buddha asked calmly.

“I have never been optimistic, but even if I were, why do you think it is groundless?”

“Then do you believe I can become the successor?”

The Demon Buddha stared into Geom Muyang’s eyes and answered firmly.

“I do.”

“That is the problem. Your eye for judging the state of affairs is this dim. While we were stagnating and rotting, Mugeuk burst forth like a waterfall and flowed out to the sea.”

“No matter how outstanding the Second Young Master may be, we only need to do one thing well.”

“What is that?”

As if shouting from a mountaintop, the Demon Buddha raised his voice.

“The Eldest Young Master is better suited for the seat of Cult Leader!”

His shout echoed and rang out several times.

When the echoes faded, the Demon Buddha spoke.

“That is all we need to convince everyone of. There is no need to have your pride wounded because the Second Young Master has won the hearts of the Demon Venerables, and no need to be angry. Eldest Young Master, you need only accomplish that one thing.”

Geom Muyang burst out in frustration, his tone somewhat raised.

“You are truly stifling! Demon Buddha, do you really think I am better suited for the position of Cult Leader?”

The Demon Buddha spoke as if certain.

“Yes. You are better suited.”

“This is what I mean by groundless conviction. Were you not simply left with me because Mugeuk did not choose you?”

Geom Muyang spat out words he absolutely should not have said, words that would deeply wound the Demon Buddha, without filtering them at all.

Unexpectedly, the Demon Buddha did not take much offense. Because those words sounded like a scream.

Until now, Geom Muyang had dealt with him while hiding his true feelings. He had made him wonder what he was thinking. But now, he was screaming that he was in pain.

“Those words are the ones without any basis.”

When the Demon Buddha answered composedly, Geom Muyang took a small medicine bottle from his robes.

“Do you know what is inside this?”

“What is inside?”

“The Formless Deadly Poison.”

At the words Formless Deadly Poison, the Demon Buddha was startled.

“Are you saying you will kill the Second Young Master with that?”

Geom Muyang answered without hesitation.

“It is not as though I can’t.”

The Demon Buddha stared at him and asked.

“Then why take it out here? Ask the Second Young Master to share a drink with you and mix it in! Feed it to him! Kill him!”

After driving him into a corner like that, the Demon Buddha soothed him with all sincerity.

“You know you must not do that, don’t you? The Second Young Master declared that he would fight for the succession without bloodshed. If you poison him to death, no one will follow you, Eldest Young Master. Not the Demon Venerables, nor the demonic practitioners beneath them.”

Geom Muyang looked down at the medicine bottle and said,

“That is why… I am considering using it on myself.”

Slap!

Geom Muyang’s head snapped to the side.

The Demon Buddha had leapt up and slapped him mercilessly across the cheek.

Startled, Geom Muyang looked at him. He had never dreamed the Demon Buddha would lay a hand on his body, so he was more than shocked.

The Demon Buddha held out his hand.

“Give it to me.”

Along with golden radiance, a cold aura was pouring from the Demon Buddha’s body.

Geom Muyang had never seen it before. The Demon Buddha getting this angry in front of him.

“Give it to me!”

Overwhelmed by his force, Geom Muyang handed him the medicine bottle.

The Demon Buddha opened the stopper and poured it straight down the cliff. Then he threw the bottle away as well.

For a long while, neither of them spoke.

“That was the first time I’ve ever been hit.”

“It was also my first time striking the Cult Leader’s blood kin.”

He had thought the prideful Geom Muyang would go mad with rage, but unexpectedly, he was calm. If he had struck him when he said he would kill Geom Mugeuk, perhaps he might have exploded.

“That poison cost a great deal. What a waste.”

“What was wasted was the time you spent thinking such futile thoughts.”

The corner of Geom Muyang’s mouth, which had been curled upward all along, returned to its place. Geom Muyang sat down. Until now, he had been speaking while looking down at the Demon Buddha, but only now did their eye levels meet.

“Eldest Young Master, look at the sky once.”

The Demon Buddha looked up at the sky.

Geom Muyang, who had only been gazing at the distant Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, also turned his eyes toward the sky.

Originally, the Demon Buddha had not been this kind of person. Geom Muyang felt it. That he himself, and the Demon Buddha as well, had changed a great deal. Everyone was changing as they became entangled with Geom Mugeuk.

“You must overcome your anxiety to become the successor. If the Second Young Master has swept in like a typhoon, then you, Eldest Young Master, must become a boulder. Become a boulder that stands firm even against a typhoon.”

Geom Muyang’s eyes trembled. This man, the Demon Buddha, was sincere.

The last Demon Venerable remaining to him.

Come to think of it, the Demon Buddha had been there from the beginning.

He was the Demon Venerable who had remained from the beginning to the end.

“I am not a boulder, but merely a pebble rolling in the typhoon. Elder, you should go to Mugeuk too! Mugeuk will understand your heart better. He will speak better than I do, and he will be more considerate too.”

“That is why I dislike it. That is my role, so why should the Second Young Master do it? I am the older one, so I should be considerate and I should understand. Just as I am now trying to understand you, Eldest Young Master.”

Geom Muyang choked up.

The last time he had met Geom Mugeuk, he had said this.

—If you think the Demon Venerables are your people just because they are smiling in front of you, that is a grave mistake. In the end, at the final moment, they will make the choice that benefits them.

He had lived with such a heart until now.

“Why did you choose me?”

The wind that blew in shook the hems of their clothes.

“It seems I like you better than the Second Young Master, Eldest Young Master.”

The Demon Buddha said it with a joking smile, but Geom Muyang did not smile.

When his life had been driven all the way to the edge of a cliff, someone had been protecting him before that cliff.

The Demon Buddha, who was only half the height of an adult, was standing in front of him like a giant, blocking the way.

“Then I will go down first.”

The Demon Buddha gave his farewell and turned around.

“Demon Buddha.”

“Yes?”

“Actually, that medicine earlier was not the Formless Deadly Poison. It was an internal injury remedy made by the Demonic Physician. I had not the slightest intention of drinking it.”

“I know.”

Geom Muyang was startled.

“You knew?”

The Demon Buddha took an identical medicine bottle from his robes.

“I have one too.”

“You hit me even though you knew?”

The Demon Buddha gave an awkward smile.

“When else would I ever get a chance to hit you, Eldest Young Master?”

Without waiting for an answer, the Demon Buddha quickly turned and descended the mountain.

Watching him, Geom Muyang eventually let out a dumbfounded laugh.

Geom Muyang also rose from his seat. Instead of looking at the distant Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, he looked up at the sky.

“…If I go on like this, I will lose in the end.”

Perhaps he had already lost.

Still, he could not simply lose. He thought he would have to do his best until the very end.

For some reason, he felt that at least today, he would not have nightmares.

* * *

“Are you going to keep getting on my nerves?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had been reading a book, lifted his head and looked at me.

I had been pacing back and forth in the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s room.

“I’m in the middle of an important dilemma.”

“So why are you having that important dilemma here?”

“Maybe because there are so many books here, but I feel like I’m getting smarter.”

“Then go into the Heavenly Demon Library and think there. You’ll become the smartest person in the world.”

“But you’re not there, Elder.”

I stealthily approached the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“What can I do when you’re the one who comes to mind whenever I’m in a crisis?”

“Don’t you have that masked fellow you like? You’re close enough to yield ten-thousand-year snow ginseng to him. Go ask him.”

I forcefully held back my laughter.

This stiff, fussy man would get jealous in ways that didn’t suit him. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was especially jealous of the Smiling Demon.

“Who could compare to our elder? Tell the Smiling Demon to sit over there in the Valley of Villains and stare at the wall.”

“That’s not it, is it? You’re just not disturbing him so he can have a good time with the woman he came with.”

“So you know that the Mistress of Cheonhwaru has come too.”

“Even if I don’t want to know, reports of every kind fly in all day.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon read his book.

“So take that Golden Grand Operation or whatever it is to the masked fellow or the drunkard and discuss it with them.”

I sat on the floor beside the window seat where the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was reading.

“I gave the operation a grand name, but I haven’t made a single detailed plan. Since you’ve read so many books, there must be a method inside one of them.”

“You brat, books are where I come to rest from those ridiculous operations. Find your answer there.”

“Good. Then I’ll stay here until I find the answer!”

“Who said you could?”

The moment I threw myself onto the bed, an invisible force pushed me away.

“Don’t lie on the bed in clothes you wore outside!”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon moved me to the door with telekinesis.

I leaned against the window beside the door and pondered.

“What exactly is troubling you?”

“What makes this operation difficult is that it isn’t about blindly making the Demon Buddha my person.”

“Why can’t you make him your person?”

There was no way the Blood Heaven Blade Demon did not know the reason.

“Because if I take even the Demon Buddha away, I’ll be pushing my brother too far into a corner.”

That was why I had been worried my brother might make a move he should not.

But the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was looking at this situation from a different perspective than I was.

“Is that really so?”

“Is it not?”

After a brief pause, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon spoke.

“If even the Demon Buddha turns away, wouldn’t the Eldest Young Master give up cleanly? I had thought the Eldest Young Master was at least that much of a person.”

“!”

It seemed I had trusted my brother less than the Blood Heaven Blade Demon did. It was probably because he had tormented me when we were young. I had a prejudice that if driven into a corner, he would end up doing something vile and petty.

But apart from me, there also existed my brother’s relationships with the Demon Venerables. To the Demon Venerables, my brother was someone who stepped back when it was time to step back.

“You must give the Eldest Young Master a proper chance to admit defeat. The things you’re worried about will instead happen when lingering attachment remains.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon, who had been speaking while looking down at his book until now, raised his head and looked at me.

“When you step on someone, you step on them properly. For the Eldest Young Master’s sake as well.”

I looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon without a word.

“Why are you looking at me like that?”

“I say this all the time, but I was thinking how I would live without you, Elder.”

“And I always say this, but you’d live well sharing ten-thousand-year snow ginseng with the masked fellow.”

“You are persistent.”

“Yes, so worry about the grudge on this side. Why are you worrying about those people’s relationship? So impertinent.”

I bowed respectfully to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Thank you. Thanks to you, I found the answer.”

The Blade Demon was right.

Only when I faced him with all my strength would my brother laugh hollowly and acknowledge his defeat.

Half-hearted consideration would only wound my brother’s pride. I would make the Demon Buddha unquestionably my person. Rather, only then would the relationship between my brother and the Demon Buddha reach a new point as well.

Leaving the Blade Demon’s residence, I headed straight toward the Demon Buddha’s quarters, bustling with mad monks.

Chapter 243: As Much as I Suffer, So Does Mabul
-----------------------------------------------
The place where Mabul resided was a massive temple called the Golden Buddha Temple (黃金佛寺).

It consisted of dozens of large and small buildings, most of them painted golden. The pagodas erected here and there also emitted a golden light.

The Four Heavenly Kings statues placed at the entrance to ward off evil ghosts looked different from those at ordinary temples. They were so fearsome in appearance that they seemed like demons themselves.

In the center of the temple stood a Great Golden Buddha statue. It was as large as the giant stone statues erected throughout the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, and it was said that when Mabul's cultivated Golden Darama Demonic Art reached its zenith and he unleashed his martial art, one could see the Great Golden Buddha appear and then vanish.

The monks belonging to the Demon Cult were originally called Demon Monks (魔僧), but the monks of the Golden Buddha Temple used the character for madness (狂), calling them Mad Monks.

When I announced my arrival, a Mad Monk of enormous stature came out to greet me.

“I shall guide you.”

I knew who this Mad Monk was. He was Daegwang (大狂), Mabul's right-hand man. He was great in size, and great in madness.

It wouldn't have been strange at all if he had suddenly lunged at me and strangled me with those prayer beads.

When this large man stood beside Mabul, Mabul looked even smaller. Nevertheless, Mabul had taken this towering man as his right arm.

I wondered if he had chosen Daegwang to show that he felt no inferiority whatsoever about his short stature.

The passing Mad Monks greeted me with their palms together. After my outburst at the training ground last time, I had heard my popularity had risen quite a bit in this place filled with crazy monks. Of course, since Mabul was someone who fervently supported my elder brother, they too officially supported my brother.

Mabul was chanting sutras in the Grand Dharma Hall.

Having only ever seen him following my elder brother around, I found this appearance of him draped in monastic robes and a kasaya, reciting Buddhist scriptures, extremely unfamiliar.

If I could tell that man was indeed Mabul, it was only because of the golden Buddha statue behind him, which looked far more fearsome than ordinary ones.

After paying my respects to the Buddha statue, I sat quietly behind Mabul.

Since he showed no intention of finishing his chant, I quietly closed my eyes and cultivated the Heavenly Demon Guardian God Art. These days, my martial arts training focused on two things. When I had to be aware of my surroundings: the Heavenly Demon Guardian God Art; when no one was around: the Heaven-Soaring Secret Art.

Every martial art I had learned was important, but these two were also extremely important.

The Heavenly Demon Guardian God Art was a martial art akin to a second life, and the Heaven-Soaring Secret Art was necessary to achieve the Great Perfection of the Nine Transformations Demonic Art in time; perhaps from now on, it was the most important martial art after the Nine Transformations Demonic Art.

Unfortunately, I was still studying the formulas of the Heaven-Soaring Secret Art. Because the formulas were so abstruse, unleashing the martial art was no easy task.

How much time had passed?

The sutra chant Mabul had been reciting began to grow louder, and demonic energy began to coil around me. Naturally, the Heavenly Demon Guardian God Art activated, protecting my body.

Just as I thought I heard the chanting in my head, an illusion appeared.

Within pitch-black darkness.

A golden radiance shone from afar, and it began walking toward me.

It was Mabul. In this illusion, Mabul was tall.

He walked right up to me and stopped. I could see his flustered face.

Mabul extended his hand. A hand that couldn't cross over and was repelled. There was some invisible, transparent barrier between him and me that prevented him from crossing. And it seemed Mabul couldn't see me beyond the barrier.

I could tell. That Mabul had tried to invade my mind with the Golden Darama Demonic Art to read my thoughts but had been blocked. The Heavenly Demon Guardian God Art had activated and repelled the intrusion. Because the Heavenly Demon Guardian God Art I had learned, together with the Nine Transformations Demonic Art, was an absolute demonic art that took precedence over any other demonic art.

Mabul made a troubled expression, then soon turned and walked back into the darkness. The golden light vanished into the dark.

Why had he appeared before me with such a tall stature?

I thought that perhaps, in this brief moment, it was I who had peered into his heart instead.

The chanting that had been echoing in my head gradually faded and soon disappeared.

When I opened my eyes, Mabul was sitting turned toward me.

He had the look of someone flustered that his demonic art hadn't worked.

But soon, as if nothing had happened, Mabul pressed his palms together and spoke.

“Second Young Master. I'm relieved you returned safely this time.”

“It's thanks to your concern.”

I also pressed my palms together and paid my respects.

“But wouldn't it have been better if I hadn't returned safely?”

Mabul slowly rolled his prayer beads as he spoke.

“The Buddha said all life is precious.”

To hear about the preciousness of life from someone whose prayer beads seemed liable to fly like hidden weapons at any moment.

My gaze naturally turned toward the golden Buddha statue behind him. Looking at the Buddhist painting (佛畫) drawn behind the statue, I said,

“Over there, Demon Monks are tearing people apart and killing them, aren't they?”

At my unexpected words, the corner of Mabul's mouth rose slightly. He was forcibly holding back an incredulous snicker.

“Why have you come to see me? Perhaps if you wish to take refuge in Buddhism, I shall personally shave your head. That would be good. As you wish, there would be no need for bloodshed between brothers.”

“I like to play around, so I'd be expelled for breaking the precepts soon enough. My elder brother is steadfast and taciturn, so he'd be a better fit. And his shaved head would look prettier too.”

Though our words passed like jokes, we were engaged in a fierce battle of auras.

“The reason I came to see you today is...”

After a brief pause, I spoke to him.

“It is because of Operation: Golden Buddha.”

By informing him of the operation's name, I initiated this operation.

Mabul asked with a suspicious look.

“Operation: Golden Buddha? What is that?”

“It is the operation to make you my man. Today is the day it begins.”

In the end, Mabul couldn't hold back his laughter.

“I truly cannot help but acknowledge that you are an unusual person. I acknowledge it, I acknowledge it.”

“You used to call me a crazy bastard before. I've improved.”

Mabul suddenly stopped laughing and spoke.

“Do not underestimate the Eldest Young Master. The successor will be the Eldest Young Master.”

“When I went to visit the Demon Lords, I should have started with the elders. Then right now, a Demon Lord would be somewhere saying, 'Do not underestimate the Second Young Master. The successor will be the Second Young Master.'”

Mabul scoffed.

“Do you think they would fall for you just because of that?”

“What do you think?”

“Not a chance.”

Watching this unwavering loyalty toward my elder brother, his first impression as a politically ambitious monk consumed by greed for power was so regrettable that he could very well feel aggrieved by it.

“Why do you like my elder brother so much?”

“Your elder brother is...”

There seemed to be some reason, but he didn't tell me. I thought that I would only be able to hear that reason once Mabul became my man.

“I believe the Eldest Young Master is better suited to be the Heavenly Demon.”

I nodded silently.

“Does that mean you think the same?”

“I acknowledge that there are certainly areas where my elder brother does things more meticulously.”

“Then concede.”

Instead of answering, I asked him.

“Isn't it hard serving my elder brother?”

“Are you trying to drive a wedge between me and the Eldest Young Master? It won't work.”

“As I've said before, Mabul, you are a little giant. Obtaining you was my elder brother's greatest fortune. I thank you.”

“Why are you thanking me?”

Mabul gave a suspicious look. Knowing he wouldn't easily open his heart, I calmly conveyed my feelings.

“I told you before that I intended to put an end to the killing in this generation's succession struggle. Since my elder brother's pride is strong, controlling him is difficult, so I asked you to help him from the side. Do you remember?”

“I remember.”

“I believe it is all thanks to you, Mabul, that my elder brother hasn't gone on a rampage until now.”

“I said that if the Eldest Young Master went on a rampage, I would pour oil on the fire. That I would urge him to blow everything away. So why do you think I stop him?”

I gazed at him and spoke calmly.

“Because no one knows my elder brother better than you. If you weren't by his side, he wouldn't have been able to endure like this.”

It was my sincere feeling. Regardless of whether I could draw him to become my man or not, I was truly grateful to him. For steadfastly staying by my elder brother's side until I came to visit like this.

“I shall take my leave for today.”

I pressed my palms together respectfully and bid farewell.

Mabul wordlessly turned back and began chanting sutras again.

Through my conversation with him, I realized for certain.

That though he was the smallest in stature, he was the most difficult person to win over.

Now it begins.

* * *

The next day, I greeted Mabul as he exited the Golden Buddha Temple and approached him.

“Have you eaten?”

“I have not.”

“Will you join me?”

I thought he would naturally refuse, but he readily accepted.

“Very well.”

When I made a surprised expression, Mabul asked.

“Why are you so surprised?”

“I didn't think you would permit it so easily.”

“You are mistaken about one thing. I am a very moderate and compassionate person.”

Moderation and compassion? To spout such shameless words so nonchalantly.

“That's a relief. I was planning to follow you around every day.”

Mabul took the first step, and I followed behind him.

“What do you like? Just say the word!”

Passing demonic followers looked at us. Their eyes contained the sentiment: Is even Mabul now associating with the Second Young Master?

But Mabul paid them no mind. In the past, he was someone who would have been uncomfortable because such talk would reach my elder brother and cause misunderstandings.

Mabul, walking ahead with a resolute expression, looking only forward... had definitely changed.

* * *

That evening, I went to see the Blood Heaven Blade Demon again.

Seo Daeryong, who had returned after finishing his duties, was receiving martial arts instruction.

With the affairs of the Hwangcheon Pavilion and his martial arts training, Seo Daeryong was always the busiest person.

“I will wait in the room.”

“Why did you come again?”

“Didn't I say I would stay here until I found the answer?”

At that, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon shouted.

“Don't lie on the bed without washing!”

“It's not a bed where women visit; aren't you being too fastidious?”

Then Seo Daeryong stepped up for his master and raised his voice.

“Who said that? That women don't come.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon and I looked at Seo Daeryong. The one more startled by those words was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Don't disrespect our master!”

Heavy silence fell.

I spoke with my eyes. Daeryong, this doesn't seem right!

Seo Daeryong spoke with his eyes. Pavilion Lord, what should I do?

“Women used to line up for him in his youth. He's still vigorous even now.”

I sighed with my eyes, and eventually Seo Daeryong's voice trailed off.

“...So don't disrespect him.”

In the end, Seo Daeryong hung his head deeply and spoke.

“I will prepare for hell training starting tomorrow.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon said in a low voice.

“Tomorrow? It starts now!”

* * *

Seo Daeryong's training only ended when the night had grown late.

After sending his disciple away, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon entered the room. Until then, I had been reading a book. I couldn't remember how long it had been since I'd read a book that wasn't a martial arts manual.

“Do you teach this late?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon nodded and answered.

“The boy is clever, so teaching him is quite enjoyable.”

“He is the one who entered the Hwangcheon Pavilion as the top student, after all.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon sat in a chair by the window and asked.

“But why has someone who should be carrying out a grand operation come here?”

“Didn't I tell you? When I feel a crisis in my life, you're the only one I think of.”

“It's not easy, is it?”

“He's impregnable. He's bound tight by loyalty to my elder brother. Might I say it's similar to the relationship between you and me?”

“Then wouldn't a ten-thousand-year snow ginseng root solve it?”

I let out a sigh, pretending not to have heard.

“It's not easy.”

Then the Blood Heaven Blade Demon laughed and spoke of past events.

“I once drank with Mabul at a pleasure house in the past. Back then, I asked him the same thing. 'It's not easy, is it?'”

“And what did he say?”

“Mabul answered just like you, saying it wasn't easy. It's amusing, the two of you telling me to my face that it's not easy.”

I pictured the two of them drinking.

It must have been a very stifling time for Mabul. The very fact that he drank with the Blood Heaven Blade Demon showed as much.

Hidden behind the resolute face he showed me was the anguish of Mabul confessing to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon that it wasn't easy.

Leaning my body against the window frame, I gazed at the moon and repeated once more.

“It's not easy, truly.”

I was reminded once again that nothing is as difficult as winning people's hearts. Given my experiences with various Demon Lords, it should have gotten easier by now, but human relationships were always as difficult as they were the first time.

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked up at the moon with me and spoke.

“You are trying to become the successor to the Heavenly Demon; how could it be easy?”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon's words were a comfort.

Then the Blood Heaven Blade Demon suddenly spoke.

“Mugeuk.”

I was startled for a moment. It was the first time he had called my name.

I looked at him with a trembling heart. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was still looking up at the moon and spoke calmly.

“I know how hard you've tried to win the hearts of us old men. You can take a break when it's hard.”

I looked at the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, deeply moved. The one who used to poke my side with disapproving eyes was now becoming my sanctuary.

I had been about to joke, “What would I do without you, Elder?” but the Blood Heaven Blade Demon beat me to it.

“You can't survive without me.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon looked at me, smiling. I smiled brightly too.

“Thank you for your words. I should take a break as well...”

It was at that very moment. A thought flashed through my mind. If it's not easy for me, and it's not easy for Mabul either, then...?

“Ah! If it's as hard for me, then it must be as hard for Mabul, right? He may not show it on the outside, but his heart must be as urgent as mine, mustn't it?”

The best thing Mabul could do in the current situation was to persuade me. The fact that he readily ate with me today must have been due to such feelings.

“I was too hasty. This isn't the time to push with all my might, but to slowly pull him over to my side.”

It was the moment the first detailed plan of Operation: Golden Buddha was finally being established.

Chapter 244: Because I Do Nothing.
----------------------------------
Mabul came out of the Great Dharma Hall.

It was time to eat, so Geom Mugeuk would be waiting at the entrance.

Today marked the fifth day since he had begun eating with Geom Mugeuk.

They had not talked about anything in particular. They exchanged a few idle jokes, ate, and parted ways. It was much like a probing exchange in a spar, each throwing out feints to test the other.

Geom Mugeuk probably thought this would gradually bring the two of them closer, but that was utter nonsense. The Great Golden Operation? Why should someone else carry out an operation using his gold?

‘Sorry, but that operation is mine.’

Yes, they would grow close. And then, in reverse, he would persuade Geom Mugeuk to give up the seat of Heavenly Demon.

That was Mabul’s plan.

If he could do that, there would be no need to win over the other Demon Venerables. It was the fastest and surest method.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk would not give up easily, but Mabul saw a possibility because of Geom Mugeuk’s personality.

‘The Second Young Master is a man ruled by his moods.’

He was more emotional than anyone Mabul had ever seen. That must have been why the other Demon Venerables had fallen for him. And there was one more thing.

‘He is also someone who always tries to keep his word once he has spoken it.’

And most decisively, he was trying to end this succession struggle peacefully.

‘There is definitely an opportunity.’

His had not been a life spent merely chanting sutras in the Dharma Hall. The fact that people said he was blinded by power meant he had gone about diligently meeting people. He would use that experience to deal with Geom Mugeuk.

‘I am different from the other Demon Venerables!’

Just as he left the Golden Buddhist Temple, firming his resolve—

Whoooosh.

There was no one at the entrance. Geom Mugeuk, who had waited for him every day, was nowhere to be seen.

‘Why didn’t he come today?’

From what Mabul had seen so far, Geom Mugeuk was not the sort of person to give up so easily.

Mabul waited there for Geom Mugeuk for a while.

Had he ever stood outside like this, waiting for someone?

Even by the time he had exhausted that precious patience, Geom Mugeuk did not come.

Geom Mugeuk did not come the next day, nor the day after that. On the third day, unable to endure it any longer, Mabul went to Geom Mugeuk’s residence.

‘What is going on?’

And there, Mabul witnessed an unexpected sight.

Geom Mugeuk was sprawled like a corpse on a broad rock in the yard.

Startled, Mabul hurried over and looked down at Geom Mugeuk. Only then did Geom Mugeuk open his eyes. Their gazes, one looking up and the other looking down, met in the air.

“You’ve come?”

“I thought you were dead.”

“You must have been pleased, if only for a moment.”

Mabul sat down on the rock.

“This is comfortable. It cools your back too. Why don’t you lie down?”

“I’m fine.”

Geom Mugeuk was definitely different from usual.

“You came every day, then suddenly stopped showing up, so I came to see if something had happened.”

After staring up at the sky for a moment, Geom Mugeuk spoke quietly.

“Three days ago, I was on my way to see you, Mabul, when I happened to look up at the sky.”

Mabul’s gaze naturally turned toward the sky as well.

“There wasn’t a single cloud, and one bird was flying across it. As I watched that bird, this thought suddenly came to me: I’m envious.”

Mabul looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“Why am I envious? I have qinggong, so I can fly like that. I can even fly higher and faster. That was when I realized it. Ah, I’m worn out.”

It was a scheme, yes, but it was a scheme that held Geom Mugeuk’s sincerity.

“I’ve been running forward too much, looking only ahead.”

Needless to say, he had run himself ragged in the life before his regression, and even after regressing, he had run without pause. So what he had just said was the truth.

Mabul could not tell what Geom Mugeuk’s intention was in saying such things.

But one thing was certain. His own life had been one spent running forward, looking only ahead.

Born small, he had not wanted to be mocked until the day he died. Pity and help were even more hateful. He had endured hardships truly bad enough to kill him to reach this place.

‘Second Young Master, you have no idea what it means to run forward looking only ahead.’

At that moment, the rock carried two men who had run through life looking only straight ahead.

“From that day on, I spent three or four days just lazing around. I apologize for not sending word.”

To Mabul’s eyes, it did not seem as though Geom Mugeuk was playing some other trick. For a trick to be a trick, there had to be something to gain. What could he gain by lying here like a ruined man?

‘Well, at such a young age he’s accomplished so much. It’s only natural he’d be exhausted.’

He thought that if he used this exhaustion, he might be able to make Geom Mugeuk give up the succession more easily than expected.

Mabul made a casual suggestion.

“Why don’t you take this chance to rest for a while? Set down the burden of having to become the successor.”

Inwardly, Mabul prayed that this answer would come.

—Yes, I think I’ll just throw away the succession and everything else.

But instead of answering, Geom Mugeuk suddenly asked Mabul,

“Aren’t you tired, Mabul?”

Even at the abrupt question, Mabul did not reveal his feelings at all.

“I’m tired because of you. If you stepped down from the succession competition, I’d be brimming with energy.”

Geom Mugeuk smiled faintly, then suddenly shouted.

“Ah! That’s the bird. The bird that ruined your meal with me!”

A small bird was flying here and there.

“How do you know it’s that bird?”

“The way it flies is exactly that bastard. Look over there. Can’t you see it saying, I’m free, aren’t I? You’re envious, aren’t you?”

“To my eyes… it just seems to be saying that because it keeps flying only forward, its wings hurt too much. That it’s exhausted.”

Geom Mugeuk looked at Mabul.

Mabul was still looking at the bird. He had said it like a joke, but he was sincere. He did not envy that bird at all.

The sight of it flapping its wings ceaselessly as it flew was like looking at himself. He had always been busy. Moving with the First Young Master, meeting people, controlling the Mad Monks, training in martial arts.

It’s hard because you’re weak. Only those with rotten spirits say they’re tired. He had lived with such words on his lips. And all the while, the years had passed far too quickly. That had made him even more anxious. That had made his life even busier.

‘And so what remains for me is…’

After being lost in various thoughts for a long while, Mabul looked at Geom Mugeuk.

At some point, he had fallen asleep beside him. Seeing him sleep with Mabul right there beside him, Mabul was inwardly startled.

‘He trusts me this much?’

He thought it was an act. Since the Second Young Master was such a fox, it had to be an act meant to deceive him into thinking he was trusted this much.

‘That won’t work on me, Second Young Master.’

Mabul rose from the rock without a sound.

‘It won’t work on me…’

As Mabul walked far away, out of the house, Geom Mugeuk spoke quietly with his eyes still closed.

“Mabul, forget the successor seat and everything else. Let’s rest a little.”

I’ll tug gently, so be gently drawn in.

* * *

The next day, Geom Mugeuk still did not come.

Mabul naturally made his way to Geom Mugeuk’s residence.

Geom Mugeuk was not home, and just then a maidservant came out of the building.

“Where has the Second Young Master gone?”

“He received word from the Thousand Poisons Forest and went there.”

“The Thousand Poisons Forest?”

Mabul, who had been about to return to his own residence, turned his steps toward the Thousand Poisons Forest.

He thought the Poison King was on the First Young Master’s side, but the Poison King had not yet officially announced his support for the First Young Master.

That Poison King had called Geom Mugeuk, so Mabul intended to go and see. What they talked about, and how they got along.

His steps toward the Thousand Poisons Forest quickened.

* * *

It had been a while since I came to the Thousand Poisons Forest.

On the way, I met several poison adepts. After I had barked with the Poison King on the training ground, the looks they cast at me had become quite varied. They ranged from “We actively support the Second Young Master” to “How dare you turn our Poison King into a dog!”

When I reached his residence, the Poison King was making something. I went closer and saw that it was not a poison he had made before.

“It seems like an extraordinary poison.”

Absorbed in making the poison, the Poison King did not even answer.

Seeing him fully immersed in his own world after so long was welcome.

I looked around the place for a moment.

This place was exactly as it had been before. The furniture, of course, and even the medicine bottles and tools placed on top of it. They were truly still in the same places.

Then one sight caught my eye.

“Gasp! This is!”

When I was too shocked, the Poison King raised his head.

“It’s something I was eating before and left behind.”

It was still sitting there exactly as it had been.

Instead of nagging him, Please, for heaven’s sake, clean up after yourself! I wrapped that laziness up nicely for him.

“The more dangerous the things one handles, the more everything must remain where it is, in a familiar place. Is that it?”

At that, the Poison King turned his gaze back to the poison he was making and answered.

“No. After coming back, I was busy making this, so I couldn’t clean. The next time someone says it’s dirty, I should answer like that.”

Even before, Sangseon had not entered this space carelessly. The Poison King did the cleaning himself. Thinking about it, he had allowed me into a space that belonged solely to him.

While I did a rough job of cleaning, the Poison King completed the pill.

“It’s done!”

Seeing the rapture flash across his face, it was clearly a poison that had not been easy to make.

It possessed vivid, colorful hues like a flower.

“It looks poisonous. Who on earth did you make it to kill? The Murim Alliance Leader? The Unorthodox Alliance Leader?”

At that, the Poison King abruptly held it out to me.

“Eat it.”

“You’re going to poison the Second Young Master of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult this openly?”

“Can’t I?”

“Then my elder brother must be hiding behind that curtain.”

Even as I said that, I accepted the pill.

“What exactly is this?”

“It’s the Cheongi Pill (天氣丹).”

At the words Cheongi Pill, I was startled.

Among those who used poison, the Cheongi Pill was one of the three greatest poisons of all time, and at the same time a spiritual medicine. It was a spiritual medicine, but not one that increased internal energy.

“I discovered Gillyeongcho (奇靈草) in a corner of the safe house. No one recognized something so precious.”

“Ah! Investigator Seo told me. He said you dug up the entire safe house.”

“I was trying to find the Hwaning Snake (花寧蛇) that would be nearby. Wherever there is Gillyeongcho, there is always a Hwaning Snake. The Cheongi Pill is made by combining the fruit of the Gillyeongcho and the inner core of the Hwaning Snake.”

“Why are you giving me something this terrifying?”

“You should be asking why I’m giving you something this precious.”

As if boasting of the Cheongi Pill’s effects, the Poison King said,

“If you take the Cheongi Pill, your constitution will become one that can withstand any heat or cold. Even with no internal energy, you could be stripped naked and thrown into the desert, or stripped naked and thrown into a snowy mountain, and still survive.”

And that was not all. It would make one far more advantageous when facing martial arts of extreme yang or extreme yin.

“But the problem is that its toxicity is so strong that you die if you eat it, don’t you?”

“That’s right. More than a few people melted while greedily trying to endure it for the sake of the Cheongi Pill’s effects.”

The Poison King stared straight into my eyes and added,

“But you can live, can’t you?”

“!”

For a moment, silence flowed between us.

From his expression and his eyes, I could tell.

“You knew.”

“Yes. I already knew you had a body impervious to all poisons. Did you think I wouldn’t know after being with you for so long?”

As expected, the Poison King was the Poison King.

“What a shame. I was planning to reveal that fact someday at a dramatic moment.”

“I was planning to reveal that I knew at a dramatic moment myself.”

“You succeeded. I was very surprised.”

Even though the Poison King knew I was impervious to all poisons, he was not as displeased as I had expected.

“I thought you would be very offended if you knew this.”

“Why would I be offended? You’re someone I’ve been waiting for a long time.”

“My immunity to all poisons?”

The Poison King’s eyes gleamed.

“I can test all sorts of poisons on you. Now, take this first and tell me everything. How it tastes, how poisonous it is, what would be good to add.”

I playfully stepped back.

“I am the Second Young Master. The man who will become the Heavenly Demon. I am a hellhound who ran across the training ground with you. I’m not a test subject.”

At my joke, the Poison King laughed. I had naturally assumed that because he was the Poison King, he would dislike immunity to all poisons. Only after living this second life did I come to realize that too could be prejudice and a preconceived notion.

“Then I will take it.”

I sat down cross-legged and took the Cheongi Pill. It truly was an extreme poison. If I had not been impervious to all poisons, I would not have been able to fully dissolve this vicious energy.

Its taste, too, was more bitter and poisonous than any spiritual medicine I had taken until now. It set a new record for bitterness and toxicity.

“You made it this bitter on purpose to give me a hard time, didn’t you?”

It was a complaint I truly could not help making.

Thus, I dissolved all the energy of the Cheongi Pill. Unlike other spiritual medicines, the Cheongi Pill’s energy seeped not into my dantian, but throughout the blood vessels of my entire body.

Now, even if I fell into ice-cold water, I would not die of the cold. Now, I would be able to bask in ordinary flames as if warming myself by a campfire.

I bowed deeply to the Poison King.

“I will never forget the grace of you bestowing such a precious spiritual medicine upon me.”

“Forget grace. Can you answer one question?”

“Even a hundred would be fine.”

“Who is the one you are trying to face?”

The Poison King had sensed it. The fact that there was some powerful goal behind my endless drive forward.

“If I discover Gillyeongcho, I will send word to you immediately.”

I had said, in a roundabout way, that I could not tell him. With that alone, I had given him half an answer. Because it was not “There is no such person.”

The Poison King’s gaze toward me deepened. Just as he was about to say something, Sangseon came and reported.

“Mabul has arrived.”

“Why has Mabul come here?”

Startled, the Poison King glared at me.

“As if everything else weren’t enough, you even made Mabul come to the Thousand Poisons Forest. What on earth did you do?”

It seemed Mabul had probably never once come to the Thousand Poisons Forest.

“If I had done something, he would never have come here. Because I did nothing, he came.”

“I don’t know what you mean.”

The Poison King quickly tried to leave through the back door.

“Tell him I’m not here. That man Mabul makes me uncomfortable.”

I swiftly blocked his way.

“I have a favor to ask.”

“Next time.”

“If you grant me this favor, I’ll come running whenever you need me for poison experiments.”

The Poison King let out a sigh, then ordered Sangseon to bring Mabul in.

“If I don’t grant it, you’ll pretend to be busy that day, won’t you? Or run away through the back door.”

“You know I won’t do that.”

“That’s why I’m granting it. Fine, what favor is it? Do you want me to persuade Mabul to abandon the First Young Master and come to your side? Or should I poison him without a trace and bury him in the Thousand Poisons Forest?”

“It may be even harder than that.”

My request was something the Poison King had never expected.

“Please play with us for just one day.”

Chapter 245: I Will Play with the Bloodstorm
--------------------------------------------
The Poison King was unable to say anything for a moment.

Blinking, he asked,

“Are you asking me to play with you right now? And with the Demon Buddha as well?”

“Yes.”

The Poison King pressed his palm to Geom Mugeuk’s forehead, checking whether he had a fever.

“This is a side effect of the Heavenly Secret Pill.”

The Poison King even took his wrist and checked his pulse.

“Is spending one day having fun really that hard for you?”

Geom Mugeuk blocked the Poison King as he tried to flee out the back door.

“You don’t need to feel too burdened. I’ll be there too.”

“Just tell me to go to the training ground and bark again instead. What on earth is wrong with you?”

“I want to rest for a day. I also want to have fun with you, Poison King. Since we’re at it, spend the day with me.”

Just as the Poison King let out a sigh, Sangseon announced from outside that the Demon Buddha had arrived.

The Poison King cursed Geom Mugeuk with his expression and went outside.

“An honored guest has come.”

“Have you been well?”

The two exchanged courteous greetings. Unlike how he had tried to run away, saying the Demon Buddha made him uncomfortable, the Poison King treated him properly, never losing the dignity of a Demon Venerable.

“This is your first time in the Thousand Poison Forest, is it not?”

“It is. As I came in, I saw that there were scenic views everywhere.”

“There are many even better places.”

In truth, the Poison King had also never been to the Golden Buddha Temple where the Demon Buddha resided. Since he loved secluding himself in the Thousand Poison Forest so much, he had never had reason to visit the residences of the other Demon Venerables.

After finishing his greeting, the Demon Buddha looked at Geom Mugeuk.

“Second Young Master, so you were here.”

He said it as though he had not known Geom Mugeuk was here when he came. His acting was quite good. Geom Mugeuk, though perfectly aware that he had come because of him, responded to acting with acting.

“As expected! It seems you and I are connected by fate, Demon Buddha. To think we would meet even in a place like this.”

“It seems so.”

“Seeing how glad I am to see you, it seems the time I spent with you recently was not wasted.”

Even with the Poison King beside him, Geom Mugeuk revealed his feelings without hesitation.

“Would that not make the Poison King feel slighted?”

The Poison King cut in and asked,

“Why would I feel slighted?”

“Is there anyone in our cult who does not know of the special friendship between you two?”

He was referring to how they had barked together in the training ground.

The Poison King looked at Geom Mugeuk and shook his head.

“Ever since I met the Second Young Master, I’ve been through all manner of strange things.”

The Poison King’s sound transmission immediately followed to Geom Mugeuk.

—This is the strangest thing of all!

Geom Mugeuk turned his head, pretending not to hear.

Once the greetings were over, the Poison King led the Demon Buddha to the guest hall of the Thousand Poison Sect.

“Now, please come this way.”

It was a place like an open pavilion, and the atmosphere was excellent, harmonizing with the surrounding natural scenery. Since hardly anyone ever visited, it was not usually used, but Sangseon always kept it clean.

One person looked abnormally young, and one person was abnormally small. With Geom Mugeuk there as well, the gathering truly looked special.

“Was the matter you left the cult for resolved well?”

“Thanks to your concern, it was resolved well.”

The conversation cut off, and silence continued for a moment.

Though the Demon Buddha met many people, even he felt awkward in this setting. He had hardly interacted with the Poison King normally, and that youthful appearance was truly difficult to get used to. Of course, the other party might feel the same when looking at him.

The Poison King’s sound transmission flew to Geom Mugeuk.

—I’m already suffocating, and you want me to have fun? A life-and-death duel would be more entertaining.

Fortunately, Sangseon brought tea, refreshing the atmosphere.

“The tea tastes excellent!”

Geom Mugeuk drank his tea comfortably, but the Demon Buddha only fiddled with his teacup and did not bring it to his lips. If one of his subordinates had drunk tea here as comfortably as Geom Mugeuk, he would have thundered at him. You drank tea served by the Poison King? You damned fool! Better you die by my hand! That was how fearsome the Poison King was to everyone.

“To be honest, the reason I came today is because of the Second Young Master.”

The Demon Buddha glanced at Geom Mugeuk, then continued.

“I heard that the Second Young Master was here and came to pay a visit. These days, I have become deeply engrossed in the pleasure of associating with him.”

Geom Mugeuk could clearly feel that the Demon Buddha was no longer the Demon Buddha of the past. As though he had staked everything on this matter with him, he was showing a new side of himself. Such frankness had not been the Demon Buddha’s way.

“I am curious. What sort of conversations do you usually have with the Second Young Master, Poison King?”

At that, the Poison King said,

“Today, I called him over and had him do some cleaning.”

The Demon Buddha flinched in surprise at those words. He thought the Poison King was expressing displeasure at his question.

“It seems I said something rude for no reason.”

Just then, Geom Mugeuk cut in.

“I really did clean.”

To the startled Demon Buddha, Geom Mugeuk spoke as if making a complaint.

“Did you think I got close to the Demon Venerables just by wagging my tongue? I put in tremendous effort too.”

If he had truly gone so far as to clean, then the Demon Buddha could tell that Geom Mugeuk saying it was hard had not been mere trickery.

“Even so, choose your work carefully.”

He did not add, “When you are someone who may become the Heavenly Demon.” After all, making sure he could not become the Heavenly Demon was his goal.

At those words, another awkward silence flowed. Put another way, it meant the Poison King had made him do work without choosing carefully.

Even so, the Poison King did not express displeasure to the Demon Buddha. He could do that to Geom Mugeuk instead.

—You heard that, didn’t you? Those words that make people uncomfortable. And how am I supposed to have fun with him?

—He means no harm. You know that.

—I can’t even have fun by myself, so how am I supposed to have fun together?

It was not as though only he was like that. Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Buddha were also people who had never once truly played in their lives. That was precisely why Geom Mugeuk wanted to spend a good time with them. It was not an opportunity that came often.

—First, why don’t you show us around the Thousand Poison Forest?

No sooner had Geom Mugeuk’s sound transmission ended than the Poison King said to the Demon Buddha,

“Since you have come all this way, I will show you around the Thousand Poison Forest.”

The Demon Buddha had been about to say he would come and see it another time, but Geom Mugeuk sprang to his feet first.

“Let’s go. It’ll be fun.”

And so the Poison King guided them into the Thousand Poison Forest.

The Thousand Poison Forest was broad and dense, and after they walked for about a quarter-hour, they came upon a splendid bamboo grove with a walking path laid out.

“Oh, so there was a place like this.”

It was a direction Geom Mugeuk had not come before. After passing through the bamboo grove, there was a giant tree that must have lived for a thousand years, and beyond it was a field where nameless flowers bloomed in full.

The three of them walked slowly, intoxicated by the beautiful scenery.

The Demon Buddha, who had not been inclined at first, gradually became absorbed in the scenery of the Thousand Poison Forest as well. Exclamations of how wonderful it was kept slipping from his mouth.

“Be careful.”

A brilliantly colored snake was flicking its tongue from a tree.

“It is a Hundred-Spot Snake (百點蛇). Without an antidote, even an expert cannot last a day against its deadly venom.”
