Geom Mugeuk thought that the Poison King must have named that snake too.

Along the way, Geom Mugeuk asked about the poisonous herbs, poisonous insects, and venomous snakes they saw, and the Poison King excitedly explained them to him.

The Demon Buddha thought that Geom Mugeuk’s behavior was meant to put the Poison King in a good mood.

‘At least I must acknowledge his diligence.’

But for that to be the case, Geom Mugeuk asked about absolutely everything. The Poison King also told him everything he knew, and Geom Mugeuk listened with utmost seriousness.

At that moment, the Demon Buddha said,

“The Second Young Master is quite curious.”

“How many chances does a person have in life to hear explanations about poison-related things directly from the Poison King?”

In truth, Geom Mugeuk was committing as much of what the Poison King said to memory as he could.

He himself was immune to all poisons, so it did not matter, but who knew when someone else might be poisoned?

The Demon Buddha also listened to the explanations beside him. He had always been the one teaching others, but for the first time in a long while, he learned many things. There was pleasure in learning.

After walking for quite some time, they arrived at a valley where a waterfall came pouring down.

Swaaaaaaa.

They decided to rest there for a while.

“I’m hot, so I’ll swim a bit.”

Geom Mugeuk stripped off his upper garment and tossed it aside, then plunged into the water with a splash.

“It is good that one does not get bored when with the Second Young Master.”

At the Demon Buddha’s words, the Poison King nodded. In this moment, if he was merely bored because Geom Mugeuk was absent, that would have been fortunate. To the Poison King right now, this was an ordeal.

Just as the Poison King was wondering whether he should also jump into the water, the Demon Buddha brought up the matter of the successor.

“I acknowledge that the momentum is tilting toward the Second Young Master. That is why the First Young Master is in a situation where he absolutely needs your support, Poison King.”

The Demon Buddha intended to use this opportunity to obtain the Poison King’s official support for the First Young Master.

“Whether the First Young Master will become the better cult leader, or the Second Young Master will become the better cult leader, that is something we cannot know, is it not?”

Seeing the Poison King nod, the Demon Buddha continued.

“There are times when I feel fear when I look at the Second Young Master. I wonder whether that strange fervor of his might even draw in the murim.”

At that, the Poison King, who had only been listening, suddenly said,

“Would that be wrong?”

“It would not necessarily be wrong, but…”

“I think what we should worry about is not that fervor, but the absence of any fervor at all.”

Though the Poison King had been awkward, he said what needed to be said clearly.

On the other hand, the Demon Buddha had to feel the shock of having been struck at the heart of the matter. That was because he had always felt regret that the First Young Master did not possess even a little of the same fervor as Geom Mugeuk.

The Poison King looked at Geom Mugeuk. Geom Mugeuk, who had been swimming, was shouting as he moved under the waterfall. It was impossible to tell whether he liked it or whether it hurt.

“I believe the Second Young Master is more suited to become the Heavenly Demon.”

At the Poison King’s words, the Demon Buddha was flustered. The Poison King had chosen Geom Mugeuk.

“Being stronger in martial arts, or being better with words, does not necessarily make one a better Heavenly Demon, does it?”

“That is not why. I see it in the Second Young Master.”

“What is it that you see?”

The Poison King looked at Geom Mugeuk, who was making a fuss while sitting beneath the waterfall, and said,

“I see our fate.”

The Poison King acknowledged that because he liked his own world so much, he was poor at considering other people’s feelings.

But he felt it. The more he looked at Geom Mugeuk, the more he sensed that some immense destiny was approaching them.

Rather, he believed he could feel it precisely because he had spent so much time in a world of his own. Something that the Demon Buddha, entangled in countless relationships, could never see.

“You have fallen for the Second Young Master’s intentions. You have been taken in by his tricks and schemes.”

“If the Second Young Master were that kind of person, then what he asked of me today would have been to persuade you, Demon Buddha.”

“Then what did he ask?”

“He said the three of us should have fun today.”

“!”

In that instant, the Demon Buddha had a thought. Ah, it will not be easy to defeat the Second Young Master. How does one defeat a madman like this? The confidence he had possessed at the start shriveled all at once.

“If the Second Young Master becomes the Heavenly Demon, a storm of blood may instead blow.”

“The Second Young Master will be playing together with that storm of blood.”

The Demon Buddha said no more. He sensed that the Poison King’s resolve was firm.

‘This will not be easy.’

If he could not change the Poison King’s mind, then the Demon Venerables supporting the First Young Master would be only himself and the Fist Demon. And even the Fist Demon’s support was halfhearted.

At that moment, Geom Mugeuk, who had been pretending to be a cultivator beneath the waterfall, swam toward them.

“Did you see my waterfall training? As if I had become a martial artist dreaming of revenge through extreme training…”

Without listening to the rest, the Poison King stood and said,

“There are still things left to see. Let us go.”

The Demon Buddha also sprang to his feet and followed behind him.

Geom Mugeuk shouted as he came out of the water.

“Why? Did I do something wrong? Let’s go together! I said, let me dry my clothes first!”

I told them to rest. It seemed they had used that brief time to have an important conversation. Well, how could people who had never played in their lives learn to do so easily? It would be fortunate if they did not fight.

Fwoooooosh!

As Geom Mugeuk walked, he released his blazing yang energy. Steam rose in wisps, and his clothes began drying in an instant.

* * *

I was grateful to the Poison King.

He was doing his best to grant my request. Otherwise, he would not have made this sort of suggestion.

“Let us make a wager.”

At the word wager, the Demon Buddha showed interest. He was curious what kind of wager it was, and he had a strong competitive spirit and liked wagers in themselves.

“What kind of wager?”

“In this forest, there is a poisonous herb called Seven-Life Grass (七生草), with leaves split into seven branches and purple fruit. Let us wager on who finds it first.”

“Is that not too disadvantageous for us? You must know the terrain here thoroughly, Poison King.”

“I do not know where the Seven-Life Grass is either. I know for certain that it exists, but perhaps it is not fated with me, for I have been unable to find it.”

Since he did not seem likely to lie about something like this, the Demon Buddha asked again,

“If it is a wager, there should be a reward.”

As the two of them pondered, I spoke.

“Next time we have time, the losers can buy the one who found it first a grand round of drinks at Pungryu Tavern.”

The Poison King and the Demon Buddha nodded. A competitive fire blazed in the Demon Buddha’s eyes. At least to him, this meant more than a simple wager over drinks.

“Good. Let us do it.”

And so we wandered around searching for the Seven-Life Grass.

Even after searching for a long while, it was not easy to find. I even used the Divine Eye Art to search, but I still could not find it.

“I found it!”

The one who found the Seven-Life Grass was the Demon Buddha, who had darted all over the forest with his small body.

The Poison King and I rushed to him with qinggong.

“Is this it?”

“Oh! It is! This is the Seven-Life Grass.”

The Poison King looked genuinely moved.

“To think you discovered something this precious. You truly have talent for gathering spiritual herbs. Later, let us go to that forest over there as well.”

As he said that, the Poison King carefully dug up the Seven-Life Grass. His manner was so full of care that I asked, half in doubt,

“You didn’t make this wager because you needed the Seven-Life Grass, did you?”

In that instant, the Poison King’s back flinched as he was digging up the Seven-Life Grass.

The Poison King tucked the Seven-Life Grass carefully into his robe and said to the Demon Buddha,

“When you come next time, let us come out to have fun again. Demon Buddha, you may come whenever you like!”

After greeting him as though bidding farewell, the Poison King launched himself away.

He left just like that.

As the Poison King disappeared into the distance, the Demon Buddha looked at me with eyes that seemed to ask, What on earth just happened?

“We were tricked! He mobilized us as herb gatherers!”

The Demon Buddha took my words, which might have been sincere, as a joke.

“It seems he found this gathering quite uncomfortable.”

From far away, the Poison King glanced back at me.

His gaze said this: I had a good time.

And…

My efforts end here, so now it is your turn.

Chapter 246: No More of That Cramped Stifling Place
---------------------------------------------------
“I want to play a little longer.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Mabul nodded.

“Then play some more.”

Mabul staked all his hopes on changing Geom Mugeuk’s mind.

“But let us leave this place. I have no desire to wander around somewhere teeming with venomous insects without the Poison King.”

“Let’s do that.”

Geom Mugeuk and Mabul retraced the path by which they had come.

Once the Poison King was gone, Mabul’s expression looked much more at ease. It seemed Mabul, too, had been uncomfortable around the Poison King.

“Thank you.”

“For what?”

“You came all the way here to find me, didn’t you?”

“You seemed to be having a hard time lately.”

“I swam for the first time in a while, and took a walk. I feel much better. What about you, Lord Mabul?”

“I enjoyed it as well.”

But Mabul was not actually in such a good mood. When even the Poison King declared his support for Geom Mugeuk, Mabul’s sense of urgency only grew. Now there was only one method left: changing the mind of Geom Mugeuk himself.

“They say those who know how to play are the ones who play well. I meant to have a grand time playing with you today, Lord Mabul, but even that isn’t easy.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s candid words, Mabul replied.

“You are young, so you may feel that you must do something for it to count as playing, but when you get older, resting is playing.”

Mabul stopped for a moment and looked up at the sky.

“It has been a long time since I rested like this.”

That Mabul was someone who lived constantly on edge could be seen even in this brief moment of gazing at the sky. He looked as though he had risen from the deep sea to take a short breath. When he spat venom beside his brother, he had seemed like nothing more than a vicious Demon Supreme, but…

“The Blade Demon always tells me this. If it is hard, rest before going on.”

Mabul looked at Geom Mugeuk and asked.

“Do I look like I am having a hard time?”

Then came an unexpected answer.

“Whenever I see you, Lord Mabul, I think you must have lived more earnestly than anyone else.”

It was not the Divine Art of Flattery. It was sincere.

“You have endured much until now.”

Though Mabul would likely take it as flattery.

This was precisely what Geom Mugeuk had been striving to keep to lately.

If there was something he ought to say to someone, he would not put it off, but say it.

Mabul, too, must have wished for someone to truly recognize his hardships, and so Geom Mugeuk expressed it like this.

You’ve had it hard, Mabul.

Mabul felt it. What was contained in Geom Mugeuk’s gaze was sincerity.

Mabul tried hard not to reveal his emotions, but a storm of feeling flashed through his eyes.

‘Damn it!’

How could someone so young look at a person with such depth in his eyes?

Mabul moved first, and Geom Mugeuk quietly followed behind him.

The two walked in silence for a long time before stopping for a while at a place with a fine view.

“Let’s rest here for a bit before we go.”

“Let’s.”

Geom Mugeuk dipped his feet into the clear stream flowing past.

“It is cool and feels wonderful. Lord Mabul, you should dip your feet in too.”

“Are you not afraid?”

“Of what?”

“They say the Thousand Poisons Forest has all manner of poisonous things. How do you know what kind of poison might be in that stream?”

“I heard it from the Poison King before. He said there are no poisonous things in running streams. Come on.”

“I am fine.”

“When was the last time you dipped your feet in a stream?”

Mabul could not even remember. Other than when he was very young, when had he ever dipped his feet? Had he perhaps never done it at all?

“It is my wish! Please dip them in with me just once.”

“You have so few wishes to make?”

“I want to brag about it later. When I was young, you see, I even dipped my feet in a stream with Lord Mabul, took walks with him, and made bets on finding poisonous herbs. I did it all.”

Mabul laughed with an expression of disbelief.

“They are feet that have only splashed through places where blood flows. Sometimes they should enjoy such luxury too.”

When Geom Mugeuk forcibly pulled him along, Mabul pretended to give in, took off his shoes, and dipped his feet in. They were small, shining feet.

“I have truly done all sorts of things in my life.”

The same was true of the Poison King. The Poison King had said it was the strangest among strange things.

“It is nice and cool, isn’t it?”

Mabul stared intently at Geom Mugeuk.

“Why are you looking at me like that?”

“At times like this, you do not seem like a young man.”

“It is because I grew up watching my father’s moods from an early age. You know, don’t you? What kind of man my father is.”

“He is not an easy man.”

His father was a cure-all, in a way. If something was hard to explain, using his father as an excuse made people understand to some degree.

“When in your life were you the happiest, Lord Mabul?”

At Geom Mugeuk’s question, Mabul fell into thought for a moment.

When was it?

The years past flowed through Mabul’s mind like the stream in which his feet were soaking. But happy memories did not readily come to mind. Surely there had been some.

Instead of answering, Mabul asked Geom Mugeuk in return.

“What about you?”

“I have had several recently. As you know, I have been causing quite a stir. If I had to choose just one among them, I enjoyed the time I went hunting with my father.”

How could that have been the only happy time? He simply did not tell Mabul about what had happened with the Demon Supremes.

“What did the Cult Leader say when you went hunting?”

“Whenever I say anything, he always sneers at me. You know that, don’t you?”

Geom Mugeuk imitated his father sneering. At that, Mabul nodded.

“That is similar.”

“I have suffered it so often that I am good at imitating him now.”

Mabul still had not managed to recall a moment when he had been happy, so Geom Mugeuk thought up other joys.

“It was fun when I left the cult and pretended to be someone else. Ah, there is an Inspector Seo, my right-hand man. It is fun to tease him too. You know those kinds of people, don’t you? People who accept everything when you tease them. Not because they are fools, but because their hearts are big. Ah, there is more. I have a subordinate named Ian, the prettiest in the Central Plains. Her face is pretty, but she is also clever and kindhearted. Just thinking of her makes me happy. And… I enjoy being quietly alone too.”

Even during all that, Mabul could not think of anything.

“What about you, Lord Mabul?”

“I am fine.”

“No matter how happy you were, if you cannot remember it, it simply passes you by, doesn’t it? That is too much of a waste, isn’t it? So try to think of just one thing, even if it is trivial.”

Geom Mugeuk was persistent.

“If it were me, I would answer with anything at all just because it was annoying.”

It was right then. Mabul suddenly blurted out.

“A cave.”

Mabul recalled his childhood from a very long time ago.

“There was a mountain behind my home that I often climbed when I was young. There was a small cave there. I felt good when I was inside that cave.”

Mabul’s gaze deepened.

Small from childhood, he had been different from other children. He could endure his friends mocking and ostracizing him. But the fact that even his parents and siblings treated him no differently from strangers left a deep wound in Mabul’s heart.

The day he first discovered the cave was the day he was beaten by his younger brother for the first time. Because his body was so small, he could not match even a younger brother several years below him in strength. What had angered him and made him feel wronged even more than being beaten was that his watching older brothers did not stop it.

He wandered the mountain alone, crying.

What he discovered then was that cave. Because he was so small, he could enter. The entrance was so narrow that even children would have difficulty getting in, but Mabul could. Once he passed through the narrow entrance, there was a small space inside where the young Mabul could sit. And there was a crack through which he could see outside.

Being there made his heart feel at ease and reassured him.

From that day onward, that place became Mabul’s hiding place.

“I remember feeling good when I was in that cave.”

Geom Mugeuk felt pity. He had asked about a happy moment, and Mabul had to go all the way back to that childhood?

“Could you describe that cave and the scenery around it in detail?”

“Why?”

“Just because.”

When Mabul gave a rough explanation, Geom Mugeuk asked and asked again until he heard the details. Only after he had heard enough to draw a picture did he stop questioning him.

“Why on earth did you ask?”

“Do not be surprised.”

Snap!

The moment Geom Mugeuk snapped his fingers, the two of them were standing in front of the cave.

Mabul was startled. It was that cave from his childhood. The sanctuary of his own, where he had crawled inside and hidden from the world.

“Is this illusion art?”

Mabul’s voice trembled. He knew Geom Mugeuk’s martial arts were outstanding, but he had not known he could even use illusion arts.

“It is a martial art called Space-Time Illusion Art.”

When Geom Mugeuk answered honestly, Mabul asked in a trembling voice.

“By any chance, is it the martial art of Cult Leader Pungcheon?”

“That is right.”

“Ah!”

Having once been the closest to Cult Leader Pungcheon, he knew of his martial arts as well.

“He passed this on to you?”

“Yes. Other than Cult Leader Pungcheon, only one Demon Supreme knows that I learned illusion arts. It is a secret even my father does not know.”

“!”

Mabul was astonished.

“Why tell me such a secret?”

“Because I had to tell you in order to bring you to this cave, Lord Mabul.”

At that, Mabul pulled his feet from the stream and sprang to his feet.

“Second Young Master!”

He was truly angry.

“This is what worries me. This emotional side of yours! This impulsive emotion of yours, telling such a secret to me, your enemy!”

Still gazing at the stream, Geom Mugeuk said,

“Why are you my enemy, Lord Mabul?”

“!”

“You will become my Demon Supreme, Lord Mabul. Just like the other Demon Supremes.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze slowly turned toward Mabul.

Their eyes met in the air. Geom Mugeuk was seated and Mabul was standing, yet their eye levels were similar.

And so Mabul could see clearly. Geom Mugeuk’s eyes were firmer and more intense than ever. The glib Geom Mugeuk was gone, and there stood the Geom Mugeuk who sought to become the next Heavenly Demon.

“Please become my Demon Supreme.”

The moment he heard those words, Mabul’s chest surged. An overwhelming emotion and an emotion denying those words mingled together.

Geom Mugeuk continued calmly.

“That is right. There are Demon Supremes I like more. They are people I follow like my parents or my master, and people I think of as brothers or friends. Even the Poison King just now was someone I was willing to become a dog with and bark alongside.”

“Yes, and yet why? Even if I am not your enemy, as you say, among the Demon Supremes you cherish, I would be standing at the very end of the line. Why did you tell me your secret?”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze returned to his two feet submerged in the stream.

Splash, splash.

Moving his feet as though playing in the water, Geom Mugeuk spoke calmly.

“To be honest, I do not know either. Whether I should say something grand to you like, ‘This is my decisive move!’ or whether, as you said, it is nothing more than an impulsive emotion, or whether it is respect for the loyalty you show my brother, Lord Mabul.”

Geom Mugeuk’s splashing feet stopped.

“Or perhaps it is the cheap sentiment my father said would get me killed. I do not know either.”

Mabul said in a low voice,

“One day, you will make a great mistake because of such emotions.”

“Then when that time comes, please stop me, Lord Mabul.”

“!”

“I am working hard so that you and I can become that kind of pair. When I said I cleaned the Poison King’s residence, did you tell me to be selective about what work I do? No. Even after I become the Heavenly Demon, if necessary, I will clean the rooms of the Demon Supremes. That is the kind of Heavenly Demon I will become. Please stay beside such an emotional Heavenly Demon and stop him. Say, ‘You must not do that, you emotional Heavenly Demon bastard!’”

After a brief pause, Mabul refused.

“I do not want to.”

He was still the man who devoted his single-minded loyalty to the Eldest Young Master.

For a while, neither of them spoke.

Then Mabul suddenly said,

“Make the cave entrance bigger. I will go inside once. Ah, and there was a small crack in front of the cave, so I could see the village below.”

Geom Mugeuk changed the cave as Mabul had described.

Mabul crawled inside.

“Do you like it?”

“Not at all. It is narrow and stifling. I can hardly breathe.”

What had he liked about such a cramped cave that made him come here every day?

For a while, he looked out at the village from there. Geom Mugeuk had made it so carefully that smoke was rising far away from cooking fires.

He used to sit like this, then leave the cave and look down from where Geom Mugeuk now stood at the path leading up here. With the slightest expectation that perhaps his mother or his older brother might come looking for him, telling him to eat. Of course, not once had that expectation ever been fulfilled.

Then one day, instead of going down the mountain, he descended in the opposite direction and left his hometown. He had never returned to his hometown since.

Mabul looked at Geom Mugeuk through the narrow crack. Geom Mugeuk was sitting with his back turned, looking down at the village.

“If you think about it, I am sure there were many times you were happy. Surely this old memory cannot be the best one? Like when you first learned martial arts, or the day you became a Demon Supreme. There must also have been a day when someone truly acknowledged you, Lord Mabul. There must have been many happier days that you simply do not remember now. What about today? Was it not enjoyable?”

A golden radiance poured out through the narrow crack in the cave.

“Who on earth are you?”

Still with his back turned, Geom Mugeuk said,

“I am the one who will become your Heavenly Demon. So now, please come out of that narrow, stifling place.”

It would not have been strange for words beginning with, “Good grief, Second Young Master!” to come out, but Mabul emerged from the cave without a word. Then he sat down beside Geom Mugeuk.

The two of them looked down for a while at the village in the distance. Then, when they looked up at the sky, a bird was flying there.

“That is the bird from back then. You bastard, I am not jealous of you at all anymore!”

At Geom Mugeuk’s shout, Mabul had this thought. It truly seemed he needed to find something to call him besides a madman.

Geom Mugeuk flopped backward onto the ground.

“I am going to lie down for a bit.”

A large, billowing cloud was drifting across the blue sky.

“Look at that cloud. I made it myself, but is it not truly incredible? Ah, this is nice.”

Recently, he had looked up at the sky with Mabul several times, but right now felt the most relaxed. He could feel the ever-taut tension in Mabul loosening.

Yes, this was enough. That looseness was enough.

Geom Mugeuk was not doing this in order to become the successor. Mabul, too, was someone who would become his Demon Supreme, and that was why he cherished him.

He only hoped that this heart of his would reach him.

It was right then.

Mabul, too, slowly lay back.

After supporting his head with both arms, he lay there and looked up at the sky.

Then he closed his eyes.

He recalled the moments when, as a child, he had lain here and looked up at the sky. The feeling of the grass tickling his back, the birdsong he had heard then, the sound of the stream. The ants tickling his feet. As those memories merged with his present senses, they rose vividly in his mind.

Watching a faint smile form at the corners of Mabul’s mouth, Geom Mugeuk also closed his eyes with a peaceful heart.

A cool breeze blew, and drowsiness gently came over him.

Chapter 247: Drunk on Wine and Drunk on Moonlight.
--------------------------------------------------
The Demon Buddha opened his eyes.

Startled, he sprang upright, but he was still in the world within the Spacetime Transposition Art, and Geom Mugeuk was asleep beside him.

‘I fell asleep?’

He, who had thought Geom Mugeuk was only pretending when he saw him lying asleep on the rock?

Since becoming a Demon Supreme—no, even long before he had become one—he had never once fallen asleep so defenselessly. This was the first time.

The Demon Buddha looked down at Geom Mugeuk in silence.

He was sleeping with a peaceful expression.

‘Now I understand.’

No other words were needed. He could understand why the other Demon Supremes had lost their hearts to the Second Young Master. If a person like this asked you to become his Demon Supreme, how could you refuse?

Sensing the Demon Buddha’s presence, Geom Mugeuk also woke from his sleep.

“Did I wake you for no reason?”

“No. It was brief, but I slept deeply.”

“Let us leave now.”

“Very well.”

Before leaving, the Demon Buddha looked back at the cave behind them.

“I don’t think I’ll have any reason to miss this cave anymore.”

The Demon Buddha, who had hidden there as a child, had now come out of that cave. Suddenly, he thought that perhaps it was time for his grown self to come out of the cave as well.

“Then I’ll release the Spacetime Transposition Art.”

When the Spacetime Transposition Art vanished, the two of them were once again standing by the stream in the Thousand Poisons Forest.

“Let us return.”

“Already?”

“What do you mean, already?”

“I want to play more.”

“What is so amusing about playing with an old man like me?”

“It is amusing. And I need to get someone to buy us drinks.”

“Drinks?”

“We should make the Poison King, who used us as herb gatherers, buy us drinks.”

They had promised that the one who found the Seven Lives Grass first would be treated to drinks.

“Today? Right now?”

“Can you come here next time and say it? ‘See here, Poison King. Did we not agree that the loser of our last wager would buy drinks?’ Can you make such a demand?”

Of course, such a thing would never happen. Even if they ran into each other in passing, he would not mention it. In truth, if Geom Mugeuk had not said anything, he would have forgotten the matter entirely.

“If today passes, we won’t be able to punish the Poison King.”

“You truly intend to take this to the end?”

“How can you already be tired? It may feel like we played a lot, but we only played for half a day. I am not comparing, but with the other Demon Supremes, I even went outside the cult for dozens of days. You know that, don’t you?”

“That is comparing.”

“Come, come, let’s go. You’re not yet at the age where you get tired of playing already!”

Though he let himself be dragged along as if he had no choice, the Demon Buddha’s steps toward the Poison King’s residence were quick and light.

* * *

Jo Chunbae, the owner of Pungryu Tavern, spoke politely to the youthful young man entering the tavern.

“We are no longer accepting guests today. My apologies.”

Speaking carelessly just because someone looked young could bring disaster. If anything, the younger they were, the more careful one had to be. From a merchant’s point of view, the most frightening thing was the immature blade of a reckless youth who swung without thinking of the consequences.

Just then, Geom Mugeuk, standing by the second-floor railing of the tavern, looked down and said,

“You should bid farewell to the owner.”

He had come here with the Demon Buddha about half an hour earlier.

“Are you leaving already?”

“No, it’s just that I don’t think we’ll be able to see the owner anymore.”

“What do you mean by that?”

To the puzzled Jo Chunbae, Geom Mugeuk said coldly,

“You just told our cult’s Poison King that you wouldn’t sell him alcohol and that he should leave.”

Jo Chunbae’s gaze turned toward the young man. A moment later, Jo Chunbae was scared out of his wits.

“Aigo! I failed to recognize you. Forgive me!”

As Jo Chunbae was about to bow deeply, the Poison King stopped him.

“No need to make a fuss.”

Then Geom Mugeuk said,

“He has no choice but to make a fuss. I can’t say for certain, but among the Demon Supremes, our owner here probably fears you the most, Poison King.”

When the Poison King looked at Jo Chunbae, he could not dare meet his eyes and lowered his head.

“Am I truly the one you fear most?”

“You were, originally, but I was startled because you are so young and handsome. You are the most handsome man in the Central Plains.”

Geom Mugeuk, who had been watching, did not let that pass.

“Owner, what about me?”

“You, Pavilion Master, are the most handsome man in all ages, past and present.”

At that, the Poison King asked coldly,

“Are you saying I am not the greatest?”

“Aigo! That is not what I meant. What I meant was…”

Leaving the flustered man behind, the Poison King went up to the second floor.

Jo Chunbae looked up at Geom Mugeuk on the second floor with an expression full of emotion.

‘Thanks to you, Pavilion Master, I even get to serve the Poison King. I have no more regrets.’

Geom Mugeuk smiled and spoke with his eyes.

‘You never know. No one knows who else might come.’

And so, even the Poison King joined them at the second-floor table.

“I thought you wouldn’t come.”

“How could I not come when you left something like this?”

The letter he took out was one Geom Mugeuk had written. When he and the Demon Buddha had gone to the Poison King’s residence, the Poison King had naturally fled through the back door. So Geom Mugeuk had left a letter with Sangseon, and the Poison King had read it.

“If you do not buy us drinks, we will buy alcohol ourselves and storm your residence, Poison King. Not only the Demon Buddha, but Investigator Seo, Ian, Lord Ma, and Cheon Sohui as well!”

The Poison King grimaced as he said,

“With this written down, how could I not come!”

If the letter had said that Geom Mugeuk would spread a rumor that the Poison King had lost a wager and refused to keep his promise, he would not have come. He was someone who did not care what others thought.

But what if many people went to the Thousand Poisons Forest, drank there, and made a racket? That was something the Poison King could never tolerate.

Of course, that was not the only reason he had come. He had once again taken this letter as Geom Mugeuk asking for help. It was an extension of: Please play with us today.

“I apologize. It was only because I wanted to play with you so badly, Poison King.”

“You already played to your heart’s content, didn’t you?”

“You have to play for seven days and nights before you can say you went somewhere and had some fun. I am prepared to stay up all night tonight.”

The Poison King waved his hands, saying that if he slept past midnight, he would have a hard time in the morning and it would not do.

“The person who looks the youngest is acting so weak!”

“You try becoming my age!”

The Demon Buddha became uncomfortable again now that the Poison King had arrived, but at the same time, he was also just a little glad. The Demon Buddha knew. Would the Poison King have come because of that letter alone? It meant his relationship with Geom Mugeuk was that deep.

He could understand Geom Mugeuk becoming close to the Drunken Demon. They could have grown close over drinks. But to think he could become this close to the Poison King, who lived in a world entirely his own. It was unbelievable even seeing it with his own eyes. To the Demon Buddha, the Poison King was the most uncomfortable and difficult person in the world.

“Now, let us have a drink.”

The three toasted and drank. It was the first time he had ever drunk with them.

Seeing the Demon Buddha empty his cup refreshingly, Geom Mugeuk asked,

“Wasn’t it originally your principle never to eat any food at all in the Poison King’s presence?”

“That is correct.”

“But you are eating comfortably.”

“If the Poison King sets his mind on poisoning me, what meaning is there in whether I eat or not?”

With an expression that seemed to say, Yes, that is exactly right, the Poison King smiled faintly.

That was why the Poison King was frightening. If he resolved to poison someone, even a Demon Supreme could not stop him.

The Poison King sent a voice transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

—You came out quite late. What were the two of you doing in there?

—We soaked our feet in the stream and rested.

—The Demon Buddha too?

—Yes.

—I cannot imagine it.

He would never believe that they had fallen asleep side by side.

We drank another cup. With good liquor that Jo Chunbae had kept in reserve and delicious dishes, our mood improved on its own.

“This is why I come to Pungryu Tavern.”

When Jo Chunbae brought out a new dish, Geom Mugeuk said to the Demon Buddha,

“Please leave a phrase or a trace of your martial arts on that wall over there to show that you came here.”

“Why?”

“Most of the Demon Supremes have come to this very seat, and even Father came here. From now on, whenever each of you comes, I intend to have you leave a trace. Isn’t it nice? You will be the first, Demon Buddha!”

The word first stirred the Demon Buddha’s heart. If everyone was going to write something eventually, then it would be better to be first, would it not? A pointless competitive spirit rose within him.

“Then let me…”

At that moment, a sound came from behind him.

Scratch, scratch, scratch, scratch.

Geom Mugeuk and the Demon Buddha turned around.

Before they knew it, the Poison King was writing something on the wall with a chopstick.

A single line written in magnificent handwriting.

The Poison King was here.

The Poison King said to Geom Mugeuk,

“This is what you meant, correct?”

“Yes, but I was in the middle of saying that I would give the first opportunity to the Demon Buddha.”

“Was that so? I did not hear it.”

Geom Mugeuk could believe that the Poison King had once again sunk into his own world and failed to listen to the conversation to the end, but the Demon Buddha took it as a challenge.

The Demon Buddha’s body shone with golden light, and his hand formed a mudra.

Flash!

A trace of the Golden Great Rama Demonic Art was left on the wall. It looked like a golden dragon opening its mouth, about to devour the words the Poison King had left behind.

It was the petty revenge of the Demon Buddha, whose first place had been stolen, and at the same time, the manifestation of supreme martial learning that left only a trace without breaking through the wall.

“Waaaah!”

Before they knew it, the people in the tavern had come up and were admiring what had been left on the wall.

The Poison King’s writing and the Demon Buddha’s martial trace harmonized so perfectly that it seemed as if they had been matched that way on purpose. How could the onlookers possibly imagine it? That the dragon wanted to eat those words.

At the people’s cheers, the Demon Buddha sat back down as if it were nothing, but his face was filled with satisfaction.

The Poison King sent a voice transmission to Geom Mugeuk.

—To sulk over something like this. A child. A child.

—And what about the one who couldn’t wait even a moment and quickly wrote first? The two of you should become friends!

—I refuse. That man makes the world uncomfortable!

When Geom Mugeuk looked back, Jo Chunbae was smiling so widely in the distance that his mouth nearly split.

We drank again. Freely. We did not insistently offer each other cups, nor did we try to keep the conversation going. If silence flowed, we let it flow; if it became this or that trivial talk, then that was fine too. We drank at ease.

While drinking, Geom Mugeuk looked outside through the second-floor window. Since it was not yet late at night, many people were passing by.

As he emptied his mind and idly watched the people, he saw the Poison King disappear among the crowd. Before he knew it, the Poison King had even paid for the drinks and left without saying goodbye.

Just then, the Demon Buddha asked from behind him,

“How can I trust you? How can I know your heart will not change when you become the Heavenly Demon?”

I told him to put everything down, drink comfortably, and have fun.

Without turning around, Geom Mugeuk answered,

“You don’t know.”

These were people for whom resting for even one day was this difficult.

* * *

When the Demon Buddha returned to his residence, someone was waiting in the yard. It was none other than Geom Muyang.

“Young Master!”

The Demon Buddha was drunk, and in his hand was a bottle of liquor.

“Did you have a drink?”

“Yes, it has been a long time since I had one.”

“Please give me a cup as well.”

“Very well. I will bring a cup.”

“No, just give it to me.”

At this moment, both of them were strange.

The Demon Buddha was not someone who bought liquor and carried it back, and Geom Muyang was not someone who drank straight from the bottle.

“Today, I met the Second Young Master and the Poison King. I am also returning after drinking with the two of them.”

“I see.”

After each taking a sip of liquor, both their gazes naturally turned toward the night sky.

The Demon Buddha thought that he had looked up at the sky quite a lot today. In the daytime with Geom Mugeuk, and at night like this with Geom Muyang.

Geom Muyang glanced at the Demon Buddha. His flushed cheeks, reddened by the liquor, mingled with the golden light and shone with a unique color. It was not simply because the hue was different. Today, the Demon Buddha was different from usual.

Because he could guess where that difference came from, Geom Muyang’s heart grew heavy. Even if everyone else left, he had hoped that at least the Demon Buddha would stay by his side. Though he had never expressed it, he had been worried sick over it.

To be honest, there had been times when he had wished that the Blood Heaven Blade Demon or another Demon Supreme would become his right hand. That was before he had known the Demon Buddha would go this far for him.

“It seemed the Poison King has completely turned to the Second Young Master’s side.”

The Demon Buddha spoke honestly.

Now he had to make him face reality. It will go well. I will try harder. From now on, such words would only drive Geom Muyang closer to the edge of the cliff.

Now was the time to walk away from that cliff’s edge together with him.

It seems this is as far as we go, Eldest Young Master.

But the words would not come. He could not bear to open his mouth.

The Demon Buddha looked at Geom Muyang.

Even at the words that the Poison King had turned, Geom Muyang remained calm. In the past, he might have pretended to be fine and then suddenly spat out a cold remark, or shifted the responsibility onto the Demon Buddha. He would have reacted sharply.

But Geom Muyang was calm, as if he had expected it.

“Now only the Fist Demon remains.”

The Demon Buddha was actually grateful that he had said only the Fist Demon, rather than the Demon Buddha and the Fist Demon.

At the very least, it meant he believed the Demon Buddha was an exception and would not change.

Through this process, he could tell that Geom Muyang had grown. He had endured and endured again. Several times a day, he must have been tormented by the desire to kill Geom Mugeuk. He must have been tempted to use every means available to him and simply kill him first.

And he had endured it splendidly. Whether because he was watching the Heavenly Demon’s mood, because of his relationship with Geom Mugeuk, or because he was not inherently that sort of wicked person. In any case, he had endured well.

That was why the Demon Buddha truly wanted to say this to him.

Let us see it through to the end! I will play the role of one against a hundred.

But he did not. Because that would have been deceiving the Eldest Young Master instead.

The Second Young Master is an opponent you cannot defeat.

Had Geom Muyang read the resignation contained in his gaze?

Drunk on liquor and drunk on moonlight, he now understood.

Why he had remained until the very end. It was not because Geom Mugeuk had approached him last. Even if he had approached him first, the result would have been the same.

His parents had failed to protect him, but he wanted to protect the Eldest Young Master until the end. It was because of that feeling. And so, to the Demon Buddha, the Eldest Young Master was both his child and, at the same time, himself.

Even if the Eldest Young Master failed to become the successor, he would not leave his side.

“In your eyes, Demon Supreme, what kind of person is Mugeuk? Please tell me honestly.”

After a brief pause, the Demon Buddha said,

“I do not know what kind of person he is.”

With that answer, Geom Muyang was certain. Even the Demon Buddha had been shaken by Geom Mugeuk. He could tell just from the fact that the Demon Buddha had come back carrying liquor. A liquor bottle in the Demon Buddha’s hand? He had never seen such a thing.

“In the fact that I do not know what kind of person he is, Mugeuk resembles Father more than I do.”

Geom Muyang smiled at the Demon Buddha, bowed his head slightly, and then turned away.

As he walked some distance away, the Demon Buddha said to him,

“Eldest Young Master!”

“What is it?”

“The one who resembles the Cult Leader is you, Eldest Young Master.”

“Is that so?”

“Yes, I am certain.”

Geom Muyang, who had been walking slowly, looked back.

The Demon Buddha, who had been looking up at the night sky, drank straight from the liquor bottle. He drank for a long while without stopping.

Geom Muyang realized that the time had come for him to make his decision.

Chapter 248: When Will You Kill Me?
-----------------------------------
Geom Muyang, having emerged from Mabul’s residence, walked slowly through the inner compound of the Heavenly Demon Cult.

He walked, carefully observing each of the buildings in the inner compound. Never before had he looked closely at these buildings.

The martial artists standing guard greeted him respectfully.

When Geom Muyang accepted their greeting, the martial artists exchanged puzzled glances. Because Geom Muyang had always passed by in a hurry, he had never properly acknowledged their greetings before.

After walking through the inner compound in that manner, Geom Muyang arrived at the Eastern Fist Gate.

Though it was late, Gwonma was awake.

Geom Muyang arrived before the cliff, following a guiding martial artist.

Gwonma, standing before the cliff, spoke without turning around.

“I intend to destroy this cliff in a single blow. That is my dream.”

Geom Muyang looked up at the cliff. The massive cliff, its end out of sight, declared with absolute certainty that it was impossible. Geom Muyang thought that even for Gwonma, this would be difficult.

“If it is Gwonma-nim, you will surely achieve that dream.”

Only then did Gwonma turn toward him.

“I greet Gwonma-nim.”

“Eldest Young Lord, welcome.”

Whenever Geom Muyang saw Gwonma’s fearsome face, which could make even the most sinister demonic visages of the Heavenly Demon Cult look gentle by comparison, he couldn’t help but feel afraid.

“I apologize for visiting so late at night.”

“Not at all. I was awake for late-night training.”

Surprisingly, Gwonma still attended the late-night training sessions these days. Since returning after leaving the cult, Geom Muguk had been unable to attend, but Ian and Cheon Sohui had been coming steadily.

Gwonma had acted like he would go, because he disliked the appearance of skipping out just because Geom Muguk wasn’t there. That was why he had gone, but these days he didn’t miss a session thanks to Cheon Sohui.

He hadn’t known it when Geom Muguk first mentioned it, but as time passed, he realized that her talent and character were second to none for becoming the next Gwonma. So in the middle of training, he gave her separate instruction.

Gwonma found these late-night training sessions enjoyable.

Honestly, it might have been because he was with two young and beautiful women. It might have been because of Cheon Sohui, who held absolute respect for Gwonma, or because of Ian, who occasionally made him laugh with unexpected jokes. For him, who had been sincere about teaching his whole life, these late-night training sessions had become a very valuable and enjoyable time.

“So, what brings you here?”

Just from the fact that he had come at such a late hour, Gwonma could sense it. That Geom Muyang stood on the verge of some great resolution.

“You are the only one who announced that you would officially support me.”

“I am not one to break promises.”

“If it had been after meeting Muguk, would you have made that promise?”

Gwonma glared at Geom Muyang as if piercing through him. Because Geom Muyang still hadn’t grown accustomed to Gwonma’s face, he lowered his head slightly to avert his gaze.

“If that were the case, I would have announced my support for the Second Young Lord.”

Geom Muyang was rather glad that Gwonma answered honestly. He had come to find Gwonma today because he knew the man was like this.

“What kind of person is Muguk?”

“As his older brother, wouldn’t you know better?”

“No, I do not know.”

Gwonma’s gaze turned toward the cliff again.

“I said to the Second Young Lord what I said to you earlier. That it is my dream to destroy this cliff.”

Geom Muyang was curious. How Geom Muguk had answered.

“He said he wanted to destroy it as well.”

In that moment, Geom Muyang felt it. That those words had been the correct answer, at least to Gwonma.

“Perhaps the one who will someday destroy this cliff will be the Second Young Lord. Your younger brother is that kind of person.”

It must have been because of this small difference. These small differences gathering and accumulating must have created the current gap between Geom Muguk and himself.

“Could those words not be false? Could they not be words spoken merely to look good before Gwonma-nim?”

They were desperate words he would not normally have spoken, but Geom Muyang revealed his honest feelings as if he had made up his mind.

“Of course, that is possible.”

“But why did you believe him?”

Gwonma understood what Geom Muyang was feeling. Because even he himself was swept up by Geom Muguk; it must have been impossible for the young Geom Muyang to face him.

“Because the Second Young Lord showed it through action. Because he came to the Eastern Fist Gate and rose from White Fist to Black Fist.”

“With Muguk’s martial prowess, that would not have been a difficult task!”

Gwonma asked calmly of the agitated Geom Muyang.

“If it were such an easy matter, why did you not do it?”

“!”

Geom Muyang could not answer. He too knew. That what Gwonma spoke of was not merely a matter of martial prowess. That when action follows, faith gains strength as well.

Gwonma’s gaze turned toward the cliff again.

After a moment, Geom Muyang asked.

“May I ask one final question?”

“Ask.”

“Do you think it would be better for the main cult as well if Muguk becomes the Heavenly Demon?”

“Why do you ask me that?”

“Because I thought you would answer most honestly among the Demon Venerables.”

Gwonma fell into thought for a moment. He had a premonition that his answer would exert some great influence on Geom Muyang. Even so, Gwonma spoke honestly.

“Before answering, I will say this one thing. Regardless of my answer, my official position supporting you will remain unchanged.”

“Thank you.”

Gwonma looked at Geom Muyang and spoke without the slightest sign of apology.

“I look forward to the Second Young Lord’s Heavenly Demon Cult more.”

It was a cruel answer for Geom Muyang, but Gwonma answered honestly.

Those words flew heavily into Geom Muyang’s chest and lodged there.

“Thank you for speaking honestly.”

Geom Muyang bowed respectfully with a trembling voice and turned around. Before leaving, he looked up at the cliff one last time. Even looking again, he could not conceive of destroying it.

Watching his retreating figure, Gwonma moved his steps in the opposite direction to go to the late-night training.

He offered him no words of comfort or encouragement.

Because the cliff given to each person was something each had to overcome themselves.

* * *

Hyung had come.

Hyung, standing under the moonlight properly dressed in martial attire with a sword at his waist, felt different from usual.

Hyung spoke to me abruptly.

“Let’s fight.”

I could tell. That this match would decide the successor. That he had come even knowing he would lose.

“Good. I’ve wanted to beat you up for a long time.”

“I’m not joking.”

“I’m not joking either. I’m the vicious older brother who tried to instill fear of the martial world in his younger brother who was to become a martial artist. I deserve a beating, don’t I?”

“If that became such a deep grudge, then by all means.”

“Don’t say it like it’s nothing.”

“No matter what, I’m not going to say I’m sorry.”

We didn’t say we were fighting over the successor as if we had made a promise. We simply acted as if settling an old grudge.

Hyung drew his sword, and I drew mine. Except for when we had martial arts competitions as children, this was the first time we had pointed blades at each other.

Flying Heaven Sword Art versus Flying Heaven Sword Art.

Hyung had achieved Ten Star Grand Perfection, while I had achieved Twelve Star Grand Perfection. Even aside from that difference, the gap in experience, skill, and internal energy was so vast that Hyung could not defeat me.

I would not hold back. However, I didn’t subdue him at once, instead letting Hyung use every form he was capable of.

“Be careful. I’m really going.”

Hyung, having warned me seriously, unfurled the First Form, Equal Heaven.

Shwaaaa!

A streak of sword light flew to bisect me, but my Equal Heaven was faster and more powerful.

Kwang!

Watching the First Form dissipate futilely in the air, Hyung was shocked.

With a surprised face, he unleashed the Second Form, but this time too, a stronger Second Form neutralized it.

Third Form, Fourth Form…….

As the exchanges continued, Hyung’s despair grew. He must have known I was stronger, but he wouldn’t have known I was this strong.

Hyeolcheon Doma had said so.

That stepping on him firmly when the time came would be the path for Hyung. I agreed with those words.

I revealed all of my strength to the point where Hyung felt despair.

That was the path for Hyung. So that he would never harbor other intentions going forward. So that he would accept failing to become the successor not as a regrettable thing, but as a natural one.

Hyung must have been looking at a wall. A wall even taller and greater than the cliff Gwonma had been looking at.

When the Flying Heaven Sword Art didn’t work, Hyung swung his sword and rushed in. He must have come knowing he would lose. But when defeat actually approached, Hyung grew desperate.

‘I want to become the successor!’

But that desperation could not bridge the gap in our strength.

Taaang.

The sword that flew from Hyung’s grasp fell to the ground.

Hyung, staring blankly at the sword for a moment, glared at me and spoke.

“Actually, I was planning to beat you with my fists.”

“Then! A man’s fists are what matter!”

I sheathed the Black Demon Sword and spoke to Hyung.

“But what to do? I’m Gwonma-nim’s disciple.”

“……Don’t use forms.”

At Hyung’s words, I laughed loudly. That he would say such a thing in this situation was a good sign.

Yeah, Hyung. I’ll give you a proper beating.

We clashed with our fists. Whether it was swordsmanship, fist arts, or brawling. How could Hyung defeat me?

Puk! Peok!

My fists embedded themselves in Hyung’s face and body.

This was punishment for injecting fear into his younger brother out of his own greed. I had to settle our old grudge.

Puk! Peoreok!

They were fists meant to forgive Hyung.

Even though he took a severe beating, Hyung never used internal energy. He could have tried to kill me by taking advantage of an opening, but he didn’t. He didn’t even attempt it.

That’s why, Hyung. The reason I wanted to save you and bring you along. Father probably saw it first. What kind of person Hyung was.

And just as Hyung, his strength drained, was about to collapse!

Peok!

Hyung’s fist landed squarely on my face.

I let him land a clean hit. It seemed there was guilt in my heart toward Hyung as well. If I hadn’t regressed, Hyung would have become the successor.

But Hyung, me becoming the successor is also for your sake. Let’s live this life, at least. Whether we get close or fight like hell, let’s survive.

And so a fight that wasn’t a fight came to an end.

Having succeeded in landing the final blow, Hyung smiled satisfactorily and flopped down on his back.

Huu, huu, huu.

Hyung’s rough breathing—everything was contained in that breath.

The breathing slowly subsided.

Until then, I too lay beside Hyung and looked up at the night sky.

Lying down, Hyung asked.

“Can you do it well?”

He might have expected me to say no. Or if there were even the slightest chance, he might have hoped I would say, “Hyung, you do it.”

“I can do it better than any Heavenly Demon in history.”

“Damn it!”

Hyung shouted while lying down.

“Uaaaaaaaah!”

He screamed as if thrashing in death throes. Many people in the main cult must be hearing this shout. This shout that decided Hyung’s and my fate.

Hyung, having stopped shouting, let out a deep sigh. He expelled the last remaining lingering attachment with that breath.

“You really have to do well.”

“I will.”

“The successor of the Heavenly Demon Cult fooling around and running his mouth. You can’t do that!”

“It’s because you’ve never seen me be serious. Are you going to beg me to joke around?”

“Don’t do this kind of thing!”

“Okay.”

Hyung had completely given up on being the successor. That was why this question was sincere too.

“When are you going to kill me? Give me enough time to settle my affairs before you do.”

“Why is your head filled with only such negative thoughts? I’ll grant that you injected my darkness, but who planted it in you?”

“You’re the strange one.”

Hyung had given up on everything, but there was no need to. Because I needed Hyung.

“From now on, Hyung has to help me.”

“Nonsense.”

“I’m sincere. I don’t need a puppet Hyung. I don’t need a wounded and depressed Hyung either. I need the real Hyung.”

I got up first and extended my hand to Hyung, who was lying down.

Once we get rid of the Hwamugi, perhaps I might even give that position to Hyung. Because who knows what state we’ll be in by then. Hyung, so take my hand.

Hyung didn’t take my hand and sat up by himself. Sitting there, he wiped his nosebleed and spoke.

“It’s embarrassing.”

“Be proud. You’ve got a killer younger brother, don’t you?”

“I want to kill you. Truly.”

And the next moment.

Hyung extended his hand to me. It was a hand held out to be grasped so I could pull him up.

The moment I saw that extended hand, my heart choked up. There was so much to say that I couldn’t say anything at all.

I grasped that hand tightly. I could feel Hyung gripping my hand tightly too.

I powerfully pulled Hyung to his feet.

Hyung, gazing at me for a moment, raised his fist before me and spoke.

“If you don’t properly act as the successor from now on, you’ll get beaten by me again!”

I looked at Hyung and smiled. It was the first smile I had smiled because I liked him.

“If someone bullies me, can I call for Hyung?”

“Crazy bastard.”

Hyung moved toward the Heavenly Demon Hall first.

I had said I would take Hyung to Father, but Hyung was taking me.

The demonic warriors standing guard were startled. Because brothers were walking side by side in the dead of night, and with their faces all beaten up at that.

“Shouldn’t we wash up a bit before seeing Father?”

“We have to go like this so people know you’re the ruthless younger brother who beats his older brother.”

And Hyung said.

“When you kill me, make sure to tell me in advance… and if someone bullies you, make sure to tell me too.”

A gust of wind blew, fluttering the hems of our clothes. It was a wind heralding a new beginning.

Chapter 249: The Reason I Summoned You Today.
---------------------------------------------
I entered the Heavenly Demon Hall with my brother.

Father was in the Heavenly Demon Hall with General Strategist Sima Ming.

The fact that he was out here when he should have been asleep meant he had received a report that we had fought a round and were on our way to the Heavenly Demon Hall.

Together with my brother, I walked side by side through that place, where a strange tension hung in the air.

Father looked down at us in silence as we approached.

“Father.”

My brother, who had always addressed him as “Cult Leader,” called him “Father.” My brother, who had always found it difficult to even look at him, looked him straight in the eye today.

And then the words that were the hardest, the most difficult of his entire life flowed out.

“I will withdraw from the competition to be successor.”

Father was a man who usually never revealed his emotions, but in this moment, he showed a quiet passion. It was an emotion mixed with joy and pity.

“Will you not regret it?”

“No, I will not regret it.”

“Once you make this decision, there will be no turning back.”

My brother looked at me and said,

“I want to see the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult led by my younger brother.”

Our gazes intertwined in midair. For a very long time, this moment today would torment my brother. He would regret it, again and again.

Thank you, Brother. Though I may not be able to stop that regret, I’ll make sure it becomes the most precious emotion in your life.

My brother looked back at Father.

“This is my earnest request, Father.”

I had not expected my brother to come forward this actively. He was not the brother from before my regression.

After I regressed, my brother, too, had undergone various emotional changes and transformed. He had grown. Now that he could look at Father like that, his relationship with Father would also change.

I sent Father a sound transmission.

—I brought Brother here in truly splendid form, didn’t I?

—He was always a splendid child.

—Are you discriminating because he’s your eldest son?

The corners of Father’s mouth curled up slightly as he looked at me. Father, I’ll spend my whole life making jokes just to see that sneer. Please, don’t let that smile fade!

Father’s gaze returned to my brother.

Father must know how difficult a decision my brother had made.

And Father accepted my brother’s sincerity. Right here, right now.

Father gave an order to Sima Ming.

“Activate the Eight Demon Assembly Order at once.”

The Eight Demon Assembly Order.

It was a supreme command that summoned not only the Eight Demon Venerables, but every demonic practitioner under their command.

“I shall obey your command.”

Deep in the night, an order of truly unprecedented nature was given.

* * *

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was teaching Seo Daeryong late into the night.

At the technique Seo Daeryong displayed, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon smiled in satisfaction.

“Well done!”

At his master’s praise, Seo Daeryong felt as if he might fly. Because his master was stingy with compliments, the emotion was all the greater.

“Thank you!”

When he was with Geom Mugeuk, Seo Daeryong was full of every kind of flippant banter, but when he was with his master, he observed every courtesy. In his heart, his master was someone more precious than even the Heavenly Demon.

When their training ended, Seo Daeryong held out a book.

“I asked an investigator who was out in the field to obtain this.”

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was startled when he saw the book. It was one he had wanted to read.

“It couldn’t have been easy to get.”

“I was lucky.”

“What money do you have?”

It was a rare volume, so it must have cost quite a bit to acquire.

“I’m still unmarried, so my monthly stipend remains untouched.”

“For the crime of squandering money, your hell training resumes tomorrow.”

“Ah! But today was the day hell training ended.”

Though he had snapped out of spite for no reason, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was inwardly moved.

It was just then.

Dooong! Dooong!

The sound of a bell rang out.

“What bell could that be at this hour?”

It was no ordinary bell. It was a clear and deep sound he had never heard before.

At Seo Daeryong’s question, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon answered.

“It is the bell announcing the Eight Demon Assembly Order.”

At those words, Seo Daeryong asked in surprise,

“What could be happening at this hour?”

Looking toward the distant Heavenly Demon Hall, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon said quietly,

“Either a war has broken out…”

At the word war, Seo Daeryong tensed sharply.

“Or what was bound to come has finally come.”

Of course, a war would not have broken out, so it must be the latter.

“If that is what this is, you’ll have to train even harder in martial arts. If you want to keep serving as his right arm.”

At the words right arm, he realized it was related to Geom Mugeuk. But Seo Daeryong had no idea what, exactly, was happening.

“Open the wardrobe at the very end and bring me the blood robe!”

Seo Daeryong went into the house and opened the wardrobe at the far end. Inside hung a ceremonial robe as red as blood.

* * *

At that hour, the Drunken Demon was drinking with the One Flower Sword Venerable at the Chwimong Pavilion.

The third-ranked drunkard, Yeo Bin, had just set down the side dishes and was about to turn away when the One Flower Sword Venerable spoke to her.

“Accept a cup from me.”

Yeo Bin respectfully received and drank her cup.

Then, after pouring a drink for the One Flower Sword Venerable, she withdrew from the place.

Once she had gone far away, the One Flower Sword Venerable said,

“When she says she likes you, accept her. Don’t regret it later.”

The Drunken Demon drank the liquor in his hand.

“A woman suffers when she meets a man who loves drinking.”

“She’s one of the Three Great Drunkards too, isn’t she?”

“That’s exactly why. If both of us are drunkards, are we supposed to earn money just to spend it all on drink?”

The One Flower Sword Venerable shook her head at the Drunken Demon, who was making absurd excuses.

“You’re my friend, but you really are hard to understand…”

Dooong!

At the sound of the bell ringing out, the Drunken Demon, who had been leaning crookedly, rose. Before he knew it, the One Flower Sword Venerable was already flying across the lake, her white martial robes fluttering.

Looking at her, the Drunken Demon shook his head this time.

“If it were the Cult Leader’s command, you’d leap into hell that quickly too, wouldn’t you?”

Yeo Bin came running back into the Chwimong Pavilion.

Setting down the cup in his hand, the Drunken Demon said,

“Sober everyone up!”

* * *

The Poison King was concocting poison.

It was a poison called Seven Lives Paradise, whose main ingredient was the Seven Lives Herb the Demon Buddha had discovered in the Thousand Poisons Forest. It was a mysterious poison: those afflicted by it would feel pleasure for seven days, and on the final seventh day, they would die while experiencing pain equal and opposite to all the pleasure they had felt.

He was working while wearing the Poison-Repelling Heavenly Silkworm gloves Geom Mugeuk had gifted him.

Dooong!

Whether the bell rang or not, the Poison King remained focused only on his work.

At that moment, Sang Seon entered, carrying clothes. Without raising his head, the Poison King said,

“Tell them I can’t go.”

“Do you think you can withstand refusing the Eight Demon Assembly Order with that poison?”

Sang Seon went to him and held out the clothes.

The Poison King reluctantly accepted them and grumbled.

“When I get there, it probably won’t even be that important.”

“Please change your clothes first. The Poison Children are preparing.”

When Sang Seon brought over the poison pouches hanging on the wall, the Poison King was mixing poison again. Sang Seon had to drag him by force and make him put on the clothes.

Once the Poison King had properly dressed and even fastened twelve poison pouches to his waist, he was truly a tall and handsome young noble of murim.

As the Poison King left his quarters, he said,

“I’ve now made the Second Young Master do all the cleaning.”

He knew exactly what today’s summons was about. It had been worth playing along with him.

* * *

The moment he heard the bell, the Demon Buddha’s hand, which had been turning prayer beads, stopped.

He could tell that the First Young Master had finally made his decision.

It was the moment he had been waiting for, but at the same time, it was also the moment he had wished and wished would never come.

“…First Young Master.”

A golden radiance burst from his body. As if revealing his intense emotions, the light was more powerful than ever before.

Only after praying for the First Young Master did the Demon Buddha rise from his seat.

“First Young Master, let us see where fate leads us.”

* * *

The Extreme Evil Small Demon was circulating his qi.

He had now fully recovered from his injuries, and thanks to having taken the Ten-Thousand-Year Snow Ginseng, his internal energy had grown deeper and more majestic. His body itching for action, he wanted to rush out at once and fight alongside Geom Mugeuk.

At that moment, the Heavenly Flower Pavilion Mistress, who was sitting beside him, said quietly,

“I had a dream that you went out for a stroll at night, Elder Brother.”

She was sensing the wind of fate blowing through the Divine Cult.

The Extreme Evil Small Demon opened his eyes and rose from his seat.

“If that is your dream, then I must prepare.”

The Extreme Evil Small Demon opened a cabinet hidden inside the white wall.

Srrrrrk.

Inside, more than ten masks were hanging. Each held a different meaning.

“You choose for me.”

“It seems it will be an occasion for celebration, so how about that mask?”

The one she pointed to, brilliant and dazzling in five colors, was the Pleasure Demon Mask worn during major events or banquets within the cult.

The Extreme Evil Small Demon took out the Pleasure Demon Mask as she told him.

The Heavenly Flower Pavilion Mistress turned away and did not watch him change masks. She was walking the path of a fate in which she loved a man whose face she did not even know.

Only when the Extreme Evil Small Demon had changed masks did the sound of the bell begin to reach them.

* * *

During the midnight training gathering, the sound of the bell rang out.

The Fist Demon stopped training and said to Ian,

“You’ll need to prepare a congratulatory gift.”

Because the First Young Master had visited him, the Fist Demon could guess what this order of summons in the dead of night meant.

Quick-witted Ian immediately grasped the meaning of his words.

“Finally!”

Tears welled in both her eyes. Perhaps she had been waiting for this moment even more than the person involved.

“Today’s training ends here.”

Then came the order given to Cheon Sohui.

“You go ahead and prepare the Iron Fists!”

In such a major matter, an order had been given to her. Cheon Sohui accepted it with a moved expression.

“I shall obey your command.”

Cheon Sohui was the first to launch herself away and vanish.

Before leaving, the Fist Demon said one thing to Ian.

“The Second Young Master will want to see you. Hurry and go prepare.”

“Yes! Thank you.”

Ian launched herself toward her quarters. This was a moment she had waited so long for; she could not be late.

She would take out the clothes she had been saving, put them on, and do her makeup. At this important moment, she would congratulate Geom Mugeuk looking her prettiest and most splendid.

* * *

Torches flared up on all sides of the great training ground.

Geom Ujin was seated atop the central platform, and Geom Mugeuk stood facing him.

Geom Muyang had stepped aside and stood next to Sima Ming.

The elite demonic practitioners of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult, including the Demonic Army, surrounded the great training ground like an iron wall.

Now, the Eight Demon Venerables and the demonic practitioners under their command began entering the place.

The first to arrive was the Blood Heaven Blade Demon, dressed in red.

With the colossal Heaven-Destroying Great Blade slung diagonally across his back, he entered the training ground, and behind him followed the Blade Ghosts of the Southern Blade Sect.

Thud, thud, thud, thud, thud, thud!

They were famous for being fierce and rough, but in this moment, they entered in perfect ranks without the slightest disorder.

The next to enter was the One Flower Sword Venerable, dressed in pure white martial robes.

Behind her followed the Demon Swords of the Northern Heaven Sword Clan. Usually, when they met the Blade Ghosts, they would bicker, glare fiercely, and exchange curses, but today, not one of them cast a glance at the other side. As if competing with the Blade Ghosts, they too lined up behind the One Flower Sword Venerable without the slightest disorder.

The next to arrive was the Fist Demon.

Those who followed him were the Iron Fists of the Eastern Fist Gate, who had plunged into murim with nothing but their bare fists. Having dressed themselves properly for the occasion, they wore protectors around their wrists, upon which was drawn the image of the evil spirit that symbolized the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. Though bare-handed, they were not pushed back in the contest of presence at all. Whenever they gathered, their struggle for dominance was always fiercer than even that of the Demon Venerables.

Next, the Soul-Seizing Demon Venerable entered.

Though she was still young and her martial arts relatively inferior, the ghost art practitioners of the Western Phantom Hall who followed behind her were not lacking in momentum compared to any other demonic practitioners. Even amid the demonic energy filling the training ground, their ghostly aura eerily announced who they were.

The procession continued.

The next Demon Venerable to enter was the Drunken Demon.

He walked with steps that seemed drunk and yet not drunk, and behind him followed the Drinking Guests. Though they were intoxicated, they too showed not the slightest disorder. Thus, the drunken air they emitted made even the demonic and ghostly energies that had dominated the venue seem drunk.

Next, the Demon Buddha entered.

The Demon Buddha wore a calmer expression than ever before.

But the Mad Monks who followed him were different. Draped in golden kasayas, they entered chanting Buddhist prayers with eyes full of madness, and the venue was swept up in yet another kind of energy.

This time, the Poison King entered.

The Poison Fangs following behind the Poison King wore expressions more confident than any other demonic practitioners there. Beyond confidence, they were filled with arrogance. If we so choose, all of you will die—that conviction was written all over their faces.

The final Demon Venerable to enter was Geukak Soma.

Behind the Pleasure Ghost Mask he wore, a searing heat could be felt in his eyes.

Behind him followed the Faceless Guests in white masks. Across each white mask were red lines, as if smeared with blood. With gazes as cold as they were chilling, they made it known exactly who they were.

As the elite demonic practitioners of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult gathered in one place, the hall was swept up in a heat that seemed ready to explode at any moment.

There was one person who could cool that heat in an instant.

The Heavenly Demon, Geom Ujin, slowly rose from his seat and walked forward. Even amid this multitude of demonic practitioners, he still revealed a presence that was utterly singular.

The demonic practitioners all bent at the waist and shouted in unison.

“Heavenly Demon Supreme, Divine Cult Invincible!”

It was a thunderous cry that truly shook heaven and earth.

When the repeated shouts ended and the surroundings grew quiet, only then did Geom Ujin speak.

“I have summoned you today to make one important announcement.”

His low yet weighty voice flew into everyone’s ears as if driving itself into them.

Tension flowed through the hall, and not even the sound of breathing could be heard.

“Mugeuk, hear me.”

Geom Mugeuk paid his respects and spoke in a loud voice.

“I receive your solemn command.”

In a voice more resonant and powerful than ever before, Geom Ujin declared,

“From this moment forth, I shall make Mugeuk the next Cult Leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult.”

It was the moment Geom Mugeuk officially became the successor.

Geom Mugeuk raised his head and looked at his father. There were so many things he wanted to say to him, but now was the time to observe the proper rites and follow the mandate.

“For the future of the Divine Cult, I will devote my body and soul, and I will not bring dishonor upon the cult’s name.”

Geom Ujin patted Geom Mugeuk on the shoulder. The encouragement contained in his father’s gaze and touch was sincere.

—Well done, my son.

The moment he heard that single transmitted message, Geom Mugeuk felt as though every difficult moment he had endured until now had been repaid.

—Thank you, Father.

Geom Mugeuk slowly turned around to face the demonic practitioners gathered there.

Their gazes flew toward him.

Geom Mugeuk released his aura. Without concealing it, he opened up the entirety of his presence.

Those who knew Geom Mugeuk and those who did not were all overwhelmed by his presence and powerful aura. If his father was a blood-red dot in a black-and-white painting, then Geom Mugeuk was a dot as blue as the sky. If his father was a large dot, then Geom Mugeuk was a small one.

The eight Demon Venerables all bent at the waist in unison and paid their respects.

“We greet the Young Cult Leader.”

The demonic practitioners of the Eight Demons standing behind them, as well as the demonic practitioners surrounding the area, all cupped their fists at once and bowed their heads.

“We greet the Young Cult Leader!”

The resounding cry echoed throughout the hall.

The Second Young Master and the Young Cult Leader were positions as different as heaven and earth. Not only the demonic practitioners of the cult, but now no one in the martial world could treat Geom Mugeuk lightly.

Waaaaaaaaaaaaaah!

Cheers that shook heaven and earth continued.

Geom Mugeuk’s chest swelled with emotion.

He had run all this way for this very moment. Now, not only for Geom Mugeuk himself, but for the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult as well, it was the moment a new history would unfold.

When the endless cheers finally subsided and the surroundings grew quiet, Geom Mugeuk slowly walked toward the Demon Venerables.

Toward his own Demon Venerables.

It was his first step as the Young Cult Leader.

Chapter 250: In the Midst of the Heat.
--------------------------------------
The eight Demon Venerables stood in a line, staring at Geom Mugeuk. Every demon gathered there was focused on his movements.

Geom Mugeuk walked first toward the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“Lord Blade Demon.”

“Young Cult Leader.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze toward the Blood Heaven Blade Demon was full of warmth.

“I was able to become the successor entirely thanks to you, Elder.”

With every demon watching, Geom Mugeuk loudly acknowledged the Blood Heaven Blade Demon’s contributions.

At once, the Blade Demons standing behind him let out a unified roar and released their demonic energy. If the Heavenly Demon had not been absent, they would have shouted, “Blood Heaven! Blood Heaven!”

Geom Mugeuk looked at them with satisfaction. Thanks to those fierce and rough fellows, he had been able to complete the final stage of the Heavenly Meridian Strengthening Art, and thanks to that, he had achieved immunity to all poisons.

‘Thank you, Blade Demons!’

It was something that would have shocked anyone else who heard it, but Geom Mugeuk found them adorable.

Geom Mugeuk formally expressed his feelings to the Blood Heaven Blade Demon.

“I respect you, Elder!”

He meant it sincerely. The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was the first Demon Venerable to become his man, and the one who had given him everything without reservation.

“It was only because of the Young Cult Leader’s outstanding abilities. What could this old man possibly have done?”

“Please continue to guide me in the future.”

Since this was a formal occasion, the two treated each other with due courtesy.

Of course, Geom Mugeuk was not going to let things end there. In a voice utterly different from his current calm and subdued tone, he sent a voice transmission in his usual manner.

―Elder, let’s have a drink later! We should throw a grand celebration! Oh, right, I can lie down on the bed without washing up now that I’m the Young Cult Leader, right?

The Blood Heaven Blade Demon was overjoyed by Geom Mugeuk’s unchanged banter.

Yes, the Blood Heaven Blade Demon knew that Geom Mugeuk would remain this way even after becoming the Heavenly Demon rather than merely the Young Cult Leader. It was precisely because of that nature that he had fallen so deeply for the person known as Geom Mugeuk.

―Keep fooling around and you’ll get scolded by the Cult Leader. Focus!

Next, Geom Mugeuk walked toward the One-Hua Sword Venerable standing beside him.

“Lord Sword Venerable.”

“Young Cult Leader.”

“I ask for your guidance in the future as well. My swordsmanship has improved greatly thanks to you, Lord Sword Venerable.”

The One-Hua Sword Venerable was inwardly flustered. The spar he had shared with Geom Mugeuk was not something worthy of such praise. He had even lost that spar. Geom Mugeuk was saving his face in front of his subordinates.

Sure enough, the expressions of the Sword Demons brightened, and they too let out a roar as they released their demonic energy.

They tried to shout even louder than the Blade Demons had and release even greater demonic energy.

Even at a gathering like this, the Blade Demons and Sword Demons were engaged in a battle of nerves.

The One-Hua Sword Venerable smiled and said,

“The Young Cult Leader’s talent in martial arts is heaven-given. My assistance must have been insignificant.”

“You are far too modest. Please continue to guide me in the future.”

“Very well.”

Next, Geom Mugeuk stood before the Fist Demon.
